<a id="page-158"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 158]*

Tatruddānam1 || || Ambapālivaggo pathamo[^158-2] || || Ambapāli[^158-3] Sato Bhikkhu || Sāllam[^158-4] Kusalarāsi[^158-5] ca || Sakunagghi Makkaṭo[^158-6] Sūdo || Gilāno Bhikkhuni-vāsakoti[^158-7] || ||

CHAPTER II. NĀLANDAVAGGO DUTIYO.

SN_5,47(3).11. (1) Mahāpuriso.

1. Sāvatthi-nidānaṃ[^158-8] || ||

2. Atha kho āyasmā Sāriputto yena Bhagavā tenupa saṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā || pe || Ekam antam nisinno kho āyasmā Sāriputto Bhagavantam etad avoca || ||

3. Mahāpuriso mahāpuriso ti bhante vuccati || Kittāvatā nu kho bhante mahāpuriso hotī ti || || Vimuttacittattā kho haṃ[^158-9] Sāriputta Mahāpuriso ti vadāmi || avimuttacittattā No mahāpuriso ti vadāmi || || Kathañ ca Sāriputta vimuttacitto hoti || ||

4. Idha Sāriputta bhikkhu kāye kāyanupassī viharati || ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassam || tassa kāye kāyānupassino viharato cittaṃ virajjati vimuccati anupādāya āsavehi || || Vedanāsu || || Citte || || Dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati || ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || tassa dhammesu dhammānupassino viharato cittaṃ virajjati vimuccati anupādāya āsavehi || ||

5. Evaṃ kho Sāriputta vimuttacitto hoti || || Vimuttacittattā[^158-10] kho haṃ Sariputta Mahāpuriso ti vadāmi || avimuttacittattā[^158-11] No mahāpuriso ti vadāmī ti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 159 -->
<a id="page-159"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 159]*

SN_5,47(3).12. (2) Nālandam.

1. Ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Nālandāyaṃ viharati Pāvārikambavane || ||

2. Atha kho āyasmā Sāriputto yena Bhagavā tenupasarikami || upasaṅkamitvā || [pe] || Ekam antam nisinno kho āyasmā Sāriputto Bhagavantam etad avoca || ||

3. Evam pasanno[^159-1] ham bhante Bhagavati || Na cāhu na ca bhavissati na cetarahi vijjati añño samaṇo vā brāhmaṇo vā Bhagavatā bhiyyo bhiññataro yad idaṃ sambodhiyan ti || || Uḷārā kho tyāyaṃ[^159-2] Sāriputta āsabhi[^159-3] vācā bhāsitā ekaṃso gahito sīhanādo nadito || || Evam pasanno[^159-4] ham bhante Bhagavati na cāhu na ca bhavissati na cetarahi vijjati añño samaṇo vā brāhmaṇo vā Bhagavatā bhiyyo bhiññataro yad idaṃ sambodhiyan ti || ||

4. Kinnu te Sāriputta ye te ahesum atītam addhānam arahanto sammāsambuddhā[^159-5] || sabbe te bhagavanto[^159-6] cetasā cetopariccaviditā || Evaṃsīlā te bhagavanto ahesuṃ iti vā || Evaṃ dhammā te bhagavanto ahesuṃ iti vā || Evam paññā te bhagavanto ahesum iti vā[^159-7] || {Evaṃvihārino} te bhagavanto ahesuṃ iti vā Evaṃvimuttā te bhagavanto ahesum iti vā ti[^159-8] || || No hetam bhante || ||

5. Kim pana te[^159-9] Sāriputta ye te bhavissanti anāgatam addhānam arahanto sammāsambuddhā || sabbe te bhagavanto cetasā cetopariccaviditā evaṃsilā te bhagavanto bhavissanti iti vā evaṃ dhammā te bhagavanto bhavissanti iti vā evampaññā te bhagavanto bhavissanti iti vā evaṃvihārino te bhagavanto bhavissanti iti vā[^159-10] || evaṃvimuttā kho[^159-11] bhagavanto bhavissanti iti vā ti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 160 -->
<a id="page-160"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 160]*

No hetam bhante || ||

6. Kim pana tyāhaṃ[^160-1] Sāriputta etarahi arahaṃ sammāsambuddho cetasā cetopariccavidīto || evaṃsīlo bhagavā iti vā evaṃdhammo bhagavā iti vā evampañño bhagavā iti vā evaṃvihārī bhagavā iti vā evaṃvimutto bhagavā iti vā ti[^160-2] || || No hetam bhante || ||

7. Ettha ca te Sāriputta atītānāgatapaccuppannesu arahantesu sammāsambuddhesu cetopariyāyañāṇaṃ natthi || atha kiṃcarahi tayā[^160-3] Sāriputta ulārā āsabhi vācā[^160-4] bhāsitā[^160-5] ekaṃso gahito sīhanādo nadito || Evam pasannoham[^160-6] bhante Bhagavati na cāhu na ca bhavissati na cetarahi vijjati añño samaṇo va brāhmaṇo vā Bhagavato[^160-7] bhiyyo bhiññataro yadidaṃ sambodhiyan ti || Na kho me tam bhante atītānāgatapaccuppannesu arahantesu sammāsambuddhesu cetopariyāyañāṇam atthi || api ca dhammanvayo vidito || ||

8. Seyyathāpi bhante rañño paccantimaṃ nagaram daḷhuddāpaṃ daḷhapākāratoraṇam ekadvāraṃ || tatrassa dovāriko paṇḍito viyatto[^160-8] medhāvī aññātānaṃ nivāretvā ñātānam pavesetā || || So[^160-9] tassa nagarassa samantā anupariyāya[^160-10] patham anukkamamāno na passeyya pākārasandhiṃ vā pākāravivaraṃ vā antamaso biḷāranissakkanamattam[^160-11] pi || tassa evam assa || Ye kho[^160-12] keci oḷarikā pāṇā imaṃ nagaram pavisanti vā nikkhamanti vā sabbe te iminā va dvārena pavisanti vā nikkhamanti vā ti || || Evam eva kho me[^160-12] bhante dhammanvayo vidito || ||

9. Ye pi[^160-12] te bhante[^160-12] ahesum atītam addhānaṃ arahanto sammāsambuddhā sabbe te bhagavanto pañcanīvaraṇe pahāya cetaso upakkilese paññāya dubbalīkaraṇe[^160-13] catusu satipaṭṭhānesu supatiṭṭhitacittā[^160-14] sattabojjhaṅge


<!-- PTS Page 161 -->
<a id="page-161"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 161]*

yathābhūtam bhāvetvā anuttaraṃ sammāsambodhim abhisambujjhiṃsu || || Ye pi te bhante bhavissanti anāgatam addhānam arahanto sammāsambuddhā sabbe te bhagavanto[^161-1] pañcanīvaraṇe pahāya cetaso upakkilese paññāya dubbalīkaraṇe catusu satipaṭṭhānesu supatiṭṭhitacittā[^161-2] sattabojjhaṅge yathābhūtam bhāvetvā anuttaraṃ sammāsambodhim abhisambujjhissanti || || Bhagavā pi bhante etarahi arahaṃ sammāsambuddho pañcanīvaraṇe pahāya cetaso uppakkilese[^161-3] paññāya dubbalīkaraṇe catusu satipaṭṭhānesu supatiṭṭhitacitto[^161-4] sattabojjhaṅge yathābhūtam bhāvetvā anuttaram sammāsambodhim abhisambuddhoti || ||

10. Sādhu sādhu Sāriputta tasmā ti ha tvaṃ Sāriputta imam dhammapariyāyam abhikkhaṇam bhāseyyāsi[^161-5] bhikkhūnam bhikkhūnīnam upāsakānam upāsikānam || yesam pi hi Sāriputta moghapurisānam bhavissati Tathāgate kaṅkhā vā vimati vā tesam pi mam dhammapariyāyam sutvā yā tesaṃ[^161-6] Tathāgate kaṅkhā vā vimati vā sā pahīyissatī ti || ||

SN_5,47(3).13. (3) Cuṇḍo.

1. Ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme || ||

2. Tena kho pana samayena āyasmā Sāriputto Magadhesu viharati Nālagāmake[^161-7] || ābādhiko dukkhito bāḷhagilāno || Cundo ca[^161-8] samaṇuddeso āyasmato Sāriputtassa upaṭṭhāko[^161-9] hoti || ||

3. Atha āyasmā Sāriputto tena ābādhena parinibbāyi || ||

4. Atha kho Cundo samaṇuddeso āyasmato Sāriputtassa pattacīvaram adāya yena Sāvatthi-Jetavanam Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmo yenāyasmā Ānando tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantam Ānandam abhivādetvā ekam antaṃ nisīdi || || Ekam antaṃ nisinno kho Cundo sama-


<!-- PTS Page 162 -->
<a id="page-162"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 162]*

ṇuddeso āyasmantam Ānandam etad avoca || || Āyasmā bhante Sariputto parinibbuto idam assa pattacīvaranti[^162-1] || ||

5. Atthi kho idam āvuso Cunda[^162-2] kathāpābhatam Bhagavantaṃ dassanāya || āyāmāvuso Cunda yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkamissāma || upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavato[^162-3] etam attham ārocessāmāti || || Evam bhante ti kho Cundo samaṇuddeso āyasmato Ānandassa paccassosi || ||

6. Atha kho āyasmā ca Ānando Cundo ca samaṇuddeso yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkamiṃsu || upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antaṃ nīsīdiṃsu || || Ekam antaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavantam etad avoca || || Ayam bhante Cundo samaṇuddeso evam āha || Āyasmā bhante Sāriputto parinibbuto idam assa pattacīvaran ti[^162-1] || || Api ca[^162-4] me bhante madhurakajāto[^162-5] viya kāyo || disā pi me na pakkhāyanti dhammā pi me[^162-6] na paṭibhanti āyasmā Sāriputto parinibbuto ti sutvā ti[^162-7] || ||

7. Kinnu te[^162-8] Ānanda Sāriputto sīlakkhandhaṃ vā ādāya parinibbuto || samādhikkhandhaṃ vā ādāya parinibbuto || paññakkhandhaṃ vā ādāya parinibbuto[^162-9] || vimuttikkhandhaṃ vā ādāya parinibbuto || vimuttiññanadassanakkhandhaṃ vā ādāya parinibbuto ti || || Na ca[^162-4] kho me tam[^162-10] bhante āyasmā Sāriputto sīlakkhandhaṃ vā ādāya parinibbuto || pe || vimuttiññāṇadassanakkhandhaṃ vā ādāya parinibbuto || Api ca me[^162-4] bhante āyasmā Sāriputto ovādako ahosi otiṇṇo[^162-4] viññāpako sandassako samādapako samuttejako sampahaṃsako akilāsu dhammadesanāya anuggāhako sabrahmacārīnaṃ tam[^162-11] mayam āyasmato Sāriputtassa dhammojaṃ dhammabhogaṃ dhammānuggaham anussarāmāti || ||

8. Nanu tam Ānanda mayā paṭigacceva[^162-12] akkhātaṃ sabbehi piyehi manāpehi nānābhāvo vinābhāvo aññathā-


<!-- PTS Page 163 -->
<a id="page-163"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 163]*

bhāvo || tam kutettha Ānanda labbhā || Yan taṃ jātam bhūtaṃ saṅkhatam palokadhammaṃ[^163-1] taṃ vata mā palujjīti netaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati || ||

9. Seyyathāpi Ānanda mahato rukkhassa tiṭṭhato sāravato yo mahantataro[^163-2] khandho so palujjeyya || evam eva kho Ānanda mahato bhikkhusaṅghassa tiṭṭhato sāravato Sāriputto parinibbuto taṃ kutettha Ānanda labbhā yan taṃ jātam bhūtaṃ saṅkhataṃ palokadhammaṃ taṃ vata mā palujjīti netaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati || ||

10. Tasmā ti hānanda attadīpā viharatha attasaraṇā anaññasaraṇā dhammadīpā dhammasaraṇā anaññasaraṇā || || Kathañcānanda bhikkhu attadīpo[^163-3] viharati attasaraṇo anaññasaraṇo dhammadīpo dhammasaraṇo anaññasaraṇo[^163-4] || ||

11. Idhānanda bhikkhu kāye kāyānupassī viharati || ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || Vedanāsu || Citte || Dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati || ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || Evaṃ kho Ānanda bhikkhu attadīpo viharati attasaraṇo anaññasaraṇo dhammadīpo dhammasaraṇo anaññasaraṇo[^163-5] || ||

12. Ye hi keci Ānanda etarahi vā mamaccaye[^163-6] vā attadipā viharissanti attasaraṇā anaññasaraṇā dhammadīpā dhammasaraṇā anaññasaraṇā || tamatagge[^163-7] pete[^163-8] Ānanda bhikkhū bhavissanti ye keci sikkhākāmā ti || ||

SN_5,47(3).14. (4) Ceḷam.

1. Ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Vajjīsu viharati Ukkacelāyaṃ[^163-9] Gaṅgāya nadiyā[^163-10] tīre mahatā bhikkhusaṅghena saddhim aciraparinibbutesu Sāriputta-Moggalānesu || ||

2. Tena kho pana samayena Bhagavā bhikkhu-saṅghaparivuto ajjhokāse[^163-11] nisinno hoti || || Atha kho Bhagavā tunhībhūtam[^163-12] bhikkhusaṅgham anuviloketvā bhikkhū āmantesi || ||


<!-- PTS Page 164 -->
<a id="page-164"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 164]*

3. Api myāyam[^164-1] bhikkhave parisā suññā viya khāyati || parinibbutesu Sāriputta-Moggalānesu[^164-2] suññā[^164-3] me bhikkhave parisā hoti || anapekkhā tassam[^164-4] disāyam hoti yassaṃ dīsāyaṃ Sāriputta-Moggalānā viharanti || ||

4. Ye hi te bhikkhave ahesum atītamadhānam arahanto sammāsambuddhā || tesam pi bhagavantānam etaparamam yeva sāvakayugam ahosi || seyyathāpi mayhaṃ SāriputtaMoggalānā || || Ye pi te bhikkhave bhavissanti anāgatam addhānam arahanto sammāsambuddhā || tesam pi bhagavantānam etaparamaṃ yeva sāvakayugam bhavissati || seyyathāpi mayhaṃ Sāriputta-Moggalānā[^164-5] || ||

5. Acchariyam bhikkhave sāvakānam abbhutam bhikkhave sāvakānam satthu ca nāma sāsanakarā[^164-6] bhavissanti ovādapatikarā || catunnaṃ ca parisānam piyā bhavissanti manāpā garū ca[^164-7] bhāvaniyā ca || || Acchariyam bhikkhave Tathāgatassa abbhutam bhikkhave Tathāgatassa || evarūpe pi nāma sāvakayuge[^164-8] parinibbute natthi Tathāgatassa soko vā paridevo vā || tam kutettha bhikkhave[^164-9] labbhā || yantaṃ jātaṃ bhūtaṃ saṅkhataṃ palokadhammaṃ[^164-10] taṃ vata ma palujjīti netaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati || ||

6. Seyyathāpi bhikkhave mahato rukkhassa tiṭṭhato sāravato ye[^164-9] mahantatarā[^164-11] khandhā te palujjeyyuṃ || Evam eva kho bhikkhave mahato bhikkhusaṅghassa tiṭṭhato sāravato Sāriputta-Moggalānā parinibbutā || taṃ kutettha[^164-12] bhikkhave labbhā || yan taṃ jātam bhūtam saṅkhatam palokadhammaṃ taṃ vata mā palujjīti || netaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati || ||

7. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave attadīpā viharathā- -anaññasaraṇā || ||

8. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu kāye kāyānupassī- -anaññasaraṇo || ||

<a id="page-165"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 165]*

9. Ye hi keci bhikkhave etarahi vā mamaccaye[^165-1] vā attadīpā viharissanti- -tamatagge[^165-1] bhikkhave bhikkhu bhavissanti ye keci sikkhākāmā ti[^165-2] || ||

SN_5,47(3).15. (15) Bāhiyo (or Bāhiko).

1. [^165-3]Sāvatthi nidanaṃ || ||

2. Atha kho āyasmā Bāhiyo[^165-4] yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā || [pe] || ekam antaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Bāhiyo Bhagavantam etad avoca || || Sādhu me bhante saṅkhittena dhammaṃ desetu || yaṃ aham Bhagavato dhammaṃ sutvā eko vūpakaṭṭho apamatto ātāpī pahitatto vihareyyanti || Tasmā ti ha tvam Bāhiya ādim eva visodhehi[^165-5] kusalesu dhammesu || || Ko cādi kusalānaṃ[^165-6] dhammānaṃ || sīlañca suvisuddhaṃ diṭṭhi ca[^165-7] ujukā || ||

3. Yato ca[^165-8] kho te Bāhiya sīlañca suvisuddham[^165-9] bhavissati diṭṭhi ca[^165-10] ujukā || tato tvam[^165-11] Bāhiya sīlam nissāya[^165-12] sīle patiṭṭhāya cattāro satipaṭṭhāne bhāveyyāsi || katame cattāro || ||

4. Idha tvam Bāhiya kāye kāyānupassī viharāhi || ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || Vedanāsu || Citte || Dhammesu dhammānupassī viharāhi || ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || || Yato kho tvam Bāhiya sīlam nissāya sīle patiṭṭhāya ime cattāro satipaṭṭhāne evam bhāvessasi[^165-13] || tato {tuyham} Bāhiya yā[^165-14] ratti vā divaso vā āgamissasi || vuddhi[^165-15] yeva paṭikaṅkhā kusalesu dhammesu no parihānī ti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 166 -->
<a id="page-166"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 166]*

5. Atha kho āyasmā Bāhiyo Bhagavato bhāsitam abhinanditvā anumoditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pakkāmi || ||

6. Atha kho āyasmā Bāhiyo eko vupakaṭṭho apamatto ātāpī pahitatto viharanto na cirasseva yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadeva agārasmā anagāriyam pabbajanti tad anuttaram brahmacariyapariyosānaṃ[^166-1] diṭṭheva dhamme sayam abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja vihāsi || Khīnā jāti vusitam brahmacariyam kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ nāparam ittatthāyā ti abbhaññāsi || ||

7. Aññataro ca panāyasmā Bāhiyo[^166-2] arahatam ahosī ti || ||

SN_5,47(3).16. (6) Uttiyo.

1. Sāvatthi nīdānam[^166-3] || || 2--4. Atha kho āyasmā Uttiyo[^166-4] yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkami || [pe] || tato tvam Uttiya gamissasi maccudheyyassa pāranti || pe || ||

5.6.7. Aññataro ca panāyasmā Uttiyo arahatam ahosī ti[^166-5] || ||

SN_5,47(3).17. (7) Ariyo.

1. Sāvatthi[^166-6] || ||

2. Cattāro me bhikkhave satipaṭṭhānā bhāvitā bahulīkatā ariyā niyyānikā niyyanti[^166-7] takkarassa sammādukkhākkhayāya || katame cattāro || ||

3. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu kāye kāyānupassī viharati || ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhadomanassaṃ || Vedanāsu || Citte || Dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || ||


<!-- PTS Page 167 -->
<a id="page-167"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 167]*

4. Ime kho bhikkhave[^167-1] cattāro satipaṭṭhānā bhāvitā bahulīkatā ariyā niyyānikā niyyanti[^167-2] takkarassa sammādukkhakkhayāyā ti || ||

SN_5,47(3).18. (8) Brahmā.

1. Ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Uruvelāyaṃ viharati najjā Nerañjarāya tīre Ajapālanigrodhe pathamābhisambuddho || ||

2. Atha kho Bhagavato rahogatassa paṭisallīnassa evaṃ cetaso parivitakko udapādi || || Ekāyano yam[^167-3] maggo sattānam visuddhiyā sokaparidevānam[^167-4] samatikkamāya dukkhadomanassānam atthagamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya yad idaṃ cattāro satipaṭṭhānā || katame cattāro || ||

3. Kāye vā bhikkhu[^167-5] kāyānupassī vihareyya || ātāpī sampajāno satimā vinneyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || Vedanāsu vā bhikkhu || Citte vā bhikkhu || || Dhammesu vā bhikkhu dhammānupassī vihareyya || ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || || Ekāyano yam maggo sattānaṃ visuddhiyā sokaparidevānaṃ samatikkamāya dukkhadomanassānam atthagamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya yadidaṃ[^167-6] cattāro satipaṭṭhānā ti || ||

4. Atha kho Brahmā sahampati Bhagavato cetasā cetoparivitakkam aññāya[^167-7] seyyathāpi nāma balavā puriso sammiñjitaṃ[^167-8] vā bāham pasāreyya pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ sammiñjeyya[^167-8] || evam eva kho Brahmaloke antarahito Bhagavato purato pāturahosi || || Atha kho Brahmā sahampati ekaṃsam uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yena Bhagavā tenañjalim paṇāmetvā Bhagavantam etad avoca || ||

5. Evam etam Bhagavā evam etaṃ Sugata || ekāyano yam bhante maggo sattānaṃ visuddhiyā sokaparidevānam samatikkamāya dukkhadomanassānam atthagamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya yadidaṃ cattāro


<!-- PTS Page 168 -->
<a id="page-168"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 168]*

satipaṭṭhānā || katame cattāro || || Kāye vā bhante bhikkhu kāyanupassī vihareyya[^168-1] || ātāpī sampajāno satimā || vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || Vedanāsu vā bhante bhikkhu || Citte vā bhante bhikkhu || Dhammesu vā bhante dhammānupassī vihareyya || ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || || Ekāyano yam bhante maggo sattānaṃ visuddhiyā sokaparidevānam samatikkamāya dukkhadomanassānam atthagamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya yad idam[^168-2] cattāro satipaṭṭhānāti || ||

6. Idam avoca Brahmā sahampati || idaṃ vatvā Brahmā sahampati[^168-3] athāparam etad avoca || || Ekāyanaṃ[^168-4] jātikhayantadassī || Maggam pajānāti hitānukampi || Etena maggena atariṃsu[^168-5] pubbe || Tarissanti[^168-6] ye[^168-7] taranti ca[^168-7] oghan ti || ||

SN_5,47(3).19. (9) Sedakam or Ekantaka(?).

1. Ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Sumbhesu[^168-8] viharati Sedakaṃ[^168-9] nāma Sumbhānaṃ nigamo || ||

2. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi || || Bhūtapubbam bhikkhave caṇḍālavaṃsiko caṇḍālavaṃsam ussāpetvā Medakathālikam antevāsim[^168-10] āmantesi || Ehi tvaṃ samma Medakathālike[^168-11] Caṇḍālavaṃsam abhirūhitvā mama uparikhandhe titthāhī ti || Evam ācariyā ti kho bhikkhave Medakathālikā antevāsī Caṇḍālavaṃsikassa paṭissutvā Caṇḍālavaṃsam abhirūhitvā ācariyassa uparikhandhe aṭṭhāsi || ||

3. Atha kho bhikkhave Caṇḍālavaṃsiko[^168-12] Medakathālikam antevāsim etad avoca || Tvaṃ samma Medakathālike mamaṃ[^168-13] rakkha aham tvaṃ[^168-14] rakkhissāmi evaṃ mayam


<!-- PTS Page 169 -->
<a id="page-169"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 169]*

aññamaññaguttā[^169-1] aññamaññarakkhitā[^169-1] sippāni ceva dassessāma[^169-2] lābhañ[^169-3] ca lacchāma sotthinā ca[^169-4] caṇḍālavaṃsam[^169-5] orohissāmā ti || ||

4. Evaṃ vutte bhikkhave Medakathālikā[^169-6] antevāsī Caṇḍālavaṃsikam etad avoca || || Na kho panetam[^169-7] ācariya evam bhavissati || tvam ācariya attānaṃ rakkha aham attanaṃ[^169-8] rakkhissāmi || || Evam mayam attaguttā attarakkhitā sippāni ceva dassessāma lābhañca lacchāma sotthinā ca[^169-4] caṇḍalavaṃsā orohissāmā[^169-9] ti || || So tattha ñāyo ti || ||

5. Bhagavā[^169-10] avoca || || Yathā Medakathālikā antevāsī[^169-11] ācariyam avoca || Attānam bhikkhave rakkhissāmī ti satipaṭṭhānaṃ sevitabbaṃ || Param rakkhissamā ti satipaṭṭhānaṃ sevitabbaṃ || || Attānam bhikkhave rakkhanto paraṃ rakkhati || paraṃ rakkhanto attānaṃ rakkhati || ||

6. Kathañca bhikkhave attānam rakkhanto param rakkhati || āsevanāya bhāvanāya bahulīkammena[^169-12] || || Evaṃ kho bhikkhave attānaṃ rakkhanto paraṃ rakkhati || ||

7. Kathañca bhikkhave paraṃ rakkhanto attānaṃ rakkhati || khantiyā avihiṃsāya mettatāya[^169-13] anudayatāya[^169-14] || || Evaṃ kho bhikkhave paraṃ rakkhanto attānaṃ rakkhati || ||

8. Attānaṃ bhikkhave rakkhissāmī ti satipaṭṭhānaṃ[^169-15] sevitabbam || Paraṃ rakkhissāmī ti satipaṭṭhānaṃ sevitabbaṃ || attānam bhikkhave rakkhanto paraṃ rakkhati || paraṃ rakkhanto attānaṃ rakkhatī ti || ||

SN_5,47(3).20. (10) Janapada or Ekantaka(?).

1. Evam me sutam[^169-16] Ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Sumbhesu[^169-17] viharati Sedakam[^169-18] nāma Sumbhānaṃ nigamo || ||


<!-- PTS Page 170 -->
<a id="page-170"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 170]*

2. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi || [pe] ||

3. Seyyathāpi bhikkhave Janapadakalyāṇī janapadakalyāṇī ti kho bhikkhave mahājanakāyo sannipateyya || Sā kho panassa[^170-1] janapadakalyāṇī paramapāsāvinī nacce paramapāsāvinī gīte || Janapadakalyāṇī naccati gāyatīti kho bhikkhave bhiyyosomattāya mahā janakāyo sannipateyya || || Atha puriso āgaccheyya jīvitukāmo amaritukāmo sukhakāmo dukkhapaṭikūlo tam enaṃ evaṃ[^170-2] vadeyyum[^170-3] || Ayan te ambho purisa samatittiko[^170-4] telapatto antarena ca[^170-5] mahāsamajjam[^170-6] antarena ca[^170-5] janapadakalyāṇiṃ[^170-7] parihātabbo[^170-8] || puriso ca te[^170-9] ukkhitāsiko piṭṭhito piṭṭhito anubandhissati yattheva[^170-10] naṃ thokam[^170-11] pi chaddessati[^170-12] tattheva te siro pātessatī ti[^170-13] || || Taṃ kim maññatha bhikkhave || api nu so puriso amum telapattam[^170-14] amanasikaritvā bahiddhā pamādam āhareyyāti[^170-15] || || No hetam bhante || ||

4. Upamā kho myāyam bhikkhave katā atthassa viññāpanāya || ayaṃ cevettha[^170-16] attho || Samatittiko telapatto ti kho bhikkhave kāyagatāya etaṃ satiyā adhivacanaṃ || ||

5. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave evam sikkhitabbaṃ || || Kāyagatā sati no[^170-17] bhāvitā bhavissati bahulīkatā yānikatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā[^170-18] ti || Evaṃ hi vo bhikkhave sikkhitabbanti || || Nāḷandavaggo dutiyo[^170-19] || || Tassa[^170-20] uddānaṃ || ||

##### *[PTS Page 171]*

Mahāpuriso1 Nāḷandam2 || Cundo[^171-3] Celañca Bāhiyo[^171-4] || || Uttiyo Ariyo Brahmā || Sedakaṃ Janapadena cā ti[^171-5] || ||

CHAPTER III. SīLAṬṬHITIVAGGO TATIYO.

SN_5,47(3).21. (1) Sīlam.

1. Evam me sutaṃ[^171-6] Ekaṃ samayam āyasmā ca Ānando āyasmā ca Bhaddo Pāṭaliputte viharanti Kukkuṭārāme || ||

2. Atha kho āyasmā Bhaddo sāyaṇhasamayam paṭisallānā vuṭṭhito yenāyasmā Ānando tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā Ānandena saddhiṃ sammodi || pe || āyasmantam Ānandam etad avoca || ||

3. Yānimani āvuso Ānanda kusalāni sīlāni vuttāni Bhagavatā || imāni kusalāni sīlāni kimatthiyāni vuttāni Bhagavatā ti || Sādhu sādhu āvuso Bhadda[^171-7] || bhaddako kho te āvuso Bhadda[^171-7] ummaggo[^171-8] bhaddakam paṭibhānam kalyāṇī[^171-9] paripucchā || || Evaṃ hi tvam avuso Bhadda pucchasi || Yānimāni āvuso Ānanda kusalāni sīlāni vuttāni Bhagavatā || imāni kusalāni sīlāni kimatthiyāni vuttāni Bhagavatā ti || || Evam āvuso ti[^171-10] || ||

4. Yānimani āvuso Bhadda kusalāni sīlāni vuttāni Bhagavatā imāni kusalāni sīlāni yāvad eva catunnam satipaṭṭhānānam bhāvanāya vuttāni Bhagavatā || katamesam catunnaṃ || ||

5. Idhāvuso bhikkhu kāye[^171-11] kāyānupassī viharati || ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || Vedanāsu || Citte || Dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati || ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhadomanassaṃ || ||


<!-- PTS Page 172 -->
<a id="page-172"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 172]*

Yānimanī āvuso Bhadda kusalāni sīlāni vuttāni Bhagavatā || imani kusalāni sīlāni yāvad eva imesaṃ catunaṃ satipaṭṭhānānam bhāvanāya vuttāni Bhagavatā ti || ||

SN_5,47(3).22. (2) Ṭhiti.

1. Taṃ yeva nidānaṃ[^172-1] || ||

2. Ekam antam nisinnam- -etad avoca[^172-2] || ||

3. Ko nu kho āvuso Ānanda hetu ko paccayo yena tathāgate parinibbute saddhammo na ciraṭṭhitiko hoti || ko panāvuso hetu ko paccayo yena tathāgate parinibbute saddhammo ciraṭṭhitiko hotī ti || || Sādhu sādhu āvuso Bhadda || bhaddako kho te āvuso Bhadda ummaggo[^172-3] bhaddakam[^172-4] paṭibhānaṃ kalyaṇī[^172-5] paripucchā || evaṃ hi tvam āvuso Bhadda pucchasi || Ko nu kho āvuso Ānanda hetu ko paccayo yena tathāgate parinibbute saddhammo na ciraṭṭhitiko hoti || ko panāvuso Ānanda hetu ko paccayo yena tathāgate parinibbute saddhammo ciraṭṭhitiko hotī ti || || Evam āvuso ti || ||

4. Catunnaṃ kho āvuso satipaṭṭhānānam abhāvitattā abahulīkatattā Tathāgate parinibbute saddhammo na ciraṭṭhitiko hoti || Catunnaṃ ca kho āvuso satipaṭṭhānānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā[^172-6] Tathāgate parinibbute saddhammo ciraṭṭhitiko hoti || katamesaṃ catunnaṃ || ||

5. Idhāvuso bhikkhu kāye kāyānupassī viharati || ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || Vedanāsu || Citte || Dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati || ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || || Imesaṃ kho āvuso catunnaṃ satipaṭṭhānānam abhāvitattā abahulīkatattā tathāgate parinibbute saddhammo na ciraṭṭhitiko hoti || imesaṃ ca kho āvuso catunnaṃ satipaṭṭhānānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā tathāgate parinibbute saddhammo ciraṭṭhitiko hotī ti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 173 -->
<a id="page-173"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 173]*

SN_5,47(3).23. (3) Parihānaṃ. 1--2. Pāṭaliputte[^173-1] viharanti[^173-2] Kukkuṭārāme[^173-3] || ||

3. Ko nu kho āvuso Ānanda hetu ko paccayo yena saddhammaparihānaṃ[^173-4] hoti || ko nu kho āvuso[^173-5] Ānanda hetu ko paccayo yena saddhamma-aparihānaṃ[^173-4] hetu || || Sādhu sādhu āvuso Bhadda || bhaddhako kho te āvuso Bhadda ummaggo[^173-6] bhaddakam[^173-7] paṭibhānaṃ kalyāṇī paripucchā || evaṃ hi tvam āvuso Bhadda pucchasi || Ko nu āvuso hetu ko paccayo yena saddhammaparihānaṃ[^173-4] hoti || ko panāvuso Ānanda hetu ko paccayo yena saddhammaaparihānaṃ[^173-4] hoti ti || || Evam āvuso ti || ||

4. Catunnaṃ kho āvuso satipaṭṭhānānam abhāvitattā abahulīkatattā saddhammaparihānaṃ[^173-8] hoti || catunnaṃ ca[^173-9] kho āvuso satipaṭṭhānānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā saddhamma-aparihānaṃ[^173-10] hoti || katamesaṃ catunnaṃ || ||

5. Idhāvuso bhikkhu kāye[^173-11] kāyānupassī viharati || ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || Vedanāsu || Citte || Dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati || ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || || Imesaṃ kho āvuso[^173-12] catunnaṃ satipaṭṭhānānam abhāvitattā abahulīkatattā saddhammaparihānaṃ hoti || imesaṃ[^173-13] kho āvuso catunnaṃ satipaṭṭhānānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā saddhamma-aparihānaṃ[^173-8] hotī ti || ||

SN_5,47(3).24. (4) Suddhakam.

1. Sāvatthinidānam[^173-14] || ||

2. Cattāro me bhikkhave satipaṭṭhānā || katame cattāro || ||

3. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu kāye kāyānupassī viharati || ātāpi sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ ||


<!-- PTS Page 174 -->
<a id="page-174"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 174]*

Vedanāsu || Citte || Dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati || ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || || Ime kho bhikkhave cattāro satipaṭṭhānā ti || ||

SN_5,47(3).25. (5) Brāhmaṇa.

1. Evam me sutaṃ ekam samayam Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme[^174-1] || ||

2. Atha kho aññataro brāhmaṇo yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammodi || sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ vītisāretvā ekam antaṃ nisīdi || || Ekam antaṃ nisinno kho so brāhmaṇo Bhagavantam etad avoca || ||

3. Ko nu kho bho Gotama hetu ko paccayo yena Tathāgate parinibbute saddhammo na ciraṭṭhitiko hoti || ko pana bho Gotama hetu ko paccayo yena tathāgate parinibbute saddhammo ciraṭṭhitiko hotī ti || ||

4. Catunnaṃ kho brāhmaṇa satipatthānānam abhāvitattā- [^174-5]. Idha brāhmaṇa bhikkhu kāye kāyānupassī- -domanassaṃ || Imesaṃ kho brāhmaṇa catunnaṃ- -ciraṭṭhitiko hotī ti[^174-2] || ||

6. Evaṃ vutte so brāhmaṇo Bhagavantam etad avoca || || Abhikkantam bho Gotama || la-pe || upāsakam mam bhavaṃ Gotamo dhāretu[^174-3] ajjatagge pāṇupetaṃ saraṇaṃ gatan ti || ||

SN_5,47(3).26. (6) Padesam.

1. Ekaṃ samayam āyasmā ca Sāriputto āyasmā ca Mahā-Moggalāno āyasmā ca Anuruddho Sākete viharanti[^174-4] Kanṭakīvane[^174-5] || ||

2. Atha kho āyasmā ca Sāriputto āyasmā ca MahāMoggalāno sāyaṇhasamayam patisallāṇā vuṭṭhito yenāyasmā Anuruddho tenupasaṅkamiṃsa || upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā Anuruddhena saddhiṃ sammodiṃsu || sammodanīyaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 175 -->
<a id="page-175"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 175]*

Kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ vītisāretvā ekam antaṃ nisīdiṃsu || || Ekam antaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Sāriputto āyasmantam Anuruddham etad avoca || ||

3. Sekho sekho ti āvuso Anuruddha vuccati || kittāvatā nu kho āvuso Sekho ti hotī ti || || Catunnam kho āvuso satipaṭṭhānānam padesaṃ bhāvitattā sekho hoti || katamesaṃ catunnaṃ || ||

4. Idhāvuso bhikkhu kāye kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyyyaloke abhijjhadomanassaṃ || Vedanāsu || Citte || Dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || || Imesaṃ kho āvuso catunnaṃ satipaṭṭhānānam padesam bhāvitattā sekho hotī ti || ||

SN_5,47(3).27. (7) Samattam. 1--2. Taṃ yeva nidānaṃ || || Ekam antam- -Anuruddham etad avoca[^175-1] || ||

3. Asekho asekho ti āvuso Anuruddha vuccati || kittāvatā nu kho āvuso Asekho hotī ti || || Catunnaṃ kho āvuso satipaṭṭhānānam samattam bhāvitattā asekho hoti[^175-2] || || Katamesaṃ catunnaṃ || ||

4. Idhāvuso bhikkhu kāye kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || Vedanāsu || Citte || Dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassam || || Imesaṃ kho āvuso catunnaṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ samattā[^175-3] bhāvitattā asekho hotī ti || ||

SN_5,47(3).28. (8) Loko. 1--2. [^175-4] Taṃ yeva nidānaṃ || || Ekam antam nisinno- -Anuruddham etad avoca || ||

3. Katamesam āyasmā[^175-5] Anuruddha dhammānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mahābhiññatam[^175-6] patto ti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 176 -->
<a id="page-176"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 176]*

Catunnaṃ āvuso[^176-1] satipaṭṭhānānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mahābhiññatam[^176-2] patto || katamesaṃ catunnaṃ || ||

4. Idhāham āvuso kāye kāyanupassī viharāmi ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhadomanassaṃ || Vedanāsu || Citte || Dhammesu dhammanupassī viharāmi ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || || Imesaṃ khvāham[^176-3] āvuso catunnaṃ satipaṭṭhānānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mahabhiññatam patto || ||

5. Imesaṃ ca[^176-4] panāham āvuso catunnaṃ satipaṭṭhānānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā sahassaṃ lokam abhijānāmīti ||

SN_5,47(3).29. (9) Sirivaḍḍho.

1. Ekaṃ samayam āyasmā Ānando Rājagahe viharati Veḷuvane Kalandakanivāpe || ||

2. Tena kho pana samayena Sirivaḍḍho[^176-5] gahapati ābādhiko hoti || dukkhito bāḷhagilāno || ||

3. Atha kho Sirivaḍḍho gahapati aññataram purisam āmantesi || Ehi tvam ambho purisa yenāyasmā Ānando tenupasaṅkama || upasaṅkamitvā mama vacanena āyasmato Ānandassa pāde sirasā[^176-6] vanda || Sirivaḍḍho bhante[^176-7] gahapati ābādhiko dukkhito bāḷhagilāno || so āyasmato Ānandassa pāde sirasā vandatī ti evañca vadeti || Sādhu kira bhante āyasmā Ānando yena[^176-8] Sirivaḍḍhassa gahapatissa[^176-9] nivesanaṃ || tenupasaṅkamatu anukampam upādāyāti || || Evam bhante ti kho so[^176-10] puriso Sirivaḍḍhassa gahapatissa paṭissutvā yenāyasmā Ānando tenupasaṅkami || ||

4. Upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ Ānandam abhivādetvā ekam antaṃ nisīdi || ekam antaṃ nisinno kho so puriso āyasmantam Ānandam etad avoca || Sirivaḍḍho bhante gahapati ābādhiko dukkhito bāḷhagilāno || so āyasmato Ānandassa pāde sirasā vandati || evañca vadeti[^176-11] || Sādhu kira bhante āyasmā Ānando yena Sirivaḍḍhassa gahapatissa nivesanaṃ tenupasaṅkamatu anukampam upādāyā ti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 177 -->
<a id="page-177"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 177]*

Adhivāsesi kho āyasmā Ānando tuṇhībhāvena || ||

5. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando[^177-1] nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya yena Sirivaḍḍhassa gahapatissa nivesanam upasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā paññatte āsane nisīdi || || Nisajja kho āyasmā Ānando Sirivaḍḍham gahapatim etad avoca || ||

6. Kacci te gahapati khamanīyaṃ kacci yāpanīyaṃ kacci dukkhā vedanā paṭikkamanti[^177-2] no abhikkamanti paṭikkamosānam paññāyati no abhikkamo[^177-3] ti || || Na me bhante khamanīyaṃ na yāpanīyam bāḷhā me dukkhā vedanā abhikkamanti no[^177-4] paṭikkammanti abhikkamo sānam paññāyati no paṭikkamo ti || ||

7. Tasmā ti ha te gahapati[^177-5] evaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ || || Kāye kāyānupassī viharissāmi ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || Vedanāsu || Citte || Dhammesu dhammānupassī viharissāmi ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassan ti || || Evaṃ hi te gahapati sikkhitabban ti || ||

8. Ye me bhante Bhagavatā cattāro satipaṭṭhānā desitā saṃvijjanti te dhammā[^177-6] mayi ahaṃ ca[^177-7] tesu dhammesu sandissāmi || ahaṃ hi[^177-8] bhante kāye kāyānupassī viharāmi ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || Vedanāsu || Citte || Dhammesu dhammānupassī viharāmi ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ[^177-9] || ||

9. Yāni cimāni bhante Bhagavatā pañcorambhāgiyāni saṃyojanāni desitāni || nāhaṃ tesaṃ[^177-10] kiñci attani appahīnaṃ samanupassāmī ti || ||

10. Lādhā[^177-11] te gahapati || suladdhaṃ te gahapati || anāgāmiphalaṃ[^177-12] tayā gahapati vyākatan ti || ||

<a id="page-178"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 178]*

SN_5,47(3).30. (10) Mānadinna.

1. Taṃ yeva nidānaṃ[^178-1] || ||

2. Tena kho pana samayena Mānadinno gahapati ābādhiko hoti dukkhito bāḷhagilāno || || 3--6. Atha kho Mānadinno gahapati aññataram purisam āmantesi || pe[^178-2] || ||

7. Evarūpāya[^178-3] cāham bhante dukkhāya vedanāya puṭṭho samāno kāye kāyānupassī viharāmi ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || Vedanāsu || Citte || Dhammesu dhammānupassī viharāmi ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || ||

8. Yānicimāni[^178-4] bhante Bhagavatā pañcorambhāgiyani[^178-5] saṃyojanāni desitāni || nāham bhante yaṃ kiñci attani appahīnaṃ samanupassāmīti || ||

9. Lābhā te gahapati || suladdhan te gahapati || anāgāmiphalaṃ tayā gahapati vyākatan ti || || Sīlavaggo tatiyo[^178-6] || || Tassa[^178-7] uddānam || || Sīlaṃ Ṭhiti[^178-8] Parihānam Suddhakaṃ || Brāhmaṇena ca Padesam[^178-9] || Samattam Loko ca[^178-10] Sirivaḍḍho[^178-11] || Mānadinnena te dasā ti[^178-12] || || Satipaṭṭhāna-saṃyuttassa[^178-13] Sīlaṭṭhitivaggo tatiyo || ||

SN_5,47(3).31. (1) Anussutam. 1--2. Sāvatthi nidānam[^178-14] || ||

ātāpī[^183-1] sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || || 4 Ayam[^183-2] bhikkhave catunnam satipaṭṭhānānam bhāvanā ti || ||

2. Satipaṭṭhānañca vo bhikkhave desissāmi satipaṭṭhānabhāvanañca satipaṭṭhānabhāvanāgaminiñca paṭipadaṃ || tam suṇātha || ||

3. CHAPTER V. AMATAVAGGO PAÑCAMO.

SN_5,47(3).41. (1) Amatam.

1. Sāvatthi[^184-7] || ||

2. Catusu bhikkhave satipaṭṭhānesu supatiṭṭhitacittā viharatha || mā vo amatam panassa || katamesu catusu || ||

3. Idha bhikkhave kāye kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || Vedanāsu || Citte || Dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassam || ||

4. Imesu bhikkhave catusu satipaṭṭhānesu supatiṭṭhitacittā viharatha || mā vo amatam panassā ti || ||

SN_5,47(3).42. (2) Samudayo.

2. Catunnam bhikkhave satipaṭṭhānānam samudayañca atthagamañca desissāmi || taṃ suṇātha || ||

3. Ko ca bhikkhave kāyassa samudayo || || Āhārasamudayā kāyassa samudayo || āhāranirodhā kāyassa atthagamo || ||

4. Phassasamudayā vedanānaṃ[^184-8] samudayo || phassanirodhā vedanānam atthagamo || ||

5. Nāmarūpasamudayā cittassa samudayo || nāmarūpanirodhā cittassa atthagamo || ||

6. Manasikārasamudayā dhammānam samudayo || manasikāranirodhā dhammānam atthagamo ti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 185 -->
<a id="page-185"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 185]*

SN_5,47(3).43. (3) Maggo.

1. Sāvatthi[^185-1] || ||

2. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū amantesi[^185-2] || || Ekam idāham bhikkhave samayam Uruvelāyaṃ viharāmi najjā Nerañjarāya[^185-3] tīre Ajapālanigrodhe pathamābhisambuddho || ||

3. Tassa mayham bhikkhave rahogatassa paṭisallīnassa evaṃ cetaso parivitakko udapādi || Ekāyanvāyam[^185-4] maggo sattānaṃ visuddhiyā sokaparidevānam[^185-5] samatikkamāya dukkhadomanassānam atthagamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya yad idaṃ cattāro satipaṭṭhānā || katame cattāro || || Kāye vā bhikkhu[^185-6] kāyānupassī vihareyya[^185-7] Vedanāsu vā bhikkhu vedanānupassī vihareyya || Citte vā bhikkhu cittānupassī vihareyya || Dhammesu vā bhikkhu dhammānupassī vihareyya || ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || Ekāyanvāyam[^185-8] maggo sattānam visuddhiyā sokaparidevānaṃ samatikkamāya dukkhadomanassānam atthagamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya yad idaṃ cattāro satipaṭṭhānā ti || ||

4. Atha kho bhikkhave Brahmā sahampati mama cetasā ceto parivitakkam aññāya || seyyathāpi nāma balavā puriso sammiñjītaṃ[^185-9] vā bāham pasāreyya pasāritaṃ vā[^185-10] bāham sammiñjeyya[^185-11] || evam eva[^185-12] Brahmaloke antarahito pi mama purato pātur ahosi || ||

5. Atha kho bhikkhave Brahmā sahampati ekaṃsam uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yenāham tenañjalim paṇametvā mam etad avoca || Evam etam Bhagavā evam etaṃ Sugata || ekāyanvāyam[^185-13] maggo sattānaṃ visuddhiyā sokaparidevānam[^185-14] samatikkamāya dukkhadomanassānam atthagamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya yad idaṃ

cattāro satipaṭṭhānā || katame cattāro || || Kāye vā bhante bhikkhu kāyānupassī vihareyya[^186-1] || Vedanāsu vā[^186-2] || Citte vā[^186-2] || Dhammesu vā bhante {bhikkhu} dhammānupassī vihareyya || ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || || Ekāyanvāyam bhante maggo sattānam visuddhiyā sokaparidavānaṃ samatikkamāya dukkhadomanassānaṃ atthagamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya[^186-3] nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya yad idaṃ cattāro satipaṭṭhānā ti || ||

6. Kattha[^196-12] ca bhikkhave samādhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ || || Catusu jhānesu[^196-13] || ettha samādhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbam || ||

bodhiṃ || Iti pi so Bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddho vijjācaraṇasampanno sugato lokavidū anuttaro purisadhammasārathi satthā devamanussānaṃ buddho bhagavā ti || || Idam vuccati bhikkhave saddhindriyam || ||

2. Pañcimāni bhikkhave indriyāni || katamāni pañca || saddhindriyaṃ || la-pe || paññindriyaṃ || ||

3. Katamañ ca bhikkhave saddhindriyaṃ || || Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako saddho hoti saddahati tathāgatassa bodhiṃ || Iti pi so Bhagavā || pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || || Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave samādhindriyaṃ || ||


<!-- PTS Page 198 -->
<a id="page-198"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 198]*

4. Katamañca bhikkhave viriyindriyam || || Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako āraddhaviriyo viharati || akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya kusalānaṃ dhammānam upasampadāya thāmavā daḷhaparakkamo anikkhittadhuro kusalesu dhammesu[^198-1] || so anuppannānam pāpakānam akusalānam dhammānam anuppādāya chandam janeti vāyamati viriyam ārabhati[^198-2] cittam paggaṇhāti padahati || uppannānam pāpakānam akusalānam pahānāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati viriyam ārabhati cittam paggaṇhāti padahati anuppannānaṃ kusalānam dhammānam uppādāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati viriyam ārabhati cittam paggaṇhāti padahati || uppannānaṃ kusalānam dhammānam ṭhitiyā[^198-3] asammosāya bhiyyobhāvāya[^198-4] vepullāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janeti vāyamati viriyam ārabhati cittam paggaṇhāti padahati || || Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave viriyindriyaṃ || ||

5. Katamañca bhikkhave satindriyaṃ || || Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako satimā hoti paramena satinepakkena samannāgato cirakatam pi cirabhāsitam pi saritā anussaritā || so kāye kāyānupassī viharati || pe || vedanāsu || citte || dhammesu dhammanupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || || Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave satindriyaṃ || ||

6. Katamañca bhikkhave samādhindriyaṃ || || Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako vossaggārammaṇaṃ[^198-5] karitvā labhati samādhim labhati cittassa ekaggataṃ || So vivicceva kāmehi vivicca akusalehi dhammehi savitakkaṃ savicāram vivekajam pītisukham pathamajjhānam upasampajja viharati || vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā || pe || dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ[^198-6] || tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ[^198-6] || upasampajja viharati || || Sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbeva somanassadomanassānam atthagamā adukkham asukham upekhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati || || Idam vuccati bhikkhave samādhindriyaṃ || ||


<!-- PTS Page 199 -->
<a id="page-199"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 199]*

7. Katannaṃ ca bhikkhave paññindriyaṃ || || Idha bhikkhave ariyāsāvako paññavā hoti udayatthagāminiyā[^199-1] paññāya samannāgato ariyāya nibbedhikāya sammādukkhakkhayagāminiyā || so Idaṃ dukkhanti yathābhūtam pajānāti || Ayaṃ dukkhasamudayo ti yathābhūtam pajānāti || pe || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminipatipadā ti yathā bhūtam pajānāti || ||

8. Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave paññindriyaṃ || imāni kho bhikkhave pañcindriyānī ti || || Suddhikavaggo.[^199-2] pathamo[^199-3] || || Tassuddānaṃ[^199-4] || || Suddhikañ ceva[^199-5] dve Sotā || Arahantā[^199-6] apare duve[^199-7] || Samaṇabrāhmaṇā[^199-8] Daṭṭhabbam[^199-9] || || Vibhaṅgā[^199-10] apare duve[^199-11] ti || ||

2. Pañcimāni bhikkhave indriyāni || katamāni pañca || || Sukhindriyaṃ dukkhindriyaṃ somanassindriyaṃ domanassindriyaṃ upekhindriyaṃ || ||

3. Sukhavedaniyam bhikkhave phassam paṭicca uppajjati sukhindriyaṃ || so sukhito va samāno Sukhito smīti[^211-6] pajānāti || || tasseva[^211-7] sukhavedaniyassa[^211-8] phassassa nirodhā yaṃ[^211-9] tajjaṃ vedayitaṃ sukhavedaniyam phassam paṭicca Uppannaṃ sukhindriyaṃ taṃ nirujjhati taṃ[^211-10] vūpasammatīti[^211-11] pajānātīti || ||

4. Dukkhavedaniyam bhikkhave[^211-12] phassam paṭicca uppajjati dukkhindriyam[^211-13] so dukkhito[^211-14] va samāno Dukkhito smīti[^211-15] pajānāti || tasseva dukkhavedaniyassa phassassa nirodhā yaṃ tajjaṃ vedayitaṃ taṃ[^211-16] dukkhavedaniyam


<!-- PTS Page 212 -->
<a id="page-212"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 212]*

phassam paṭicca Uppannaṃ dukkhindriyaṃ tam nirujjhati[^212-1] taṃ vūpasammatīti pajānāti || ||

5. Somanassavedaniyaṃ bhikkhave phassam paṭicca uppajjati somanassindriyaṃ || so sumano va samāno Sumano smīti[^212-2] pajānāti || tasseva somanassa vedaniyassa phassassa[^212-3] nirodhā yaṃ tajjaṃ vedayitaṃ somanassavedaniyaṃ phassam paṭicca Uppannaṃ somanassindriyaṃ taṃ nirujjhati taṃ[^212-4] vūpasammatīti pajānāti || ||

6. Domanassavedaniyam bhikkhave phassam paṭicca uppajjati domanassindriyaṃ || so dummano[^212-5] va samāno dummano smīti pajānāti || tasseva domanassavedaniyassa phassassa nirodhāyaṃ tajjaṃ vedayitam domanassavedaniyam phassam[^212-6] paṭicca Uppannam domanassindriyaṃ taṃ nirujjhati taṃ vūpasammatīti pajānāti || ||

7. Upekhāvedaniyam bhikkhave phassam paṭicca uppajjati upekhindriyaṃ || so upekhako va[^212-7] samāno Upekhako smīti pajānāti ||tasseva upekhāvedaniyassa phassassa nirodhā yaṃ tajjaṃ vedayitaṃ upekhāvedaniyam phassa paṭicca uppannam upekhindriyaṃ taṃ nirujjhati taṃ vūpasammatīti pajānāti || ||

8. Seyyathāpi bhikkhave dvinnam kaṭṭhānaṃ saṅghaṭṭanā[^212-8] samodhānā usmā jāyati[^212-9] || tejo abhinibbattati || tesaṃ yeva kaṭṭhānaṃ nānābhāvā[^212-10] nikkhepā yā[^212-11] tajjā usmā[^212-12] sā nirujjhati sā[^212-13] vūpasammati || || Evam eva kho bhikkhave sukhavedaniyam phassam paṭicca uppajjati sukhindriyaṃ || so sukhito va samāno Sukhito smīti pajānāti || tasseva sukhavedaniyassa phassassa nirodhā yaṃ tajjaṃ vedayitam sukhavedaniyam[^212-14] phassam paṭicca uppannami[^212-5] sukhindriyaṃ taṃ

##### *[PTS Page 213]*

nirujjhati taṃ vūpasammatīti pajānāti || || Dukkavedaniyam bhikkhave phassam paṭicca || la[^213-1] || Somanassavedaniyaṃ[^213-1] || Domanassavedaniyaṃ[^213-1] || Upekhāvedaniyam bhikkhave[^213-2] phassam paṭicca uppajjati upekhindriyaṃ || so upekhako va samāno Upekhako smīti pajānāti || tasseva upekhāvedaniyassa phassassa nirodhā yaṃ tajjaṃ vedayitam upekhāvedaniyaṃ phassam[^213-3] paṭicca uppannam[^213-4] upekhindriyaṃ || taṃ nirujjhati taṃ vūpasammatīti pajānātīti[^213-5] || ||

SN_5,48(4).40. (10) Uppatika.

2. Pañcimāni bhikkhave indriyāni || katamāni pañca || || Dukkhindriyam domanassindriyam sukhindriyaṃ somanassindriyam upekhindriyaṃ || ||

3. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhuno appamattassa ātāpino pahitattassa viharato uppajjati dukkhindriyaṃ || so evam pajānāti Uppannaṃ kho me idam dukkhindriyaṃ || taṃ ca kho sanimittaṃ sanidānaṃ sasaṅkhāraṃ sappaccayaṃ || tañca animittam anidānaṃ asaṅkhāram appaccayaṃ[^213-6] dukkhindriyaṃ uppajjissatīti netaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati[^213-7] || so dukkhindriyañ[^213-8] ca pajānāti dukkhindriyasamudayañca pajānāti dukkhindriyanirodhañca pajānāti || yattha cuppannaṃ dukkhindriyaṃ aparisesam nirujjhati || tañca pajānāti || || Kattha cuppannaṃ dukkhindriyam aparisesaṃ nirujjhati || || Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu vivicceva kāmehi vivicca akusalehi dhammehi savitakkaṃ savicāraṃ vivekajaṃ pītisukham pathamam jhānam upasampajja viharati || ettha cuppannaṃ[^213-9] dukkhindriyam aparisesaṃ nirujjhati || || Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave bhikkhu aññāsi dukkhindriyassa nirodhaṃ tathattāya[^213-10] cittam upasaṃhāsi[^213-11] || ||

4. Idha pana bhikkhave bhikkhuno appamattassa ātāpino pahitattassa viharato uppajjati domanassindriyaṃ ||


<!-- PTS Page 214 -->
<a id="page-214"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 214]*

So evam pajānāti || Uppannaṃ kho me idaṃ domanassindriyam || tañca kho sanimittaṃ sanidānam sasaṅkhāraṃ sappaccayaṃ || taṃ vata[^214-1] animittam anidānam asaṅkhāram appaccayaṃ domanassindriyam uppajjissatīti netaṃ thānaṃ vijjati[^214-2] || so[^214-3] domanassindriyaṃ ca pajānāti domanassindriyasamudayañca pajānāti || domanassindriyanirodhaṃ ca pajānāti || yattha cuppannaṃ domanassindriyam aparisesaṃ nirujjhati tañca pajānāti || || Kattha cuppannaṃ domanassindriyam aparisesaṃ nirujjhati || || Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ cetaso ekodibhāvam avitakkam avicāraṃ samādhijam pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānam upasampajja viharati || ettha cuppannaṃ domanassindriyaṃ aparisesaṃ nirujjhati || || Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave bhikkhu aññāsi domanassindriyassa nirodhaṃ tathattāya cittam upasaṃhāsi || ||

5. Idha pana[^214-2] bhikkhave bhikkhuno appamattassa ātāpino pahitattassa viharato uppajjati sukhindriyaṃ || so evam pajānāti || Uppannaṃ kho me idam sukhindriyaṃ || taṃ ca kho sanimittam sanidānaṃ sasaṅkhāraṃ sappaccayaṃ || taṃ ca animittaṃ anidānam asaṅkhāram appaccayaṃ sukhindriyam uppajjissatīti netaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati[^214-4] || so sukhindriyaṃ ca[^214-2] pajānāti || sukhindriyasamudayañca pajānāti || sukhindriyanirodhañca pajānāti[^214-5] || yattha cuppannaṃ sukhindriyaṃ aparisesam nirujjhati taṃ ca pajānāti || || Kattha cuppannaṃ sukhindriyam aparisesaṃ nirujjhati || || Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā upekhako ca viharati sato ca sampajāno || sukhaṃ ca kāyena paṭisaṃvedeti[^214-6] || yan tam[^214-7] ariyā ācikkhanti upekhako satimā sukhavihārīti || tatiyaṃ[^214-8] jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati || || Ettha cuppannaṃ sukhindriyam aparisesaṃ nirujjhati || || Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave bhikkhu aññāsi sukhindriyassa[^214-9] nirodhaṃ tathattāya cittam upasaṃhāsi || ||


<!-- PTS Page 215 -->
<a id="page-215"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 215]*

6. Idha pana bhikkhave bhikkhuno appamattassa ātāpino pahitattassa viharato uppajjati somanassindriyaṃ || so evam pajānāti || Uppannaṃ kho me[^215-1] idam somanassindriyaṃ || taṃ ca kho[^215-1] sanimittaṃ sanidānaṃ sasaṅkhāraṃ sappaccayaṃ || taṃ ca[^215-2] animittam anidānam asaṅkhāram appaccayaṃ somanassindriyam uppajjissatīti netaṃ ṭhānam vijjati || so[^215-3] somanassindriyam pajānāti || somanassindriyasamudayañca pajānāti || somanassindriyanirodhañ ca pajānāti || yattha cuppannaṃ somanassindriyam aparisesaṃ nirujjhati tañca pajānāti || || Kattha cuppannam somanassindriyam aparisesaṃ nirujjhati || || Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbeva somanassadomanassānam atthagamā adukkhamasukham upekhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ[^215-4] jhānam upasampajja viharati || ettha cuppannaṃ somanassindriyam aparisesaṃ nirujjhati || || Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave bhikkhu aññāsi somanassindriyassa nirodhaṃ tathattāya cittam upasaṃhāsi || ||

7. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhuno appamattassa ātāpino pahitattassa viharato uppajjati upekhindriyam || so[^215-5] evam pajānāti || Uppannaṃ kho me idam upekhindriyaṃ || tañca kho sanimittam sanidānaṃ sasaṅkhāraṃ sappaccayaṃ || tañca kho animittam anidānam asaṅkhāram appaccayam upekhindriyam uppajjissatīti netaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati[^215-6] || so upekhindriyaṃ ca pajānāti[^215-7] upekhindriyasamudayañca[^215-8] pajānāti || upekhindriyanirodhañca pajānāti || yattha cuppannam upekhindriyam aparisesam nirujjhati tañca pajānāti || || Kattha cuppannam upekhindriyam[^215-9] aparisesaṃ nirujjhati || || Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu sabbaso nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma saññāvedayitanirodham[^215-10] upasampajja viharati || ettha cuppannam upekhindriyam aparisesam nirujjhati || || Āhaṃ vuccati bhikkhave bhikkhu


<!-- PTS Page 216 -->
<a id="page-216"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 216]*

aññāsi upekhindriyassa nirodhaṃ tathattāya cittam upasaṃhāsīti[^216-1] || || Sukhindriyavaggo2 catuttho Tassa uddānaṃ3 || || Suddhikañca Soto[^216-4] Arahaṃ[^216-5] || Dve Samaṇabrāhmaṇā ca[^216-6] || Vibhaṅgena[^216-7] tayo vuttā || Araṇi[^216-8] Uppaṭikena[^216-9] cā ti || ||

CHAPTER V. JARĀVAGGO PAÑCAMO.

SN_5,48(4).41. (1) Jarā.

1. Evam me sutaṃ[^216-10] Ekam samayam Bhagavā Sāvatthivaṃ viharati Pubbārāme Migāramātupāsāde || ||

2. Tena kho pana samayena Bhagavā sāyaṇhasamayam paṭisallāṇā vuṭṭhito pacchātape[^216-12] nisinno hoti piṭṭhim otāpayamāno || ||

3. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā Bhagavato gattāni pāṇinā anomajjanto Bhagavantam etad avoca || Acchariyam bhante abbhutam bhante na ceva[^216-12] dāni bhante Bhagavato tāva parisuddho chavivaṇṇo pariyodāto sithilāni ca[^216-13] gattāni sabbāni[^216-14] baliyajātāni[^216-15] purato pabbhāro ca[^216-16] kāyo dissati ca indriyānam aññathattaṃ[^216-17] cakkhundriyassa sotindriyassa ghānindriyassa jivhindriyassa kāyindriyassā ti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 217 -->
<a id="page-217"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 217]*

4. Evaṃ hetam Ānanda hoti jarādhammo yobbaññe[^217-1] vyādhidhammo ārogye[^217-2] maraṇadhammo jīvite[^217-3] na ceva[^217-4] tāva parisuddho hoti chavivaṇṇo pariyodāto sithilāni ca[^217-5] honti gattāni sabbāni[^217-6] baliyajātāni[^217-7] purato pabbhāro ca[^217-8] kāyo || dissati ca indriyānam aññathattaṃ cakkhundriyassa[^217-9] sotindriyassa ghānindriyassa || pe || kāyindriyassā ti || ||

5. Idam avoca Bhagavā || idaṃ vatvā ca[^217-10] sugato athāparam etad avoca satthā || || Dhītaṃ jammī jare[^217-11] atthu || dubbaṇṇakaraṇī jare || tāva manoramaṃ vimbaṃ || jarāya abhimadditaṃ || || Yo pi[^217-12] vassasataṃ jīve || so pi[^217-13] maccuparāyaṇo[^217-14] || na kiñci parivajjeti || sabbam evābhimaddatī ti[^217-15] || ||

SN_5,48(4).42. (2) Uṇṇābho brāhmaṇo.

1. Sāvatthi[^217-16] || ||

2. Atha kho Uṇṇābho brāhmaṇo yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammodi || sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ vītisāretvā ekam antaṃ nisīdi || ekam antaṃ nisinno kho Uṇṇābho brāhmaṇo Bhagavantam etad avoca || ||

3. Pañcimāni bho Gotama indriyāni nānāvisayāni nānāgocarāni na aññam aññassa gocaravisayam[^217-17] paccanubhonti[^217-18] || katamāni pañca || || Cakkhundriyaṃ sotindriyaṃ ||pe || kāyindri-


<!-- PTS Page 218 -->
<a id="page-218"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 218]*

yaṃ || Imesaṃ nu kho bho[^218-1] Gotama pañcannam indriyānaṃ nānāvisayānaṃ nānāgocarānaṃ na aññamaññassa gocaravisayam paccanubhontānam kim[^218-2] patisaraṇam ko ca[^218-3] nesaṃ gocaravisayam paccanubhotī ti || ||

4. Pañcimāni brāhmaṇa indriyāni nānāvisayāni nānāgocarāni na aññamaññassa gocaravisayaṃ paccanubhonti[^218-4] || katamāni pañca || || Cakkhundriyaṃ || pe || kāyindriyaṃ || || Imesaṃ kho brāhmaṇa pañcannam indriyānam nānāvisayānaṃ nānāgocarānaṃ na aññamaññassa gocaravisayam paccanubhontānam mano[^218-5] paṭisaraṇam mano ca[^218-6] nesaṃ gocaravisayam paccanubhotī ti || ||

5. Manassa pana bho Gotama kim paṭisaraṇanti || || Manassa kho brāhmaṇa sati paṭisaraṇanti || ||

6. Satiyā pana bho Gotama kim paṭisaraṇanti || || Satiyā pana kho[^218-7] brāhmaṇa vimutti paṭisaraṇanti || ||

7. Vimuttiyā pana bho Gotama kim paṭisaraṇanti || || Vimuttiyā kho brāhmaṇa nibbānam paṭisaraṇanti || ||

8. Nibbānassa pana bho Gotama kim paṭisaraṇanti || || Ajjhaparam[^218-8] brāhmaṇa pañhaṃ nāsakkhi pañhassa pariyantaṃ gahetuṃ || nibbānogadam hi brāhmaṇa brahmacariyaṃ vussati nibbānaparāyanaṃ nibbānapariyosānanti || ||

9. Atha kho Uṇṇābho brāhmaṇo Bhagavato bhāsitam abhinanditvā anumoditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pakkāmi || ||

10. Atha kho Bhagavā acirapakkante Uṇṇābhe brāhmaṇe[^218-9] bhikkhū āmantesi || ||

11. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave kuṭāgāre[^218-10] vā kuṭāgārasālāyaṃ[^218-11] vā[^218-12] pācīnavātapānā suriye uggacchante vātapānena rasmiyo[^218-13] pavisitvā kāya[^218-14] patiṭṭhitā ti || || Pacchimāyam bhante bhittiyanti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 219 -->
<a id="page-219"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 219]*

12. Evam eva kho bhikkhave Uṇṇābhassa brāhmaṇassa Tathāgate saddhā niviṭṭhā mūlajātā patiṭṭhitā daḷhā asaṃhāriyā sāmaṇena vā brāhmaṇena vā devena vā Mārena vā[^219-1] Brahmunā vā kenaci vā lokasmiṃ || || Imamhi ce bhikkhave samaye Uṇṇābho brāhmaṇo kālaṃ kareyya natthi taṃ saṃyojanaṃ yena saṃyojanena saṃyutto Uṇṇābho brāhmaṇo puna imaṃ lokam āgaccheyyāti || ||

SN_5,48(4).43. (3) Sāketo.

1. Evam me sutaṃ[^219-2] Ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Sākete viharati Añjanavane[^219-3] Migadāye || ||

2. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi || Atthi nu kho bhikkhave pariyāyo yam pariyāyam āgamma yāni pañcindriyāni tāni pañcabalāni honti || yāni pañcabalāni tāni pañcindriyāni hontī ti[^219-4] || || Bhagavaṃmūlakā no bhante dhammā ||pe || ||

3. Atthi bhikkhave pariyāyo yam pariyāyam āgamma yāni pañcindriyāni tāni pañcabalāni honti || yāni pañcabalāni tāni pañcindriyāni honti || || Katamo ca bhikkhave pariyāyo yam pariyāyam āgamma yāni pañcindriyāni tāni pañcabalāni honti || yāni pañcabalāni tāni pañcindriyāni honti || ||

4. Yam bhikkhave saddhindriyam taṃ saddhābalaṃ || yaṃ saddhābalaṃ taṃ saddhindriyaṃ || || Yam viriyindriyaṃ taṃ viriyabalaṃ || yaṃ viriyabalaṃ taṃ viriyindriyaṃ || pe || Yam paññindriyam taṃ paññābalaṃ || yam paññābalaṃ tam paññindriyaṃ || ||

5. Seyyathāpi bhikkhave nadī pācīnaninnā pācīnapoṇā pācīnapabbharā || tassā[^219-5] majjhe dīpo || Atthi bhikkhave pariyāyo yam pariyāyam āgamma tassā nadiyā eko soto tveva saṅkhyaṃ[^219-6] gacchati || || Atthi pana bhikkhave pariyāyo yam pariyāyam āgamma tassā nadiyā dve sotāni tveva {saṅkhyaṃ}[^219-6] gacchanti[^219-7] || ||


<!-- PTS Page 220 -->
<a id="page-220"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 220]*

6. Katamo ca bhikkhave pariyāyo yam pariyāyam āgamma[^220-1] tassā nadiyā eko soto tveva saṅkhyaṃ[^220-2] gacchati || || Yaṃ ca[^220-3] bhikkhave tassa dīpassa puratthimante[^220-4] udakaṃ yaṃ ca pacchimante[^220-5] udakaṃ || || Ayaṃ[^220-6] kho bhikkhave || pe || gacchati || ||

7. Katamo ca bhikkhave pariyāyo yam pariyāyam āgamma tassā nadiyā dve sotāni tveva saṅkhyaṃ gacchanti[^220-7] || yaṃ ca[^220-8] bhikkhave tassa dīpassa uttarante udakaṃ yañca dakkhiṇante udakaṃ[^220-9] || || Ayaṃ kho[^220-3] bhikkhave pariyāyo yam pariyāyam āgamma tassā[^220-10] nadiyā dve sotāni tveva saṅkhyaṃ[^220-10] gacchanti[^220-12] || ||

8. Evam eva kho bhikkhave[^220-13] || yaṃ[^220-14] saddhindriyaṃ taṃ saddhābalaṃ || yaṃ saddhābalaṃ[^220-15] taṃ saddhindriyaṃ || pe || yam paññindriyaṃ tam paññābalaṃ || yam paññābalaṃ tam paññindriyaṃ || ||

9. Pañcannam bhikkhave indriyānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttim paññāvimuttiṃ diṭṭheva dhamme sayam abhiññā[^220-16] sacchikatvā upasampajja viharatī ti || ||

SN_5,48(4).44. (4) Pubbakoṭṭhako.

1. Evam me sutaṃ[^220-17] Ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Savatthiyaṃ viharati Pubbakoṭṭhake || ||

2. Tatra kho Bhagavā āyasmantam Sāriputtam āmantesi || ||

3. Saddahāsi[^220-18] tvaṃ Sāriputta || Saddhindriyam bhāvitaṃ bahulīkatam amatogadhaṃ hoti amataparāyanaṃ[^220-19] amatapariyosānaṃ || la || paññindriyam bhāvitam bahulīkatam amatogadhaṃ hoti amataparāyanam amatapariyosānanti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 221 -->
<a id="page-221"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 221]*

4. Na khvāham ettha bhante Bhagavato saddhāya gacchāmi saddhindriyam ||la || paññindriyam bhāvitaṃ bahulīkatam amatogadham hoti || amataparāyanam[^221-1] amatapariyosānaṃ || || Yesaṃ hi tam bhante aññātam assa adiṭṭham aviditam asacchikatam aphusitam[^221-2] paññaya te tattha paresam saddhāya[^221-3] gaccheyyuṃ || Saddhindriyaṃ || la || paññindriyam bhavitam bahulīkatam amatogadhaṃ hoti amataparāyanam amatapariyosānaṃ[^221-4] || || Yesañca kho etam bhante[^221-5] ñātaṃ diṭṭhaṃ[^221-7] viditaṃ sacchikatam phusitam[^221-7] paññāya nikkaṅkhā[^221-8] te tattha nibbicikiccā Saddhindriyaṃ || pa-pe || paññindriyam bhāvitam bahulīkatam amatogadham hoti amataparāyanam amatapariyosānaṃ || || Mayhañca[^221-9] kho etam bhante ñātaṃ diṭṭhaṃ viditaṃ sacchikatam phusitam paññāya nikkaṅkhāham[^221-10] tattha paresaṃ[^221-11] nibbicikiccho[^221-12] || Saddhindriyaṃ || la || paññindriyaṃ bhāvitam bahulīkatam amatogadhaṃ hoti amataparāyanam amatapariyosānan ti || ||

5. Sādhu sādhu Sāriputta || Yesaṃ hi taṃ[^221-13] Sāriputta aññātam assa[^221-14] adiṭṭham aviditam[^221-15] asacchikatam aphussitam[^221-16] paññāya te tattha paresaṃ saddhāya[^221-17] gaccheyyuṃ || Saddhindriyam bhāvitam bahulīkatam amatogadhaṃ hoti amataparāyanam amatapariyosānaṃ || la-pe || paññindriyam bhāvitam bahulīkatam amatogadhaṃ hoti amataparāyanam amatapariyosānaṃ || Yesaṃ ca kho etaṃ Sāriputta ñataṃ diṭṭhaṃ viditaṃ sacchikatam phusitam paññāya nikkaṅkhā te tattha nibbicikicchā Saddhindriyam bhāvitam bahulī-


<!-- PTS Page 222 -->
<a id="page-222"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 222]*

katam amatogadhaṃ hoti amataparayānam amatapariyosānaṃ || la-pe || paññindriyam bhāvitam bahulīkatam amatogadhaṃ hoti amatapariyosānan ti || ||

SN_5,48(4).45. (5) Pubbārāma1.

1. Evam me sutaṃ[^222-1] Ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Pubbārāme Migāramātupāsāde[^222-2] || ||

2. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi[^222-3] || || Katinnaṃ[^222-4] nu kho bhikkhave indriyānam bhavitattā bahulīkatattā khīnāsavo bhikkhu aññaṃ vyākaroti || || Khīṇā jāti vusitam brahmacariyaṃ kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ nāparam itthattāyā ti pajānāmī ti || || Bhagavaṃmūlakā no bhante dhammā || la-pe || ||

3. Ekassa kho bhikkhave indriyassa bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā khīṇāsavo bhikkhu aññaṃ vyākaroti || Khīnājāti- -nāparam itthattāyā ti pajānāmī ti || || Katamassa ekassa indriyassa[^222-5] || ||

4. Paññāvato bhikkhave ariyasāvakassa tadanvayā saddhā saṇṭhāti || tadanvayaṃ[^222-6] viriyaṃ saṇṭhāti || tadanvayā sati saṇṭhāti || tadanvayo[^222-7] samādhi saṇṭhāti || ||

5. Imassa kho bhikkhave ekassa indriyassa bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā khīnāsavo bhikkhu aññaṃ vyākaroti || || Khīnā jāti vusitam brahmacariyaṃ kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ nāparam itthattāyāti pajānāmī ti || ||

SN_5,48(4).46. (6) Pubbārāma2.

1. [^222-8] Taṃ yeva nidānam || ||

2. Katinnaṃ nu kho bhikkhave indriyānam bhāvitattā bahulikatattā khīnāsavo bhikkhu aññam vyākaroti || Khīnā jāti[^222-00] nāparam itthattāyāti pajānāmī ti || || Bhagavaṃmūlakā no bhante dhammā || la-pe || ||


<!-- PTS Page 223 -->
<a id="page-223"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 223]*

3. Dvinnaṃ kho bhikkhave indriyānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā khīṇāsavo bhikkhu aññaṃ vyākaroti || Khīṇājāti- -nāparam itthattāyati pajānāmīti || katamesaṃ dvinnaṃ || ||

4. Ariyāya ca paññāya ariyāya ca vimuttiyā || || Yā[^223-1] hissa bhikkhave ariyā paññā tad assa pāññindriyaṃ || yā hissa bhikkhave ariyā vimutti tad assa samādhindriyaṃ || ||

5. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave dvinnam indriyānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā khīnāsavo bhikkhu aññaṃ vyākaroti || Khīṇā jāti- -nāparam itthattāyāti pajānāmī ti || ||

SN_5,48(4).47. (7) Pubbārāma3.

1. Tam eva nidānaṃ || ||

2. Katinnam nu kho bhikkhave indriyānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā khīṇāsavo bhikkhu aññaṃ vyākaroti || Khīṇā jāti- -nāparam itthattāyā ti pajānāmī ti || || Bhagavaṃmūlakā no bhante dhammā || la-pe || ||

3. Catunnaṃ kho bhikkhave indriyānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā khīṇāsavo bhikkhu aññaṃ vyakaroti || Khīnā jāti- -nāparam itthattāyāti pajānāmīti[^223-2] || || Katamesaṃ catunnaṃ || ||

4. Viriyindriyassa satindriyassa samādhindriyassa paññindriyassa || ||

5. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave catunnam indriyānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā khīṇāsavo bhikkhu aññaṃ vyākaroti[^223-3] || Khīnā jāti- -nāparam itthattāyā ti pajānāmī ti || ||

SN_5,48(4).48. (8) Pubbārāma4.

1. Taṃ yeva nidānaṃ || ||

2. Katinnaṃ kho bhikkhave indriyānam bhāvitattā- onāparam itthattāyāti pajānāmīti || || Bhagavaṃmūlakā no bhante dhammā || la-pe || ||

3. Pañcannaṃ kho bhikkhave indriyānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā khīnāsavo bhikkhu aññaṃ vyākaroti || Khīṇā jāti- -nāparam itthattāyāti pajānāmī ti || || Katamesam pañcannaṃ || ||


<!-- PTS Page 224 -->
<a id="page-224"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 224]*

4. Saddhindriyassa viriyindriyassa satindriyassa samādhindriyassa paññindriyassa || ||

5. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave pañcannam indriyānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā khīnāsavo bhikkhu aññaṃ vyākaroti || Khīṇā jāti vusitam brahmacariyaṃ kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ nāparam itthattāyāti pajānāmī ti || ||

SN_5,48(4).49. (9) Piṇḍolo.

1. Evam me sutaṃ[^224-1] Ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Kosambiyaṃ viharati Ghositārāme || ||

2. Tena kho pana samayena āyasmatā Piṇḍolabharadvājena aññā vyākatā hoti || Khīṇā jāti vusitam brahmacariyaṃ kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ nāparam itthattāyāti pajānāmī ti || ||

3. Atha kho sambahulā bhikkhū yena Bhagavā tenupa. saṅkamiṃsu[^224-2] || upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antaṃ nisīdiṃsu || ekam antaṃ nisinnā[^224-3] kho te bhikkhū Bhagavantam etad avocuṃ || || Āyasmatā bhante[^224-4] Piṇḍolabhāradvājena aññā vyākatā Khīnā jāti- -nāparam itthattāyāti pajānāmītī || || Kiṃ nu kho bhante atthavasaṃ sampassamānena āyasmatā Piṇḍolabhāradvājena aññā vyākatā Khīṇā jāti- -nāparam itthattāyāti pajānāmī ti || ||

4. Tiṇṇannam kho pana[^224-5] bhikkhave indriyānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā Piṇḍolabhāradvājena bhikkhunā aññā vyākatā Khīṇā jāti- -nāparam itthattāyāti pajānāmī ti || || Katamesaṃ tiṇṇānaṃ || ||

5. Satindriyassa samādhindriyassa paññindriyassa || ||

6. Imesaṃ kho bhante tiṇṇannam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā Piṇḍolabhāradvājena bhikkhunā aññā vyākatā || Khīnā jāti- -nāparam itthattāyā ti pajānāmī ti || ||

7. Imāni ca bhikkhave tīṇi indriyāni[^224-6] || || Kiṃnantāni[^224-7] || khayantāni || || Kissa khayantāni[^224-8] || jātijarāmaraṇassa || || Jātijarāmaraṇaṃ khayan ti[^224-9] kho bhikkhave sampassamānena[^224-10]


<!-- PTS Page 225 -->
<a id="page-225"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 225]*

Piṇḍolabhāradvājena bhikkhunā aññā vyākatā || Khīṇā jāti vusitam brahmacariyaṃ kataṃ karāṇīyaṃ || nāparam itthattāyā ti pajānāmī ti || ||

SN_5,48(4).50. (10) Saddha or Āpaṇa.

1. Evam me sutaṃ[^225-1] Ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Aṅgesu viharati Āpanaṃ nāma Aṅgānaṃ nigamo || ||

2. Tatra kho Bhagavā āyasmantam Sāriputtam āmantesi || || Yo so Sāriputta ariyasāvako Tathāgate ekantagato[^225-2] abhippasanno || api nu[^225-3] so Tathāgate vā Tathāgatasāsane vā khaṅkheyya vā vicikiccheyya vā ti || ||

3. Yoso bhante ariyasāvako Tathāgate ekantagato[^225-2] abhippasanno || na so Tathāgate vā Tathāgatasāsane[^225-4] vā kaṅkheyya vā vicikiccheyya vā || || Saddhassa[^225-5] hi bhante ariyasāvakassa etam pāṭikaṅkhaṃ[^225-6] || yam āraddhaviriyo viharissati akusalānaṃ dhammānam pahānāya kusalānaṃ dhammānam upasampadāya thāmavā daḷhaparakkamo anikkhittadhuro kusalesu[^225-7] dhammesu || ||

4. Yaṃ hissa bhante viriyaṃ[^225-8] tad assa viriyindriyam[^225-9] || || Saddhassa hi bhante ariyasāvakassa āraddhaviriyassa etam pāṭikaṅkhaṃ[^225-6] || yaṃ satimā bhavissati paramena satinepakkena samannāgato cirakatam pi cirabhāsitam pi[^225-10] saritā anussaritā || ||

5. Yā[^225-11] hissa bhante sati tad assa satindriyaṃ || || Saddhassa hi bhante ariyasāvakassa āraddhaviriyassa upaṭṭhitasatino etam pātikaṅkhaṃ[^225-12] || yaṃ vossaggārammaṇaṃ[^225-13] karitvā labhissati samādhim labhissati cittassa ekaggataṃ[^225-14] || ||

6. Yo[^225-15] hissa bhante samādhi tad assa samādhindriyaṃ[^225-16] || || Saddhassa hi bhante ariyasāvakassa upaṭṭhita-


<!-- PTS Page 226 -->
<a id="page-226"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 226]*

satino samāhitacittassa etam pāṭikaṅkhaṃ[^226-1] || yam evam pajānissati[^226-2] Anamataggo kho saṃsāro pubbā[^226-3] koṭi na paññāyati || avijjānīvaraṇānaṃ sattānaṃ taṇhāsaṃyojanānaṃ sandhāvatam saṃsarataṃ[^226-4] avijjāya tveva tamokāyassa[^226-5] asesavirāganirodho santam etam padam phanitam etam padam yad idaṃ sabbasaṅkhārasamatho[^226-6] sabbupadhipaṭinissaggo taṇhakkhayo virāgo nirodho nibbānaṃ || ||

7. Yā hissa bhante paññā tad assa paññindriyaṃ || Saddho[^226-7] so bhante ariyasāvako evam padahitvā padahitvā evaṃ saritvā saritvā evaṃ samādahitvā[^226-8] samādahitvā evam pajānitvā pajānitvā[^226-9] evam abhisaddahati || Ime kho te dhammā ye me pubbe sutavā ahesuṃ || te dānāham[^226-10] etarahi kāyena ca phusitvā[^226-11] viharāmi || paññāyaca ativijjha[^226-12] passāmīti || ||

8. Yā hissa bhante saddhā tad assa saddhindriyan ti || ||

9. Sādhu sādhu Sāriputta || || Yo so Sāriputta ariyasāvako Tathāgate ekantagato abhippasanno na so Tathāgate vā Tathāgatasāsane vā kaṅkheyya vā vicikiccheyya vā || || Saddhassa hi Sāriputta ariyasāvakassa etam pātikaṅkhaṃ[^226-13] || yaṃ[^226-14] āraddhaviriyo viharissati akusalānaṃ dhammānam pahānāya kusalānam dhammānam upasampadāya thāmavā daḷhaparakkamo anikkhittadhuro kusalesu dhammesu || || 10--13. Yaṃ hissa Sāriputta viriyam tad assa viriyindriyaṃ[^226-15] || || Saddhassa[^226-16] hi Sāriputta ariyasāvakassa ||pe || Saddho[^226-7] so Sāriputta ariyasāvako evam padahitvā padahitvā evam saritvā saritvā[^226-17] evaṃ samādahitvā samādahitvā[^226-17] evam pajānitvā pajānitvā evam abhisaddahati[^226-18] || || Ime kho te dhammā ye me pubbe sutavā ahesuṃ || te dānāham


<!-- PTS Page 227 -->
<a id="page-227"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 227]*

etarahi kāyena ca phusitvā[^227-1] viharāmi paññāya[^227-2] ca ativijjha[^227-3] passāmī ti || ||

14. Yā hissa Sāriputta saddhā tad assa saddhindriyanti || || Jarāvaggo pañcamo || ||4 Tassa uddānaṃ5 || || Jarā Uṇṇābho[^227-6] brāhmaṇo[^227-7] || Sāketo[^227-8] Pubbakoṭṭhako[^227-9] || Pubbārāme ca[^227-10] cattāri || Piṇḍolo Saddhena te dasāti[^227-11] || ||

CHAPTER VI. VAGGO CHAṬṬHO.

SN_5,48(4).51. (1) Sālam.

1. Evam me sutaṃ[^227-12] Ekam samayam Bhagavā Kosalesu viharati sālāyaṃ[^227-13] brāhmaṇagāme || ||

2. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi || pe [pa] || ||

3. SN_5,48(4).53. (3) Sekho.

1. Evam me sutaṃ[^229-2] Ekam samayam Bhagavā Kosambiyaṃ viharati Ghositārāme || ||

2. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi || || Atthi nu kho[^229-3] bhikkhave pariyāyo yam pariyāyam āgamma sekho bhikkhu sekhabhūmiyaṃ[^229-4] ṭhito Sekhosmī ti pajāneyya || asekho bhikkhu asekhabhūmiyaṃ ṭhito Asekhosmīti pajāneyyāti || || Bhagavaṃmūlakā no bhante dhammā || la[^229-5] || ||

3. Atthi bhikkhave pariyāyo yam pariyāyam āgamma sekho bhikkhu sekhabhūmiyaṃ ṭhito[^229-6] Sekhosmīti pajāneyya || asekho bhikkhu asekhabhūmiyaṃ ṭhito[^229-7] Asekhosmīti pajāneyya || ||

4. Katamo ca bhikkhave pariyāyo yam pariyāyam āgamma sekho bhikkhu sekhabhūmiyaṃ ṭhito[^229-8] Sekho smīti pajānāti || || Idha bhikkhave sekho bhikkhu Idaṃ dukkhan ti yathābhūtam pajānāti || Ayam dukkhasamudayo ti ya- pa- || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodho ti ya- pa- || Ayam dukkhanirodhagāminī patipadā ti ya- pa- || || Ayam pi[^229-9] kho bhikkhave pariyāyo yam pariyāyam āgamma sekho bhikkhu sekhabhumiyaṃ ṭhito[^229-8] Sekhosmīti pajānāti || ||

5. Puna ca param bhikkhave sekho bhikkhu iti paṭisañcikkhati[^229-10] || || Atthi nu kho ito bahiddhā añño samaṇo vā brāhmaṇo vā yo evam bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ tathā[^229-11] dhammaṃ

##### *[PTS Page 230]*

deseti yathā Bhagavā ti || so evam pajānāti || Natthi kho[^230-1] ito bahiddhā añño samaṇo vā brāhmaṇo vā yo evam bhūtam taccham tathā[^230-2] dhammam deseti yathā[^230-3] Bhagavā ti || || Ayam pi kho bhikkhave pariyāyo yam pariyāyam āgamma sekho bhikkhu sekhabhūmiyaṃ ṭhito Sekhosmī ti pajānāti || ||

6. Puna ca param bhikkhave sekho bhikkhu pañcindriyāni pajānāti || saddhindriyam || viriyo || sati-[^230-1] || samādhi- || paññindriyam || yaṃ[^230-4] gatikāni yam paramāni yam phalāni[^230-5] yam pariyosanāni na heva[^230-6] kho kāyena phusitvā[^230-7] viharati paññāya ca ativijjha[^230-8] passati || || Ayam pi[^230-4] kho bhikkhave pariyāyo yam pariyāyam āgamma sekho bhikkhu sekhabhūmiyaṃ ṭhito Sekhosmī ti pajānāti || ||

7. Katamo ca bhikkhave pariyāyo yam pariyāyam āgamma asekho bhikkhu asekhabhūmiyaṃ ṭhito Asekhosmīti pajānāti || || Idha bhikkhave asekho bhikkhu pañcindriyāni pajānāti || saddhindriyaṃ || pe || paññindriyaṃ || yaṅgatikāni yamparamāni yamphalāni yampariyosanāni || Kāyena ca phusitvā[^230-9] viharati paññāya ca ativijjha[^230-8] passati || || Ayam pi kho bhikkhave pariyāyo yam pariyāyam āgamma asekho bhikkhu asekhabhūmiyaṃ ṭhito Asekho smīti pajānāti || ||

8. Puna ca param bhikkhave asekho bhikkhu cha indriyāni pajānāti || cakkhundriyaṃ sotindriyaṃ ghānindriyaṃ jivhindriyam kāyindriyam manindiyaṃ || imāni kho[^230-4] cha indriyāni sabbena sabbam sabbathā sabbam aparisesā nirujjhissanti[^230-10] || aññāni ca[^230-4] cha indriyāni na kuhiñci kismiñci uppajjissantī ti pajānāti || || Ayam pi kho bhikkhave pariyāyo || yam pariyāyam āgamma asekho bhikkhu asekhabhūmiyaṃ ṭhito Asekhosmī ti pajānātī ti || ||

SN_5,48(4).56. (6) Patiṭṭhito.

1. Sāvatthi[^232-1] || ||

SN_5,48(4).57. (7) Brahmā.

1. Evam me sutaṃ[^232-7] Ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Uruvelāyaṃ viharati najjā Nerañjarāya tīre Ajapālanigrodhe pathamābhisambuddho || ||

2. Atha kho Bhagavato rahogatassa[^232-8] patisallīnassa evam cetaso parivitakko udapādi || Pañcindriyāni bhāvitāni bahulīkatāni amatogadhāni honti amataparāyanāni amatapariyosānāni || Katamāni pañca || saddhindriyam bhāvitam bahulīkatam amatogadhaṃ hoti amataparāyanam amatapariyosānaṃ || viriyindriyaṃ || satindriyaṃ || samādhindriyam || paññindriyam bhāvitam bahulīkatam amatogadhaṃ hoti amataparāyanam amatapariyosānam || imani pañcindriyāni bhāvitāni bahulīkatāni amatogadhāni honti amataparāyanāni amatapariyosānānī ti || ||

3. Atha kho Brahmā sahampati Bhagavato cetasā cetoparivitakkam aññāya seyyathāpi nāma balavā puriso sammiñjitaṃ[^232-9] vā bāham pasāreyya pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ sammiñjeyya[^232-10] || evam eva[^232-11] Brahmaloke[^232-12] antarahito Bhagavato purato pātur ahosi || ||

4. Atha kho Brahmā sahampati ekaṃsam uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yena Bhagavā tenāñjalim paṇāmetvā Bhagavantam etad avoca || || Evam etam Bhagavā evam etaṃ Sugata pañcindriyāni bhāvitāni bahulīkatāni amatogadhāni honti amataparāyanāni amatapariyosānāni || || Katamāni pañca || saddhindriyam bhāvitam bahulīkatam amatogadhaṃ hoti amataparāyanam amatapariyosānaṃ || la-pe || paññindriyam bhāvitamo -amatapariyosānaṃ || || Imāni pañcindriyāni bhāvitāni bahulīkatāni amatogadhāni honti amataparāyanāni amatapariyosānāni[^233-1] || ||

5. Bhūtapubbāham bhante Kassape {sammāsambuddhe} brahmacariyam acariṃ[^233-2] || || Tatra pi mam evaṃ jānanti Sahako bhikkhu sahako bhikkhūti || so khvāham[^233-3] bhante imesaṃ yeva[^233-4] pañcannam indriyānam bhavitattā bahulīkatattā kāmesu kāmacchandam virājetvā kāyassa bhedā param maraṇā sugatim brahmalokam upapanno || tatra pi mam evaṃ jānanti[^233-5] Brahmā sahampati[^233-6] Brahmāsahampatī ti || ||

SN_5,48(4).58. (8) Sūkarakhatā.

1. Evam me sutaṃ[^233-8] Ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Rājagahe viharati Gijjhakūṭe pabbate Sūkarakhatāyaṃ[^233-9] || ||

2. Tatra kho Bhagavā āyasmantaṃ Sāriputtam āmantesi || kiṃ[^233-10] nu kho Sāriputta atthavasaṃ sampassamāno[^233-11] khīṇāsavo bhikkhu Tathāgate vā Tathāgatasāsane vā paramanipaccākāraṃ[^233-12] pavattamāno[^233-13] pavatteti[^233-14] || ||


<!-- PTS Page 234 -->
<a id="page-234"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 234]*

Anuttaraṃ[^234-1] hi bhante[^234-2] yogakkhemaṃ[^234-3] sampassamāno khīṇāsavo bhikkhu Tathāgate vā Tathāgatasāsane vā paramanipaccākāram pavattamāno[^234-4] pavattetī ti[^234-5] || ||

3. Sādhu sādhu Sāriputta anuttaraṃ[^234-6] hi Sāriputta yoyakkhemam[^234-7] sampassamāno khīṇāsavo bhikkhu Tathāgate vā Tathāgatasāsane vā paramanipaccākāram pavattamāno pavattetī ti[^234-8] || ||

4. Katamo ca Sāriputta anuttaro yogakkhemo yaṃ sampassamāno khīṇāsavo bhikkhu Tathāgate vā Tathāgatasāsane vā paramanipaccākāram pavattamāno pavattetī ti || ||

5. Idha bhante khīṇāsavo bhikkhu saddhindriyam bhāveti upasamagāmiṃ sambodhagāmim || viriyindriyam[^234-9] || satindriyaṃ[^234-9] || samādhindriyam[^234-9] || paññindriyam bhāveti upasamagāmiṃ sambodhagāmiṃ || || Ayaṃ kho bhante anuttaro yogakkhemo yaṃ[^234-10] sampassamāno khīṇāsavo bhikkhu Tathāgate vā Tathāgatasāsane vā paramanipaccākāram pavattamāno pavattetī ti || ||

6. Sādhu sādhu Sāriputta || eso hi Sāriputta anuttaro yogakkhemo yam[^234-10] sampassamāno khīnāsavo bhikkhu Tathāgate vā Tathāgatasāsane vā paramanipaccākāram pavattamāno pavatteti || ||

7. Katamo ca Sāriputta paramanipaccākāro yaṃ[^234-2] khīṇāsavo bhikkhu Tathāgate vā[^234-11] Tathāgatasāsane vā paramanipaccākāram pavattamāno pavattetī ti || ||

8. Idha bhante khīṇāsavo bhikkhu satthari sagāravo viharati sappatisso[^234-12] || dhamme sagāravo viharati sappatisso || saṅghe- || sikkhāya- || samādhismiṃ sagāravo viharati sappatisso || || Ayaṃ kho bhante paramanipaccākāro yaṃ khīṇāsavo bhikkhu Tathāgate vā Tathāgatasāsane vā paramanipaccākāram pavattamāno pavattetī ti || ||

9. Sādhu sādhu Sāriputta || eso hi Sāriputta paramanipaccākāro yaṃ khīṇāsavo bhikkhu Tathāgate vā Tathāgatasāsane vā paramanipaccākāram pavattamāno pavattetī ti || ||

3. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu anuppannānam pāpakānam akusalānaṃ dhammānam anuppādāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati viriyam ārabhati cittam paggaṇhāti padahati || ||

5. Anuppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānam uppādāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati viriyam ārabhati cittam paggaṇhāti padahati || ||

6. uppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammosāya bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janeti vāyamati viriyam ārabhati cittam paggaṇhāti padahati || ||

SN_5,50(6).58--68. (2--12). [Uddānam] Cha pācīnato ninnā samuddato dve ca Gaṅgā dasā honti vaggo tena pavuccati[^252-1] || || Gaṅgāpeyyāla balasaṃyuttassa rāgavinayavasena vitthāretabbo vaggo || ||

CHAPTER VII. APPAMĀDAVAGGO (Rāga-).

SN_5,50(6).69--78. (1--10). Tathāgatam Padam Kūṭam Mulam Sārañca Vassikaṃ || Rājā Candimasuriyo ca Vatthena dasamam padaṃ || || Appamādavaggo balasaṃyuttassa rāgavinayavasena vitthāretabbo || ||

CHAPTER VIII. BALA-VAGGO (Rāga-).

SN_5,50(6).79--90. (1--12). Balaṃ Bījañca Nāgo ca Rukkham Kumbhena Suriyaṃ || Ākāsena dve Meghā Navā Āgantukā Nadī || || Balakaraṇīyavaggo balasaṃyuttassa rāgavinayavasena vitthāretabbaṃ || ||

CHAPTER IX. ESANĀ-VAGGO (Rāga-).

SN_5,50(6).91--100. (1--10). Esanā Vidhā Āsavo Bhavo Dukkhā ca Tisso ca || Khila-Malañca Nīgho ca Vedanaṃ Taṇhāyena ca ti || || Esanavaggo balasaṃyuttassa vasena vitthāretabbo vaggo || ||

Ye hi keci bhikkhave atītam addhānam bhikkhū āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttim paññāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhevadhamme sayam abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja vihariṃsu || sabbe te catunnam iddhipādānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā || || Ye hi keci bhikkhave anāgatam addhānam bhikkhū āsavānam khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttiṃ paññavimuttiṃ diṭṭheva dhamme sayam abhiññā[^257-1] sacchikatvā upasampajja viharissanti || sabbe te catunnam iddhipādānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā || || Ye hi keci bhikkhave etarahi bhikkhū āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttim paññāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhevadhamme sayam abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharanti || sabbe te catunnam iddhippādānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā || || Katamesaṃ catunnaṃ || ||
