SN_5,51(7).10. (10) Cetiya.

1. Evam me sutaṃ[^258-5] Ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Vesāliyaṃ viharati Mahāvane Kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ || ||


<!-- PTS Page 259 -->
<a id="page-259"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 259]*

2. Atha kho Bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya Vesāliyaṃ[^259-1] piṇḍāya pāvisi[^259-2] || Vesāliyam piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattam piṇḍapātapaṭikkanto āyasmantam Ānandam āmantesi || Gaṇhāhi Ānanda nisīdanaṃ yena Cāpālacetiyam[^259-3] tenupasaṅkamissāmi divāvihārāyati || || Evam bhante ti kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavato paṭissutvā nisīdanam ādāya Bhagavantam piṭṭhito piṭṭhito anubandhi[^259-4] || ||

3. Atha kho Bhagavā yena Cāpālacetiyaṃ tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā paññatte āsane nisīdi || || Āyasmā pi kho Ānando Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antaṃ nisīdi || ||

4. Ekam antam nisinnaṃ kho āyasmantam Ānandam Bhagavā etad avoca || || Ramaṇiyā Ānanda Vesālī ramaṇiyam Udenacetiyam[^259-5] ramaṇiyaṃ Gotamakacetiyaṃ[^259-6] ramaṇiyaṃ Sattambacetiyaṃ ramaṇiyam Bahuputtakacetiyaṃ[^259-7] ramaṇiyaṃ Sārandadam[^259-8] cetiyaṃ ramaṇiyaṃ Cāpālacetiyaṃ || || Yassa kassaci Ānanda cattāro iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānikatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā || so ākaṅkhamāno kappaṃ vā tiṭṭheyya kappāvasesaṃ vā || || Tathāgatassa kho Ānanda cattāro iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānikatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā || ākaṅkhamāno Ānanda Tathāgato kappaṃ vā tiṭṭheyya kappāvasesaṃ vā ti || ||

5. Evam pi kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavatā oḷārike nimitte kayiramāne[^259-9] oḷārike obhāse kayiramāne nāsakkhi paṭivijjhituṃ || na Bhagavantam yāci Tiṭṭhatu bhante Bhagavā kappam tiṭṭhatu Sugato kappāvasesaṃ[^259-10] bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya lokānukampāya[^259-11] atthāya hitāya sukhāya devamanussānan ti || yathā tam Mārena[^259-12] pariyuṭṭhitacitto[^259-13] || || 6--7. Dutiyam pi kho bhagavā[^259-14] || ||


<!-- PTS Page 260 -->
<a id="page-260"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 260]*

8. Tatiyam pi kho Bhagavā[^260-1] āyasmantam Ānandam āmantesi[^260-2] || Ramaṇiyā Ānanda Vesālī ramaṇiyam Udeṇacetiyaṃ ramaṇiyaṃ Gotamakacetiyam ramaṇiyaṃ Sattambacetiyaṃ ramaṇiyam Bahuputtakacetiyaṃ[^260-3] ramaniyaṃ Sārandadam[^260-4] cetiyam ramaṇiyaṃ Cāpālacetiyaṃ[^260-5] || || Yassa kassaci Ānanda cattāro iddhipadā bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānikatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā || so ākaṅkhamāno[^260-6] kappaṃ vā tiṭṭheyya kappāvasesaṃ vā || Tathāgatassa kho Ānanda cattāro iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānikatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā || so ākaṅkhamāno Ānanda Tathāgato kappaṃ vā tiṭṭheyya kappāvasesaṃ vā ti || ||

9. Evam pi kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavatā oḷārike nimitte kayiramāne[^260-7] oḷārike obhāse kayiramāne[^260-8] nāsakkhi paṭivijjhituṃ || na Bhagavantaṃ[^260-9] yāci Tiṭṭhatu Bhagavā kappaṃ tiṭṭhatu Sugato kappāvasesaṃ[^260-10] bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya[^260-11] lokanukampāya[^260-12] atthāya hitāya sukhāya devamanussānan ti || yathā tam Mārena pariyuṭṭhitacitto || ||

10. Atha kho Bhagavā āyasmantam Ānandam āmantesi || Gaccha kho tvam Ānanda yassa dāni tvaṃ[^260-13] kālam maññasī ti || || Evam bhante ti āyasmā Ānando Bhagavato paṭissutvā uṭṭhāyāsanā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā[^260-14] avidūre[^260-15] aññatarasmiṃ rukkhamūle nisīdi || ||

11. Atha kho Māro pāpimā acīrapakkante āyasmante Ānande[^260-16] yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā etad avoca || Parinibbātu dāni bhante Bhagavā parinibbātu dāni Sugato parinibbānakālo dāni bhante Bhagavato || || Bhāsitā kho panesā bhante Bhagavatā vācā || na tāvāham[^260-17]


<!-- PTS Page 261 -->
<a id="page-261"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 261]*

Pāpima parinibbāyissāmi yāva me bhikkhū na sāvakā bhavissanti viyattā vinītā visāradā pattayogakkhemā[^261-1] bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammapaṭipannā sāmīcipaṭipannā anudhammacārino[^261-2] sakam ācariyakam[^261-3] uggahetvā ācikkhissanti desissanti paññāpessanti paṭṭhapessanti vivarissanti[^261-4] vibhajissanti uttanīkarissanti[^261-5] || uppannam parappavādam sahadhammena suniggahītaṃ niggahetvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammam desissantī ti[^261-6] || || Santi kho pana bhante etarahi bhikkhū Bhagavato sāvakā viyattā vinītā visāradā pattayogakkhemā[^261-1] bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammapaṭipannā sāmīcipaṭipannā[^261-7] anudhammacārino sakam ācariyakam uggahetvā ācikkhanti desenti paññāpenti[^261-8] vivaranti vibhajanti uttānīkaronti[^261-5] || uppannam parappavādaṃ sahadhammena suniggahītaṃ niggahetvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ desenti || ||

12. Parinibbātu dāni bhante Bhagavā parinibbātu dāni Sugato parinibbanakālo dāni bhante Bhagavato || || Bhāsitā kho panesā bhante Bhagavatā vācā || Na tāvāham[^261-9] Pāpima parinibbāyissami yāva me bhikkhuniyo na sāvikā bhavissanti || pe[^261-10] || ||

13. Yāva me upāsakā na sāvakā bhavissanti[^261-11] || yāva me upāsikā na sāvikā bhavissanti viyattā vinītā visāradā pattayogakkhemā[^261-1] bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammapaṭipannā sāmīcipaṭipannā anudhammacāriniyo || sakam ācariyakam uggahetvā ācikkhissanti desissanti paññāpessanti paṭṭhapessanti vivarissanti vibhajissanti uttānīkarissanti[^261-12] uppannam parappavādaṃ sahadhammena suniggahītam niggahetvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ desissantī ti[^261-12] || || Santi[^261-13] kho pana bhante etarahi upāsakā[^261-14] || upāsikā Bhagavato sāvikā[^261-15] viyattā vinītā vīsāradā pattayogakkhemā[^261-12] bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhamma-


<!-- PTS Page 262 -->
<a id="page-262"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 262]*

paṭipannā sāmīcipaṭipannā anudhammacāriniyo sakam ācariyakam[^262-1] uggahetvā ācikkhanti desenti paññāpenti paṭṭhapenti vivaranti vibhajanti uttānīkaronti[^262-2] || uppannam parappavādaṃ sahadhammena suniggahītaṃ niggahetvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ desenti || ||

14. Parinibbātu dāni bhante Bhagavā parinibbātu dāni[^262-3] Sugato || parinibbānakālo dāni bhante Bhagavato || || Bhāsitā kho panesā[^262-4] Bhagavatā vācā || Na tāvāham[^262-5] Pāpima parinibbāyissāmi yāva me idam brahmacariyam na iddhaṃ ceva[^262-6] bhavissati[^262-7] || phītaṃ ca vitthāritaṃ[^262-8] bāhujāññaṃ puthubhutaṃ yāvad eva manussehi supakāsitan ti || || Tayidam[^262-9] bhante Bhagavato brahmacariyam iddham ceva phitañca vitthāritam[^262-8] bahujaññam puthubhūtam yāvad eva manussehi supakāsitaṃ || || Parinibbātu dāni bhante Bhagavā[^262-10] parinibbātu dāni[^262-3] Sugato parinibbānakālo dāni bhante Bhagavato ti || ||

15. Evaṃ vutte Bhagavā Māram pāpimantam etad avoca[^262-11] || || Apossukko tvam Pāpima hohi || na ciraṃ[^262-12] Tathāgatassa parinibbānam bhavissati || ito tiṇṇam māsānam accayena Tathāgato parinibbāyissatī ti || ||

16. Atha kho Bhagavā Cāpāle cetiye sato sampajāno āyusaṅkhāram ossaji[^262-13] || || Ossaṭṭhe[^262-14] pana[^262-15] Bhagavatā āyusaṅkhāre mahābhūmicālo ahosi bhiṃsanako lomahaṃso || devadundubhiyo[^262-16] ca caliṃsu[^262-17] || ||

17. Atha kho Bhagavā etam atthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ velāyam imam udānam udānesi || ||

##### *[PTS Page 263]*

Tulam atulañca sambhavam || bhavasaṅkhāram avassaji[^263-1] muni || ajjhattarato[^263-2] samāhito[^263-3] || abhindi[^263-4] kavacam ivattasambhavan ti[^263-5] || || Cāpālavaggo pathamo || || Tatruddānaṃ[^263-6] || || Apārāpi[^263-7] Viraddho[^263-8] ca || Ariyā Nibbidā yathā[^263-9] || || Padesaṃ Sammattā[^263-10] Bhikkhu[^263-11] || Buddha-Ñāṇena Cetiyā ti[^263-12] || ||

CHAPTER II. PĀSĀDAKAMPANA-VAGGO DUTIYO.

SN_5,51(7).11. (1) Pubbe or Hetu.

1. Sāvatthi || ||

2. Pubbeva[^263-13] me bhikkhave sambodhāya anabhisambuddhassa bodhisattasseva sato etad ahosi || Ko nu kho hetu ko paccayo iddhipādabhāvanāyā ti || || Tassa mayham bhikkhave etad ahosi || ||

3. Idha bhikkhave chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgatam iddhipadam bhāveti || || Iti me chando na ca atilīno[^263-14] bhavissati || na ca atipaggahīto bhavissati || na ca[^263-15] ajjhattaṃ[^263-16] saṅkhitto bhavissati || na ca bahiddhā vikkhitto bhavissati || paccāpuresaññi ca viharati || yathā pure tathā pacchā yathā pacchā tathā pūre || yathā adho tathā uddhaṃ yathā uddhaṃ tathā adho || yathā divā tathā rattiṃ yathā rattiṃ tathā divā || || Iti vivaṭena cetasā apariyonaddhena cetasā sappabhāsaṃ cittam bhāveti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 264 -->
<a id="page-264"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 264]*

4. Viriyasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgatam iddhipādam bhāveti || || Iti me viriyaṃ na ca atilīnam bhavissati || na ca atipaggahītam bhavissati || na ca ajjhattaṃ saṅkhittam bhavissati || na ca bahiddhā vikkhittam bhavissati || pacchāpuresaññī ca viharati yathā pure tathā pacchā yathā pacchā tatthā pure || yathā adho tathā uddhaṃ || yathā uddhaṃ tathā adho || yathā divā tathā rattiṃ || yathā rattiṃ tathā divā || || Iti vivaṭena cetasā apariyonaddhena[^264-1] sappabhāsaṃ cittam bhāveti || ||

5. Cittasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgatam iddhipādam bhāveti || || Iti me cittam na ca atilīnam bhavissati na ca atipaggahītam bhavissati || na ca ajjhattaṃ saṅkhittam bhavissati || na ca bahiddhā vikkhittam bhavissati || pacchāpuresaññī ca[^264-2] viharati yathā pure tathā pacchā yathā pacchā tathāpure || yatha adho tathā uddhaṃ yathā uddhaṃ tathā adho || yathā divā tathā rattiṃ yathā rattiṃ tathā divā || || Iti vivaṭena cetasā apariyonaddhena[^264-1] sappabhāsaṃ cittam bhāveti || ||

6. Vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgatam iddhipādam bhāveti || || Iti me vīmaṃsā na ca atilīnā bhavissati || na ca atipaggahitā bhavissati || na ca ajjhattaṃ saṅkhittā bhavissati || na ca bahiddhā vikkhittā bhavissati || pacchāpuresaññī ca viharati yathā pure tathā pacchā yathā pacchā tathā pure || yathā adho tathā uddhaṃ yathā uddhaṃ tathā adho || yathā divā tathā rattiṃ yathā rattiṃ tathā divā || || Iti vivaṭena cetasā apariyonaddhena sappabhāsaṃ cittam bhāveti || ||

7. Evam bhāvitesu kho bhikkhu catusu iddhipādesu evam[^264-3] bahulīkatesu anekavihitam iddhividham paccanubhoti || eko pi hutvā bahudhā hoti bahudhā pi hutvā eko hoti || āvibhāvam tirobhāvam tirokuḍḍam[^264-4] tiropākāraṃ tiropabbatam asajjamāno gacchati seyyathāpi ākāse || pathaviyam[^264-5] pi ummujja nimujjaṃ karoti seyyathāpi udake[^264-6] || udake abhijjamāno[^264-7] gacchati seyyathāpi pathaviyaṃ[^264-5] ||ākāsepi


<!-- PTS Page 265 -->
<a id="page-265"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 265]*

pallaṅkena caṅkamati[^265-1] seyyathāpi pakkhī sakuṇo || ime pi[^265-2] candimasuriye evam mahiddhike evam mahānubhāve pāṇinā parimasati[^265-3] parimajjati || yāva[^265-4] brahmalokā pi[^265-5] kāyena vasam pavatteti[^265-6] || ||

8. Evam bhāvitesu kho bhikkhu[^265-7] catusu iddhipādesu evam bahulīkatesu dibbāya sotadhātuyā visuddhāya atikkantamanussakāya[^265-8] ubho sadde suṇāti dibbe ca mānusse[^265-9] ca ye dūre santike vā ti || ||

9. Evam bhāvitesu kho bhikkhu catusu iddhipādesu evam bahulīkatesu parasattānam parapuggalānam cetasā ceto[^265-10] paricca pajānāti || || Sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ Sarāgaṃ cittanti pajānāti || vītarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ Vītarāgaṃ[^265-11] cittanti pajānāti || || Sadosaṃ vā cittaṃ Sadosaṃ cittanti pajānāti || vītadosaṃ vā cittaṃ Vītadosaṃ cittan ti pajānāti || || Samohaṃ vā cittaṃ Samohaṃ cittan ti pajānāti || vītamohaṃ vā cittam Vītamohaṃ cittan ti pajānāti || || Saṅkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ Saṅkhittaṃ cittan ti pajānāti || vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ Vikkhittaṃ cittan ti pajānāti || || Mahaggataṃ[^265-12] vā cittaṃ Mahaggataṃ[^265-12] cittan ti pajānāti || amahaggataṃ vā cittam Amahaggataṃ cittan ti pajānāti || || Sauttaraṃ vā cittaṃ Sauttaraṃ cittan ti pajānāti || anuttaraṃ vā cittaṃ Anuttaraṃ cittan ti pajānāti || || Asamāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ Asamāhitaṃ cittan ti pajānāti || samāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ Samāhitaṃ cittan ti pajānāti || || Avimuttam vā cittaṃ Avimuttaṃ cittanti pajānāti || vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ Vimuttaṃ cittan ti pajānāti || ||

10. Evam bhāvitesu kho bhikkhu catusu iddhipādesu evam bahulīkatesu anekavihitam pubbenivāsam anussarati || seyyathīdam ekam pi jātim dve pi[^265-13] jātiyo tisso pi jātiyo catasso pi jātiyo pañca pi jātiyo dasa pi jātiyo vīsam[^265-14] pi jātiyo tiṃsam pi jātiyo cattālisam[^265-15] pi jātiyo paññāsam


<!-- PTS Page 266 -->
<a id="page-266"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 266]*

pi jātiyo jātisatam pi jātisahassam pi jātisatasahassam pi aneke pi saṃvaṭṭakappe aneke pi vivaṭṭakappe aneke pi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappe amutrāsiṃ evam nāmo || evaṅgotto || evaṃvaṇṇo evamāhāro evaṃsukhadukkhapaṭisaṃvedī evam āyupariyanto || so tato cuto amutra uppādiṃ[^266-1] || tatravāsim[^266-2] evaṃnāmo evaṅgotto evaṃvaṇṇo evamāhāro evaṃsukhadukkhapaṭisaṃvedī evam āyupariyanto || so tato cuto idhupapanno ti || iti sākāraṃ sauddesam anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsam anussarati || ||

11. Evam bhāvitesu kho bhikkhu[^266-3] catusu iddhipādesu evam bahulīkatesu dibbena cakkhunā visuddhena atikkantamānussakena[^266-4] satte passati || cavamāne upapajjamāne hīne paṇīte suvaṇṇe dubbaṇṇe sugate duggate yathākammupage satte pajānāti || Ime vata bhonto sattā kāyaduccaritena samannāgatā vacīducaritena samannāgatā manoduccaritena samannāgatā ariyānam upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā[^266-5] micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā || te kāyassa bhedā param maraṇā[^266-6] apāyaṃ duggatim vinipātaṃ nirayam upapannā[^266-7] || || Ime kho pana bhonto sattā kāyasucaritena[^266-8] sammannāgatā vacīsucaritena[^266-9] samannāgatā manosucaritena[^266-9] samannāgatā ariyānam anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā || te kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggam lokam upapannā[^266-10] ti || || Iti dibbena cakkhunā visuddhena atikkantamānussakena satte[^266-11] passati || cavamāne upapajjamāne[^266-12] hīne paṇīte suvaṇṇe dubbaṇṇe || sugate duggate yathākammupage satte pajānāti || ||

12. Evam bhāvitesu kho bhikkhu[^266-13] catusu iddhipādesu evam bahulīkatesu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttim paññāvimuttim diṭṭhevadhamme sayam abhiññā[^266-14] sacchikatvā upasampajja viharatī ti || ||

SN_5,51(7).12. (2) Mahapphala.

2. Cattāro me bhikkhave iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā honti mahānisaṃsā || || Katham bhāvitā ca[^267-1] bhikkhave cattāro[^267-2] iddhipādā katham bahulīkatā mahapphalā honti mahānisaṃsā || ||

3. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgatam iddhipādam bhāveti || Iti kho[^267-3] me chando na ca atilīno bhavissati || na ca atipaggahīto bhavissati || na ca ajjhattaṃ saṅkhitto[^267-4] bhavissati || na ca bahiddhā vikkhitto bhavissati || pacchāpuresaññī ca viharati yathāpure tathā pacchā yathā pacchā tathāpure || yathā adho tathā uddhaṃ yathā uddhaṃ tathā adho || yathā divā tathā rattiṃ yathā rattiṃ tathā divā || iti vivaṭena cetasā apariyonaddhena[^267-5] sappabhāsaṃ cittam bhāveti || || 4--5. Viriyasamādhi || pe [la] || Cittasamādhi || ||

6. Vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkharasamannāgatam iddhipādham bhāveti || Iti kho[^267-6] me vīmaṃsā na ca atilīnā bhavissati || na ca atipaggahitā bhavissati || na ca ajjhattaṃsaṅkhittā bhavissati || na ca bahiddhā vikkhittā[^267-7] bhavissati || pacchāpure saññī ca viharati yathāpure tathā pacchā yathā pacchā tathāpure || yathā adho tathā uddhaṃ yathā uddhaṃ tathā adho || yathā divā tathā rattiṃ yathā rattiṃ tathā divā || || Iti vivaṭena cetasā apariyonaddhena sappabhāsaṃ cittam bhāveti || || Evam bhāvitā kho bhikkhave cattāro iddhipādā evam bahulīkatā mahapphalā honti mahānisaṃsā || || 7--11. Evam bhāvitesu kho bhikkhave bhikkhu[^267-9] catusu iddhipādesu evam bahulīkatesu anekavihitam iddhividham paccanubhoti || eko pi hutvā bahudhā hoti || la-pe || yāva brahmalokā pi kāyena vasaṃ vatteti || pe || [la] || ||

12. Evam bhāvitesu kho bhikkhave bhikkhu catusu iddhipādesu evam bahulīkatesu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavam cetovimuttim paññāvimuttiṃ diṭṭheva dhamme sayam abhiññā[^268-1] sacchikatvā upasampajja viharatī ti || ||

SN_5,51(7).13. (3) Chando.

2. Chandaṃ ce[^268-2] bhikkhave bhikkhu nissāya[^268-3] labhati samādhiṃ labhati cittassa ekaggataṃ[^268-4] || ayam vuccati chandasamādhi || || So anuppannānam pāpakānam akusalānaṃ dhammānam anuppādāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati viriyam ārabhati cittam paggaṇhāti padahati || || Uppannānaṃ pāpakānam akusalānam dhammānam pahānāya chandam janeti vāyamati viriyam āravhati cittam paggaṇhāti padahati || Anuppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānam uppādāya chandaṃ janeti vāyamati viriyam ārabhati cittam paggaṇhāti padahati || || Uppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammosāya bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janeti vāyamati viriyam ārabhati cittam paggaṇhāti padahati || || Ime vuccanti padhānasaṅkhārā[^268-5] || || Iti ayaṃ ca chando ayaṃ ca chandasamādhi ime ca padhānasaṅkhārā || ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgato iddhipādo || ||

3. Viriyaṃ ce[^268-6] bhikkhave bhikkhu nissāya[^268-7] labhati samādhim labhati[^268-8] cittassa ekaggatam[^268-4] || ayaṃ vuccati viriyasamādhi || || So anuppannānaṃ || la[^268-9] || Uppannānaṃ kusalānam dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammosāya bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janeti vāyamati viriyam ārabhati cittam paggaṇhāti padahati || || Ime vuccanti padhānasaṅkharā[^268-10] || || Iti idaṃ ca viriyam ayañca viriyasamādhi ime ca padhānasaṅkhārā || ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave viriyasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgato iddhipādo || ||


<!-- PTS Page 269 -->
<a id="page-269"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 269]*

4. Cittaṃ ce[^269-1] bhikkhave bhikkhu nissāya[^269-2] labhati samādhiṃ labhati cittassa ekaggatam || ayaṃ vuccati cittasamādhi || || So anuppannānam pāpakānaṃ[^269-3] || la || uppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānam ṭhitiyā asammosāya bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandam janeti vāyamati viriyam ārabhati cittam paggaṇhāti padahati || || Ime vuccanti padhānasaṅkhārā ti || || Iti[^269-4] idaṃ ca cittaṃ ayaṃ ca cittasamādhi ime ca padhānasaṅkharā || ayam vuccati bhikkhave cittasamādhi padhānasaṅkhārasamannāgato iddhipādo || ||

5. Vīmaṃsaṃ ce bhikkhave bhikkhu nissāya[^269-2] labhati samādhiṃ labhati cittassa ekaggataṃ[^269-5] || ayam vuccati vīmaṃsāsamādhi || || So anuppannānam pāpakānam[^269-4] akusalānaṃ dhammānam anuppādāya || pe[^269-6] || uppannānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammosāya bhiyyobhāvāya vepullāya bhāvanāya paripūriyā chandaṃ janeti vāyamati viriyam ārabhati[^269-7] cittam paggaṇhāti padahati || || Ime vuccanti padhānasaṅkhārā || || Iti ayaṃ ca vīmaṃsā ayaṃ ca vīmaṃsāsamādhi ime ca padhānasaṅkhārā || Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgato iddhipādoti || ||

SN_5,51(7).14. (4) Moggalāno.

1. Evam me sutaṃ[^269-8] Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Pubbārāme Migāramātupāsāde || ||

2. Tena kho pana samayena sambahulā bhikkhū heṭṭhā Migāramātupāsāde viharanti uddhatā unnaḷā capalā mukharā vikiṇṇavācā muṭṭhassatino asampajānā asamāhitā vibbhantacittā[^269-9] pākatindriyā || ||

3. Atha kho Bhagavā āyasmantam Mahā-Moggalānam āmantesi || || Ete kho[^269-10] Moggalāna sabrahmacāriyā[^269-11] heṭṭhā


<!-- PTS Page 270 -->
<a id="page-270"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 270]*

Migāramātupāsāde viharanti uddhatā unnaḷā capalā mukharā vikiṇṇavācā[^270-1] muṭṭhassatino asampajānā asamāhitā vibbhantacittā[^270-2] pākatindriyā || gaccha Moggalāna te bhikkhū saṃvejehīti[^270-3] || || Evam bhante ti kho āyasmā Mahā-Moggalāno Bhagavato paṭissutvā tathārūpam iddhābhisaṅkhāram abhisaṅkhāresi[^270-4] || yathā pādaṅguṭṭhakena Migāramātupāsādam saṅkampesi[^270-5] sampakampesi sampacālesi || ||

4. Atha kho te bhikkhū {saṃviggā} lomahaṭṭhajātā ekam antam aṭṭhaṃsu || Acchariyaṃ vata bho abbhutaṃ vata bho nivātañca vata ayaṃ ca Migaramatupāsādo gambhiranamo[^270-6] sunikhāto acalo asampakampi[^270-7] || atha ca pana saṅkampito sampakampito sampacālitoti || ||

5. Atha kho Bhagavā yena te bhikkhū tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā te bhikkhū Bhagavā etad avoca || || Kiṃ nu tumhe bhikkhave {saṃviggā} lomahaṭṭhajātā ekam antaṃ ṭhitā ti || || Acchariyam bhante abbhutam bhante nivātañca vata ayaṃ ca Migāramātupāsādo gambhīranemo[^270-8] sunikhāto acalo asampakampi[^270-7] || atha ca pana saṅkampito sampakampito sampacālito ti || ||

6. Tumheva kho bhikkhave saṃvejetukāmena[^270-9] Moggalānena bhikkhunā pādaṅguṭṭhakena[^270-10] Migāramātupāsādo saṅkampito sampakampito sampacālito || || Taṃ kim[^270-11] maññatha bhikkhave || katamesaṃ dhammānam bhāvitattā[^270-12] bahulīkatattā Moggalāno bhikkhu evam mahiddhiko evam mahānubhāvo ti || || Bhagavaṃmūlakā no bhante dhammā Bhagavaṃnettikā- -Bhagavato sutvā bhikkhū dhāressantī ti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 271 -->
<a id="page-271"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 271]*

7. Tena bhikkhave suṇātha[^271-1] || || Catunnaṃ kho bhikkhave iddhipādānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā Moggalāno bhikkhu evam mahiddhiko evam mahānubhāvo || katamesaṃ catunnaṃ || ||

8. Idha bhikkhave Moggalano bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgatam iddhipādam bhāveti[^271-2] || viriyasamādhi || cittasamādhi || vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgatam iddhipādam bhāveti || || Iti me vīmaṃsā na ca atilīnā bhavissati na ca atipaggahītā bhavissati || na ca ajjhattaṃsaṅkhittā bhavissati || na ca bahiddhā vikkhittā bhavissati || pacchāpuresaññī ca viharati yathā pure tathā pacchā yathā pacchā tathā pure || yathā adho tathā uddhaṃ yathā uddhaṃ tathā adho || yathā divā tathā rattiṃ yathā rattiṃ tathā divā || || Iti vivaṭena cetasā apariyonaddhena[^271-3] sappabhāsaṃ cittam bhāveti || ||

9. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave catunnam iddhipādānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā Moggalāno bhikkhu evam mahiddhiko evam mahānubhāvo || ||

10. Imesaṃ ca pana bhikkhave catunnam[^271-4] iddhipādānam bhavitattā bahulīkatattā Moggalāno bhikkhu anekavihitam iddhividham paccanubhoti || la-pe || yāva brahmalokā pi[^271-5] kāyena vasaṃ vatteti || ||

11. imesaṃ ca pana bhikkhave catunnam iddhipadānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā Moggalāno bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttim ||pe || upasampajja viharatīti || ||

12. Evaṃ cāpi abhiññā[^271-6] vitthāretabbā[^271-7] || ||

SN_5,51(7).15. (5) Brāhmaṇa.

1. Evam me sutaṃ[^271-8] Ekaṃ samayam āyasmā Ānando Kosambiyaṃ viharati Ghositārāme || ||


<!-- PTS Page 272 -->
<a id="page-272"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 272]*

2. Atha kho Uṇṇābho brāhmaṇo yenāyasmā Ānando tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā Ānandena saddhim sammodi || sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ vītisāretvā ekam antaṃ nisīdi || || Ekam antaṃ nisinno kho Uṇṇābho brāhmaṇo āyasmantaṃ Ānandam etad avoca || ||

3. Kim atthi yaṃ[^272-1] nu kho bho Ānanda samaṇe Gotame[^272-3] brahmacariyaṃ vussatī ti || || Chandapahānatthaṃ kho brāhmaṇa Bhagavati[^272-3] brahmacariyaṃ vussatī ti || ||

4. Atthi pana bho[^272-4] Ānanda maggo atthi paṭipadā etassa chandassa pahānāyā ti || || Atthi kho brāhmaṇa maggo atthi paṭipadā etassa chandassa pahānāyā ti || ||

5. Katamo pana bho Ānanda maggo katamā paṭipadā etassa chandassa pahānāyā ti || || Idha brāhmaṇa bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgatam[^272-5] iddhipādam bhāveti || viriyasamādhi || cittasamādhi || vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgatam iddhipādam bhāveti || ayaṃ kho brāhmaṇa maggo ayam paṭipadā etassa chandassa pahānāyā ti || ||

6. Evaṃ sante[^272-6] kho[^272-7] Ānanda santakaṃ[^272-8] hoti no asantakam[^272-9] chandena ca[^272-10] chandam pajahissatī ti netam thānaṃ vijjatīti[^272-11] || || Tena hi brāhmaṇa taññevettha[^272-12] paṭipucchissāmi || yathā te khameyya tathā taṃ vyākareyyāsi || ||

7. Taṃ kim maññasi brāhmaṇa || ahosi te pubbe chando Ārāmaṃ gamissāmīti || tassa te ārāmagatassa yo tajjo chando so paṭippassadho[^272-13] ti || || Evam bho || || Ahosi te[^272-14] pubbe viriyaṃ Ārāmam gamissāmīti || tassa te ārāmagatassa yaṃ tajjaṃ viriyaṃ tam paṭipassaddhanti[^272-15] || ||


<!-- PTS Page 273 -->
<a id="page-273"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 273]*

Evam bho || || Ahosi te pubbe cittaṃ Ārāmaṃ gamissāmī ti || tassa te ārāmagatassa yam tajjaṃ cittaṃ tam paṭippassaddhanti[^273-1] || || Evam bho || || Ahosi te pubbe vīmaṃsā Ārāmaṃ gamissāmīti || tassa te ārāmagatassa yā tajjā vīmaṃsā sā paṭippassaddhā ti[^273-2] || || Evam bho || ||

8. Evam eva kho brāhmaṇa yo so bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavo vusitavā katakaraṇīyo ohitabhāro anuppattasadattho parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyojano sammadaññā vimutto || tassa yo pubbe[^273-3] chando ahosi arahattapattiyā arahatte[^273-4] patte yo tajjo chando so paṭippassaddho || || Yam pubbe viriyam ahosi arahattapattiyā arahatte patte yaṃ tajjaṃ viriyaṃ tam paṭippassaddhaṃ || || Yam pubbe cittam ahosi arahattapattiyā[^273-5] arahatte patte yaṃ[^273-6] tajjaṃ cittaṃ tam paṭippassaddhaṃ || || Yā pubbe vīmaṃsā ahosi arahattapattiyā arahatte patte yā tajjā vīmaṃsā sā paṭippassaddhā || ||

9. Taṃ kim maññasi brāhmaṇa || Iti evaṃ sante santakam vā hoti[^273-7] asantakaṃ[^273-8] vā ti || || Addhā bho Ānanda evaṃ sante santakaṃ hoti no asantakaṃ[^273-8] || ||

10. Abhikkantam bho Ānanda || pe || ajjatagge pāṇupetaṃ saraṇaṃ gatanti || ||

SN_5,51(7).16. (6) Samaṇabrāhmaṇā1 or Mahiddhi.

2. Ye hi keci bhikkhave atītam addhānam samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā mahiddhikā ahesuṃ mahānubhāvā || sabbe te catunnam iddhipādānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā || ||

3. Ye hi[^273-9] keci bhikkhave anāgatam addhānam samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā mahiddhikā bhavissanti- -bahulīkatattā || ||

4. Ye hi keci bhikkhave etarahi samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā mahiddhikā mahānubhāvā sabbe te catunnam iddhipādānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā || || Katamesam catunnaṃ || ||


<!-- PTS Page 274 -->
<a id="page-274"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 274]*

5. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgataṃ iddhipādam bhāveti || viriyasamādhi || cittasamādhi || vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgatam iddhipādam bhāveti || ||

6. Ye hi keci bhikkhave atītam addhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā mahiddhikā ahesuṃ mahānubhāvā || pe || mahiddhikā bhavissanti mahānubhāvā || [pe] || etarahi mahiddhikā mahānubhāvā || sabbe te imesaṃ yeva catunnam iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā ti || ||

SN_5,51(7).17. (7) Samaṇabrāhmaṇā2 or Vidhā.

2. Ye hi keci bhikkhave atītam addhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā anekavihitam iddhividham paccanubhosuṃ || eko pi hutvā bahudhā ahesuṃ || bahudhā hutvā eko ahesuṃ || āvibhāvaṃ tirobhāvaṃ tirokuḍḍam[^274-1] tiropākāraṃ tiropabbataṃ[^274-2] asajjamānā agamaṃsu seyyathāpi ākāse || pathaviyam[^274-3] pi ummujjanimujjam[^274-4] akaṃsu[^274-5] seyyathāpi udake || udake pi abhijjamānā[^274-6] agamaṃsu seyyathāpi pathaviyaṃ || akāse[^274-7] pi[^274-8] pallaṅkena agamiṃsu[^274-9] seyyathāpi pakkhī sakuṇo || ime pi candimasuriye[^274-10] evam mahiddhike evam mahānubhāve pāṇinā parimasiṃsu parimajjiṃsu || yāva Brahmalokā pi kāyena vasaṃ vattesaṃ || sabbe te catunnam iddhipādānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā || ||

3. Ye hi keci bhikkhave aṇāgatam addhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā anekavihitam iddhividham paccanubhossanti || eko pi hutvā bahudhā bhavissanti || bahudhā pi hutvā eko bhavissanti || āvibhāvaṃ tirobhāvam tirokuḍḍam tiropākāraṃ tiropabbatam asajjamānā[^274-11] gamissanti[^274-12] seyyathāpi ākāse || pathaviyam[^274-13] pi ummujjanimujjaṃ[^274-14] karissanti


<!-- PTS Page 275 -->
<a id="page-275"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 275]*

seyyathāpi udake || udake pi abhijjamānā[^275-1] gamissanti seyyathāpi pathaviyaṃ || akāse pi pallaṅkena bhavissanti[^275-2] seyyathāpi pakkhī sakuṇo || ime pi[^275-3] candimasuriye evam mahiddhikke evam mahānubhāve pāṇinā parimasissanti parimajjissanti yāva Brahmalokā pi kāyena vasaṃ vattessanti[^275-4] || sabbe te catunnam iddhipādānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā || ||

4. Ye hi keci bhikkhave etarahi samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā anekavihitam iddhividam paccanubhonti || eko pi hutvā bahudhā honti || bahudhā pi hutvā eko[^275-5] honti || āvibhāvaṃ tirobhāvaṃ tirokuḍḍam tiropākāram tiropabbatam asajjamānā gacchanti seyyathāpi[^275-6] ākāse || pe || yāva Brahmalokā pi kāyena vasaṃ vattenti || sabbe te catunnam iddhipādānam bhavitattā bahulīkatattā[^275-7] || katamesaṃ catunnaṃ || ||

5. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgatam iddhipādam bhāveti[^275-8] || viriyasamādhi || cittasamādhi || vīmaṃsasamādhipaḍhānasaṅkhārasamannāgatam bhāveti || ||

6. Ye hi keci bhikkhave atītam addhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā anekavihitam iddhividham paccanubhosuṃ || pe || paccanubhossanti || [pe] || paccanubhonti || [pe]|| sabbe te imesaṃ yeva catunnam iddhipādānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā ti || ||

SN_5,51(7).18. (8) Bhikkhu.

2. Catunnam bhikkhave iddhipādānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttim paññāvimuttiṃ diṭṭheva dhamme sayam abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharati || Katamesaṃ catunnaṃ || ||

3. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgatam iddhipādam bhāveti || viriyasamādhi || cittasamādhi || vimaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgatam iddhipādam bhāveti || || Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave catunnam iddhipādānam bhāvitattā bhikkhu āsavānaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 276 -->
<a id="page-276"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 276]*

khayā anāsavam cetovimuttim paññāvimuttim diṭṭheva dhamme sayam abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharatī ti[^276-1] || ||

SN_5,51(7).19. (9) Desanā or Bhāvanā.

2. Iddhiṃ vo[^276-2] bhikkhave desissāmi iddhipādaṃ ca iddhipādabhāvanaṃ ca iddhipādabhāvanāgāminiṃ ca paṭipadaṃ || taṃ suṇātha || ||

3. Katamā ca bhikkhave iddhi || || Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu anekavihitam iddhividham paccanubhoti || eko pi hutvā bahudhā hoti || pe-la || yāva brahmalokā pi kāyena vasaṃ vatteti || || Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave iddhi || ||

4. Katamo ca bhikkhave iddhipādo || || Yo[^276-3] bhikkhave maggo yā[^276-4] paṭipadā iddhilābhāya[^276-5] iddhipaṭilābhāya saṃvattati || ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave[^276-6] iddhipādo || ||

5. Katamā ca bhikkhave iddhipādabhāvanā || || Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgatam iddhipādam bhāveti || viriyasamādhi || cittasamādhi || vimaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgatam iddhipādam bhāveti || || Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave iddhipādabhāvanā || ||

6. Katamā ca bhikkhave iddhipādabhāvanāgāminī paṭipadā || ayam eva ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo || seyyathīdam sammāditthi || pe || sammāsamādhi || || Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave iddhipādabhāvanāgāminī paṭipadā ti || ||

SN_5,51(7).20. (10) Vibhaṅga. I.

2. Cattāro me[^276-6] bhikkhave iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā honti mahānisaṃsā || || Katham bhāvitā ca bhikkhave cattāro iddhipādā katham bahulīkatā mahapphalā honti mahānisaṃsā || ||

3. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgatam iddhipādam bhāveti || Iti me chando


<!-- PTS Page 277 -->
<a id="page-277"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 277]*

na ca atilīno[^277-1] bhavissati || na ca atipaggahīto bhavissati || na ca ajjhattaṃ saṅkhitto[^277-2] bhavissati || na ca bahiddhā vikkhitto bhavissati || pe || Iti vivaṭena cetasā apariyonaddhena[^277-3] sappabhāsam cittam bhāveti || viriyasamādhi || cittasamādhi || vimaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgatam iddhipādam bhāveti || Iti me[^277-4] vīmaṃsā na ca atilīnā bhavissati || na ca atipaggahītā[^277-5] bhavissati || na ca ajjhattaṃ saṅkhittā bhavissati na ca bahiddhā vikkhittā bhavissati || pacchāpuresaññī ca viharati || yathā pure tathā pacchā yathā pacchā tathā pure || yatha adho tathā uddhaṃ yathā uddhaṃ tathā adho || yathā divā tathā rattiṃ yathā rattiṃ tathā divā || iti vivaṭena cetasā apariyonaddhena[^277-3] sappabhāsaṃ cittam bhāveti || || II.

4. Katamo ca bhikkhave atilīno chando || || Yo bhikkhave chando kosajjasahagato kosajjasampayutto || ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave atilīno chando || ||

5. Katamo ca bhikkhave atipaggahīto chando || || Yo bhikkhave chando[^277-6] uddhaccasahagato uddhaccasampayutto || ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave atipaggahīto chando || ||

6. Katamo ca bhikkhave ajjhattaṃ[^277-7] saṅkhitto chando || || Yo[^277-8] bhikkhave chando[^277-9] thīnamiddhasahagato thīnamiddhasampayutto || ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave ajjhattaṃ[^277-7] saṅkhitto chando || ||

7. Katamo ca bhikkhave bahiddhā vikkhitto chando || yo[^277-10] bhikkhave chando bahiddhā pañcakāmaguṇe ārabbha anuvikkhito anuvisaṭo[^277-11] || ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave bahiddhā vikkhitto chando || ||

8. Kathañca bhikkhave bhikkhu pacchāpuresaññī[^277-12] ca viharati yathā pure tathā pacchā yathā pacchā tathāpure || ||


<!-- PTS Page 278 -->
<a id="page-278"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 278]*

Idha bhikkhave bhikkhuno[^278-1] pacchāpure saññā suggahītā hoti sumanasikatā supadhāritā suppaṭividdhā paññāya || Evaṃ kho bhikkhave bhikkhu pacchāpure saññī ca[^278-2] viharati yathā pure tathā pacchā yathā pacchā tathā pure || ||

9. Kathañca bhikkhave bhikkhu yathā adho tathā uddham yatha uddhaṃ tathā adho viharati || || Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu imam eva kāyam uddham pādatalā adho kesamatthakā tacapariyantam pūram nānappakārassa asucino paccavekkhati || Atthi imasmiṃ kāye kesā lomā nakhā dantā taco maṃsaṃ nahārū[^278-3] aṭṭhī aṭṭhimiñjā[^278-4] vakkaṃ hadayaṃ yakanaṃ kilomakam pihakam papphāsam[^278-5] antaṃ antaguṇam udariyaṃ karīsaṃ pittaṃ semhaṃ pubbo lohitaṃ sedo medo assu vasā kheḷo siṅghānikā kasikā[^278-6] muttan ti || || Evaṃ kho bhikkhave bhikkhu yathā adho tathā uddhaṃ yathā uddhaṃ tathā adho viharati || ||

10. Kathañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhu yathā divā tathā rattiṃ yathā rattiṃ tathā divā viharati || || Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu yehi ākārehi yehi liṅgehi yehi nimittehi divā chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgatam iddhipādam bhāveti || so tehi ākārehi tehi liṅgehi tehi nimittehi rattiṃ chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgatam[^278-7] iddhipādam bhāveti || || Yehi vā pana[^278-8] ākārehi yehi[^278-9] liṅgehi yehi nimittehi rattiṃ chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgatam iddhipādam bhāveti || so tehi ākārehi tehi liṅgehi tehi nimittehi divā chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgatam iddhipādam bhāveti || || Evaṃ kho bhikkhave bhikkhu yathā divā tathā rattiṃ yathā rattiṃ tathā divā viharati || ||

11. Kathañca bhikkhave bhikkhu vivaṭena cetasā apariyonaddhena[^278-10] sappabhāsaṃ cittam bhāveti || || Idha bhikkhave bhikkhuno ālokasaññā suggahītā hoti divāsaññā svādhiṭṭhitā[^278-11] || || Evaṃ kho bhikkhave bhikkhu vivaṭena cetasā apariyonaddhena[^278-10] sappabhāsam cittam bhāveti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 279 -->
<a id="page-279"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 279]*

III.

12. Katamañca bhikkhave atilīnaviriyaṃ[^279-1] || || Yam bhikkhave viriyaṃ kosajjasahagataṃ kosajjasampayuttaṃ || idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave atilīnaviriyaṃ[^279-1] || ||

13. Katamañca bhikkhave atipaggahītaviriyaṃ[^279-2] || Yam bhikkhave viriyaṃ uddhaccasahagatam uddhaccasampayuttaṃ || idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave atipaggahītaṃ viriyaṃ || ||

14. Katamañca bhikkhave ajjhattaṃ saṅkhittaṃ[^279-3] viriyam || || Yam bhikkhave viriyaṃ thīnamiddhasahagataṃ thīnamiddhasampayuttaṃ || idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave ajjhattaṃ saṅkhittaṃ[^279-3] viriyaṃ || ||

15. Katamañca bhikkhave bahiddhā vikkhittaṃ viriyaṃ || || Yam bhikkhave viriyaṃ bahiddhā pañcakāmaguṇe ārabbha anuvikkhittam[^279-4] anuvisaṭaṃ || || Idam vuccati bhikkhave bahiddhā vikkhittaṃ viriyaṃ || || 16--19. Peyyālo || || Kathañca bhikkhave bhikkhu vivaṭena cetasā apariyonaddhena sappabhāsam bhāveti || || Idha bhikkhave bhikkhuno ālokasaññā sugahitā hoti divāsaññā svādhiṭṭhitā[^279-5] || || Evaṃ kho bhikkhave bhikkhu vivaṭen cetasā apariyonaddhena sappabhāsaṃ cittam bhāveti[^279-6] || || IV.

20. Katamañca bhikkhave atilīnaṃ cittaṃ || yam pi bhikkhave cittaṃ kosajjasahagataṃ kosajjasampayuttaṃ || idam vuccati bhikkhave atilīnaṃ cittaṃ || ||

21. Katamañca bhikkhave atipaggahītaṃ cittaṃ || || Yam bhikkhave cittam uddhaccasahagatam[^279-7] uddhaccasampayuttaṃ || idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave atipaggahītaṃ cittaṃ || ||

22. Katamañca bhikkhave ajjhattaṃ saṅkhittaṃ cittaṃ || || Yam bhikkhave cittaṃ thīnamiddhasahagatam[^279-8] thīnamiddhasampayuttaṃ || idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave ajjhattaṃ saṅkhittaṃ cittaṃ || ||

##### *[PTS Page 280]*

23. Katamañca bhikkhave bahiddhā vikkhittaṃ cittaṃ || || Yam bhikkhave cittam bahiddhā pañcaguṇe ārabbha anuvikkhittam anuvisaṭaṃ || idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave bahiddhā[^280-1] vikkhittaṃ cittaṃ || || 24--27. Peyyālam || evam kho bhikkhave bhikkhu vivaṭena cetasā apariyonaddhena sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāveti[^280-2] || || V.

28. Katamā[^280-3] ca bhikkhave atilīṇā[^280-4] vīmaṃsā || || Yā bhikkhave vīmaṃsā kosajjasahagatā kosajjasampayuttā || ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave atilīnā vīmaṃsā || ||

29. Katamā[^280-3] ca bhikkhave atipaggahītā vīmaṃsā || || Yā bhikkhave vīmaṃsā uddhaccasahagatā[^280-4] uddhaccasampayuttā || ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave atipaggahītā vīmaṃsā || ||

30. Katamāca bhikkhave ajjhattaṃ[^280-5] saṅkhittā vīmaṃsā || || Yā bhikkhave vīmaṃsā thīnamiddhassahagatā thīnamiddhasampayuttā || ayam vuccati bhikkhave ajjhattaṃsaṅkhittā vīmaṃsā || ||

31. Katamā[^280-3] ca bhikkhave bahiddhā vikkhittā vīmaṃsā || || Yā bhikkhave vīmaṃsā bahiddhā pañcakāmaguṇe ārabbha anuvikkhittā anuvisaṭā || ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave bahiddhā vikkhittā vīmaṃsā || || 32--35. la[^280-6] || || Evaṃ kho bhikkhave bhikkhu vivaṭena cetasā apariyonaddhena sappabhāsam cittam bhāveti || ||

Evam bhāvitā kho bhikkhave cattāro iddhipādā evam bahulīkatā mahapphalā honti mahānisaṃsā || evam bhāvitesu kho bhikkhave bhikkhu catusu iddhipādesu evam bahulīkatesu anekavihitam iddhividham paccanubhoti || || Eko pi hutvā bahudhā hoti || bahudhā pi hutvā eko hoti[^280-7] || la || yāva Brahmalokā pi kāyena vasaṃ vatteti || pe || ||

37. Evam bhāvitesu kho bhikkhave bhikkhu catusu iddhipādesu evam bahulīkatesu[^280-8] āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ

cetovimuttim paññāvimuttim diṭṭheva dhamme sayam abhiññā[^281-1] sacchikatvā upasampajja viharatī ti || ||

38. Cha pi abhiññāyo vitthāretabbā[^281-2] || || Pāsādakampanavaggo dutiyo[^281-3] || || Imassa vaggassa[^281-4] uddānaṃ || || Pubbe[^281-5] Mahapphalā[^281-6] Chando || Moggalāno ca Brāhmaṇo || Dve Samaṇabrāhmaṇā[^281-7] Bhikkhu[^281-8] || Desanā[^281-9] Vibhaṅgena cā ti || ||

CHAPTER III. VAGGO TATIYO.

SN_5,51(7).21. (1) Maggo.

1. Sāvatthi || ||

2. Pubbe[^281-10] me bhikkhave sambodhā[^281-1] anabhisambuddhassa bodhisattasseva etad ahosi || Ko nu kho maggo kā paṭipadā iddhipādabhāvanāyā ti || || Tassa mayham bhikkhave etad ahosi || ||

3. So bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgatam iddhipādam bhāveti || || Iti me chando na atilīno bhavissati || pe || sappabhāsaṃ cittam bhāveti || || 4--5. Viriyasamādhi || cittasamādhi || ||

6. Vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgatam iddhipādam bhāveti || || Iti me vīmaṃsā na ca atilīnā bhavissati || na ca atipaggahītā[^281-11] bhavissati || na ca ajjhattaṃ saṅkhittā bhavissati || na ca bahiddhā vikkhittā bhavissati || pacchāpuresaññī ca viharati || yathāpure tathā pacchā yathā pacchā tathā pure || yathā adho tathā uddhaṃ yathā uddhaṃ tathā adho || yathā divā tathā rattiṃ[^281-12] yathā rattiṃ[^281-12] tathā divā || Iti vivaṭena cetasā apariyonaddhena[^281-13] sappabhāsaṃ cittam bhāveti || ||

3. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave pañcannam uddhambhāgiyānam saṃyojanānam abhiññaya pariññāya parikkhayāya pahānāya cattāro iddhipādā bhāvetabbā || katame cattaro.

4. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgatam iddhipādam bhāveti || viriyasamādhi || cittasamādhi || vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasamannāgatam iddhipādam bhāveti[^292-5] ||

5. Imesaṃ kho bhikkhave pañcannam uddhambhāgiyanaṃ saṃyojanānam abhiññāya pariññāya parikkhayāya pahānāya ime cattāro iddhipādā bhāvetabbā ti || || Oghavaggo iddhipādavasena vitthāretabbo || ||


<!-- PTS Page 293 -->
<a id="page-293"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 293]*

Yadipi[^293-1] satipaṭṭhānā tad api iddhipādaṃ vitthāretabbaṃ || || [Uddānam] Ogho Yogo Upādānaṃ Ganthā Anusayena ca || Kāmaguṇā Nīvaraṇa-Gandhā Oruddhambhāgiyāni[^293-2] || || Iddhipādasamyuttaṃ sattamaṃ[^293-3] || ||


<!-- PTS Page 294 -->
<a id="page-294"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 294]*

BOOK VIII. ANURUDDHA-SAṂYUTTAM.

CHAPTER I. RAHOGATAVAGGO PATHAMO.

SN_5,52(8).1. (1) Rahogata1.

1. Evam me sutaṃ[^294-1] Ekaṃ samayam āyasmā Anuruddho Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme || ||

2. Atha kho āyasmato Anuruddhassa rahogatassa paṭisallīnassa evaṃ cetaso parivitakko udapādi || || Yesaṃ kesañci cattāro satipaṭṭhānā viraddhā || viraddho tesam ariyo maggo sammādukkhakkhayagāmī || Yesaṃ kesañci cattāro satipaṭṭhānā āraddhā || āraddho tesam ariyo maggo sammādukkhakkhayagāmī ti[^294-2] || ||

3. Atha kho āyasmā Mahā-Moggalāno āyasmato Anuruddhassa cetasā cetoparivitakkam aññāya seyyathāpi nāma balavā[^294-3] puriso sammiñjitam vā bāham pasāreyya pasāritaṃ vā bāham sammiñjeyya[^294-4] || evam eva[^294-5] āyasmato Anuruddhassa sammukhe[^294-6] pātur ahosi || ||

4. Atha kho āyasmā Mahā-Moggalāno āyasmantam Anuruddham etad avoca || || Kittāvatā nu kho āvuso Anuruddha bhikkhuno cattāro satipaṭṭhānā āraddhā hontīti || ||

5. Idhāvuso bhikkhu ajjhattaṃ kāye samudayadhammānupassī viharati || pa [pe] || ajjhattaṃ kāye vayadhammānupassī viharati || ajjhattam kāye samudayavayadhammānupassī


<!-- PTS Page 295 -->
<a id="page-295"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 295]*

viharati || ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || ||

6. Bahiddhā kāye vayadhammānupassī viharati || la [pe] || bahiddhā kāye samudayavayadhammānupassī viharati || ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || ||

7. Ajjhattabahidhā kāye samudayadhammānupassī viharati || ajjhattabahiddhā kāye vayadhammānupassī viharati || ajjhattabahiddhā kāye samudayavayadhammānupassī viharati || ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || ||

8. So sace ākaṅkhati Appatikkūle[^295-1] paṭikkūlasaññī vihareyyan ti || paṭikkūlasaññī tattha[^295-2] viharati || || Sace ākaṅkhati Paṭikkūle appaṭikkūlasaññī vihareyyan ti || appaṭikūlasaññī tattha viharati || || Sa ce ākaṅkhati Appaṭikkule ca paṭikkule ca paṭikkūlasaññī vihareyyanti patikkūlasaññī tattha viharati || || Sa ce ākaṅkhati Patikkūpe ca appaṭikkūle ca appatikkūlasaññī vihareyyan ti || appatikkūlasaññī tattha viharati || || Sa ce ākaṅkhati Appaṭikkūlañca paṭikkūlañca tadubhayam abhinivajjetvā upekhako vihareyyaṃ[^295-3] sato sampajāno ti || upekhako tattha[^295-4] viharati sato sampajāno[^295-5] || ||

9. Ajjhattaṃ vedanāsu samudayadhammanupassī viharati[^295-6] || ajjhattaṃ[^295-7] vedanāsu vayadhammānupassī viharati || ajjhattaṃ[^295-7] vedanāsu samudayavayadhammānupassī[^295-8] viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyyaloke abhijjhādomanassaṃ[^295-9] || ||

10. Bahiddhā vedanāsu samudayadhadhammānupassī viharati || bahiddhā vedanāsu vayadhammānupassī viharati || bahiddhā vedanāsu samudayavayadhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyyaloke abhijjhādomanassaṃ[^295-9] || ||

11. Ajjhattabahiddhā vedanāsu samudayadhammānupassī viharati || ajjhattabahiddhā vedanāsu vayadhammānupassī viharati || ajjhattabahiddhā vedanāsu samudayavayadhammā-


<!-- PTS Page 296 -->
<a id="page-296"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 296]*

nupassī[^296-1] viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassam || ||

12. So sa ce ākaṅkhati pe || ||

13. Ajjhattaṃ citte || la || ||

14. Bahiddhā citte || la || ||

15. Ajjhattabahiddhā citte samudayadhammānupassī viharati || ajjhattabahiddhā citte vaya- || ajjhattabahiddhā citte samudayavayadhammānupassī viharati || ātāpī || la || abhijjhādomanassaṃ[^296-2] || ||

16. So sa[^296-3] ce ākaṅkhati || pe || ||

17. Ajjhattaṃ dhammesu || la || ||

18. Bahiddhā dhammesu || la || ||

19. Ajjhattabahiddhā dhammesu samudayadhammānupassī viharati || ajjhattabahiddhā dhammesu vayadhammānupassī viharati || ajjhattabahiddhā dhammesu samudayavayadhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || ||

20. So[^296-4] sa ce ākaṅkhati Appaṭikūle paṭikūlasaññī vihareyyan ti paṭikūlasaññī tattha[^296-5] viharati || la || upekhako tattha viharati sato sampajāno || ||

21. Ettāvatā kho āvuso bhikkhuno cattāro satipaṭṭhānā āraddhā hontī ti || ||

SN_5,52(8).2. (2) Rahogata2.

1. Sāvatthi nidānaṃ[^296-6] || ||

2. Atha kho āyasmato Anuruddhassa rahogatassa paṭisallīnassa evaṃ cetaso parivitakko udapādi || || Yesaṃ kesañci cattāro satipaṭṭhānā viraddhā viraddho tesam ariyo maggo sammādukkhakkhayagāmī || || Yesaṃ kesañci cattāro satipaṭṭhānā āraddhā āraddho tesam ariyo maggo sammādukkhakkhayagāmī ti || ||

3. Atha kho āyasmā Mahā-Moggalāno āyasmato Anuruddhassa cetasā ceto parivitakkam aññāya[^296-7] seyyathāpi


<!-- PTS Page 297 -->
<a id="page-297"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 297]*

nāma balavā puriso || pe || || Evam eva[^297-1] āyasmato Anuruddhassa pamukhe[^297-2] pātur ahosi || ||

4. Atha kho āyasmā Mahā-Moggalāno āyasmantam Anuruddham etad avoca || || Kittāvatā nu kho āvuso Anuruddha bhikkhuno cattāro satipaṭṭhānā āraddhā honti[^297-3] || ||

5. Idhāvuso bhikkhu ajjhattaṃ kāye kāyānupassī viharati || ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || || Bahiddhā kāye kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī || la-pe[^297-4] || Ajjhattabahiddhā kāye kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī || abhijjhadomanassam || ||

6. Ajjhattaṃ vedanāsu vedanānupassī viharati ātāpī- -abhijjhādomanassaṃ || || Bahiddhā vedanāsu vedanānupassī viharati ātāpī -abhijjhādomanassaṃ || || Ajjhattam[^297-5] bahiddhā[^297-6] vedanāsu[^297-7] vedanānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || ||

7. Ajjhattaṃ[^297-5] citte || bahiddhā citte || ajjhattabahiddhā citte[^297-8] cittānupassī viharati || ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || ||

8. Ajjhattaṃ dhammesu[^297-9] || bahiddhā dhammesu[^297-9] || ajjhattabahiddhādhammesu dhammānupassī viharati: ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || ||

9. Ettāvatā kho āvuso bhikkhuno cattāro satipaṭṭhānā āraddhā hontī ti || ||

SN_5,52(8).3. (3) Sutanu.

1. Ekaṃ samayam āyasmā Anuruddho Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Sutanutīre || ||

2. Atha kho sambahulā bhikkhū yenāyasmā Anuruddho tenupasaṅkamiṃsu || upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā Anuruddhena saddhiṃ sammodiṃsu || || Sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyam vītisāretvā ekam antaṃ nisīdiṃsu || ||


<!-- PTS Page 298 -->
<a id="page-298"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 298]*

3. Ekam antaṃ nisinnā kho te bhikkhū āyasmantam Anuruddham etad avocuṃ || || Katamesaṃ āyasmā Anuruddho dhammānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mahābhiññātam[^298-1] patto ti || || 4 Catunnam khvāham[^298-2] āvuso satipaṭṭhānānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mahābhiññātam[^298-1] patto || katamesaṃ catunnaṃ || || Idhāham āvuso kāye kāyānupassī viharāmi ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || vedanāsu || citte || dhammesu dhammānupassī viharāmi || ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || || Imesaṃ khvāham[^298-3] āvuso catunnaṃ satipaṭṭhānānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mahābhiññātam[^298-1] patto || ||

5. Imesaṃ ca panāham āvuso catunnaṃ satipaṭṭhānānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā hīnaṃ dhammaṃ hīnato abbhaññāsiṃ || majjhimaṃ dhammam majjhimato abbhaññāsiṃ || paṇītaṃ dhammam paṇītato abbhaññāsin ti || ||

SN_5,52(8).4. (4) Kaṇṭakī1.

1. Ekaṃ samayam āyasmā Anuruddho āyasmā ca Sāriputto āyasmā ca Mahā-Moggalāno Sākete viharanti[^298-4] Kaṇṭakīvane[^298-5] || ||

2. Atha kho āyasmā ca Sāriputto āyasmā ca MahāMoggalāno sāyaṇhasamayam paṭisallāṇā vuṭṭhitā yenāyasmā Anuruddho tenupasaṅkamiṃsu || upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā Anuruddhena saddiṃ sammodiṃsu || sammodanīyaṃ katham sārāṇīyaṃ vītisāretvā ekam antaṃ nisīdiṃsu || ||

3. Ekam antaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Sāriputto āyasmantam Anuruddham etad avoca || || Sekhenāvuso Anuruddha bhikkhunā katame[^298-6] dhammā upasampajja vihātabbā[^298-7] ti || || Sekhenāvuso Sariputta bhikkhunā cattāro satipaṭṭhānā upasampajja vihātabbā[^298-7] || katame cattaro || ||

4. Idhāvuso bhikkhu kāye kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampājāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ[^298-8] ||


<!-- PTS Page 299 -->
<a id="page-299"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 299]*

vedanāsu || citte || dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati || ātāpī || sampajāno[^299-1] satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || || Sekhenāvuso Sāriputta ime cattāro satipaṭṭhānā upasampajja vihātabbā[^299-2] ti || ||

SN_5,52(8).5. (5) Kaṇṭakī2. 1--2. Sākete nidānaṃ || Ekam antaṃ ni- kho ā- Sāriputto ā- Anuruddham etad avoca[^299-3] || ||

3. Asekhenāvuso Anuruddha bhikkhunā katame dhammā upasampajja vihātabbā ti || || Asekhenāvuso Sāriputta bhikkhunā cattāro satipaṭṭhānā upasampajja vihātabbā || katame cattāro[^299-4] || ||

4. Idhāvuso bhikkhu kāye[^299-5] kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassam || vedanāsu || citte || dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī- -domanassaṃ || || Asekhenāvuso Sāriputta bhikkhunā ime cattāro satipaṭṭhānā upasampajja vihātabbā ti[^299-6] || ||

SN_5,52(8).6. (6) Kaṇṭakī3. 1--2. [^299-7] Sāketanidānaṃ || Ekam antaṃ- -etad avoca || ||

3. Katamesaṃ āyasmā Anuruddho dhammānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mahābhiññātam[^299-8] patto ti || || Catunnaṃ khvāham āvuso satipaṭṭhānānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mahābhiññatam[^299-8] patto || katamesaṃ catunnaṃ || ||

4. Idhāham āvuso kāye kāyānupassī viharāmi[^299-9] ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || vedanāsu || citte || dhammesu dhammānupassī viharāmi[^299-9] ātāpī- -abhijjhādomanassaṃ || || Imesaṃ khvāham āvuso catunnam satipaṭṭhānānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mahābhiññātam[^299-8] patto || ||

5. Imesañca panāham āvuso catunnam satipaṭṭhānānam bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā sahassaṃ lokam abhijānāmī ti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 300 -->
<a id="page-300"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 300]*

SN_5,52(8).7. (7) Taṇhakkhaya.

1. Sāvatthinidānaṃ[^300-1] || ||

2. Tatra kho āyasmā Anuruddho bhikkhū āmantesi || || pe || ||

3. Cattāro me āvuso satipaṭṭhānā bhāvitā bahulīkata taṇhakkhayāya saṃvattanti[^300-2] || katame cattāro || ||

4. Idhāvuso bhikkhu kāye kāyānupassī viharati || pe [pa] || vedanāsu || citte || dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyyaloke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || || Ime[^300-3] kho āvuso cattāro satipaṭṭhānā bhāvitā bahulīkatā taṇhākkhayāya saṃvattantī ti || ||

SN_5,52(8).8. (8) Salaḷāgāram.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā Anuruddho Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Salaḷāgāre[^300-4] || ||

2. Tatra kho āyasmā Anuruddho bhikkhū āmantesi || la[^300-5] || etad avoca || ||

3. Seyyathāpi āvuso Gaṅgā nadī pācinaninnā pācinapoṇā pācinapabbhārā || atha mahājanakāyo āgaccheyya kuddālapiṭakam[^300-6] ādāya Mayam imam Gaṅgānadim pacchāninnaṃ karissāma pacchāpoṇam pacchāpabbharan ti || Taṃ kim maññathāvuso api nu[^300-7] so mahājanakāyo Gaṅgānadim pacchāninnaṃ kareyya pacchāpoṇam pacchāpabbhāranti || || No hetam āvuso || || Taṃ kissa hetu || Gaṅgā āvuso[^300-8] nadī pācinaninnā pācīnapoṇā pācīnapabbharā sā[^300-9] na sukarā pacchāninnam kātum pacchāpoṇam pacchāpabbhāram || yāvad eva ca pana so mahājanakāyo kilamathassa vighātassa bhāgī assā ti || ||

4. Evam eva kho āvuso bhikkhuṃ[^300-10] cattāro satipaṭṭhāne bhāventam cattāro satipaṭṭhāne bahulīkarontaṃ rājāno[^300-11] vā rājamahāmattā[^300-12] vā mittā vā amaccā vā ñātisālohitā[^300-13]


<!-- PTS Page 301 -->
<a id="page-301"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 301]*

vā[^301-1] bhogehi abhihaṭṭhum pavāreyyuṃ Ehambho[^301-2] purisa kin te ime kāsāvā anudahanti || kim muṇḍokapālam anusaṃcarasi[^301-3] || ehi hīnāyāvattitvā[^301-4] bhoge ca bhuñjassu puññāni ca karohī ti || || So vata āvuso bhikkhu cattāro satipaṭṭhāne bhāvento cattāro satipaṭṭhāne bahulīkaronto sikkham paccakkhāya hīnāyāvattissatī ti[^301-5] netaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati || || Taṃ kissa hetu || yañ hi taṃ[^301-6] āvuso cittaṃ dīgharattaṃ vivekaninnaṃ vivekapoṇaṃ vivekapabbharaṃ taṃ vata[^301-7] hīnāyāvattissatī ti[^301-8] netaṃ thānaṃ vijjati || ||

5. Kathañcāvuso[^301-9] bhikkhu cattāro satipaṭṭhāne bhāveti cattāro satipaṭṭhāne bahulīkaroti || || Idhāvuso bhikkhu kāye kāyanupassī viharati || vedanāsu || citte || dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || || Evaṃ kho āvuso bhikkhu cattāro satipaṭṭhāne bhāveti cattāro satipatthāne bahulīkarotī ti || ||

SN_5,52(8).9. (9) Sabbam or Ambapāla.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ca Anuruddho āyasmā ca Sāriputto Vesāliyaṃ viharanti Ambapālivane || ||

2. Atha kho āyasmā Sāriputto sāyaṇhasamayam paṭisallāṇā vuṭṭhito || la || ||

3. Ekam antaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Sāriputto āyasmantaṃ Anuruddham etad avoca || || Vippasannāni kho te āvuso Anuruddha indriyāni parisuddho mukhavaṇṇo pariyodāto || katamenāyasmā Anuruddho vihārena etarahi bahulaṃ viharatī ti || || Catusu khvāham āvuso satipaṭṭhānesu supatiṭṭhitacitto[^301-11] etarahi bahulaṃ viharāmi || katamesu catusu || ||

4. Idhāham āvuso kāye kāyānupassī viharāmi || pe || vedanāsu || citte || dhammesu dhammānupassī viharāmi ātāpī


<!-- PTS Page 302 -->
<a id="page-302"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 302]*

sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || || Imesu khvāham āvuso catusu satipaṭṭhānesu supatiṭṭhitacitto etarahi bahulaṃ viharāmi || ||

5. Yo so āvuso bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavo vusitavā katakaraṇīyo ohitabhāro anuppattasadattho {parikkhīṇabhavasaññojano} sammadaññā vimutto || so imesu catusu satipaṭṭhānesu supatiṭṭhitacitto[^302-1] bahulaṃ viharatī ti || ||

6. Lābhā vata[^302-2] no āvuso suladdhaṃ vata[^302-2] no āvuso ye mayam āyasmato Anuruddhassa sammukhā va assumha āsabhim[^302-3] vācam[^302-4] bhāsamānassā ti || ||

SN_5,52(8).10. (10) Bāḷhagilāyam or Gihīnayo.

1. Ekaṃ samayam āyasmā Anuruddho Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Andhavanasmiṃ[^302-5] ābādhiko dukkhito bāḷhagilāno || ||

2. Atha kho sambahulā bhikkhū yenāyasmā Anuruddho tenupasaṅkamiṃsu || upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantam Anuruddham etad avocuṃ || Katamenāyasmato Anuruddhassa vihārena viharato uppannā sārīrikā dukkhā[^302-6] vedanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhantī ti || || Catūsu me āvuso satipaṭṭhānesu supatiṭṭhitacittassa[^302-7] viharato uppannā sārīrikā dukkhā vedanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhanti || || Katamesu catusu || ||

3. Idhāham āvuso kāye kāyānupassī viharāmi[^302-8] || Vedanāsu || citte || dhammesu dhammānupassī viharāmi ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || || Imesu kho me āvuso catusu satipaṭṭhānesu supatiṭṭhitacittassa[^302-7] viharato uppannā sārīrikā dukkhā vedanā cittam na[^302-9] pariyādāya tiṭṭhantī ti || || Rahogatavaggo[^302-10] pathamo[^302-11] || || Tassa uddānaṃ[^302-12] || ||


<!-- PTS Page 303 -->
<a id="page-303"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 303]*

Rahogatena[^303-1] dve vuttā[^303-2] || Sutanu Kaṇṭakī[^303-3] tayo || Taṇhakkhaya Salaḷhāgāraṃ[^303-4] Sabbaṃ Bāḷhagilāyanan[^303-5] ti || || CHAPTER II. VAGGO-DUTIYO.

SN_5,52(8).11. (1) Sahassa.

1. Ekaṃ samayam āyasmā Anuruddho Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme || || 2--3. CHAPTER I. GAṄGĀPEYYĀLO.

SN_5,53(9).1. (1) Pathamasuddhiyam. 1--2. Sāvatthi || Tatra kho[^307-1] cattāro me bhikkhave jhānā || katame cattāro || ||

4. Vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ[^307-4] sampasādanaṃ[^307-5] cetaso ekodibhāvam avitakkam avicāraṃ samādhijam pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānam[^307-6] upasampajja viharati || ||

5. Pītiyā ca virāgā upekhako ca viharati sato ca sampajāno sukhañca kāyena paṭisaṃvedeti || Yan tam ariyā ācikkhanti upekhako satimā sukhavihārī ti tatiyaṃ jhānam[^307-7] upasampajja viharati || ||

6. Sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbeva somanassadomanassānam atthagamā adukkham asukham upekhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānam upasampajja viharati || ||

7. Ime kho bhikkhave cattāro jhānā ti || ||

8. Seyyathāpi bhikkhave Gaṅgā nadī[^307-8] pācīnaninnā pācīnapoṇā pācīnapabbhārā || evam eva kho bhikkhave bhikkhu

CHAPTER I. EKADHAMMAVAGGO PATHAMO.

SN_5,54(10).1. (1) Ekadhammo.

1. Sāvatthi || ārāme[^311-1] || ||

2. Tatra kho || la || etad avoca[^311-2] || ||

3. Ekadhammo[^311-3] bhikkhave bhāvito bahulīkato mahapphalo hoti[^311-4] mahāṇisaṃso || katamo ekadhammo[^311-5] || Ānāpānasati || || Katham bhāvitā ca bhikkhave ānāpānasati || katham bahulīkatā mahapphalā hoti mahānisaṃsā[^311-6] || ||

4. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu araññagato vā rukkhamūlagato vā suññāgāragato vā nisīdati pallaṅkam ābhujitvā[^311-7] ujuṃ kāyam paṇidhāya parimukham satim upaṭṭhapetvā so sato va assasati sato va[^311-8] passasati || ||

5. Dīghaṃ vā assasanto Dīgham assasāmī ti pajānāti || dīghaṃ vā passasanto Dīgham passasāmī ti pajānāti || Rassaṃ vā assasanto Rassam assasāmī ti pajānāti || rassaṃ vā[^311-9] passasanto Rassam passāmī ti pajānāti || ||

6. {Sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvedī} Assasissāmīti sikkhati || sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvedī Passasissāmīti sikkhati || || Passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāram Assāsissāmīti sikkhati || passambhayam kāyasaṅkhāram Passissāmīti sikkhati || ||

Yathā katham pana tvam[^315-1] Ariṭṭha bhāvesi ānāpānasatinti || ||

4. Atītesu me bhante kāmesu kāmacchando pahīno || anāgatesu me kāmesu kāmacchando vigato || ajjhattam[^315-2] bahiddhā ca me dhammesu[^315-3] paṭighasaññā[^315-4] suppaṭivinītā || so sato[^315-5] assasissāmi sato passasissāmi[^315-6] || Evam khvāham bhante bhāvemi ānāpānasatin ti || ||

5. Atthesā Ariṭṭha ānāpānasati nesā natthī ti vadāmi || api ca Ariṭṭha yathā ānāpānasati vitthārena paripuṇṇā hoti || taṃ suṇāhi sādhukam manasi karohi || bhāsissāmi || || Evam bhante ti kho āyasmā- opaccassosi || ||

6. Bhagavā etad avoca[^315-7] || Kathañca Ariṭṭha ānāpānasati vitthārena paripuṇṇā hoti || || 7--13. Idha Ariṭṭha bhikkhu araññagato vā rukkhamūlagato vā suññāgāragato vā nisīdati pallaṅkamo oDīgham assassāmīti pajānāti || la || yāva[^315-8] paṭinissaggānupassī Assasissamī ti sikkhati || paṭinissaggānupassī Passasissāmīti sikkhati || ||

14. Evaṃ kho Ariṭṭha ānāpānasati vitthārena paripuṇṇā hotī ti || ||

3. Tena kho pana samayena āyasmā Mahākappino avidūre nisinno hoti pallaṅkam ābhujitvā uruṃ kāyam paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satim upaṭṭhapetvā || ||

4. Addasā kho Bhagavā āyasmantam Mahākappinam avidūre nisinnam pallaṅkam abhujitvā ujuṃ kāyam paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satim upaṭṭhapetvā || disvāna bhikkhū amantesi || || Passatha no tumhe[^315-4] bhikkhave etassa bhikkhuno kāyassa iñjitattaṃ vā phanditattaṃ vā ti || ||

5. Yadā pi mayam bhante tam āyasmantam passāma saṅghamajjhe vā nisinnam ekaṃ vā raho nisinnaṃ || tadā

##### *[PTS Page 316]*

pi mayaṃ tassa āyasmato na passāma kāyassa iñjitattaṃ vā phanditattam vā ti || ||

6. Yassa bhikkhave samādhissa bhavitattā bahulīkatattā neva kāyassa iñjitattaṃ va hoti phanditattaṃ vā || na cittassa iñjitattam vā hoti phanditattaṃ vā || tassa[^316-1] so bhikkhave bhikkhu samādhissa nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī || ||

7. Katamassa ca bhikkhave samādhissa bhavitattā bahulīkatattā neva kāyassa iñjitattaṃ vā hoti phanditattaṃ vā || na cittassa iñjitattaṃ vā hoti phanditattaṃ vā || Ānāpānasatisamādhissa bhikkhave bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā neva kāyassa iñjitattaṃ vā hoti[^316-2] phanditattaṃ vā na cittassa iñjitattaṃ vā hoti phanditattaṃ vā || ||

8. Katham bhāvite ca bhikkhave ānāpanasatisamādimhi katham bahulīkate neva kāyassa iñjitattaṃ vā hoti phanditattam vā || na[^316-3] cittassa iñjitattaṃ vā hoti phanditattaṃ vā[^316-4] || || 9--15. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu araññagato vā rukkhamūlagato vā suññāgāragato vā nisīdati- || vitthāretabbā || yāva- -sikkhati[^316-5] || paṭisissaggānupassī Passasissāmīti sikkhati || ||

16. Evam bhāvite ca kho bhikkhave ānāpānasatisamādimhi evam bahulīkate neva kāyassa iñjitattaṃ vā hoti phanditattaṃ va || na cittassa iñjitattaṃ vā hoti phanditattam vā ti[^316-6] || ||

SN_5,54(10).8. (8) Dīpo. 1--2. [^316-7] Sāvatthi -- voca || ||

3. Ānāpānasatisamādhi[^316-8] bhikkhave bhavito[^316-9] bahulīkato[^316-9] mahapphalo[^316-9] hoti māhānisaṃso[^316-9] || || Katham bhāvito ca bhikkhave ānāpānasatisamādhi katham bahulīkato mahapphalo[^316-10] hoti mahānisaṃso || ||


<!-- PTS Page 317 -->
<a id="page-317"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 317]*

4--10. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu araññagato vā rukkhamūlagato vā suññāgāragato vā nisīdati pallaṅkam- -Dīgham assassāmīti pajānāti || {vitthāretabbaṃ}[^317-1] || -patinissaggānupassī[^317-2] Passissāmīti sikkhati || ||

11. Evam bhāvito kho bhikkhave ānāpānasatisamādhi evam bahulīkato mahapphalo hoti mahānisaṃso || ||

12. Aham pi sudam bhikkhave pubbeva sambodhā anabhisambuddho bodhisatto va samāno iminā vihārena bahulaṃ[^317-3] viharāmi || tassa mayham bhikkhave iminā vihārena bahulaṃ[^317-3] viharato neva kāyo kilamati[^317-4] na cakkhunī || anupādāya ca[^317-5] me āsavehi cittaṃ vimuccati || ||

13. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave bhikkhu ce pi[^317-6] ākaṅkheyya || Neva[^317-7] kāyo kilameyya na ca cakkhunī || anupādāya ca[^317-8] me āsavehi cittaṃ vimucceyyā ti || || Ayam eva ānāpānasatisamādhi sādhukam manasi kātabbo[^317-9] || ||

14. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave bhikkhu ce[^317-10] pi ākaṅkheyya || Ye me gehasitasarasaṅkappā[^317-11] te pahīheyyunti[^317-12] || ayam eva[^317-13] anāpānasatisamādhi sādhukaṃ manasi kātabbo || ||

15. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave bhikkhu ce[^317-10] pi ākaṅkheyya Appaṭikkūle paṭikkūlasaññī vihareyyan ti || ayam eva ānāpānasatisamādhi sādhukam manasi kātabbo || ||

16. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave bhikkhu ce[^317-10] pi ākaṅkheyya Paṭikkūle appaṭikkūlasaññī vihareyyan ti || ayam eva ānāpānasatisamādhi sādhukam manasi kātabbo || ||

17. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave bhikkhu ce[^317-10] pi ākaṅkheyya Appaṭikkūle ca paṭikkūle ca[^317-14] paṭikkūlasaññī vihareyyan ti || ayam eva || pe || ||

18. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave bhikkhu ce[^317-10] pi ākaṅkheyya


<!-- PTS Page 318 -->
<a id="page-318"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 318]*

Paṭikkūle ca appaṭikkūle ca appatikkūlasaññī[^318-1] vihareyyan ti || ayam eva || pe || ||

19. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave bhikkhu ce[^318-2] pi ākaṅkheyya Appaṭikkūlañca patikkūlañca tad ubhayam abhinivajjetvā upekhako vihareyyaṃ sato sampajāno ti || ayam eva || pe || ||

20. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave bhikkhu ce[^318-2] pi ākaṅkheyya Vivicceva kāmehi vivicca akusalehi dhammehi savitakkaṃ savicāraṃ vivekajam pītisukham pathamaṃ[^318-3] jhānam upasampajja vihareyyanti || ayam eva || [pe] || ||

21. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave bhikkhu ce[^318-4] pi akaṅkheyya vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattam sampasādanaṃ cetaso ekodibhāvam avitakkam avicāraṃ samādhijam pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ[^318-5] jhānam upasampajja vihareyyanti || ayam eva || pe || ||

22. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave bhikkhu ce[^318-4] pi akaṅkheyya Pītiyā ca virāgā upekhako ca vihareyyaṃ[^318-6] sato[^318-7] sampajāno sukhaṃ ca kāyena paṭisaṃvedeyyaṃ[^318-8] yan tam ariyā ācikkhanti upekhako satimā sukhavihārī ti tatiyaṃ[^318-9] jhānam upasampajja vihareyyan ti || || Ayam eva || [pe] || ||

23. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave bhikkhu ce pi ākaṅkheyya || Sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbeva domanassānam atthagamā adukkham asukham upekhāsatiparisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ[^318-10] jhānam upasampajja viharati || ayam eva || pe || ||

24. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave bhikkhu ce pi ākaṅkheyya sabbaso rūpasaññānaṃ samatikkamā paṭighasaññānam atthagamā nānattasaññānam amanasikārā Ananto ākāso ti ākāsānañcāyatanam upasampajja vihareyyan ti || ayam eva || pe || ||

25. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave bhikkhu ce pi ākaṅkheyya || Sabbaso ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma[^318-11] Anantaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 319 -->
<a id="page-319"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 319]*

viññāṇanti viññāṇañcāyatanam upasampajja vihareyyan ti || ayam eva || pe || ||

26. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave bhikkhu ce pi ākaṅkheyya || Sabbaso viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma Natthi kiñci ākiñcaññāyatanam upasampajja vihareyyan ti || Ayam eva || pe || ||

27. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave bhikkhu ce pi ākaṅkheyya. Sabbaso ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma Nevasaññānāsaññāyatanam upasampajja vihareyyan ti || ayam eva ānāpānasamadhi sādhukam manasi kātabbo || ||

28. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave bhikkhu ce pi ākaṅkheyya || Sabbaso nevasaññānāsaññāyatānaṃ samatikkamma saññavedayitanirodham upasampajja vihareyyan ti || || Ayam eva ānāpānasatisamādhi sādhukam[^319-1] manasi kātabbo || ||

29. Evam bhāvite kho bhikkhave ānāpānasatisamādimhi evam bahulīkate sukhañ ce vedanaṃ[^319-2] vediyati[^319-3] Sā aniccā ti pajānāti[^319-4] || Anajjhositā ti pajānāti || Anabhinanditā ti pajānāti || dukkhañ ce vedanaṃ vediyati || Sā aniccā ti pajānāti || Anajjhositā ti pajānāti || Anabhinanditā ti pajānāti || adukkhaṃ asukhañ ce vedanam vediyati[^319-3] Sā aniccā ti pajānāti || Anajjhositā ti pajānāti || Anabhinanditā ti pajānāti || ||

30. So sukhañ ce vedanaṃ vediyati visaṃyutto nām vediyati || dukkhañ ce vedanaṃ vediyati visaṃyutto nam vediyati || adukkham asukhañ ce vedanaṃ vediyati visaṃyutto naṃ vediyati || || So kāyapariyantikam vedanaṃ vedayamāno[^319-5] kāyapariyantikaṃ vedanaṃ vediyāmīti[^319-6] pajānāti jīvitapariyantikaṃ vedanaṃ vediyamāno jīvitapariyantikam vedanaṃ vediyāmīti pajānāti || || Kāyassa bhedā uddhaṃ jīvitapariyādānā idheva sabbavedayitāni[^319-7] anabhinanditāni[^319-8] sītibhavissantī ti[^319-9] pajānāti || ||

31. Seyyathāpi bhikkhave telañca paticca vaṭṭiñca paṭicca telapadīpo jhāyeyya || tasseva telassa ca vaṭṭiyā ca pariyādānā anāhāro nibbāyeyya || evam eva kho bhikkhave bhikkhu


<!-- PTS Page 320 -->
<a id="page-320"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 320]*

kāyapariyantikam vedanaṃ vediyamāno[^320-1] Kāyapariyantikaṃ vedanaṃ vedayāmī ti pajānāti || jīvitapariyantikaṃ vedanaṃ vediyamāno Jīvitapariyantikaṃ vedanaṃ vedayāmīti pajānāti || || Kāyassa bhedā uddhaṃ jīvitapariyādānā idheva sabbavedayitāni[^320-1] anabhinanditāni[^320-1] sītibhavissantī ti[^320-1] pajānātī ti || ||

SN_5,54(10).9. (9) Vesālī.

1. Evam me sutaṃ[^320-2] ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Vesāliyaṃ viharati Mahāvane Kuṭāgārasālāyaṃ || ||

2. Tena kho pana samayena Bhagavā bhikkhūnam anekapariyāyena asubhakathaṃ katheti[^320-3] || asubhāya vaṇṇam bhāsati asubhabhāvanāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati[^320-4] || ||

3. Atha kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi || || Icchāmaham bhikkhave addhamāsaṃ patisalliyituṃ[^320-5] || namhi kenaci upasaṅkamitabbo aññatra ekena piṇḍapātanīhārakena ti || || Evam bhante ti kho te bhikkhū Bhagavato paṭissutvā nāssudha[^320-6] koci Bhagavantam upasaṅkamati[^320-7] aññatra ekena piṇḍapātanīhārakena || ||

4. Atha kho te bhikkhū Bhagavā[^320-8] anekapariyāyena asubhakatham kathesi[^320-9] asubhāya vaṇṇam bhāsati || asubhabhāvanāya vaṇṇam bhāsatī ti || anekākāravokāram asubhabhāvanānuyogam anuyuttā viharanti || || Te iminā kāyena aṭṭiyāmānā harāyamānā jigucchamānā satthahārakam pariyesanti || {dasa} pi bhikkhū ekāhena sattham āharanti || vīsam[^320-10] pi || la[^320-11] || tiṃsam pi ekāhena sattham āharanti || ||

5. Atha kho Bhagavā tassa uḍḍhamāsassa accayena paṭisallaṇā vuṭṭhito āyasmantam Ānandam āmantesi || || Kiṃ[^320-12] nu kho[^320-13] Ānanda tanubhūto viya bhikkhusaṅgho ti || ||

6. Tathā hi pana bhante Bhagavā bhikkhūnam anekapariyāyena asubhakatham kathesi[^320-3] asubhāya vaṇṇam


<!-- PTS Page 321 -->
<a id="page-321"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 321]*

bhāsati asubhabhāvanāya vaṇṇam bhāsati[^321-1] || [te ca bhante bhikkhū Bhagavā kho anekapariyāyena asubhakathaṃ kathesi || asubhāya vaṇṇambhāsati asubhabhāvanāya vaṇṇam bhāsatī ti] anekākāravokāram asubhabhāvanānuyogam anuyuttā viharanti || || Te[^321-2] iminā kāyena aṭṭiyamānā harāyamānā satthahārakam pariyesanti dasa[^321-3] pi bhikkhū vīsampi-[^321-4] tiṃsam pi bhikkhū ekāhena sattham āharanti || || Sādhu bhante Bhagavā aññam pariyāyam ācikkhatu tathā yathāyam bhikkhusaṅgho aññāya saṇṭhaheyyā ti[^321-5] || ||

7. Tenhānanda yāvatikā bhikkhū Vesālim[^321-6] upanissāya viharanti || te sabbe upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ sannipātehī ti || || Evam bhante ti kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavato paṭissutvā yāvatikā bhikkhū Vesālim[^321-6] upanissāya viharanti || te sabbe upaṭṭhānasālāyam sannipātetvā yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā Bagavantam etad avoca || Sannipatito bhante[^321-7] bhikkhusaṅgho yassa dāni[^321-8] Bhagavā kālam maññatī ti || ||

8. Atha kho Bhagavā yena upaṭṭhānasālā tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā paññatte āsane nisīdi || nisajja kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi || ||

9. Ayam pi kho bhikkhave ānāpānasatisamādhi bhāvito bahulīkato santo ceva paṇīto ca asecanako[^321-9] sukho ca vihāro uppannuppanne[^321-10] ca pāpake[^321-11] akusale dhamme[^321-12] ṭhānaso antaradhāpeti[^321-13] vūpasameti[^321-14] || ||

10. Seyyathāpi bhikkhave gimhānam pacchime māse ūhataṃ rajojallaṃ tam enam mahā akālamegho ṭhānaso antaradhāpeti vūpasamati || Evam eva kho bhikkhave ānāpānasati samādhi bhāvito bahulīkato santo ceva paṇīto


<!-- PTS Page 322 -->
<a id="page-322"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 322]*

ca asecanako[^322-1] ca sukho ca[^322-2] viharo uppannuppanne pāpake akusale dhamme ṭhānaso antaradhāpeti[^322-3] vūpasameti || ||

11. Katham bhāvito ca[^322-2] bhikkhave ānāpānasatisamādhi katham bahulīkato santo ceva paṇīto ca asecanako[^322-4] ca[^322-5] sukho[^322-6] ca vihāro uppannuppanne ca pāpake akusale dhamme ṭhānaso antaradhāpeti[^322-3] vūpasameti || || 12--18. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu araññagato vā rukkhamūlagato vā suññāgāragato vā || pe || vitthāretabbaṃ[^322-7] || paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmī ti sikkhati || ||

19. Evam bhāvito kho bhikkhave ānāpānasatisamādhi evam bahulīkato[^322-8] santo ceva[^322-9] paṇīto[^322-10] ca asecanako[^322-11] sukho ca[^322-12] vihāro uppannuppanne ca pāpake[^322-13] akusale dhamme ṭhānaso antaradhāpeti[^322-3] vūpasametī ti || ||

SN_5,54(10).10. (10) Kimbila.

1. Evam me sutam[^322-14] ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Kimbilāyam[^322-15] viharati Veḷuvane || ||

2. Tatra kho Bhagavā āyasmantaṃ Kimbilam[^322-16] āmantesi || Katham bhāvito nu[^322-2] kho Kimbila ānāpānasatisamādhi katham bahulīkato mahapphalo hoti mahānisaṃso ti || || Evaṃ vutte āyasmā Kimbilo tuṇhī ahosi || ||

3. Dutiyam pi kho Bhagavā[^322-17] || ||

4. Tatiyam pi kho Bhagavā āyasmantam Kimbilam āmantesi || Katham bhāvito nu[^322-2] kho Kimbila ānāpānasatisamādhi katham bahulīkato mahapphalo hoti mahānisaṃso ti || || Tatiyam pi kho āyasmā Kimbilo tuṇhī ahosi || ||


<!-- PTS Page 323 -->
<a id="page-323"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 323]*

5. Evaṃ vutte āyasmā Ānando Bhagavantam etad avoca || || Etassa Bhagavā kālo || etassa Sugata kālo yam Bhagavā ānāpānasatisamādhim[^323-1] bhāveyya || Bhagavato sutvā bhikkhū dhāressantī ti || || Tenahānanda suṇāhi sādhukam manasi karohi bhāsissāmī ti || || Evam bhante ti kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavato paccassosi || ||

6. Bhagavā etad avoca || || Katham bhāvito ca Ānanda ānāpānasatisamādhi katham bahulīkato mahapphalo hoti mahānisaṃso || || 7--13. Idhānanda bhikkhu araññagato vā rukkhamūlagato vā[^323-2] suññāgāragato vā --pe-- paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmī ti sikkhati[^323-3] || ||

14. Evam bhāvito kho Ānanda ānāpānasatisamādhi evam bahulīkato mahapphalo hoti mahānisaṃso || ||

15. Yasmiṃ samaye Ānanda bhikkhu dīghaṃ vā assasanto Dīgham assasāmīti pajānāti || dīghaṃ vā passasanto Dīgham passasāmīti pajānā ti || rassaṃ vā assasanto Rassam assasāmīti pajānāti || rassam vā passasanto Rassam passasāmīti pajānā ti || sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvedī Assasissāmī ti sikkhati || sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvedī Passasissāmī ti sikkhati || passam bhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāram Assasissāmī ti sikkhati || passam[^323-4] bhayam kāyasaṅkhāram Passasissāmī ti sikkhati || kāye kāyanupassī Ānanda bhikkhu tasmiṃ samaye viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassam || taṃ kissa hetu || ||

16. Kāyaññatarāham Ānanda etaṃ vadami yadidam assāsapassāsaṃ[^323-5] || tasmā ti hānanda kāye kāyānupassī bhikkhu tasmiṃ samaye viharati || ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || ||

17. Tasmiṃ samaye Ānanda bhikkhu {pītipaṭisaṃvedī} Assasissāmī ti sikkhati || pītipaṭisaṃvedī Passasissāmī ti sikkhati || sukhapaṭisaṃvedī Assasissāmī ti sikkhati || {sukhapaṭisaṃvedī} Passasissāmī ti sikkhati || cittasaṅkhārapaṭisaṃ-


<!-- PTS Page 324 -->
<a id="page-324"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 324]*

vedī Assasissāmī ti sikkhati || cittasaṅkhārapaṭisaṃvedī Passasissāmī ti sikkhati || passam bhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāram Assasissāmī ti sikkhati || passam bhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāram[^324-1] Passasissāmī ti sikkhati || Vedānāsu vedanānupassī Ānanda bhikkhu tasmiṃ samaye viharati ātāpi sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || taṃ kissa hetu || ||

18. Vedanāññatarāham Ānanda etam vadāmi yad idaṃ assāsapassāsaṃ[^324-2] sādhukam manasikāraṃ || tasmā ti hānanda vedanāsu vedanānupassī bhikkhu tasmiṃ samaye viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || ||

19. Yasmiṃ[^324-3] samaye Ānanda bhikkhu cittapaṭisaṃvedī Assasissāmī ti sikkhati || cittapaṭisaṃvedī Passasissāmī ti sikkhati || abhippamodoyaṃ cittam || la[^324-4] || samādaham cittam[^324-4] || vimocayaṃ cittam Assasissāmī ti sikkhati || vimocayaṃ cittam Passasissāmī ti sikkhati || || Citte cittānupassī Ānanda bhikkhu tasmiṃ samaye viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassam || taṃ kissa hetu || ||

20. Nāham Ānanda muṭṭhassatissa asampajānassa ānāpānasatisamādhibhāvanaṃ vadāmi || tasmā ti hānanda citte cittānupassī bhikkhu tasmiṃ samaye viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || ||

21. {Yasmiṃ}[^324-3] samaye Ānanda bhikkhu aniccānupassī[^324-5] Assasissāmī ti sikkhati || la || virāgānupassī[^324-4] || nirodhānupassī[^324-4] || paṭinissaggānupassī Assasissāmiti sikkhati || paṭinissaggānupassī Passasissāmī ti sikkhati[^324-6] || dhammesu[^324-7] dhammānupassī Ānanda bhikkhu tasmiṃ samaye viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || so yaṃ taṃ hoti abhijjhādomanassānam pahānam tam paññāya divā sādhukam ajjhupekkhitā hoti || tasmā ti hānanda dhammesu dhammānupassī bhikkhu tasmiṃ samaye viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || ||


<!-- PTS Page 325 -->
<a id="page-325"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 325]*

22. Seyyathāpi Ānanda catumahāpathe mahāpaṃsupuñjo[^325-1] puratthimāya ce pi disāya āgaccheyya sakaṭam vā ratho vā upahanateva[^325-2] tam paṃsupuñjaṃ || pacchimāya ce pi disāya āgaccheyya || pe [pa] uttarāya ce pi[^325-3] disāya || dakkhiṇāya ce pi disāya āgaccheyya sakaṭaṃ vā ratho vā upahanateva[^325-2] tam paṃsupuñjaṃ || evam eva kho Ānanda bhikkhu kāye kāyānupassī viharanto pi upahanateva[^325-2] pāpake akusale dhamme || vedanāsu || la [pe] || citte || dhammesu dhammānupassī viharanto pi upahanateva[^325-2] pāpake akusale dhamme[^325-4] ti || || Ānāpānasaṃyuttassa[^325-5] ekadhammavaggo pathamo || || Tassa uddānaṃ[^325-6] || Ekadhammo ca Bojjhaṅgo || Suddhakañ ca duve[^325-7] Phalā[^325-8] || Ariṭṭho[^325-9] Kappino Dīpo || Vesālī[^325-10] Kimbilena cā ti[^325-11] || ||

CHAPTER II. VAGGO DUTIYO.

SN_5,54(10).11. (1) Icchānaṅgalam.

1. Ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Icchānaṅgale[^325-12] viharati Icchānaṅgalavanasaṇḍe[^325-13] || ||

2. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi || Icchāmaham bhikkhave temāsam paṭisalliyituṃ[^325-14] || namhi kenaci upasaṅkamitabbo || aññatra ekena piṇḍapātanihārakenā ti || || Evam bhante ti kho te bhikkhū Bhagavato paṭissutvā nāssudha koci Bhagavantam upasaṅkamati aññatra ekena piṇḍapātanihārakena || ||


<!-- PTS Page 326 -->
<a id="page-326"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 326]*

3. Atha kho Bhagavā tassa temāsassa accayena paṭisallānā vuṭṭhito bhikkhū āmantesi || || Sa ce vo bhikkhave aññatitthiyā paribbājakā evam puccheyyuṃ || Katamena āvuso[^326-1] vihārena Samaṇo Gotamo vassāvāsam bahulaṃ vihāsī ti || evam puṭṭhā tumhe bhikkhave tesam aññatitthiyānam paribbājakānam evam vyākareyyātha || Ānāpānasatisamādhinā kho āvuso Bhagavā vassāvāsam bahulaṃ vihāsī ti || ||

4. Idhāham bhikkhave sato assasāmi[^326-2] sato passassāmi || || 5--10. Dīgham vā[^326-3] assasanto Dīgham assasāmī ti pajānāmi[^326-4] || dīghaṃ vā passasanto Dīgham passasāmī ti pajānāmi || pe[^326-5] || paṭinissaggānupassī Assasissāmī ti pajānāmi || paṭinissaggānupassī Passasissāmī ti pajānāmī[^326-6] || ||

11. Yañ hi tam bhikkhave sammāvadamāno vadeyya ariyavihāro iti pi brahmavihāro iti pi tathagātavihāro iti pi[^326-7] || ānāpānasatisamādhiṃ[^326-8] sammāvadamāno vadeyya ariyavihāro iti pi brahmavihāro iti pi tathāgatavihāro iti pi || ||

12. Ye te bhikkhave bhikkhū sekhā appattamānasā anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ patthayamānā viharanti || tesam ānāpānasatisamādhi[^326-9] bhāvito bahulīkato āsavānaṃ khayāya saṃvattati || ye ca kho te bhikkhave[^326-10] bhikkhū arahanto khīṇāsavā vusitavanto katakaraṇīyā ohitabhārā anuppattasadatthā parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyojanā[^326-11] sammadaññā vimuttā[^326-12] || tesam ānāpānasatisamādhi bhāvito bahulīkato diṭṭheva[^326-13] dhamme sukhavihārāya ceva saṃvattati satisampajaññāya ca || ||

13. Yañ hi tam bhikkhave sammāvadamāno vadeyya ariyavihāro iti pi[^326-11] brahmavihāro iti pi tathāgatavihāro iti pi || ānāpānasatisamādhi[^326-14] sammāvadamāno vadeyya ariyavihāro iti pi brahmavihāro iti pi tathāgatavihāro iti pī ti || ||

##### *[PTS Page 327]*

SN_5,54(10).12. (2) Kaṅkheyyam.

1. Ekaṃ samayam āyasmā Lomasavaṅgīso[^327-1] Sakkesu viharati Kapilavatthusmiṃ Nigrodhārāme || ||

2. Atha kho Mahānāmo sakko yenāyasmā Lomasavaṅgīso tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantam Lomasavaṅgīsam abhivādetvā ekam antam nisīdi || ekam antaṃ nisinno kho Mahānāmo sakko āyasmantam Lomasavaṅgīsam etad avoca || ||

3. So eva[^327-2] nu kho bhante sekho vihāro so[^327-3] tathāgatavihāro udāhu añño[^327-4] sekho vihāro añño tathāgatavihāro ti || || Na kho āvuso Mahānāma sveva sekho vihāro so tathāgatavihāro || añño kho āvuso Mahānāma sekho vihāro añño tathāgatavihāro || ||

4. Ye te āvuso Mahānāma bhikkhū sekhā appattamānasā anuttaraṃ[^327-5] yogakkhemam patthayamānā viharanti || te pañcanīvaraṇe pahāya viharanti || katame pañca || || Kāmacchandanīvaraṇam pahāya viharanti || || Vyāpādanīvaraṇaṃ || la-pe || Thīnamiddhanīvaraṇam || Uddhaccakukkuccanīvaraṇaṃ || Vicikicchānīvaraṇam pahāya viharanti || || Ye pi[^327-6] te āvuso Mahānāma bhikkhū sekhā appattamānasā anuttaraṃ yogakkhemam patthayamānā viharanti || te ime pañca nīvaraṇe pahāya viharanti || ||

5. Ye ca kho te āvuso Mahānāma bhikkhū arahanto khīnāsavā vusitavanto katakaraṇīyā ohitabhārā anuppattasadatthā parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyojanā sammadaññā vimuttā tesam pañcanīvaraṇā pahīnā ucchinnamūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvakatā[^327-7] āyatim anuppādadhammā[^327-8] || katame pañca || || Kāmacchandanīvaraṇam pahīnam ucchinnamūlam tālāvatthukatam anabhāvakatā[^327-7] āyatim anuppādadhammaṃ || Vyāpādanīvaraṇam pahīnaṃ || la-pe || Thīnamiddhanīvaraṇaṃ || Uddhaccakukkuccanīvaraṇaṃ || || Vicikicchānīvaraṇam pahīnam ucchinnamūlam talāvatthukatam anabhāvakatam

<a id="page-328"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 328]*

āyatim anuppādadhammaṃ || || Ye te[^328-1] āvuso Mahānāma bhikkhū arahanto khīnāsavā vusitavanto katakaraṇīyā[^328-2] ohitabharā[^328-3] anuppattasadatthā parikkhiṇabhavasaṃyojanā sammadaññā vimuttā || tesam ime pañcanīvaraṇā pahīnā ucchinnamūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvakatā āyatim anuppādadhammā || ||
