Dīghaṃ vā assasanto- || 17 ([^328-11]). Yañ hi tam bhikkhave- || 18 ([^328-12]). Ye te bhikkhave bhikkhū sekhā- || 19 ([^328-13]). Yañ hi tam bhikkhave- -tathāgatavihāro iti pī ti || ||

20. Iminā kho etam āvuso Mahānāma pariyāyena veditabbaṃ || yathā añño va[^328-10] sekho vihāro añño tathāgatavihāro ti || ||

SN_5,54(10).13. (3) Ānanda1. 1--2. Savatthi[^328-11] || || Atha kho āyasmā Ānando yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkami || pe || ekam antaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavantam etad avoca || ||


<!-- PTS Page 329 -->
<a id="page-329"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 329]*

3. Atthi nu kho bhante eko dhammo[^329-1] bhāvito bahulīkato cattāro dhamme paripūreti || cattāro dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satta dhamme paripūrenti || satta dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā dve dhamme paripūrentī ti || || Atthi[^329-2] kho Ānanda eko dhammo bhāvito bahulīkato cattāro dhamme paripūreti || cattāro dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satta dhamme paripūrenti || satta dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā dve dhamme paripūrentī ti || ||

4. Katamo pana[^329-3] bhante ekodhammo bhāvito bahulīkato cattāro dhamme paripūreti || cattāro dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satta dhamme paripūrenti || sattā dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā dve dhamme paripūrentī ti || || Ānāpānasatisamādhi kho Ānanda eko dhammo bhāvito bahulīkato cattāro satipaṭṭhāne paripūreti || cattāro satipaṭṭhānā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satta bojjhaṅge paripūrenti || satta bojjhaṅgā bhāvitā bahulīkatā vijjāvimuttim paripūrenti || || I.

5. Katham bhāvito ānāpānasatisamādhi katham bahulīkato cattāro satipaṭṭhāne paripūreti || || 6--12. Idhānanda bhikkhu araññagato vā rukkhamulagato vā suññāgāragato vā || pe || vitthāro[^329-4] || patinisaggānupassī Passasissāmī ti sikkhati[^329-5] || ||

13. Yasmiṃ[^329-6] samaye Ānanda bhikkhu dīghaṃ vā assasanto Dīgham assasāmī ti pajānāti || dīghaṃ vā passasanto Dīgham passasāmī ti[^329-7] pajānāti || pe[^329-8]-la-|| passambhayam kāyasaṅkhāram assāsissāmī ti sikkhati || passambhayam kāyasaṅkhāram passasissāmī ti sikkhati || kāye kāyānupassī Ānanda bhikkhu tasmiṃ samaye viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || || Taṃ kissa hetu || ||

14. Kāyaññatarāham Ānanda etam vadāmi yad idam


<!-- PTS Page 330 -->
<a id="page-330"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 330]*

assāsapassāsaṃ || tasmā ti ha Ānanda[^330-1] kāye kāyānupassī bhikkhu tasmiṃ samaye viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyyaloke abhijjhadomanassaṃ || ||

15. Yasmiṃ samaye Ānanda bhikkhu pītipaṭisaṃvedī Assasissāmī ti sikkhati[^330-2] || sukhapaṭisaṃvedī || cittasaṅkhāra- {paṭisaṃvedī} || passam bhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāram Assasissāmī ti sikkhati || passam bhayam cittasaṅkhāram Passasissāmī ti sikkhati || Vedanāsu vedanānupassī Ānanda bhikkhu tasmiṃ samaye viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyyaloke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || taṃ kissa hetu || ||

16. Vedanāññatarāham Ānanda etaṃ[^330-3] vadāmi yad idam assāsapassāsānam[^330-4] sādhukam manasikāraṃ || tasmā ti ha Ānanda[^330-1] vedanāsu vedanānupassī bhikkhu tasmiṃ samaye viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || ||

17. Yasmiṃ samaye Ānanda bhikkhu {cittapaṭisaṃvedī} Assasissāmī ti sikkhati || pe || abhippamodayaṃ cittaṃ || samodahaṃ cittaṃ Assasissāmī ti sikkhati[^330-5] || vimocayaṃ cittam Assasissāmi ti sikkhati[^330-5] || vimocayaṃ cittam Passasissāmī ti sikkhati || citte cittānupassī Ānanda bhikkhu tasmiṃ samaye viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhadomanassam || taṃ kissa hetu || ||

18. Nāham Ānanda muṭṭhassatissa asampajānassa ānāpānasatisamādhibhāvanaṃ vadāmi || tasmā ti hānanda citte cittānupassī bhikkhu[^330-6] tasmiṃ samaye viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādemanassaṃ || ||

19. Yasmiṃ samaye Ānanda bhikkhu aniccānupassī[^330-7] || la-pe || virāgānupassī || nirodhānupassī || paṭinissaggānupassī assasissāmī ti sikkhati || paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmī ti sikkhati || dhammesu dhammānupassī Ānanda bhikkhu tasmiṃ samaye viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || || So yan taṃ hoti abhijjhādomanassānam pahānaṃ tam paññāya disvā[^330-8] sādhukam


<!-- PTS Page 331 -->
<a id="page-331"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 331]*

ajjhupekkhitā hoti || tasmā ti hānanda dhammesu dhammānupassī bhikkhu tasmiṃ samaye viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || ||

20. Evam bhāvito kho Ānanda ānāpānasatisamādhi evam bahulīkato cattāro satipaṭṭhāne[^331-1] paripūreti || || II.

21. Katham bhāvitā ca Ānanda[^331-2] cattāro satipaṭṭhānā katham bahulīkatā satta bojjhaṅge paripūrenti || ||

22. Yasmiṃ samaye Ānanda bhikkhu kāye kāyānupassī viharati upaṭṭhitasati[^331-3] tasmiṃ samaye Ānanda bhikkhuno[^331-4] sati hoti asammuṭṭhā || yasmiṃ samaye Ānanda bhikkhuno[^331-5] upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā[^331-6] satisambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno āraddho hoti || satisambojjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samaye Ānanda[^331-7] bhikkhu bhāveti || satisambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno bhāvanā pāripūriṃ gacchati || so[^331-8] tathāsato viharanto taṃ dhammam paññāya pavicinati[^331-9] pavicarati[^331-10] parivīmaṃsam āpajjati || ||

23. Yasmiṃ samaye Ānanda bhikkhu tathā sato viharanto tam dhammam paññāya pavicinati parivīmaṃsam āpajjati[^331-11] || dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno āraddho hoti || dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhu bhāveti || || Dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno[^331-12] bhāvanāpāripūrim gacchati || tassa taṃ dhammam paññāya pavicinato pavicarato[^331-13] parivīmaṃsam āpajjato āraddhaṃ hoti viriyam asallīnaṃ || ||

24. Yasmim samaye Ānanda bhikkhuno taṃ dhammam paññāya pavicinato[^331-14] pavicarato[^331-15] parivīmaṃsam āpajjato


<!-- PTS Page 332 -->
<a id="page-332"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 332]*

āraddhaṃ hoti viriyam asallīnaṃ || viriyasambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno āraddho hoti || viriyasambojjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhu bhāveti || viriyasambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno bhāvanā pāripūriṃ gacchati || āraddhaviriyassa uppajjati pīti nirāmisā || ||

25. Yasmiṃ samaye Ānanda bhikkhuno āraddhaviriyassa uppajjati pīti nirāmisā || pītisambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye Ānanda bhikkhuno āraddho hoti || pītisambojjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhu bhāveti || pītisambojjhaṅgo tasmim samaye bhikkhuno bhāvanā pāripūriṃ gacchati || pītimanassa kāyo pi[^332-1] passambhati cittam pi passambhati || ||

26. Yasmiṃ samaye Ānanda bhikkhuno pītimanassa kāyo pi passambhati || cittam pi passambhati || passaddhisambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno[^332-2] āraddho hoti || passaddhisambojjhaṅgam tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhu bhāveti || passaddhisambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno bhāvanā pāripūriṃ gacchati || passaddhakāyassa sukhino cittaṃ samādhiyati || ||

27. Yasmiṃ[^332-3] samaye Ānanda bhikkhuno passaddhakāyassa sukhino cittaṃ samādhiyati || samādisambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhune āraddho hoti || samādhisambojjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhu bhāveti || samādhisambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno bhāvanā pāripūriṃ gacchati || || So tathā samāhitaṃ cittam sādhukam ajjhupekkhitā hoti || ||

28. Yasmiṃ[^332-4] samaye Ānanda bhikkhu[^332-5] tathā samāhitaṃ cittaṃ sādhukam ajjhupekkhitā hoti || upekhāsambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno āraddho hoti || upekhāsambojjhaṅgam tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhu[^332-6] bhāveti[^332-7] || upekhāsambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno bhāvanā pāripūrim gacchati || ||

29. Yasmiṃ[^332-4] samaye Ānanda bhikkhu vedanāsu citte || dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati upaṭṭhitasati[^332-8] tasmiṃ samaye Ānanda bhikkhuno[^332-9] sati hoti asammuṭṭhā || ||


<!-- PTS Page 333 -->
<a id="page-333"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 333]*

30. Yasmim[^333-1] samaye Ānanda bhikkhuno upatthitā[^333-2] sati hoti asammuṭṭhā || satisambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno āraddho hoti || satisambojjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhu bhāveti || satisambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno bhāvanā pāripūriṃ gacchati || || Yathā pathamaṃ satipaṭṭhānam evaṃ vitthāretabbaṃ[^333-3] || || So tathā samāhitaṃ cittaṃ sādhukam ajjhupekkhitā hoti || ||

31. Yasmiṃ samaye Ānanda bhikkhu tathā samāhitaṃ[^333-4] cittaṃ sādhukam ajjhupekkhitā hoti[^333-5] || upekhasambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno āraddho hoti || upekhāsambojjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhu bhāveti || upekhāsambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno[^333-6] bhāvanā pāripūriṃ gacchati || ||

32. Evam bhāvitā kho Ānanda cattāro satipaṭṭhānā evam bahulīkatā sattabojjhaṅge paripūrenti || || III.

33. Katham bhāvitā ca[^333-7] sattabhojjhaṅgā[^333-8] katham[^333-9] bahulīkatā vijjāvimuttim paripūrenti || ||

34. Idhānanda bhikkhu satisambojjhaṅgam bhāveti vivekanissitam || pe || upekhāsambojjhaṅgam bhāveti vivekanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ nirodhanissitam vossaggapariṇāmiṃ || ||

35. Evam bhāvitā kho Ānanda sattabojjhaṅgā evam bahulīkatā vijjāvimuttim paripūrentī ti || ||

SN_5,54(10).14. (4) Ānanda2.

dhamme paripūreti || cattāro dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satta dhamme paripūrenti || satta dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā dve dhamme paripūrenti || || I.

2. Atha kho sambahulā bhikkhū yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkamiṃsu || upasaṅkamitvā || pe || ekam antaṃ nisinnā kho te bhikkhū Bhagavantam etad avocuṃ || ||

3. Atthi nu kho bhante eko[^334-6] dhammo bhā- bahucattāro dhamme paripūreti || cattāro dhammā bhā- bahusatta dhamme paripūrenti || satta dhammā bhā- bahudve dhamme paripūrentī ti || || Atthi[^334-7] kho bhikkhave eko[^334-1] dhammo bhā- bahu- cattāro dhamme paripūreti[^334-8] || cattāro dhammā bhā- bahu- satta

dhamme paripūrenti || satta dhammā bhā- bahu- dve dhamme paripūrentī ti || ||

4. Katamo pana bhante eko[^335-1] dhammo bhā- bahu- cattāro dhamme paripūreti || cattāro dhammā bhā- bahu- satta dhamme paripūrenti || satta dhammā bhā- bahu- dve dhamme paripūrentīti || || Ānāpānasatisamādhi[^335-2] kho bhikkhave eko[^335-1] dhammo bhābahu- cattāro satipaṭṭhāne paripūreti || cattāro satipaṭṭhānā bhā- bahu- sattabojjhaṅge paripūrenti || sattabojjhaṅgā bhābahu- vijjāvimuttim paripūrenti[^335-3] || || I--III.

5. Katham bhāvito ca bhikkhave ānāpānāsatisamādhi[^335-4] katham bahulīkato cattāro satipaṭṭhāne paripūreti || || 6--34. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu araññagato vā[^335-5] || la || ||

35. Evam bhāvitā kho bhikkhave satta bojjhaṅgā evam bahulīkatā vijjāvimuttim paripūrentī ti || ||

SN_5,54(10).16. (6) Bhikkhū2.

2. [^335-6] Atha kho sambahulā bhikkhū- || Bhagavā etad avoca || ||

3. Atthi nu kho bhikkhave eko dhammo || || Bhagavammūlakā no bhante dhammā- || || Atthi bhikkhave eko dhammo- || ||

4. Katamo ca bhikkhave eko dhammo- -vijjāvimuttim paripūrentī ti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 336 -->
<a id="page-336"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 336]*

I.

5. Katham bhāvito ca bhikkhave ānāpānasatisamādhi katham bahulīkato cattāro satipaṭṭhāne paripūrenti[^336-1] || || 6--12. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu araññagato va rukkhamūlagato vā suññāgāragato vā nisīdati pallaṅkam ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyam paṇidhāya parimukham satim upaṭṭhapetvā so sato va assasati sato passasati || pa-pe || || Paṭinissaggānupassī assasissāmī ti sikkhati || paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmī ti sikkhati || ||

13. Yasmiṃ samaye bhikkhave bhikkhu dīghaṃ vā assasanto Dīgham assasāmīti pajānāti || dīghaṃ vā passasanto Dīgham passasāmī ti pajānāti[^336-2] || rassaṃ vā assasanto || pa-pe || sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvedī || la-pe || passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāram Assasissāmī ti sikkhati[^336-3] || passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāram Passasissāmīti sikkhati || kāye kāyanupassī bhikkhave bhikkhu tasmiṃ samaye viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || taṃ kissa hetu || ||

14. Kāyāññatarāham bhikkhave etam vadāmi yad idam assāsapassāsaṃ || tasmā ti ha bhikkhave kāye kāyanupassī bhikkhu tasmiṃ samaye viharati ātāpī sampajāno[^336-4] satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || ||

15. Yasmiṃ[^336-5] samaye bhikkhave bhikkhu {pītipaṭisaṃvedī} || pa || sukhapatisaṃvedī[^336-6] cittasaṅkhārapatisaṃvedī || passambhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāram Assasissāmī ti sikkhati || passambhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāram Passasissāmī ti sikkhati || vedanāsu vedanānupassī bhikkhave[^336-7] bhikkhu tasmiṃ samaye viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || taṃ kissa hetu || ||

16. Vedanāññatarāhaṃ[^336-7] bhikkhave etaṃ vedāmi yad idam assāsapassāsānam[^336-9] sādhukam manasikāraṃ || tasmā


<!-- PTS Page 337 -->
<a id="page-337"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 337]*

ti ha bhikkhave vedanāsu vedanānupassī bhikkhu tasmiṃ samaye viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhadomanassaṃ || ||

17. Yasmiṃ[^337-1] samaye bhikkhave bhikkhu cittapaṭisaṃvedī abhippamodayaṃ cittaṃ || samādahaṃ cittaṃ[^337-2] || vimocayaṃ cittaṃ Assasissāmīti sikkhati || vimocayaṃ cittam Passasissāmī ti sikkhati || citte cittānupassī bhikkhave bhikkhu tasmiṃ samaye viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || tam kissa hetu || ||

18. Nāham bhikkhave muṭṭhassatissa asampajānassa ānāpānasatisamādhibhāvanaṃ vadāmi || tasmā ti ha bhikkhave citte cittānupassī bhikkhu[^337-3] tasmiṃ samaye viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || ||

19. Yasmiṃ samaye bhikkhave bhikkhu aniccānupassī || pā[^337-4] || virāgānupassī || nirodhānupassī || paṭinissaggānupassī Assasissāmīti sikkhati[^337-5] || paṭinissaggānupassī Passasissāmīti sikkhati || dhammesu dhammānupassī bhikkhave bhikkhu tasmiṃ samaye viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyyaloke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || so yan taṃ hoti abhijjhādomanassānam[^337-6] pahānaṃ tam paññāya disvā sādhu[^337-7] ajjhupekkhitā hoti || tasmā ti ha bhikkhave dhammesu dhammānupassī bhikkhu tasmiṃ samaye viharati ātāpī sampajāno satimā vineyya loke abhijjhādomanassaṃ || ||

20. Evam bhāvito kho bhikkhave ānāpānasatisamādhi evam bahulīkato cattāro satipaṭṭhāne paripūreti || || II.

21. Katham bhāvitā ca bhikkhave cattāro satipaṭṭhānā katham bahulīkatā satta bojjhaṅge paripūrenti || ||

22. Yasmiṃ[^337-1] samaye bhikkhave bhikkhu kāye kāyānupassī viharati upaṭṭhitasati[^337-8] || tasmiṃ samaye sati hoti asammuṭṭhā || yasmiṃ[^337-1] samaye bhikkhave bhikkhuno


<!-- PTS Page 338 -->
<a id="page-338"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 338]*

upaṭṭhitā[^338-1] sati hoti asammuṭṭhā[^338-2] || satisambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno āraddho hoti || satisambojjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhu bhāveti || satisambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno bhāvanā pāripūrim gacchati || so tathā sato[^338-3] viharanto taṃ dhammam paññāya pavicinati[^338-4] pavicarati parivīmaṃsam āpajjatī || ||

23. Yasmiṃ samaye bhikkhave bhikkhu tathā sato viharanto taṃ dhammam paññāya pacinati pavicarati parivīmaṃsam āpajjati || dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo[^338-5] tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno āraddho hoti || dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgam tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhu bhāveti || dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno bhāvanā pāripūrim gacchati || tassa taṃ dhammam paññāya pavicinato pavicarato parivīmaṃsam āpajjato āraddham hoti viriyam asallīnaṃ || ||

24. Yasmim[^338-6] samaye bhikkhave bhikkhuno tam[^338-7] dhammam paññāya pavicinato pavicarato parivīmaṃsam āpajjato araddhaṃ hoti viriyam asallīnaṃ || viriyasambojjhaṅgo[^338-8] tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno āraddho hoti {viriyasambojjhaṅgaṃ}[^338-9] tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhu bhāveti || viriyasambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno bhāvanā pāripūriṃ gacchati || āraddhaviriyassa uppajjati pīti nirāmisā || ||

25. Yasmiṃ samaye bhikkhave bhikkhuno āraddhaviriyassa uppajjati pīti nirāmisā || pītisambojjhaṅgo[^338-10] tasmim samaye bhikkhuno āraddho hoti || pītisambojjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhu bhāveti || pītisambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno bhāvanā pāripūrim gacchati || || Pītimanassa kāyo pi[^338-3] passambhati cittam pi passambhati[^338-11] || ||

26. Yasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno pītimanassa kāyo pi passambhati cittam pi passambhati || passaddhisambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno āraddho hoti || passaddhisam-


<!-- PTS Page 339 -->
<a id="page-339"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 339]*

bojjhaṅgaṃ[^339-1] tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhu bhāveti || passaddhisambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno bhāvanā pāripūriṃ gacchati || || Passaddhakāyassa sukhino cittaṃ samādhiyati || ||

27. Yasmim samaye bhikkhave bhikkhuno passaddhakāyassa sukhino cittaṃ samādhiyati || samādhisambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno āraddho hoti || samādhisambojjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhu[^339-2] bhāveti || samādhisambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno bhāvanā pāripūrim gacchati || || So tathā samāhitaṃ cittaṃ sādhukam ajjhupekkhitā hoti || ||

28. Yasmim samaye bhikkhave bhikkhu tathā samāhitaṃ cittam sādhukam ajjhupekkhitā hoti || upekhāsambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno āraddho hoti || upekhāsambojjhaṅgam tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno āraddho hoti || upekhāsambojjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samaye[^339-3] bhikkhu bhāveti || upekhā- {sambojjhaṅgo} {tasmiṃ} samaye bhikkhuno bhāvanā pāripūriṃ gacchati || ||

29. Yasmiṃ samaye bhikkhave bhikkhu vedanāsu || citte || dhammesu dhammānupassī viharati upaṭṭhitasati || tasmiṃ samaye sati hoti asammuṭṭhā || ||

30. Yasmiṃ samaye bhikkhave bhikkhuno upaṭṭhitā sati hoti asammuṭṭhā || satisambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno āraddho hoti || {satisambojjhaṅgaṃ} tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhu bhāveti || satisambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno bhāvanā pāripūriṃ gacchati || pa || || So tathā samāhitaṃ cittam sādhukam ajjhupekkhitā hoti || ||

31. Yasmim samaye bhikkhave bhikkhu tathā samāhitaṃ cittaṃ sādhukam ajjhupekkhitā hoti || upekhāsambojjhaṅgo tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhuno āraddho hoti || upekhāsambojjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samaye bhikkhu bhāveti || upekhāsambojjhaṅgo {tasmiṃ} samaye bhikkhuno bhāvanā pāripūriṃ gacchati[^339-4] || ||

32. Evam bhāvitā[^339-5] kho bhikkhave cattāro satipaṭṭhānā evam bahulīkatā sattabojjhaṅge paripūrenti || ||

BOOK XI. SOTĀPATTI-SAṂYUTTAM.

CHAPTER I. VEḶUDVĀRAVAGGO PATHAMO.

SN_5,55(11).1. Rājā. 1--2. [^342-1] Sāvatthi || || Tatra kho etad avoca || ||

3. Kiñcāpi bhikkhave[^342-2] rājā cakkavatti catunnaṃ dīpānam issariyādhipaccam rajjaṃ kāretvā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatim saggaṃ lokam upapajjati[^342-3] devānaṃ Tāvatiṃsānaṃ[^342-4] sahavyataṃ || so tattha Nandane[^342-5] vane accharāsaṅghaparivuto dibbehi ca pañcahi kāmaguṇehi samappito {samaṅgibhūto} paricāreti[^342-6] || so catuhi dhammehi asamannāgato[^342-7] || || Atha kho so[^342-8] aparimutto ca nirayā aparimutto ca tiracchānayoniso || aparimutto ca pettivisayā[^342-9] || aparimutto ca[^342-10] apāyaduggativinipātā[^342-11] || ||

4. Kiñcāpi[^342-12] bhikkhave ariyasāvako piṇḍiyā lopena[^342-13] yāpeti nantakāni ca[^342-10] dhāreti || so catuhi dhammehi samannāgato || || Atha kho so parimutto ca[^342-10] nirayā || parimutto ca[^342-10] tiracchayoniyā[^342-14] || parimutto ca[^342-10] pittivisayā || parimutto ca[^342-10] apāyaduggativinipātā || || Katamehi catuhi || ||


<!-- PTS Page 343 -->
<a id="page-343"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 343]*

5. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako buddhe avecca pasādena samannāgato hoti || Iti piso Bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddho vijjācaraṇasampanno sugato lokavidū anuttaro purisadhammasārathi satthā devamanussānam buddho bhāgavā ti || ||

6. Dhamme avecca pasādena samannāgato hoti || || Svākkhāto Bhagavatā dhammo sandiṭṭhiko akāliko ehipassiko opanayiko[^343-1] paccattam veditabbo viññāhī ti || ||

7. Saṅghe avecca[^343-2] pasādena samannāgato hoti || || Suppaṭipanno Bhagavato sāvakasaṅgho || ujupaṭipanno Bhagavato sāvakasaṅgho || ñāyapaṭipanno Bhagavato sāvakasaṅgho || sāmīcipaṭipanno Bhagavato sāvakasaṅgho || yad idaṃ cattāri purisayugāni aṭṭhapurisapuggalā esa Bhagavato sāvakasaṅgho || āhuneyyo pāhuneyyo dakkhiṇeyyo añjalīkaraṇīyo anuttaram puññakhettaṃ lokassā ti || ||

8. Ariyakantehi sīlehi samaññāgato hoti akhaṇḍehi acchiddehi asabalehi akammāsehi bhūjissehi[^343-3] viññūpasatthehi[^343-4] aparāmaṭṭhehi samādhisaṃvattanikehi || ||

9. Imehi catuhi dhammehi samannāgato hoti || ||

10. Yo ca bhikkhave catunnam dīpānam paṭilābho yo ca[^343-5] catunnaṃ dhammānam paṭilābho || || Catunnaṃ dīpānam paṭilābho catunnaṃ dhammānam paṭilābhassa kalaṃ nagghati[^343-6] soḷasinti || ||

SN_5,55(11).2. (2) Ogadha or Saṭayhaṃ.

3. Catuhi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato ariyasāvako sotāpanno hoti avinīpātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano || || Katamehi catuhi || ||

4. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || || Iti pi so Bhagavā arahaṃ || pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || || 5--6. [^343-7] Dhamme || {Saṅgho} || ||

7. Ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato hoti || akhaṇḍehi || la-pe || samādhisaṃvattanikehi || ||


<!-- PTS Page 344 -->
<a id="page-344"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 344]*

8. Imehi kho bhikkhave catuhi dhammehi samannāgato ariyasāvako sotāpanno hoti avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano ti || ||

9. Idam avoca Bhagavā idam vatvā[^344-1] Sugato etad avoca satthā || || Yesaṃ saddhā[^344-2] ca sīlañca || pasādo[^344-3] dhammadassanaṃ || te ve kāle na paccanti[^344-4] || brahmacariyogadhaṃ sukhan ti || ||

SN_5,55(11).3. (3) Dighāvu.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Rājagahe viharati Veḷuvane Kalandakanivāpe || ||

2. Tena kho pana samayena Dīghāvu upāsako ābādhiko hoti dukkhito bāḷhagilāno || ||

3. Atha kho Dīghāvu upāsako pitaraṃ Jotikaṃ gahapatim āmantesi || Ehi tvaṃ gahapati yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkama || upasaṅkamitvā mama vacanena Bhagavato pāde sīrasā vanda || Dīghāvu bhante upāsako ābādhiko hoti[^344-5] dukkhito bāḷhagilāno || so Bhagavato pāde sirasā vandati[^344-6] || || Evaṃ ca vadehi Sādhu kira bhante Bhagavā yena Dīghāvussa upāsakassa nivesanaṃ tenupasaṅkamatu anukampam upādāyāti || || Evaṃ tātā ti kho Jotiko[^344-7] gahapati Dīghāvussa upāsakassa paṭissutvā yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antaṃ nisīdi || ||

4. Ekam antaṃ nisinno kho Jotiko gahapati Bhagavantam etad avoca || Dīghāvu bhante upāsako ābādhiko hoti dukkhito bāḷhagilāno || || So Bhagavato pāde sirasā vandati || evañca vadeti Sādhu kira bhante Bhagavā yena Dīghāvussa upāsakassa nivesanaṃ tenupasaṅkamatu anukampam upādāyāti || || Adhivāsesi Bhagavā tuṇhībhāvena || ||

5. Atha kho Bhagavā nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya yena Dīghāvussa upāsakassa nivesanaṃ tenupasaṅkami ||


<!-- PTS Page 345 -->
<a id="page-345"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 345]*

upasaṅkamitvā paññatte āsane nisīdi || nisajja kho Bhagavā Dīghāvum upāsakam etad avoca || || Kacci te Dīghāvu khamanīyam kacci yāpanīyaṃ kacci dukkhā vedanā paṭikkamanti no abhikkamanti patikkamo sānam paññāyati no abhikkamoti || || Na me bhante khamanīyaṃ na yāpanīyaṃ baḷhā me dukkhā abhikkamanti no patikkamanti abhikkamo sānam paññāyati no paṭikkamo ti || ||

6. Tasmā ti ha te Dīghāvu evaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ || Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato bhavissāmi || Iti pi so Bhagavā arahaṃ || pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || pe [pa] || || Dhamme || pe [pa] || Saṅghe || pe [pa] || Ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato bhavissāmi akhaṇḍehi || la-pe || samādhisaṃvattanikehi[^345-1] || || Evañhi te Dīghāvu sikkhitabban ti || ||

7. Yānimāni bhante Bhagavatā cattāri sotāpattiyaṅgāni desitāni || saṃvijjante te dhammā mayi ahañ ca tesu[^345-2] sandissāmi || ahañ hi bhante buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || Dhamme || Saṅghe || Ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato akhaṇḍehi || la-pe || samādhisaṃvattanikehī ti || || Tasmā ti ha tvam Dīghāvu imesu catusu sotāpattiyaṅgesu patiṭṭhāya cha vijjābhāgiye dhamme {uttariṃ bhāveyyāsi}[^345-3] || ||

8. Idha tvam Dīghāvu[^345-4] sabbesaṅkhāresu aniccānupassī viharāhi || anicce dukkhasaññī dukkhe anattasaññī pahānasaññī virāgasaññī nirodhasaññī ti || || Evaṃ hi te Dīghāvu sikkhitabban ti || || Ye me bhante Bhagavatā cha vijjābhāgiyā dhammā desitā || saṃvijjante te dhammā mayi ahaṃ ca tesu dhammesu sandissāmi || ahañhi bhante sabbasaṅkhāresu aniccānupassī viharāmi || anicce dukkhasaññī dukkhe anattasaññī pahānasaññī virāgasaññī nirodhasaññī || ||

9. Api ca me bhante evaṃ hoti māhevāyaṃ[^345-5] Jotiko gahapati mamaccayena vighātam āpajjīti[^345-6] || ||


<!-- PTS Page 346 -->
<a id="page-346"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 346]*

Mā tvaṃ tāta Dīghāvu evam manasākāsi || || Iṅgha[^346-1] tvaṃ tāta Dīghāvu yad eva te[^346-2] Bhagavā āha tad eva tvaṃ sādhukam manasi karohī ti || ||

10. Atha kho Bhagavā Dīghāvum upāsakam iminā ovādena ovaditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pakkāmi || ||

11. Atha kho Dīghāvu upāsako acirapakkantassa Bhagavato kālam akāsi || ||

12. Atha kho sambahulā bhikkhū yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkamiṃsu || upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antaṃ nisīdiṃsu || ekam antaṃ nisinnā kho te bhikkhū Bhagavantam etad avocuṃ || Yo so bhante Dīghāvu nāma upāsako Bhagavatā saṅkhittena ovādena ovādito so kālaṅkato[^346-3] || tassa kā gati ko abhisamparāyo ti || || Paṇḍito bhikkhave Dīghāvu upāsako ahosi paccapādi[^346-4] dhammassānudhammaṃ[^346-5] na ca[^346-6] dhammādhikaraṇaṃ vihesesi || ||

13. Dīghāvu bhikkhave upāsako pañcannam orambhāgiyānaṃ samyojanānam parikkhayā opapātiko hoti[^346-7] tattha parinibbāyi anāvattidhammo tasmā lokā ti || ||

SN_5,55(11).4. (4) Sāriputta1.8

1. Ekaṃ samayam āyasmā ca Sāriputto ayasmā ca Ānando Sāvatthiyaṃ viharanti Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme || ||

2. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando sāyaṇhasamayaṃ paṭisallānā vuṭṭhito || la[^346-9] || || Ekam antaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Ānando āyasmantaṃ Sāriputtam etad avoca || ||

3. Katīnam[^346-10] nu kho āvuso Sāriputta dhammānam samannāgamanahetu evam ayam pajā Bhagavatā vyākatā sotāpannā avinipātadhammā niyatā sambodhiparāyanā ti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 347 -->
<a id="page-347"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 347]*

Catunnaṃ kho āvuso dhammānaṃ samannāgamanahetu evam ayam pajā Bhagavatā vyākatā sotāpannā avinipātadhammā niyatā sambodhiparāyanā || katamesaṃ catunnaṃ || ||

4. Idhāvuso ariyasāvako buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || Dhamme || {Saṅghe} || ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato hoti akhaṇḍehi || la-pe || samādhisaṃvattanikehi[^347-1] || ||

5. Imesaṃ kho āvuso[^347-2] catunnaṃ dhammānaṃ samannāgamanahetu evam ayam pajā Bhagavatā vyākatā sotāpannā avinipātadhammā niyatā sambodhiparāyanā ti || ||

SN_5,55(11).5. (5) Sāriputta2.3

2. Atha kho āyasmā Sāriputto yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antaṃ nisīdi || || Ekam antaṃ nisinnaṃ[^347-4] kho āyasmantaṃ Sāriputtam Bhagavā etad avoca || ||

3. Sotāpattiyaṅgaṃ sotāpattiyaṅgan ti hidaṃ Sāriputta vuccati || katamaṃ nu kho Sāriputta sotapattiyaṅgan ti || || Sappurisasaṃsevo hi bhante sotāpattiyaṅgaṃ || saddhammasavanaṃ sotāpattiyaṅgaṃ || yonisomanasikāro sotāpattiyaṅgaṃ || dhammānudhammapaṭipatti sotāpattiyaṅgan ti || || Sādhu Sāriputta sādhu Sāriputta || sappurisasaṃsevo- -dhammānudhammapaṭipatti sotāpattiyaṅgaṃ[^347-5] || ||

4. Soto soto ti ha[^347-6] Sāriputta vuccati || katamo nu kho Sāriputta soto ti || || Ayam eva hi bhante ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo soto || seyyathīdaṃ || sammādiṭṭhi || pe || sammāsamādhī ti || || Sādhu sādhu Sāriputta || ayam eva hi Sāriputta ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo soto || seyyathīdaṃ || sammādiṭṭhi || la-pe || sammāsamādhi || ||


<!-- PTS Page 348 -->
<a id="page-348"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 348]*

5. Sotāpanno sotāpanno ti hidaṃ Sāriputta vuccati || katamo nu kho Sāriputta sotāpanno ti || || Yo hi bhante iminā ariyena aṭṭhaṅgikena maggena samannāgato || ayaṃ vuccati sotāpanno yoyam[^348-1] āyasmā evaṃ nāmo evaṃ gotto ti || || Sādhu sādhu Sāriputta yo hi Sāriputta iminā ariyena aṭṭhaṅgikena maggena samannāgato ayaṃ vuccati sotāpanno yoyaṃ[^348-1] āyasmā evaṃnāmo evaṃgotto ti || ||

SN_5,55(11).6. (6) Thapatayo.

1. Sāvatthi nidānaṃ[^348-2] || ||

2. Tena kho pana samayena sambahulā bhikkhū Bhagavato cīvarakammam karonti Niṭṭhitacīvaro Bhagavā temāsaccayena cārikam pakkamissatī tī[^348-3] || ||

3. Tena kho pana samayena Isidattapurāṇā thapatayo Sādhuke paṭivasanti kenacid eva karaṇīyena || assosuṃ kho Isidattapurāṇā thapatayo Sambahulā kira bhikkhū Bhagavato cīvarakammaṃ karonti Niṭṭhitacīvaro Bhagavā temāsaccayena cārikam pakkamissatī ti || ||

4. Atha kho Isidattapurāṇā thapatayo magge purisaṃ ṭhapesuṃ || Yadā tvam ambho purisa passeyyāsi[^348-4] Bhagavantam āgacchantam arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ || atha kho[^348-5] amhākam āroceyyāsī ti || ||

5. Dvīhatīhaṃ[^348-6] ṭhito kho so puriso addasa Bhagavantaṃ dūrato va āgacchantam || disvā yena Isidattapurāṇā thapatayo tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā Isidattapurāṇe[^348-7] thapatayo etad avoca || Ayaṃ so bhante Bhagavā āgacchati arahaṃ sammāsambuddho || yassa dāni kālam maññathā ti || ||

6. Atha kho Isidattapurāṇā thapatayo yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkamiṃsu || upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā Bhāgavantam piṭṭhito pitthito anubandhiṃsu || ||

7. Atha kho Bhagavā maggā okkamma[^348-8] yena aññataraṃ[^348-9] rukkhamūlaṃ tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā paññatte


<!-- PTS Page 349 -->
<a id="page-349"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 349]*

āsane nisīdi || Isidattapurāṇā pi kho[^349-1] thapatayo Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antaṃ nisīdiṃsu || || Ekam antaṃ nisinnā kho te[^349-2] Isidattapurāṇā thapatayo Bhagavantam etad avocuṃ || ||

8. Yadā mayam bhante Bhagavantaṃ[^349-3] suṇāma Sāvatthiyā Kosalesu cārikam pakkamissatī ti || hoti no tasmiṃ samaye anattamanatā hoti domanassaṃ Dūre no Bhagavā bhavissatī ti || Yadā pana mayam bhante Bhagavantaṃ suṇāma[^349-4] Sāvatthiyā Kosalesu cārikam pakkanto tī || hoti no tasmiṃ samaye anattamanatā hoti domanassaṃ Dūre no Bhagavā ti || ||

9. Yadā[^349-5] mayam bhante Bhagavantaṃ suṇāma Kosalehi Malle[^349-6] cārikam pakkamissatī ti || hoti no {tasmiṃ} samaye anattamatā hoti domanassaṃ Dūre no Bhagavā bhavissatī ti || Yadā pana mayam bhante Bhagavantaṃ suṇāma Kosalehi Malle[^349-6] cārikam pakkanto ti || hoti no tasmiṃ samaye anattamanatā[^349-7] hoti domanassaṃ Dūre no Bhagavā ti || ||

10. Yadā[^349-8] mayam bhante suṇāma Bhagavantaṃ Mallehi[^349-9] Vajjim[^349-10] cārikam pakkamissatī ti || hoti no tasmiṃ samaye anattamanatā hoti domanassaṃ Dūre no Bhagavā bhavissatī ti || || Yadā pana mayam bhante Bhagavantaṃ suṇāma Mallehi[^349-9] Vajjiṃ[^349-10] cārikam pakkanto ti || hoti no tasmiṃ samaye anattamanatā hoti domanassaṃ Dūre no Bhagavā ti || ||

11. Yadā[^349-8] mayam bhante Bhagavantaṃ suṇāma Vajjīhi Kāsiṃ[^349-11] cārikam pakkamissatī ti || hoti no tasmiṃ samaye anattamanatā hoti domanassaṃ Dūre no Bhagavā bhavissatī ti || || Yadā pana mayam bhante Bhagavantaṃ suṇāma Vajjīhi Kāsim[^349-11] cārikam pakkanto ti || hoti no tasmiṃ samaye anattamanatā hoti domanassaṃ Dūre no Bhagavā ti || ||

12. Yadā[^349-8] mayam bhante Bhagavantam suṇāma Kāsīhi Magadhe cārikam pakkamissatī ti || hoti no tasmiṃ samaye


<!-- PTS Page 350 -->
<a id="page-350"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 350]*

anattamanatā hoti domanassaṃ Dūre no Bhagavā bhavissatī ti[^350-1] || || Yadā pana mayam bhante Bhagavantaṃ suṇāma Kāsīhi Māgadhe cārikam pakkanto ti hoti[^350-2] anappakā[^350-3] no tasmiṃ samaye anattamanatā hoti anappakaṃ domanassaṃ Dūre no Bhagavā ti || ||

13. Yadā[^350-4] mayam bhante Bhagavantaṃ suṇāma Māgadhehi Kāsiṃ[^350-5] carikam pakkamissatī ti || hoti no tasmiṃ samaye attamanatā hoti somanassaṃ Āsanne[^350-6] no Bhagavā bhavissatī ti || || Yadā pana mayam bhante Bhagavantaṃ suṇāma Māgadhehi Kāsim[^350-7] cārikam pakkanto ti || hoti no tasmiṃ samaye attamanatā hoti somanassaṃ Āsanne[^350-6] no Bhagavā ti || ||

14. Yadā[^350-8] mayam bhante suṇoma Kāsīhi Vajjī || pe || ||

15. Vajjīhi [Malle] || ||

16. Mallehi Kosale[^350-9] || ||

17. Yadā pana bhante Bhagavantaṃ {suṇāma}[^350-10] Kosalehi Sāvatthiṃ[^350-11] cārikam pakkamissatī ti || hoti no tasmiṃ samaye attamanatā hoti somanassam Āsanne[^350-6] no Bhagavā bhavissatī ti || Yadā pana mayam bhante[^350-12] Bhagavantaṃ[^350-13] sunāma Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme ti || anappakā no tasmiṃ samaye attamanatā hoti anappakaṃ somanassam Āsanne[^350-6] no Bhagavā ti || ||

18. Tasmā ti ha thapatayo[^350-14] sambādho gharāvāso[^350-15] rajāpatho abbhokāso[^350-16] pabbajā alañca pana vo thapatayo appamādāyā ti || ||

19. Atthi kho no bhante etamhā sambādhā añño sambādhataro ceva sambādhasaṅkhātataro cāti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 351 -->
<a id="page-351"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 351]*

Katamo pana vo thapatayo etamhā sambādhā añño sambādho sambādhataro ceva[^351-1] sambādhasaṅkhātataro cā ti || ||

20. Idha mayam bhante yadā rājā Pasenadī Kosalo uyyānabhūmiṃ niyyātukāmo hoti || ye te rañño Pasenadissa Kosalassa nāgā opavayhā[^351-2] te kappetvā yā tā rañño Pasenadissa Kosalassa pajāpatiyo piyā manāpā tā[^351-3] ekam purato ekam pacchato nisīdāpema || || Tāsaṃ kho pana bhante bhāginīnam evarūpo gandho hoti seyyathāpi nāma gandhakaraṇḍakassa tāvad eva vivariyamānassa yathā taṃ rājakaññānaṃ[^351-4] gandhena vibhūsitānaṃ || tāsaṃ kho pana bhante bhaginīnam evarūpo kāyasamphasso hoti seyyathāpi nāma thulapicuno vā yathā taṃ rājakaññānaṃ sukhedhitānaṃ[^351-5] || tasmiṃ kho pana bhante samaye nāgo[^351-6] pi rakkhitabbo[^351-7] hoti || tā pi bhaginiyo rakkhitabbā || attā pi rakkhitabbo[^351-8] hoti || ||

21. Na kho pana mayam bhante abhijānāma tāsu bhaginīsu pāpakaṃ cittam uppādetā || ayaṃ kho no bhante etamhā sambādhā añño sambādho sambādhataro ceva sambādhasaṅkhātataro cā ti || ||

22. Tasmā ti ha thapatayo sambādho gharāvāso rajāpatho abbhokāso[^351-9] pabbajā alaṃ ca[^351-10] pana vo[^351-11] thapatayo appamādāya || ||

23. Catuhi kho thapatayo dhammehi samannāgato ariyasāvako sotāpanno hoti[^351-12] avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano || katamehi catuhi || ||

24. Idha thapatayo ariyasāvako buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || Dhamme || pe [pa] || Saṅghe || vigatamalamaccherena cetasā agāram ajjhāvasati[^351-13] || muttacāgo payatapāṇī vossaggarato yācayogo dānasaṃvibhāgarato[^351-14] || || Ime hi kho thapatayo catuhi dhammehi


<!-- PTS Page 352 -->
<a id="page-352"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 352]*

samannāgato ariyasāvako sotāpanno hoti avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano || ||

25. Tumhe[^352-1] kho thapatayo Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti[^352-2] Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānaṃ buddho bhagavā ti || Dhamme || Saṅghe || Yaṃ kho pana kiñci kule deyyadhammaṃ sabban tam appaṭivibhattaṃ sīlavantehi kalyāṇadhammehi || ||

26. Taṃ kim[^352-3] maññatha thapatayo[^352-4] || katividhā[^352-5] te Kosalesu[^352-6] manussā[^352-7] ye tumhākaṃ samasamā yad idaṃ dāna- {saṃvibhāge} ti || ||

27. Lābhā no bhante suladdhaṃ no bhante yesaṃ no Bhagavā evam pajānātī[^352-8] ti || ||

SN_5,55(11).7. (7) Veḷudvāreyyā.

1. Evam me sutam[^352-9] ekam samayam Bhagavā Kosalesu cārikaṃ caramāno mahatā bhikkhusaṅghena saddhim yena Veḷudvāraṃ nāma Kosalānam brāhmaṇagāmo tad avasari || ||

2. Assosuṃ kho te[^352-10] Veḷudvāreyyakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā Samaṇo khalu bho Gotamo sakyaputto sakyakulā pabbajito Kosalesu cārikaṃ caramāno mahatā bhikkhusaṅghena saddhiṃ Veḷudvāram anuppatto || || Taṃ kho pana Bhagavantam Gotamam evaṃ kalyāṇakittisaddo abbhuggato || Iti pi so Bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddho vijjācaraṇasampanno sugato lokavidū anuttaro purisadammasārathi satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || so imaṃ lokam sadevakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakam sassamaṇabrāhmaṇim pajam sadevamanussaṃ sayam abhiññā sacchikatvā pavedeti || so dhammaṃ deseti ādikalyāṇam majjhe kalyāṇam pariyosāṇakalyānam sāttham savyañjanaṃ kevalaparipuṇṇam parisuddham brahmacariyam pakāseti || || Sādhu kho pana tathārūpānam arahataṃ dassanaṃ hoti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 353 -->
<a id="page-353"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 353]*

3. Atha kho te[^353-1] Veḷudvāreyyakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkamiṃsu || upasaṅkamitvā appekacce Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antaṃ nisīdiṃsu || appekacce Bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammodiṃsu || sammodaniyaṃ katham sārāṇīyaṃ vītisāretvā ekam antaṃ nisīdiṃsu || appekacce yena Bhagavā tenañjaliṃ paṇāmetvā ekam antaṃ nisīdiṃsu || appekacce Bhagavato santike nāmagottaṃ sāvetvā ekam antaṃ nisīdiṃsu || appekacce tunhībhūtā ekam antaṃ nisīdiṃsu || ||

4. Ekam antaṃ nisinnā kho te[^353-1] Veḷudvareyyākā brāhmaṇagahapatikā Bhagavantam etad avocuṃ || || Mayam bho Gotama evaṃkāmā evaṃchandā evamadhippāyā || puttasambādhasayanam ajjhāvaseyyāma || kāsikacandanam paccanubhaveyyāma || mālāgandhavilepanam dhāreyyāma || jātarūparajataṃ[^353-2] sādiyeyyāma || kāyassa bhedā param maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokam upapajjeyyāma[^353-3] || || Tesaṃ no bhavaṃ Gotamo amhākam evaṃkāmānam evaṃchandānam evamadhippāyānaṃ tathā dhammaṃ desetu yathā mayaṃ puttasambādhasayanam ajjhāvaseyyāma || la || sugatiṃ saggam lokam upapajjeyyāmā ti[^353-3] || ||

5. Attūpanāyikaṃ[^353-4] vo gahapatayo dhammapariyāyaṃ desissāmi || tam suṇātha || sādhukam manasi karotha bhāsissāmī ti || || Evam bho ti kho te Veḷudvāreyyakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā Bhagavato paccassosuṃ || || Bhagavā etad[^353-5] avoca || || Katamo gahapatayo attūpanāyiko dhammapariyāyo || ||

6. Idha gahapatayo ariyasāvako iti paṭisañcikkhati || Ahaṃ kho smi[^353-6] jīvitukāmo amaritukāmo sukhakāmo dukkhapaṭikkūlo || yo kho maṃ jīvitukāmam amaritukāmaṃ sukhakāmaṃ dukkhapaṭikkūlam jīvitā voropeyya na me tam assa piyam[^353-1] manāpaṃ || || Ahañceva kho pana paraṃ jīvitukāmam amaritukāmam sukhakāmam dukkhapaṭikūlaṃ jīvitā voropeyyam || parassa tam assa appiyam amanāpaṃ || || Yo kho myāyaṃ dhammo appiyo amanāpo parassa peso


<!-- PTS Page 354 -->
<a id="page-354"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 354]*

dhammo appiyo amanāpo || yo kho myāyam dhammo appiyo amanāpo kathāhaṃ paraṃ tena saṃyojeyyan ti || || So iti paṭisaṅkhāya attanā ca pānātipātā paṭivirato hoti || paraṃ ca pāṇātipātā veramaṇiyā samādapeti || pāṇātipātā veramaṇiyā vaṇṇam bhāsati || || Evam assāyaṃ kāyasamācāro ti koṭiparisuddho hoti || ||

7. Puna ca param gahapatayo ariyasāvako iti paṭisañcikkhati || || Yo kho me adinnam theyyasaṅkhātam ādiyeyya || na me tam assa piyam[^354-2] manāpam || ahaṃ ce va kho pana parassa adinnam theyyasaṅkhātam ādiyeyyaṃ || parassa pi tam assa appiyam amanāpaṃ || || Yo kho myāyaṃ dhammo appiyo amanāpo parassa peso dhammo appiyo amanāpo || yo kho myāyaṃ dhammo appiyo amanāpo kathāham paraṃ tena saṃyojeyyanti || || So iti paṭisaṅkhāya attānañca adinnādānā paṭivirato hoti || parañca adinnādānā veramaṇiyā samādapeti || adinnādānā veramaṇiyā ca vaṇṇam bhāsati || || Evam assāyam kāyasamācāro ti koṭiparisuddho hoti || ||

8. Puna[^354-1] ca param gahapatayo ariyasāvako iti paṭisañcikkhati || || Yo kho me dāresu cārittam āpajjeyya || na me tam assa piyam[^354-2] manāpam || ahaṃ ceva kho pana parassa dāresu cārittam āpajjeyyaṃ || parassa pi tam assa appiyam amanāpaṃ || yo kho myāyaṃ dhammo appiyo amanāpo parassa peso[^354-3] dhammo appiyo amanāpo || kathāham paraṃ tena saṃyojeyyan ti || so iti paṭisaṅkhāya attanā ca kāmesu micchācārā paṭivirato hoti || paraṃ ca kāmesu micchācārā veramaṇiyā samādapeti || kāmesu micchācārā veramaṇiyā ca vaṇṇam bhāsati || || Evam assāyaṃ kāyasamācāro ti koṭiparisuddho ti || ||

9. Puna ca paraṃ gahapatayo ariyasāvako iti patisaṃcikkhati || || Yo kho me musāvādena attham bhañjeyya[^354-4] na me tam assa piyam[^354-2] manāpaṃ || ahañ ceva kho pana parassa musāvādena attham bhañjeyyam parassa pi tam assa appiyam amanāpaṃ || || Yo kho myāyaṃ dhammo appiyo amanāpo || parassa peso[^354-3] dhammo appiyo amanāpo ||


<!-- PTS Page 355 -->
<a id="page-355"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 355]*

yo kho myāyaṃ dhammo appiyo amanāpo || kathāham paraṃ tena saṃyojeyyan ti || || So iti paṭisaṅkhāya attanā ca musāvādā paṭivirato hoti || parañ ca musāvādā veramaṇiyā samādapeti || musāvādā veramaṇiyā vaṇṇam bhāsati || || Evam assāyam vacīsamācāro ti koṭiparisuddho hoti || ||

10. Puna ca paraṃ gahapatayo ariyasāvako iti paṭisañcikkhati || || Yo kho maṃ pisuṇāya vācāya mittehi bhedeyya[^355-1] na me tam assa piyam[^355-2] manāpam || ahañ ceva kho pana paraṃ pisuṇāya vācāya mittehi bhedeyyaṃ[^355-3] parassa pi[^355-4] tam assa appiyam amanāpam || la || Evaṃ assāyaṃ vācīsamācāro ti koṭiparisuddho hoti || ||

11. Puna ca paraṃ gahapatayo ariyasāvako iti paṭisañcikkhati || || Yo kho mam pharusāya vācāya samudācareyya na me tam assa piyam manāpam || ahañ ceva kho pana param pharusāya vācāya samudācareyyam parassa pi tam assa appiyam amanāpaṃ || || Yo kho myāyaṃ dhammo || pa || || Evam assāyaṃ vacīsamācāro ti koṭiparisuddho hoti || ||

12. Puna ca paraṃ gahapatayo ariyasāvako iti paṭisañcikkhati || Yo kho mam sampabhāsena[^355-5] samphappalāpabhāsena[^355-6] samudācareyya na me tam assa piyam[^355-2] manāpaṃ || ahañ ceva kho pana param sampabhāsena[^355-5] samphappalāpabhāsena[^355-6] samudācareyya || parassa pi tam assa appiyam amanāpaṃ || || Yo kho myāyam dhammo appiyo amanāpo parassa peso[^355-7] dhammo appiyo amanāpo || yo myāyam dhammo appiyo amanāpo kathāham paraṃ tena saṃyojeyyanti || || So iti patisaṅkhāya attanāca samphappalāpā paṭivirato hoti || paraṃ ca samphappalāpā veramaṇiyā samādapeti || samphappalāpā veramaṇiyā ca vaṇṇam bhāsati || || Evam assāyam vacīsamācāro ti koṭiparisuddho hoti || ||

13. So Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || sattha devamanussānaṃ buddho bhagavā ti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 356 -->
<a id="page-356"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 356]*

14. Dhamme || ||

15. Saṅghe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || supaṭipanno Bhagavato sāvakasaṅgho[^356-1] || la-pe || anuttaram puññakkhettam lokassā ti || ||

16. Ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato hoti akhaṇḍehi || la || {samādhisaṃvattanikehi}[^356-2] || ||

17. Yato kho gahapatayo ariyasāvako imehi sattahi saddhammehi samannāgato hoti || imehi catuhi[^356-3] ākaṅkhiyehi[^356-4] ṭhānehi so ākaṅkhamāno attanā vā[^356-5] attānaṃ vyākareyya Khīṇanirayo mhi khīṇatiracchānayoniko[^356-6] khīṇapittivisayo[^356-7] khīṇāpāyaduggativinipāto || sotāpanno ham asmi avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano ti || ||

18. Evaṃ vutte Veḷudvāreyyakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā Bhagavantam etad avocuṃ || || Abhikkantaṃ bho Gotama || la-pe || ete mayam bhagavantaṃ Gotamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāma dhammañca bhikkhusaṅghañca || Upāsake no bhavaṃ Gotamo dhāretu ajjatagge pānupete[^356-8] saraṇaṃ gate ti || ||

SN_5,55(11).8. (8) Giñjakāvasatha1.

1. Evam me sutam ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Ñātike viharati Giñjakāvasathe || ||

2. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam etad avoca || Sāḷho nāma bhante bhikkhu kālakato[^356-9] tassa kā gati ko abhisamparāyo || Nandā nāma bhante bhikkhunī kālakatā tassā kā gati ko abhisamparāyo || Sudatto nāma bhante upāsako kālakato tassa kā gati ko abhisamparāyo || Sujātā nāma bhante upāsikā kālakatā tassā kā gati ko abhisamparāyo ti || ||

3. Sāḷho Ānanda bhikkhu kālakato asavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttim paññāvimuttiṃ diṭṭheva dhamme sayam abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja vihāsi || || Nandā Ānanda bhikkhunī kālakatā pañcannam orambhāgiyānaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 357 -->
<a id="page-357"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 357]*

saṃyojanānam parikkhayā opapātikā tattha parinibbāyinī[^357-1] anāvattidhammā tasmā lokā || || Sudatto Ānanda upāsako kālakato tiṇṇaṃ saṃyojanānam parikkhayā rāgadosamohānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāmī sakid[^357-2] eva imaṃ lokam āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ karissati || || Sujātā Ānanda upāsikā kālakatā tiṇṇam saññojañānaṃ parikkhayā sotāpannā avinipātadhammā niyatā sambodhiparāyanā || ||

4. Anacchariyaṃ kho panetam Ānanda yaṃ manussabhūto[^357-3] kālaṃ kareyya || tasmim tasmiṃ ce[^357-4] maṃ kālakate upasaṅkamitvā etam attham paṭipucchissatha || vihesā pesā[^357-5] Ānanda assa Tathāgatassa || tasmā ti hānanda dhammādāsaṃ[^357-6] nāma dhammapariyāyaṃ desissāmi || yena samannāgato ariyasāvako ākaṅkhamāno attanā vā attānaṃ vyākareyya Khīṇanirayo mhi khīṇatiracchānayoniko[^357-7] khīnapittivisayo[^357-10] khīṇāpāyaduggativinipāto || sotāpanno hamasmi avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano || ||

5. Katamo ca so Ānanda dhammādāso dhammapariyāyo || yena samannāgato ariyasāvako ākaṅkhamāno attanā va attānaṃ vyākareyya || Khīṇanirayo mhi sotāpanno hamasmi avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano || ||

6. Idha Ānanda[^357-8] ariyasāvako Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || || Iti pi so Bhagavā || pa-pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || dhamme || saṅghe || ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato hoti akhaṇḍehi || la-pe || samādhisaṃvattanikehi || ||

7. Ayaṃ kho so Ānanda dhammādāso dhammapariyāyo || yena samannāgato ariyasāvako ākaṅkhamāno attanā va attānaṃ vyākareyya khīṇanirayo mhi khīṇatiracchānayoniko[^357-9] khīnapittivisayo[^357-10] khīnāpāyaduggativinipāto || sotāpanno ham asmi avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano ti || ||

8. Tīṇi pi suttāni[^357-11] ekanidānāni || ||

SN_5,55(11).9. (9) Giñjakāvasatha 2.

2. Ekam antaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavantam etad avoca || Asoko nāma bhante bhikkhu kālakato tassa kā gati ko abhisamparāyo || Asokā nāma[^358-1] bhante bhikkhunī kālakatā || la || Asoko nāma bhante upāsako kālakato || la || Asokā nāma bhante upāsikā kālakatā tassā kā gati ko abhisamparāyo ti[^358-2] || || 3--6. Asoko Ānanda bhikkhu kālakato āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttim paññāvimuttiṃ diṭṭheva dhamme sayam abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja vihāsi || || Purimaveyyākaraṇena ekanidānaṃ[^358-3] || ||

7. Ayaṃ[^358-1] kho so Ānanda dhammādāso dhammapariyāyo yena samannāgato ariyasāvako ākaṅkamāno attanā va attānam vyākareyya Khīṇanirayomhi || khīṇatiracchāyoniko khīṇapettivisayo khīṇāpāyaduggativinipāto || sotāpanno ham asmi avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano ti || ||

SN_5,55(11).10. (10) Giñjakāvasatha3.

2. Ekam antaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavantam etad avoca || || Kakkaṭo[^358-4] nāma[^358-5] bhante Ñātike upāsako kālakato tassa kā gati kho abhisamparāyo || Kāḷiṅgo[^358-7] nāma bhante Ñātike upāsako[^358-7] || Nikato[^358-8] nāma bhante Ñatike upāsako || Kaṭissaho[^358-9] nāma bhante || Tuṭṭho nāma bhante || Santuṭṭho nāma bhante || Bhaddo nāma bhante[^358-10] || Subhaddo nāma bhante Ñātike upāsake kālakato tassa kā gati ko abhisamparāyo ti || ||

3. Kakkaṭo[^358-11] Ānanda upāsako kālakato pañcannam orambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyojanānam parikkhayā[^358-12] opapātiko tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammo tasmā lokā || Kaḷiṅgo[^358-13]


<!-- PTS Page 359 -->
<a id="page-359"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 359]*

Ānanda[^359-1] || Nikato[^359-2] Ānanda || Kaṭissaho[^359-3] Ānanda || Tuṭṭho Ānanda || Santuṭṭho Ānanda || Bhaddo Ānanda || Subhaddo Ānanda upāsako kālakato pañcannam orambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyojanānam parikkhayā opapātiko tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammo tasmā lokā || sabbe ekagatikā kātabbā[^359-4] || ||

4. Paropaññāsa[^359-5] Ānanda Ñātike upāsakā kālakatā pañcannam orambhāgiyānaṃ samyojanāṇam parikkhayā opapātikā tattha parinibbāyino[^359-6] anāvattidhammā tasmā lokā || || Sādhikanavuti[^359-7] Ānanda Ñātike upāsakā kālakatā tiṇṇaṃ saṃyojanānam parikkhayā rāgadosamohānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāmino sakid eva imaṃ lokam āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ karissanti || || Cha atirekāni[^359-8] kho Ānanda pañcasatāni[^359-9] Ñātike upāsakā kālakatā tiṇṇaṃ saṃyojanānam parikkhayā sotāpannā avinipātadhammā niyatā sambodhiparāyanā || ||

5. Anacchariyaṃ kho panetam Ānanda yam manussabhūto kālaṃ kareyya || || Tasmiṃ tasmiṃ ce maṃ kālakate[^359-10] {upasaṅkamitvā} etam attham[^359-11] paṭipucchissatha[^359-12] || vihesā hesā[^359-13] Ānanda assa Tathāgatassa || tasmā ti hānanda dhammādāsaṃ[^359-14] nāma dhammapariyāyaṃ desissāmi || yena samannāgato ariyasāvako ākaṅkhamāno attanā va attānam vyākareyya || Khīṇanirayo mhi[^359-15] || khīṇatiracchānayoniko[^359-16] khīnapittivisayo[^359-17] khīnāpāyaduggativinipāto || sotāpanno ham asmi avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano || ||

6. Katamo ca so Ānanda dhammādāso[^359-18] dhammapariyāyo yena samannāgato ariyasāvako ākaṅkhamāno attanā va attānaṃ vyākareyya || pe || niyato sambodhiparāyano || ||


<!-- PTS Page 360 -->

##### *[PTS Page 360]*

7. Idhānanda ariyasāvako Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti[^360-1] || || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || Dhamme || Saṅghe || Ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato hoti akhaṇḍehi[^360-2] || la-pe || samādhisaṃvattanikehi || ||

7. Ayaṃ kho so Ānanda dhammādāso dhammapariyāyo yena samannāgato ariyasāvako ākaṅkhamāno attanā va attānaṃ[^360-3] vyākareyya || || Khīṇanirayo mhi || khīṇatiracchānayoniko[^360-3] khīṇapittivisayo[^360-3] || khīnāpāyaduggativinipāto || sotāpanno ham asmi avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano ti || || Veḷudvāravaggo pathamo[^360-4] || || Tassa[^360-1] uddānaṃ || || Rājā Ogadha[^360-5] Dīghāvu || Sāriputtāpare[^360-6] duve[^360-7] || Thapatayo[^360-8] Veḷudvāreyyā[^360-9] || Giñjakāvasathe tayo ti || ||

CHAPTER II. SAHASSAKA-OR RĀJAKĀRAMA-VAGGO-DUTIYO.

SN_5,55(11).11. (1) Sahassa.

1. Ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Rājakārāme || ||

2. Atha kho sahassabhikkhunisaṅgho[^360-10] yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antam aṭṭhāsi || ||

3. Ekam antaṃ ṭhitā kho tā bhikkhuniyo Bhagavā etad avoca || Catuhi kho bhikkhuniyo dhammehi samannāgato ariyasāvako sotāpanno hoti avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano || katamehi catuhi || ||

4. Idha bhikkhuniyo ariyasāvako Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 361 -->
<a id="page-361"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 361]*

5. [^361-6]. Dhamme || Saṅghe || ||

7. Ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato hoti akhaṇḍehi || la- [pe] || samādhisaṃvattanikehi[^361-1] || ||

8. Imehi kho bhikkhuniyo[^361-2] catuhi dhammehi samannāgato ariyasāvako sotāpanno hoti avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano ti || ||

SN_5,55(11).12. (2) Brāhmaṇā.

1. [^361-2]. [^361-3]Sāvatthi nidānam || ||

3. Brāhmanā bhikkhave udayagāminiṃ nāma paṭipadam paññāpenti || te sāvakam evaṃ samādapenti || || Ehi tvam ambho purisa kālasseva uṭṭhāya pācīnamukho[^361-4] yāhi || so tvam mā semhaṃ[^361-5] parivajjehi || mā papātaṃ || mā[^361-6] khāṇum || mā[^361-7] kaṇṭakaṃ[^361-8] ṭhānam[^361-9] || mā candanikaṃ[^361-10] || mā oḷigallaṃ[^361-11] || yattha papāteyyāsi[^361-12] tattheva maraṇam āgaccheyyāsi[^361-13] || Evaṃ[^361-14] tvam ambho purisa kāyassa bhedā param maraṇā sugatim saggam lokam upapajjissasīti[^361-15] || ||

4. Taṃ kho panetam bhikkhave[^361-16] brāhmaṇānaṃ bālagamanam[^361-17] etam mūḷhagamanam etam na nibbidāya na virāgāya na nirodhāya[^361-18] na upasamāya na abhiññāya na sambodhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati || || Ahañ ca kho bhikkhave ariyassa vinaye[^361-19] udayagāminim paṭipadam[^361-20] paññāpemi || yā ekantanibbidāya virāgāya nirodhāya upasamāya abhiññāya sambodhāya nibbānāya {saṃvattati} || || Katamā ca yā bhikkhave udayagāminī paṭipadā || yā[^361-21] ekantanibbidāya || la-pe || nibbānāya saṃvattati || ||


<!-- PTS Page 362 -->
<a id="page-362"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 362]*

5. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || Iti pi so Bhagavā || pe-la || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || Dhamme || Saṅghe || || Ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato hoti akhaṇḍehi || la-pe || samādhisaṃvattanikehi || ||

6. Ayaṃ kho sā bhikkhave udayagāminī patipadā yā[^362-1] ekantanibbidāya || la || nibbānāya saṃvattatī ti || ||

SN_5,55(11).13. (3) Ānanda.

1. Ekam samayam āyasmā ca Ānando[^362-2] āyasmā ca Sāriputto[^362-3] Sāvatthiyaṃ viharanti[^362-4] Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme || ||

2. Atha kho āyasmā Sāriputto sāyaṇhasamayam paṭisallāṇā vuṭṭhito yenāyasmā Ānando tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā Ānandena saddhiṃ sammodi || sammodanīyaṃ katham sārāṇīyaṃ vītisāretva ekam antaṃ nisīdi || ekam antam nisinno kho āyasmā Sāriputto āyasmantam Ānandam etad avoca || ||

3. Katīnam[^362-5] kho āvuso Ānanda dhammānam pahānā katīnam[^362-5] dhammānaṃ samannāgamanahetu evam ayam pajā[^362-6] Bhagavatā vyākatā sotāpannā avinipātadhammā niyatā sambodhiparāyanā ti || ||

4. Catunnaṃ kho āvuso dhammānam pahānā[^362-7] catunnam[^362-8] dhammānaṃ samannāgamanahetu evam ayam pajā Bhagavatā vyākatā sotāpannā avinipātadhammā niyatā sambodhiparāyanā ti || katamesaṃ catunnaṃ || ||

5. Yathārūpena kho āvuso Buddhe apasādena[^362-9] samannāgato assutāvā puthujjano kāyassa bhedā param maraṇā āpāyam duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ[^362-10] nirayam upapajjati[^362-11] || tathā-


<!-- PTS Page 363 -->
<a id="page-363"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 363]*

rūpassa Buddhe appasādo[^363-1] na hoti || || Yathārūpena[^363-2] ca kho[^363-1] avuso Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato sutavā ariyasāvako kāyassa bhedā param maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokam upapajjati || tathārūpassa Buddhe aveccappasādo hoti || Iti pi so Bhagavā || pa-pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || ||

6. Yathārūpena kho āvuso dhamme appasādena[^363-3] samannāgato assutavā puthujjano kāyassa bhe- pa- maapāyaṃ dug- vinipātaṃ[^363-4] nirayam upapajjati || tathārupassa dhamme aveccappasādo[^363-5] na hoti || || Yathārūpena ca kho āvuso dhamme aveccappasādena samannāgato sutavā ariyasāvako kāyassa bhe- pa- ma- sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokam upapajjati || tathārūpassa dhamme aveccappasādo hoti || svākhyāto[^363-6] Bhagavatā dhammo[^363-7] || la-pe || viññūhīti || ||

7. Yathārūpena kho āvuso saṅghe appasādena[^363-3] samannāgato assutavā puthujjano kāyassa bhe- pa- ma- apāyam duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayam upapajjati || tathārūpassa saṅghe aveccappasādo[^363-5] na hoti || || Yathārūpena ca kho āvuso saṅghe aveccappasādena samannāgato sutavā ariyasāvako kāyassa bhe- pa- ma- sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokam upapajjati || tathārūpassa saṅghe aveccappasādo hoti || supaṭipanno Bhagavato sāvakasaṅgho || la-pe || anuttaram puññakkhettaṃ lokassā ti || ||

8. Yathārūpena kho āvuso dussīlyena[^363-8] samannāgato assutavā puthujjano kāyassa bhe- pa- ma- duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayam uppajjati || tathārūpassa dussīlena[^363-9] hoti || || Yathārūpehi ca kho āvuso ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato sutavā ariyasāvako kāyassa bhe- pa- ma- sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokam upapajjati || tathārūpassa[^363-10] ariyakantāni sīlāni honti akhaṇḍāni || la-pe || samādhisaṃvattanikāni || ||

9. Imesaṃ kho āvuso catunnam dhammānam pahānā[^364-1] imesaṃ catunnaṃ dhammānaṃ samannāgamanahetu evam ayam pajā Bhagavatā vyākatā sotāpannā avinipātadhammā niyatā sambodhiparāyanā ti || ||

4. Buddhe aveccappasāde samādapetabbā || [pe] || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || Dhamme[^365-1] || Saṅghe[^365-1] || Ariyakantesu sīlesu samādapetabbā nivesetabbā patiṭṭhāpetabbā akhaṇḍesu || la || samādhisaṃvattanikesu || ||

5. Ye[^365-2] bhikkhave anukampeyyātha || ye ca sotabbam maññeyyum mittā vā amaccā vā ñāti vā sālohitā vā te vo[^365-3] bhikkhave imesu[^365-4] catusu sotāpattiyaṅgesu samādapetabbā nivesetabbā patiṭṭhāpetabbā ti || ||

SN_5,55(11).17. (7) Mittenāmaccā2.

3. Ye[^365-5] bhikkhave anukampeyyātha || ye ca[^365-6] sotabbam maññeyyum mittā vā amaccā[^365-7] vā ñātīvā[^365-8] sālohitā[^365-9] vā || te vo[^365-3] bhikkhave catusu sotāpattiyaṅgesu samādapetabbā nivesetabbā patiṭṭhāpetabbā || katamesu catusu || ||

4. Buddhe aveccappasāde samādapetabbā nivesetabbā patiṭṭhāpetabbā || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || || Siyā bhikkhave catunnam mahābhūtānam aññathattam[^365-10] pathavīdhatuyā āpodhātuyā tejodhātuyā vāyodhātuyā || na tveva Buddhe aveccappasādenasamannāgatassa ariyasāvakassa siyā aññathattaṃ || tatridam aññathattaṃ || || So vata Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato ariyasāvako nirayaṃ vā tiracchānayonim vā pittivisayaṃ[^365-11] vā upapajjissatī ti[^365-12] || netaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati || || 5--6. Dhamme || || {Saṅghe}[^365-13] || ||

7. Ariyakantesu sīlesu samādapetabbā nivesetabbā patiṭṭhapetabbā || akhaṇḍesu || la-pe || samādhisaṃvattanikesu || || Siyā bhikkhave catunnam mahābhūtānam aññathattam pathavidhatuyā āpo- tejo- vāyodhātuyā || na tveva ariya-

##### *[PTS Page 366]*

kantehi sīlehi samannāgatassa ariyasāvakassa siyā aññathattam || tatridam aññathattam || || So vata ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato ariyasāvako nirayaṃ vā tiracchānayoniṃ vā pittivisayaṃ vā uppajjissatī ti netaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati || ||

SN_5,55(11).18. (8) Devacārika1.

1. Sāvatthinidānaṃ[^366-5] || ||

2. Atha kho āyasmā Mahā-Moggalāno seyyathāpi nāma balavā puriso sammiñjitaṃ vā bāham pasāreyya pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ sammiñjeyya[^366-6] || evam eva Jetavane antarahito devesu Tāvatiṃsesu pātur ahosi || ||

3. Atha kho sambahulā Tāvatiṃsakāyikā[^366-7] devatāyo yenāyasmā Mahā-Moggalāno tenupasaṅkamiṃsu || upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantam Mahā-Moggalānam abhivādetvā ekam antam aṭṭhaṃsu || || Ekam antaṃ ṭhitā kho tā devatāyo āyasmā Mahā-Moggalāno etad avoca[^366-8] || ||

4. Sādhu kho āvuso Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgamanaṃ hoti || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || || Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgamanahetu kho āvuso evam idhekacce sattā kāyassa bhedā param maraṇā sugatim saggaṃ lokam upapajjatī ti[^366-9] || ||

5.6. Sādhu kho āvuso dhamme || Saṅghe[^366-10] || ||

7. Sādhu kho āvuso ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgamanaṃ hoti ākhaṇḍehi || la-pe || samādhisaṃvattānikehi || ||
