##### *[PTS Page 369]*

CHAPTER III. SARAṆĀNIVAGGO TATIYO.

SN_5,55(11).21. (1) Mahānāma1.

1. Evam me sutaṃ[^369-1] ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Sakkesu viharati Kapilavatthusmiṃ Nigrodhārāme || ||

2. Atha kho Mahānāmo sakko yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antaṃ nisīdi || || Ekam antaṃ nisinno kho Mahānāmo sakko Bhagavantam etad avoca[^369-2] || ||

3. Idam bhante Kapilavatthu[^369-3] iddhaṃ[^369-4] ceva[^369-5] phitañca bahujanam[^369-6] ākiṇṇamanussam[^369-7] sambādhavyūhaṃ[^369-8] || || So khvāham bhante Bhagavantaṃ vā {payirupāsitvā} manobhāvanīye vā bhikkhū[^369-9] sāyaṇhasamayaṃ Kapilavatthum pavisanto bhante na pi hatthinā samāgacchāmi || bhante na pi assena samāgacchāmi || bhante na pi rathena[^369-10] samāgacchāmi || bhante na pi sakaṭena samāgacchāmi || bhante na pi purisena samāgacchami || tassa mayham bhante {tasmiṃ} samaye mussateva Bhagavantam ārabbha sati || mussati dhammam arabbha sati[^369-11] || mussati saṅgham ārabbha sati[^369-12] || || Tassa mayham bhante evaṃ hoti || Imamhi cāhaṃ samaye kālaṃ kareyyaṃ kā mayhaṃ[^369-13] gati ko abhisamparāyo ti || ||

4. Mā bhāyi[^369-14] Mahānāma mā bhāyi Mahānāma || apāpakaṃ te maraṇam bhavissati apāpikā kālaṃkiriyā || || Yassa kassaci Mahānāma dīgharattaṃ saddhāparibhāvitaṃ cittaṃ sīlaparibhāvitaṃ cittaṃ sutaparibhāvitaṃ[^369-15] cittaṃ cāgaparibhāvitaṃ cittam paññaparibhāvitaṃ cittaṃ || tassa yo hi khvāyaṃ[^369-16] kāyo rūpī cātumahābhūtiko mātā-


<!-- PTS Page 370 -->
<a id="page-370"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 370]*

pittikasambhavo[^370-1] odanakummāsupacayo aniccucchādanaparimaddana-bhedana-viddhaṃsanadhammo[^370-2] tam idheva kākā vā khādanti gijjhā vā khādanti kulalā vā khādanti sunakhā[^370-3] vā khādanti sigālā va khādanti vividhā vā pāṇakajātā khādanti || || Yañca khvassa cittaṃ dīgharattaṃ saddhāparibhāvitam sīla-suta-cāga-paribhāvitaṃ[^370-4] || tam uddhagāmi[^370-5] hoti visesagāmi[^370-6] || ||

5. Seyyathāpi Mahānāma[^370-7] puriso sappikumbham vā telakumbham vā gambhīram udakarahadam ogāhetvā[^370-8] bhindeyya || tatra yā assa[^370-9] sakkharā vā kaṭhalā vā sā adhogāmī assa || yañca khvassa tatra sappi vā telaṃ vā tam uddhaṃgāmī assa visesāgamī || || Evam eva kho Mahānāma yassa kassaci dīgharattaṃ saddhāparibhāvitaṃ cittaṃ sīlasuta-cāga-paññāparibhāvitaṃ cittaṃ || tassa hoti khvāyam kāyo rūpī cātumahābhūtiko mātāpittikasambhavo[^370-1] odanakummāsupacayo aniccucchādana-parimaddana-bhedanaviddhaṃsanadhammo[^370-10] || tam idheva kākā vā[^370-11] khādanti || gijjhā vā khā- || kulalā vā khā- || sunakhā[^370-12] vā khā- || sigālā vā khā- || vividhā vā pāṇakajātā khādanti || || Yañca khvassa[^370-13] taṃ[^370-14] cittaṃ dīgharattaṃ saddhāparibhāvitaṃ sīla-suttacāgapaññā paribhāvitaṃ[^370-15] || tam[^370-11] uddhaṃgāmī[^370-16] hoti visesagāmī || ||

6. Tuyhaṃ kho pana Mahānama dīgharattaṃ[^370-15] saddhaparibhāvitaṃ cittam sīla-suta-cāga-paññāparibhāvitaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 371 -->
<a id="page-371"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 371]*

cittam || mā bhāyi[^371-1] Mahānāma mā bhāyi Mahānāma apāpakaṃ te maraṇam bhavissati apāpikā kālakiriyā[^371-2] ti || ||

SN_5,55(11).22. (2) Mahānāma2.

1. Evaṃ me sutaṃ-[^371-3] || ||

2. Atha kho Mahānamo- || ||

3. Idam[^371-4] bhante Kapilavatthu-[^371-5] || ||

4. Mā bhāyi Mahānāma ma bhāyi Mahānāma apāpakaṃ te[^371-6] maraṇam bhavissati apāpikā kālakiriyā || || Catuhi kho Mahānāma dhammehi samannāgato ariyasāvako nibbānaninno hoti nibbānapoṇo nibbānapabbhāro || katamehi catuhi || ||

5. Idha Mahānāma ariyasāvako Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || Dhamme || {Saṅghe} || || Ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato hoti || akhaṇḍehi || la-pe || samādhisaṃvattanikehi || ||

6. Seyyathāpi Mahānāma rukkho pācinaninno[^371-7] pācīnapoṇo pācīnapabbhāro || so mūle chinne[^371-8] katamena papateyyā ti[^371-9] || || Yena bhante ninno yena poṇo yena pabbhāro ti || || Evam eva kho Mahānāma imehi catuhi dhammehi samannāgato ariyasāvako nibbhānaninno nibbānapoṇo nibbānapabbhāro ti || ||

SN_5,55(11).23. (3) Godhā (or Mahānāma3).

1. Kapilavatthunidānaṃ[^371-10] || ||

2. Atha Kho Mahānāmo sakko yena Godhā sakko tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā Godhaṃ[^371-11] sakkam etad avoca || ||


<!-- PTS Page 372 -->
<a id="page-372"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 372]*

3. Katīhi tvam Godhe[^372-1] dhammehi samannāgataṃ sotāpannam puggalam ājānasi || avinipātadhāmmaṃ niyataṃ sambodhiparāyanan ti || || Tīhi khvāham Mahānāma dhammehi samannāgataṃ sotāpannam puggalam ājānāmi avinipātadhammam niyatam sambodhiparāyanaṃ[^372-2] || || Katamehi tīhi || ||

4. Idha Mahānāma ariyāsavako Buddha aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || Dhamme || {Saṅghe} aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti[^372-3] || supaṭipanno Bhagavato sāvakasaṅgho || la-[pe] || anuttaram puññakhettaṃ lokassā ti || || Imehi khvāham[^372-4] Mahānāma[^372-3] tīhi dhammehi samannāgataṃ sotāpannam puggalam ājānāmi || avinipātadhammam niyataṃ sambodhiparāyanaṃ || ||

5. Tvam pana Mahānāma katīhi dhammehi samannāgataṃ sotāpannaṃ puggalam ājānāsi avinipātadhammaṃ niyatam sambodhiparāyanan ti || || Catuhi khvāham bho[^372-5] Godhe dhammehi samannāgatam sotāpannam puggalam ājānāmi avinipātadhammaṃ niyataṃ sambodhiparāyanaṃ[^372-2] || katamehi catuhi || || Idha Godhe ariyasāvako Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || Dhamme || {Saṅghe} || Ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato hoti akhaṇḍehi || la-pe || samādhisaṃvattanikehi || || Imehi khvāham Godhe catuhi dhammehi samannāgataṃ sotāpannam puggalam ājānāmi avinipātadhammaṃ niyataṃ sambodhiparāyanan ti || ||

6. Āgamehi[^372-6] tvam Mahānāma āgamehi tvam Mahānāma[^372-7] || Bhagavā va[^372-8] etaṃ jāneyya etehi dhammehi samannāgatam vā[^372-9] asamannāgataṃ vā ti || || Āyāma Godhe yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkameyyāma[^372-10] || upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavato etam attham ārocessāmā ti[^372-11] || ||


<!-- PTS Page 373 -->
<a id="page-373"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 373]*

7. Atha kho Mahānāmo[^373-1] sakko Godhā ca sakko yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkamiṃsu || upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antam nisīdiṃsu || Ekam antaṃ nisinno kho Mahānāmo sakko Bhagavantam etad avoca || ||

8. Idhāham bhante yena Godhā sakko tenupasaṅkamiṃ || upasaṅkamitvā Godhaṃ[^373-2] sakkam etad avocaṃ || katīhi tvaṃ Godhe dhammehi samannāgatam sotāpannam puggalam ājānāsi avinipāta- ni- sambodhi- || || Evam vutte bhante Godhā sakkho mam etad avoca || || Tīhi khvāham Mahānāma dhammehi samannāgataṃ sotāpannam puggalam ājānāmi avi- ni- sambodhi- || katamehi tīhi || || Idha Mahānāma ariyasāvako Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || Dhamme || Saṅghe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || supaṭipanno Bhagavato sāvakasaṅgho || la-pe || anuttaram puññakkhettaṃ lokassā ti || || Imehi khvāham[^373-3] Mahānāma tīhi dhammehi samannāgataṃ sotāpannam puggalam ājānāmi avi- ni- sambodhi- || || Tvam pana Mahānāma katīhi dhammehi samannāgataṃ sotāpannam puggalam ājānāsi avini- ni- sambodhiparāyanan ti || ||

9. Evaṃ vuttāham[^373-4] bhante Godham[^373-5] sakkam etad avocam || || Catuhi khvāham Godhe[^373-6] dhammehi samannāgatam sotāpannam puggalam ājānāmi avi- ni- sambodhi- || || Katamehi catuhi || || Idha Godhe ariyasāvako Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānam Buddho bhagavā ti || Dhamme || Saṅghe || ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato hoti akhaṇḍehi || || la-pe || samādhisaṃvattanikehi || || Imehi khvāhaṃ[^373-3] Godhe catuhi dhammehi samannāgatam sotāpannam puggalam ājānāmi avini- ni- sambodhiparāyanan ti || || Evam vutte bhante Godhā sakko mam etad avoca || Āgamehi tvam Mahānāma āgamehi tvam Mahānāma || Bhagavā va[^373-7] etam jāneyya[^373-8] etehi dhammehi samannāgatam vā asamannāgataṃ vā ti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 374 -->
<a id="page-374"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 374]*

10. Idha bhante kocid eva dhammasamuppādo[^374-1] uppajjeyya || ekato assa[^374-2] Bhagavā ekato bhikkhusaṅgho[^374-3] || yeneva[^374-4] Bhagavā tenevāham assaṃ[^374-5] evam pasannam mam bhante Bhagavā dhāretu || ||

11. Idha bhante kocid eva dhammasamuppādo uppajjeyya || ekato assa Bhagavā[^374-6] ekato bhikkhusaṅgho[^374-7] bhikkhunīsaṅgho ca yeneva Bhagavā[^374-8] tenevāham assam[^374-2] evam pasannam mam bhante Bhagavā dhāretu || ||

12. Idha bhante kocid eva dhammasamuppādo uppajjeyya || ekato[^374-9] assa Bhagavā ekato bhikkhusaṅgho[^374-10] bhikkhunīsaṅgho[^374-11] upāsakā ca yeneva Bhagavā tenevāham assam[^374-1] evam pasannam mam bhante Bhagavā dhāretu || ||

13. Idha bhante kocid eva dhammasamuppādo uppajjeyya || ekato assa Bhagavā ekato bhikkhusaṅgho bhikkhu- {nīsaṅgho} upāsakā upāsikāyo ca[^374-12] yeneva[^374-13] Bhagavā tenevāham assaṃ || evam pasannam mam bhante Bhagavā dhāretu || ||

14. Idha bhante kocid eva dhammasamuppādo uppajjeyya || ekato assa Bhagavā ekato bhikkhusaṅgho bhikkhu- {nīsaṅgho} upāsakā upāsikāyo sadevako ca[^374-14] loko samārako[^374-15] sabrahmako[^374-15] sassamaṇabrāhmaṇapajā[^374-16] sadevamanussā || yeneva[^374-13] Bhagavā tenevāham assam evam pasannaṃ mam bhante[^374-14] Bhagavā dhāretū ti || ||

15. Evaṃvādiṃ[^374-17] tvam Godhe Māhānāmaṃ[^374-18] sakkaṃ kiṃ vadesī ti || || Evaṃvādāham[^374-19] bhante Mahānāmaṃ[^374-18] sakkaṃ na kiñci vadāmi aññatra kalyāṇā[^374-20] aññatra kusalā ti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 375 -->
<a id="page-375"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 375]*

SN_5,55(11).24. (4) Sarakāni or Saraṇāni 1.

1. Kapilavatthu nidānaṃ[^375-1] || ||

2. Tena kho pana samayena Sarakāni[^375-2] sakko kālakato hoti so[^375-3] Bhagavatā vyākato sotāpanno avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano ti || ||

3. Tatra sudam sambahulā Sakkā saṅgamma samāgamma ujjhāyanti khīyanti vipācenti || Acchariyam vata bho abbhutaṃ vata bho etthadāniko na[^375-3] sotāpanno bhavissati || yatra hi nāma Sarakāni sakko kālakato so Bhagavatā vyākato sotāpanno avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano ti || || Sarakāni sakko sikkhādubbalyam āpādi majjapānam apāyī ti[^375-4] || ||

4. Atha kho Mahānāmo sakko yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antam nisīdi || ek- an- ni- kho Mahānamo sakko Bhagavantam etad avoca || ||

5. Idha bhante Sarakāni sakko kālakato so Bhagavatā vyākato sotāpanno avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano ti || || Tatra sudam bhante sambahulā Sakkā saṅgamma samāgamma ujjhāyanti bhīyanti vipācenti Acchariyaṃ vata bho abbhutaṃ vata bho etthadāniko na[^375-5] sotāpanno bhavissati || yatra hi nāma Sarakāṇi sakko kālakato so Bhagavatā vyākato sotāpanno avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano ti || || Sarakāni[^375-6] sakko sikkhādubbalyam āpādi[^375-7] majjapānam apāyīti[^375-8] || || Yo so Mahānāma dīgharattam upāsako Buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gato dhammaṃ sa- gato saṅghaṃ sa- gato kathaṃ vinipātam gaccheyya || ||

6. Yañ hi tam Mahānama sammāvadamāno vadeyya Dīgharattam upāsako buddham- dhammam- saṅghaṃ- saraṇaṃ gato ti[^375-9] || Sarakānisakkaṃ sammāvadamāno vadeyya || ||


<!-- PTS Page 376 -->
<a id="page-376"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 376]*

Sarakāni Mahānāma sakko dīgharattam upāsako Buddhaṃdhammaṃ- saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gato so kathaṃ vinipātam gaccheyya || ||

7. Idha Mahānāma ekacco puggalo Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || Dhamme || Saṅghe || || Hāsapañño[^376-1] javanapañño vimuttiyā ca samannāgato || || So āsavanānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttim paññāvimuttim diṭṭheva dhamme sayam abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharati || Ayam pi kho Mahānāma puggalo parimutto nirayā || parimutto tiracchānayoniyā || parimutto pittivisayā || parimutto apāyaduggativinipātā || ||

8. Idha pana[^376-2] Mahānāma ekacco puggalo Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || Dhamme || Saṅghe || hāsapañño[^376-1] javanapañño na ca vimuttiyā samannāgato || || So pañcannam orambhāgiyānam saṃyojanānaṃ parikkhayā opapātiko hoti tattha parinibbāyī[^376-3] anāvattidhammo[^376-4] asmā[^376-5] lokā || || Ayam pi kho Mahānāma puggalo parimutto nirayā parimutto tiracchāyoniyā || parimutto pittivisayā || parimutto apāyaduggati vinipātā || ||

9. Idha pana Mahānāma ekacco puggalo Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || Dhamme || Saṅghe || na[^376-2] hāsapañño[^376-6] na javanapañño na ca[^376-7] vimuttiyā samannāgato || so tiṇṇam saṃyojanānam parikkhayā rāgadosamohānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāmi[^376-8] hoti sakid[^376-9] eva imaṃ lokam āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ karoti || || Ayam pi kho Mahānāma puggalo parimutto nirayā parimutto tiracchānayoniyā || parimutto pittivisayā parimutto apāyaduggativinipātā[^376-10] || ||


<!-- PTS Page 377 -->
<a id="page-377"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 377]*

10. Idha pana Mahānāma ekacco puggalo Buddhe aveccappasadena- || Dhamme || Saṅghe || na hāsapañño na[^377-1] javanapañño na[^377-2] vimuttiyā samannāgato || so tiṇṇaṃ samyojanānam parikkhayā sotāpanno hoti avinipātadhammo sambodhiparāyano ti || || Ayam pi kho Mahānāma puggalo parimutto nirayā parimutto tiracchānayoniyā[^377-3] parimutto pittivisayā parimutto apāyaduggativinipātā || ||

11. Idha pana Mahānāma ekacco puggalo na heva kho Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || na dhamme || na[^377-4] {saṅghe} || na hāsapañño na javanapañño na ca vimuttiyā samannāgato || api cassa ime dhammā honti || saddhindriyaṃ viriyindriyaṃ satindriyaṃ[^377-5] samādhindriyaṃ paññindriyaṃ || tathāgatapaveditā[^377-6] cassa dhammā paññāya mattaso nijjhānaṃ khamanti[^377-7] || || Ayam pi kho Mahānāma puggalo agantā[^377-8] nirayaṃ agantā[^377-9] tiracchānayoniṃ[^377-10] agantā pittivisayam[^377-11] agantā[^377-12] apāyaduggativinipātaṃ[^377-13] || ||

12. Idha pana Mahānāma ekacco puggalo na heva kho Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || na Dhamme || na Saṅghe || na hāsapañño na javanapañño || na ca vimuttiyā samannāgato || api cassa dhammā honti saddhindriyaṃ || la-pe || paññindriyaṃ || Tathāgate cassa[^377-14] saddhāmattam hoti pemamattaṃ || || Ayam pi kho puggalo agantā[^377-15] nirayam agantā tiracchānayonim agantā pittivisayaṃ[^377-16] agantā[^377-17] apāyaduggativinipātaṃ || ||

13. Ime ce pi Mahānāma mahāsālā subhāsitaṃ dubbhāsitam ājāneyyuṃ[^377-18] || ime cāham[^377-19] mahāsāle vyākareyyaṃ Sotāpannā avinipātadhammā niyatā sambodhiparāyanā ti || kimaṅga[^377-20] pana[^377-21] Sarakāniṃ[^377-22] sakkaṃ || Sarakāni Mahānāma sakko maraṇakāle sikkhaṃ samādiyī ti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 378 -->
<a id="page-378"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 378]*

SN_5,55(11).25. (5) Sarakāni or Saraṇāni 2.

1. Kapilavatthunidānaṃ[^378-1] || ||

2. Tena kho pana samayena Sarakāni- || ||

3. Tatra sudaṃ sambahulā sakkā- -Etthadaniko na[^378-2] sotāpanno bhavissati || -sambodhisamparāyano ti || || Sarakāni sakko sikkhāya aparipūrakārī ahosī ti || ||

4. Atha kho Mahānāmo -etad avoca || ||

5. Idha bhante Sarakāni sakko- -etthadāniko na sotapanno bhavissati- -sambodhiparāyano ti || || Sarakāni sakko sikkhāya aparipūrakārī ahosī ti || || Yo so Mahānama- -kathaṃ vinipātam gaccheyya || ||

6. Yañ hi tam Mahānama sammāvadamano- -so katham vinipātaṃ gaccheyya || ||

7. Idha Mahānāma ekacco puggalo Buddhe ekantagato[^378-3] hoti abhippasanno[^378-4] || Iti pi so Bhagavā- -parimutto apāyaduggativinipātā || ||

8. Idha pana[^378-5] Mahānāma ekacco puggalo Buddhe ekantagato hoti abhippasanno || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussāṇam buddho bhagavā ti || Dhamme || Saṅghe || || Hāsapañño javanapañño vimuttiyā ca samannāgato || so pañcannam orambhāgiyānam parikkhayā antarāparinibbāyī hoti || upahaccaparinibbāyī hoti || {sasaṅkhārapavirinibbāyī}[^378-6] hoti || asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī[^378-7] hoti || uddhaṃsoto hoti akaniṭṭhagāmī || || Ayam pi kho Mahānāma puggalo parimutto nirayā || pe || parimutto apāyaduggativinipātā || ||

9. Idha pana Mahānāma ekacco puggalo Buddhe ekantagato hoti abhippasanno || Iti pi so Bhagavā || Dhamme || {Saṅghe} || na hāsapañño na javanapañño na ca vimuttiyā samannāgato || so[^378-8] tiṇṇaṃ saṃyojanānam parikkhayā rāgadosamohānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāmī hoti sakid eva imaṃ lokam āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ karoti || ayam pi kho Mahā-


<!-- PTS Page 379 -->
<a id="page-379"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 379]*

nāma puggalo parimutto nirayā || pe || parimutto apāyaduggativinipātā || ||

10. Idha pana Mahānama ekacco puggalo Buddhe ekantagato[^379-1] hoti abhippasanno || Iti pi so Bhagavā ti || pe || Dhamme || {Saṅghe} || na hāsapañño na javanapañño na ca vimuttiyā samannāgato[^379-2] || so tiṇṇam saṃyojanānam parikkhayā sotapanno hoti avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano || ayam pi kho Mahānāma puggalo parimutto nirayā || pe || parimutto apāyaduggativinipātā || ||

11. Idha pana Mahānāma ekacco puggalo Buddhe na heva kho Buddhe ekantagato hoti abhippasanno || na Dhamme || na {Saṅghe} || na hāsa- || api cassa ime dhammā honti saddhindriyam || la || pe || paññindriyaṃ || -nijjhānaṃ khamanti || ayam pi kho Mahānāma puggalo agantā[^379-3] nirayaṃ- -apāyaduggativinipātaṃ[^379-4] || ||

12. Idha pana Mahānāma ekacco puggalo na heva kho Buddhe[^379-5] ekantagato hoti abhippasanno[^379-6] || na Dhamme || na {Saṅghe}- || -na ca vimuttiyā samannāgato || api cassa ime dhammā honti || saddhindriyaṃ || la || pe || paññindriyaṃ || Tathāgate cassa saddhāmattam hoti pemamattaṃ || ayam pi kho Mahānāma ayantā- || -apāyaduggatinininipātaṃ[^379-7] || ||

13. Seyyathāpi Mahānāma dukkhettaṃ dubbhūmi[^379-8] avihatakhānukam bījāni cassu[^379-9] khaṇḍāni pūtini vātātapahatām asārāni[^379-10] asukhasayitāni[^379-11] || devo ca[^379-12] na[^379-5] sammādhāram anupaveccheyya[^379-13] || || Api nu tāni bījāni vuddhiṃ viruḷhiṃ vepullam āpajjeyyum tī || || No hetam bhante || || Evam eva kho Mahānama idha dhammo dvākkhāto hoti duppavedito anīyāniko anupasamasaṃvattanike[^379-14] asammāsambuddhappavedito || idam aham[^379-15] dukkhettasmiṃ vadāmi ||


<!-- PTS Page 380 -->
<a id="page-380"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 380]*

tasmiṃ ca dhamme sāvako viharati dhammānudhammapaṭipanno sāmīcipaṭipanno anudhammacārī idam ahaṃ dubbījasmiṃ vadāmi || ||

14. Seyyathāpi Mahānāma sukhettaṃ subhūmi[^380-1] suvihatakhāṇukaṃ bījāni cassu[^380-2] akhaṇḍāni apūtīni avātātapahatāni sārāni[^380-3] sukhasahitāni[^380-4] || devo ca sammādhāram[^380-5] anupaveccheyya[^380-6] || || Api nu tāni bījāni vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vepullam āpajjeyyun ti || || Evam bhante || || Evam eva kho Mahānāma idha dhammo svākkhāto hoti supavedito nīyāniko upasamasaṃvattaniko sammāsambuddhapavedito idam ahaṃ sukhettasmiṃ vadāmi || tasmiṃ ca dhamme sāvako viharati dhammānudhammapaṭipanno sāmīcipaṭipanno anudhammacārī idam aham[^380-7] subījasmiṃ vadāmi[^380-8] || {kimaṅga}[^380-9] pana Sarakānisakkaṃ[^380-10] || Sarakāni Mahānāma sakko maraṇakāle sikkhāya paripūrakarī ahosī ti || ||

SN_5,55(11).26. (6) Dussilya1 or Anāthapiṇḍika1.

1. [^380-11] Sāvatthi nidānaṃ || ||

2. Tena kho pana samayena Anāthapiṇḍiko gahapati ābādhiko hoti dukkhito bāḷhagilāno || ||

3. Atha kho Anāthapiṇḍiko gahapati aññataram purisam āmantesi || Ehi[^380-12] tvam ambho purisa yenāyasmā Sāriputto tenupasaṅkama[^380-13] || upasaṅkamitvā mama vacanena āyasmato Sāriputtassa pāde sirasā vanda || Anāthapiṇḍiko bhante[^380-11] gahapati ābādhiko dukkhito bāḷhagilāno || so āyasmato Sāriputtassa pāde sirasā vandatī ti || evañca vadeti || || Sādhu kira bhante āyasmā Sāriputto yena Anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa nivesanam tenupasaṅkamatu anukampam upādāyā ti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 381 -->
<a id="page-381"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 381]*

Evam bhante ti kho so puriso Anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa patissutvā yenāyasmā Sāriputto tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ Sāriputtam abhivādetvā ekam antaṃ nisīdi || ||

4. Ekam antaṃ nisinno kho so puriso āyasmantaṃ Sāriputtam etad avoca || || Anāthapiṇḍiko bhante gahapati ābā- duk- bāla- || so āyasmato Sāriputtassa pāde si- van- || evañca vadeti[^381-1] || Sādhu kira bhante āy- Sāriputto yena Anā- gahapatissa nivesanaṃ tenupasaṅkamatu anukampam upādāyā ti || || Adhivāsesi kho āy- Sāriputto tuṃhībhāvena || ||

5. Atha kho āy- Sāriputto pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya āyasmatā Ānandena pacchā samaṇena yena Anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa nivesananaṃ tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā paññatte āsane nisīdi || || Nisajja kho āyasmā Sāriputto Anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatim etad avoca || || Kacci te gahapati khamanīyaṃ kacci yāpanīyaṃ kacci[^381-2] dukkhā vedanā patikkamanti no abhikkamati paṭikkamo sānam paññāyati no abhikkamo[^381-3] ti || || Na me bhante khamanīyaṃ na yāpanīyaṃ bāḷhā me dukkhā vedanā abhikkamanti no paṭikkamanti abhikkamo[^381-4] sānam paññāyati no paṭikkamo ti || ||

6. Yathārūpena kho gahapati Buddhe appasādena samannāgato assutavā puthujjano kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ[^381-5] duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayam upapajjati[^381-6] || tathārūpo[^381-7] te Buddhe appasādo natthi atthi ca[^381-8] kho te[^381-9] gahapati Buddhe aveccappasādo || Iti pi so Bhagavā || lape || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || || Tañ ca pana te Buddhe aveccappasādam attani samanupassato ṭhānaso vedanā paṭipassambheyyuṃ[^381-10] || ||

7. Yathārūpena kho gahapati dhamme appasādena samannāgato assutavā puthujjano kāyassa bhedā param


<!-- PTS Page 382 -->
<a id="page-382"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 382]*

maraṇā apā- dug- vini- nirayam upapajjati || tathārūpo[^382-1] te dhamme appasādo natthi || atthi[^382-2] ca kho te gahapati dhamme aveccappasādo || Svākkhāto Bhagavatā dhammo || la-pe || paccattaṃ veditabbo viññūhīti || || Tañ ca pana te dhamme aveccappasādam attani samanapassato ṭhānaso vedanā paṭippassambheyyuṃ[^382-3] || ||

8. Yathārūpena kho gahapati saṅghe appasādena samannāgato assutavā puthujjano kāyassa bhedā param maraṇā apā- dug- vi- nirayam uppajjati || tathārūpo te saṅghe[^382-4] appasādo natthi || atthi ca[^382-5] kho te[^382-5] gahapati saṅghe aveccappasādo || Supaṭipanno Bhagavato sāvakasaṅgho || la-pe || anuttaram puññakkhettaṃ lokassā ti || || Tañ ca pana te saṅghe aveccappasādam attani samanupassato ṭhānaso vedanā paṭippassambheyyuṃ[^382-6] || ||

9. Yathārūpena kho gahapati dussīlyena[^382-7] samannāgato assutavā puthujjano kāyassa bhedā param maraṇā apādug- vini- nirayam uppajjati || tathārupan[^382-8] te dussīlyaṃ natthi || atthi ca kho te gahapati ariyakantāni sīlāni[^382-9] || lape || samādhisaṃvattanikāni || || Tāni ca pane te ariyakantāni sīlāni attani samanupassato ṭhānaso vedanā paṭipassambheyyuṃ[^382-3] || ||

10. Yathārupāya kho gahapati micchādiṭṭhiyā samannāgato assutavā puthujjano kāyassa bhedā pa- ma- apā- dugvi- nirayam uppajjati || tathārūpā[^382-10] te micchādiṭṭhi natthi || atthi ca kho te gahapati sammādiṭṭhi || || Tañ ca pana te sammādiṭṭhim attani samanupassato thānaso vedanā passambheyyuṃ[^382-3] || ||

11. Yathārūpena kho gahapati micchāsaṅkappena samannāgato assutavā puthujjano kāyassa bhedā- -nirayam uppajjati || tathārūpo te {micchāsaṅkappo} natthi || atthi ca kho te gahapati sammāsaṅkappo || || Tañ ca pana te {sammāsaṅkappam} attani samanupassato ṭhānaso vedanā paṭipassambheyyuṃ[^382-3] || ||


<!-- PTS Page 383 -->
<a id="page-383"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 383]*

12. Yathārupāya kho gahapati micchāvācāya samannāgato assutavā puthujjano kāyassa bhedā- -nirayam upapajjati || tathārūpā te micchāvācā natthi || atthi ca kho te gahapati sammāvācā || || Tañ ca pana te sammāvācam[^383-1] attani samanupassato ṭhānaso vedanā paṭipassambheyyuṃ || ||

13. Yathārūpena kho gahapati micchākammantena samannāgato assutavā puthujjano kāyassa bhedā- -nirayam upapajjati || tathārūpo[^383-2] te micchākammanto natthi || atthi ca kho te gahapati sammākammanto[^383-3] || tañ ca pana te sammākammantam attani samanupassato ṭhānaso vedanā paṭipassambheyyuṃ[^383-4] || ||

14. Yathārūpena kho gahapati micchā-ājīvena samannāgato assutavā puthujjano kāyassa bhedā- -nirayam upapajjati ti || tathārūpo te micchā-ājīvo natthi || atthi ca kho te gahapati sammā-ājīvo || || Tañ ca pana te sammā-ājīvam attani samanupassato ṭhānaso vedanā paṭipassambheyyuṃ[^383-5] || ||

15. Yathārūpena kho gahapati micchāvāyāmena samannāgato assutavā puthujjano kāyassa bhedā- -nirayam uppajjati || tathārūpo te micchāvāyāmo natthi || atthi ca kho te gahapati sammāvāyāmo || || Tañ ca pana te sammāvāyāmam attani samanupassato ṭhanaso vedanā paṭipassambheyyuṃ[^383-6] || ||

16. Yathārūpāya kho gahapati micchāsatiyā samannāgato assutavā puthujjano kāyassa bhedā- -nirayam upapajjati || tathārupā te micchāsati natthi || atthi ca kho te gahapati sammāsati || etañ ca pana[^383-7] te[^383-8] sammāsatim attani[^383-9] samanupassato ṭhānaso vedanā paṭipassambheyyuṃ[^383-10] || ||

17. Yathārūpena kho gahapati micchāsamādhinā samannāgato assutavā puthujjano kāyassa bhedā- -nirayam upapajjati || tathārūpo te micchāsamādhi natthi || || Atthi ca kho te gahapati sammāsamādhi || || Tañ ca pana te sammāsamādhim attani samanupassato ṭhānaso vedanā paṭipassambheyyuṃ[^383-10] || ||


<!-- PTS Page 384 -->
<a id="page-384"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 384]*

18. Yathārūpena[^384-1] kho gahapati micchāñāṇena samannāgato assutavā puthujjano kāyassa bhedā- -nirayam upapajjati || tathārūpaṃ te micchāñāṇaṃ natthi || || Atthi ca kho te gahapati sammāñāṇam || || Tañ ca pana te sammāñānam attani samanupassato ṭhānaso vedanā paṭipassambheyyuṃ[^384-2] || ||

19. Yathārūpāya kho gahapati micchāvimuttiyā {samannāgato} assutavā puthujjano kāyassa bhedā- -nirayam upapajjati || tathārūpā te micchāvimutti natthi || || Atthi ca kho te gahapati sammāvimutti || tañ ca pana te sammāvimuttim[^384-3] attani samanupassato ṭhānaso vedanā paṭippassambheyyunti[^384-4] || ||

20. Atha kho Anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa ṭhānaso vedanā paṭipassambhiṃsu[^384-5] || ||

21. Atha kho Anāthapiṇḍiko gahapati āyasmantaṃ ca Sāriputtam āyasmantaṃ ca Ānandaṃ sakeneva thālipākena parivisi || ||

22. Atha kho Anāthapiṇḍiko gahapati upasaṅkamitvā[^384-6] āyasmantaṃ Sāriputtam bhuttāviṃ onitapattapānim aññataraṃ nīcam āsanaṃ gahetvā ekam antaṃ nisīdi || ||

23. Ekam antaṃ nisinnaṃ kho Anāthapiṇḍikam gahapatim āyasmā Sāriputto imāhi gāthāhi anumodi || || Yassa saddhā[^384-7] Tathāgate || acalā supatiṭṭhitā || || sīlañca yassa[^384-8] kalyāṇaṃ || ariyakantam pasaṃsitaṃ ||[^384-1]|| Saṅghe pasādo yassatthi || ujubhūtañ ca dassanaṃ || Adaliddo ti tam āhu || amoghaṃ tassa jīvitaṃ ||[^384-2]|| Tasmā saddhaṃ[^384-9] ca sīlañca || pasādaṃ dhammadassanaṃ || anuyuñjetha medhāvī || saram[^384-10] buddhānasāsanan ti ||[^384-3]||

24. Atha kho āyasmā Sāriputto Anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatim imāhi gāthāhi anumoditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pakkāmi || ||


<!-- PTS Page 385 -->
<a id="page-385"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 385]*

25. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antaṃ nisīdi || || Ekam antaṃ nisinnaṃ kho āyasmantam Ānandam Bhagavā etad avoca || ||

26. Handa kuto nu tvam Ānanda āgacchasi divādivassā ti || || Āyasmatā bhante Sāriputtena Anāthapiṇḍiko gahapati[^385-1] iminā ca iminā ca ovādena ovadito ti[^385-2] || || Paṇḍito Ānanda Sāriputto mahāpañño Ānanda Sāriputto || || Yatra hi nāma cattāri sotāpattiyaṅgāni dasahi ākarehi[^385-3] vibhajissatī ti || ||

SN_5,55(11).27. (7) Dussīlyam2 or Anāthapiṇḍika2.

1. Sāvatthinidānaṃ[^385-4] || ||

2. Tena kho pana samayena Anāthapiṇḍiko gahapati ābādhiko hoti dukkhito bāḷhagilāno || ||

3.4. Atha kho Anāthapiṇḍiko gahapati aññataram purisam āmantesi || || Ehi tvam ambho purisa yenāyasmā Ānando tenupasaṅkama[^385-5] || upasaṅkamitvā mama vacanena āyasmato Ānandassa pāde sirasā vanda || pe[^385-6] || ||

5. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando pubbaṇhasamayaṃ[^385-7] nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya Anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa nivesanam tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā paññatte āsane nisīdi || nisajja kho āyasmā Ānando Anāthapiṇḍīkaṃ gahapatim etad avoca || || Kacci te gahapati khamanīyam kacci yāpanīyaṃ kacci dukkhā vedanā paṭikkamanti no abhikkamanti paṭikkamo sānam paññāyati no abhikkamo[^385-8] ti || || Na me bhante khamanīyaṃ na yāpanīyaṃ[^385-9] bāḷhā me dukkhā vedanā abhikkamanti no paṭikkamanti abhikkamo sānam paññāyati no patikkamo ti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 386 -->
<a id="page-386"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 386]*

6. Catuhi kho gahapati dhammehi samannāgatassa assutavato puthujjanassa hoti uttāso hoti chambhitattaṃ hoti samparāyikam[^386-1] maraṇabhayaṃ || katamehi catuhi || ||

7. Idha gahapati assutavā puthujjano buddhe appasādena samannāgato hoti || tañ ca panassa Buddhe appasādam attani samanupassato hoti uttāso hoti chambhitattaṃ hoti samparāyikam maraṇabhayam || || Puna ca param gahapati assutavā puthujjano Dhamme appasādena samannāgato hoti || tañ ca panassa dhamme appasādam attani samanupassato hoti uttāso hoti chambhitattaṃ hoti samparāyikam maraṇabhayaṃ || || Puna ca paraṃ gahapati assutavā puthujjano Saṅghe appasādena samannāgato hoti || tañ ca panassa saṅghe appasādam attani samanupassato hoti uttāso hoti chambhitattam hoti samparāyikam maraṇabhayam || || Puna ca param gahapati assutavā puthujjano dussīlyena[^386-2] samannāgato hoti || tañ ca panassa dussīlyam attani samanupassato hoti uttāso hoti chambhitattaṃ hoti samparāyikam maraṇabhayaṃ || || Imehi kho gahapati catuhi dhammehi samannāgatassa assutavato puthujjanassa hoti uttāso hoti chambhitattam hoti samparāyikam maraṇabhayaṃ || ||

8. Catuhi kho gahapati dhammehi samannāgatassa sutavato ariyasāvakassa na hoti uttāso na hoti chambhitattam na hoti samparāyikam maraṇabhayaṃ || katamehi catuhi || || Idha[^386-3] gahapati sutavā ariyasāvako Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || tañ ca panassa buddhe aveccappasādam attani samanupassato na hoti uttāso na hoti chambhitattaṃ na hoti samparāyikam maraṇabhayaṃ || || Puna ca paraṃ gahapati sutavā ariyasāvako Dhamme || [pe] [^386-4] || {Saṅghe} [pe] [^386-4] || Ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato hoti akaṇḍehi || la-pe || samādhisaṃvattanikehi || tāni[^386-5] ca panassa ariyakantāni sīlāni attani samanupassato


<!-- PTS Page 387 -->
<a id="page-387"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 387]*

na hoti uttāso na hoti chambhitattaṃ na hoti samparāyikam maraṇabhayaṃ || || Imehi kho gahapati catuhi dhammehi samannāgatassa sutavato ariyasāvakassa na hoti uttāso na hoti chambhitattaṃ na hoti samparāyikam maraṇabhayan ti || ||

9. Nāham bhante Ānanda bhāyāmi || kyāham bhāyissāmi || ahañhi bhante Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato homi[^387-1] || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānaṃ buddha bhagavā ti || Dhamme || pe || {Saṅghe} || pe || Yāni cimāni[^387-2] bhante Bhagavatā gihisāmīcikāni[^387-3] sikkhāpadāni desitāni[^387-4] nāhaṃ tesaṃ kiñci attani khaṇḍaṃ samanupassāmī ti || ||

10. Lābhā te gahapati || suladdhan te gahapati || sotāpattiphalam tayā gahapati vyākatan ti || ||

SN_5,55(11).28. (8) Duveram or Anāthapiṇḍika3.

1. [^387-5] Sāvatthi nidānaṃ || ||

2. Atha kho Anāthapiṇḍiko gahapati yena Bhagavā tenupa- || upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antaṃ nisīdi[^387-6] || || Ekam antaṃ nisinnaṃ kho Anāthapiṇḍikam Bhagavā etad avoca || ||

3. Yato[^387-7] kho gahapati ariyasāvakassa pañcabhayāni[^387-8] verāni vūpasantāni ca[^387-1] honti || catuhi ca sotāpattiyaṅgehi samannāgato hoti || ariyo cassa ñāyo paññāya sudiṭṭho[^387-9] hoti supaṭividdho || so ākaṅkhamāno[^387-10] attanā va attānaṃ vyākareyya Khīṇanirayo mhi khīṇatiracchānayoniyo khīṇapittivisayo[^387-11] khīṇāpāyaduggativinipāto sotāpanno ham asmi avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano || || Katamāni pañcabhayāni verāni vūpasantāni honti || ||

4. Yaṃ gahapati pāṇātipātī pāṇātipātapaccayā diṭṭhadhammikam pi bhayaṃ veram pasavati samparāyikam pi[^387-1]


<!-- PTS Page 388 -->
<a id="page-388"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 388]*

bhayaṃ veram pasavati[^388-1] cetasikam pi dukkhaṃ domanassam paṭisaṃvediyati || pāṇātipātā paṭiviratassa evan tam[^388-2] bhayam veraṃ vūpasantaṃ hoti || || Yaṃ gahapati adinnādāyī || || Yaṃ gahapati kāmesu micchācārī || || Yaṃ gahapati musāvādī[^388-3] || || Yaṃ gahapati surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhāyī surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānapaccayā diṭṭhadhammikam pi bhayaṃ veram pasavati samparāyikam pi bhayaṃ veram pasavati cetasikam pi dukkhaṃ domanassam paṭisaṃvediyati || surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratassa evan tam[^388-2] bhayam veram vūpasantaṃ hoti || || Imāni pañcabhayāni verāni vūpasantāni honti || ||

5. Katamehi catuhi sotāpattiyaṅgehi samannāgato hoti || || Idha gahapati ariyasāvako Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || || Dhamme || {Saṅghe} || Ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato hoti akhaṇḍehi || la-pe || samādhisaṃvattanikehi || || Imehi catuhi sotāpattiyaṅgehi samannāgato hoti || ||

6. Katamo cassa ariyo ñāyo paññāya sudiṭṭho hoti supaṭividdho || || Idha gahapati ariyasāvako paṭiccasamuppādam yeva sādhukam yoniso manasi karoti || Iti imasmiṃ sati idaṃ hoti || imassuppadā idam uppajjati || Iti[^388-4] imasmim asati idaṃ na hoti || imassa nirodhā idam nirujjhati yad idam avijjapaccayā saṅkhārā || saṅkhārapaccayā viññānaṃ || la[^388-5] || || Evam etassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti || || Avijjāya tveva asesavirāganirodhā[^388-6] saṅkhāranirodho[^388-7] || la[^388-6] || || Evam etassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa nirodho hoti[^388-8] || ||


<!-- PTS Page 389 -->
<a id="page-389"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 389]*

Ayam assa ariyo ñāyo paññāya sudiṭṭho hoti supaṭividdho || ||

7. Yato kho gahapati ariyasāvakassa imāni pañcabhayāni verāni vūpasantāni honti || imehi catuhi sotāpattiyaṅgehi samannāgato hoti || ayaṃ cassa ariyo ñāyo paññāya sudiṭṭho hoti supaṭividdho || so ākaṅkhamāno attanā va attānaṃ vyākareyya Khīṇanirayo mhi khīṇatiracchānayoniyo khīṇapittivisayo[^389-1] khīnāpāyaduggativinipāto sotāpanno ham asmi[^389-2] avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano ti || ||

SN_5,55(11).29. (9) Bhayam or Bhikkhu.

1. [^389-3] Sāvatthinidānam || ||

2. [^389-4] Atha kho sambahulā bhikkhū yena Bhagavā tenupa- || upasaṅkamitvā || pe || ekam antam nisīdiṃsu || || Ekam antaṃ nisinnā[^389-5] kho te bhikkhū Bhagavā etad avoca || || 3--6. ||pe || la || ||

7. Yato kho bhikkhave ariyasāvakassa imāni pañcabhayāni verāni vūpasantāni honti || imehi catuhi sotāpattiyaṅgehi samannāgato hoti || ayaṃ cassa ariyo ñāyo paññāya sudiṭṭho hoti supaṭividdho || || So ākaṅkamāno attanā va attānaṃ vyākareyya Khīṇanirayo mhi khīṇatiracchānayoniyo khīṇapittivisayo[^389-1] khīṇāpāyaduggativinipāto sotāpanno ham asmi avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano ti || ||

SN_5,55(11).30. (10) Licchavi or Nandaka.

1. Ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Vesāliyaṃ viharati Mahāvane Kuṭāgārasālāyaṃ || ||

2. Atha kho Nandako licchavimahāmatto yena Bhagavā {tenupasaṅkami} || upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antaṃ nisīdi || || Ekam antaṃ nisinnaṃ kho Nandakaṃ[^389-6] licchavimahāmattam Bhagavā etad avoca || || Catuhi kho Nandaka dhammehi[^389-7] samannāgato ariyasāvako sotā-


<!-- PTS Page 390 -->
<a id="page-390"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 390]*

panno hoti avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano[^390-1] || katamehi catuhi || ||

3. Idha Nandaka[^390-2] ariyasāvako Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || Dhamme || {Saṅghe} || Ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato hoti akhaṇḍehi || la-pe || samādhisaṃvattanikehi || || Imehi kho Nandaka catuhi dhammehi samannāgato ariyasāvako sotāpanno hoti avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano[^390-1] || ||

4. Imehi ca pana Nandaka catuhi dhammehi samannāgato ariyasāvako āyunā samyutto hoti dibbena pi mānussena pi || vaṇṇena saṃyutto hoti || pe || sukhena saṃyutto hoti[^390-3] || yasena saṃyutto hoti[^390-3] || adhipateyyena samyutto hoti dibbena pi mānussena pi || ||

5. Taṃ kho panāhaṃ Nandaka nāññassa samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā sutvā vadāmi || api ca yad eva mayā sāmaṃ ñātaṃ sāmaṃ diṭṭhaṃ sāmaṃ viditaṃ tad evāhaṃ vadāmī ti || ||

6. Evam vutte aññataro puriso Nandakaṃ licchaviṃ mahāmattaṃ[^390-4] etad avoca || Nahānakālo bhante ti || || Alaṃ dāni bhaṇe etena bāhirena nahānena alam idam ajjhattaṃ[^390-5] nahānaṃ bhavissati yad idam Bhagavati pasādo ti || || Sarakānivaggo[^390-6] tatiyo || Tassa[^390-7] uddānaṃ || || Mahānāmena dve vuttā Godhā ca Saraṇā duve. Dussīlyena dve vuttā || || Duverena Bhayena ca || Licchavidasamo vutto || Vaggo tena pavuccatī ti[^390-8] || ||

##### *[PTS Page 391]*

CHAPTER IV. PUÑÑABHISANDAVAGGO CATUTTHO.

SN_5,55(11).31. (1) Abhisanda1.

1. [^391-1] Sāvatthinidānaṃ || ||

2. Cattāro me bhikkhave puññābhisandā kusalābhisandā[^391-2] sukhassāhārā || katame cattāro || ||

3. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || ayam pathamo puññābhisando kusalābhisando sukhassāhāro || ||

4. Puna ca param bhikkhave ariyasāvako Dhamme aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || Svākkhāto Bhagavatā dhammo || la-pe || paccattaṃ veditabbo viññūhī ti || ayam dutiyo puññābhisando kusalābhisando[^391-3] sakhassāhāro || ||

5. Puna ca param bhikkhave ariyasāvako saṅghe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || supaṭipanno Bhagavato sāvakasaṅgho || pa-pe || anuttaram puññakkhettaṃ lokassā ti || ayam tatiyo puññābhisando kusalābhisando sukhassā hāro || ||

6. Puna ca param bhikkhave ariyasāvako Ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato hoti akhaṇḍehi || la-pe || samādhisaṃvattanikehi || ayaṃ catuttho puññābhisando[^391-4] kusalābhisandosukhassāhāro || ||

7. Ime kho bhikkhave cattāro puññābhisandā[^391-5] kusalābhisandā {ukhassāhārā}[^391-6] ti || ||

SN_5,55(11).32. (2) Abhisanda2.

2. Cattāro me bhikkhave puññābhisandā kusalābhisandā sukhassāhārā || katame cattāro || ||

SN_5,55(11).34. (4) Devapada1.

1. [^392-5] Sāvatthinidānaṃ || ||

2. Cattārimāni bhikkhave devānaṃ devapadāni avisuddhānam sattānam visuddhiyā apariyodātānaṃ sattānaṃ pariyodapanāya || || Katamāni cattāri || ||

3. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || || Idam pathamaṃ devānam devapadam avisuddhānaṃ sattānaṃ visuddhiyā apariyodātānaṃ sattānam pariyodapanāya || ||


<!-- PTS Page 393 -->
<a id="page-393"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 393]*

4--6. Puna ca bhikkhave ariyasāvako Dhamme || {Saṅghe}[^393-1] || Ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato hoti akhaṇḍehi || la-pe || samādhisaṃvattanikehi || || Idaṃ catutthaṃ devānaṃ devapadam avisuddhānaṃ[^393-2] sattānaṃ visuddhiyā apariyodātānaṃ sattānam pariyodapanāya || ||

7. Imāni kho bhikkhave cattāri devānam devapadāni avisuddhānaṃ sattānaṃ visuddhiyā apariyodātānaṃ sattānam pariyodapanāyā ti || ||

SN_5,55(11).35. (5) Devapadam2.

2. Cattārimani bhikkhave devānaṃ devapadāni avisuddhānaṃ sattānaṃ visuddhiyā apariyodātānaṃ sattānam pariyodapanāya || katamāni cattāri || ||

3. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavāti || || So iti paṭisañcikkhati || Kiṃ nu kho devānaṃ devapadan ti || so evam pajānati[^393-3] || Abyāpajjha[^393-4] parame khvāham etarahi deve suṇāmi na ca[^393-5] kho panāham kiñci byābādhemi tasaṃ vā thāvaraṃ vā || addhāhaṃ devapadadhammasamannāgato viharāmī ti || || Idam pathamaṃ devānam devapadam avisuddhānaṃ sattānaṃ visuddhiyā apariyodātānaṃ sattānaṃ {pariyodapanāya} || ||

4.5. Puna ca param bhikkhave ariyasāvako Dhamme || {Saṅghe} || ||

6. Ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato hoti akhaṇḍehi || lape || samādhisaṃvattanikehi || || So iti paṭisañcikkhati || Kiṃ nu kho devānaṃ devapadan ti || || So evam pajānāti abyāpajjha[^393-6] parame khvāhaṃ[^393-7] etarahi deve[^393-8] suṇāmi na kho panāhaṃ kiñci vyābādhemi tasaṃ vā thāvaraṃ vā || addhāham[^393-9] devapadadhammasamannāgato[^393-10] vihārāmī ti || || Idaṃ

catutthaṃ devānam devapadam avisuddhānam sattānaṃ[^394-1] visuddhiyā apariyodātānaṃ sattānam {pariyodapanāya} || ||

7. Imāni kho bhikkhave cattāri devānam devapadāni avisuddhānam sattānaṃ visuddhiyā apariyodātānaṃ sattānam pariyodapanāyā ti || ||

SN_5,55(11).36. (6) Sabhāgatam.

2. Catuhi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgataṃ[^394-2] attamanā[^394-3] devā sabhāgataṃ[^394-4] kathenti || || Katamehi catuhi || ||

3. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānaṃ buddho bhagavā ti || || Yā tā devatā Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgatā[^394-5] ito cutā[^394-6] tatrūpapannā || tāsam evaṃ hoti || Yathārūpena kho mayam Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgatā[^394-7] tato cutā idhūpapannā || ariyasāvako[^394-8] pi tathārūpena[^394-9] Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti[^394-10] Ehī ti devānaṃ santike ti || ||

4.5. Puna ca param bhikkhave ariyasāvako Dhamme || {Saṅghe} || ||

6. Ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato hoti akhaṇḍehi || lape || samādhisaṃvattanikehi || || Yā tā[^394-11] devatā ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgatā[^394-12] ito cutā tatrūpapannā || tāsam evaṃ hoti || || Yatharūpehi kho mayam ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgatā tato cutā idhūpapannā ariyasāvako pi tathārūpehi[^394-13] ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato Ehī ti devānaṃ santike ti || ||

7. Imehi kho bhikkhave catuhi dhammehi samannāgatam attamanā[^394-14] devā sabhāgataṃ kathentī ti[^394-15] || ||

<a id="page-395"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 395]*

SN_5,55(11).37. (7) Mahānāmo.

1. Ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Sakkesu viharati Kapilavatthusmiṃ Nigrodhārāme || ||

2. Atha kho Mahānāmo sakko yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkami || pe || Ekam antaṃ nisinno kho Mahānāmo sakko Bhagavantam etad avoca || ||

3. Kittāvatā nu kho bhante upāsako hotī ti || || Yato kho Mahānāma Buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gato hoti || dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gato hoti || saṅghaṃ saraṇam gato hoti || ettāvatā kho Mahānāma upasako hotī ti || ||

4. Kittāvatā[^395-1] pana bhante upāsako sīlasampanno hotī ti || || Yato kho Mahānāma upāsako pāṇātipātā paṭivirato hoti || adinnādāna paṭivirato hoti || kāmesu micchācārā paṭivirato hoti || musāvadā paṭivirato hoti || surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānā paṭivirato[^395-2] hoti || ettāvatā kho Mahānāma upāsako sīlasampanno hotī ti || ||

5. Kittāvatā pana bhante upāsako saddhāsampanno hotī ti || || Idha Mahānāma upāsako saddho hoti saddahati Tathāgatassa bodhim || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || || Ettāvatā kho Mahānāma upāsako saddhāsamannāgato hotī ti || ||

6. Kittāvatā pana bhante upāsako cāgasampanno hotī ti || || Idha Mahānāma upāsako vigatamalamaccherena cetasā agāram ajjhāvasati muttacāgo payatapāṇī vossaggarato yācayogo dānasaṃvibhāgarato || ettāvatā kho Mahānāma upāsako cāgasampanno hotī ti || ||

7. Kittāvatā pana bhante upāsako paññāsampanno hotī ti || || Idha Mahānāma upāsako paññavā hoti udayatthagāminiyā paññāya samannāgato[^395-3] ariyāya nibbedhikāya sammādukkhakkhayagāminiyā || ettāvatā kho Mahānāma upāsako paññāsampanno hotī ti || ||

SN_5,55(11).38. (8) Vassam.

2. Seyyathāpi bhikkhave upari pabbate thullaphusitake deve[^396-1] vassante tam udakaṃ yathāninnam pavattamānam pabbatakandarapadarasākhā[^396-2] paripūreti || pabbatakandarapadarasākhā[^396-3] paripūrā kussubbhe[^396-4] paripūreti || kussubbhā paripūrā mahāsobbhe[^396-5] paripūrenti || mahāsobbhā[^396-6] paripūrā kunnadiyo paripūrenti || kunnadiyo paripūrā mahānadiyo paripūrenti || mahānadiyo paripūrā mahāsamuddasāgaram paripūrenti || ||

3. Evam eva kho bhikkhave ariyasāvakassa yo ca Buddhe aveccappasādo || yo ca dhamme aveccappasādo yo ca saṅghe aveccappasādo || yāni ca ariyakāntāni sīlāni || ime dhammā sandamānā pāraṃ gantvā āsavānaṃ khayāya saṃvattantī ti || ||

SN_5,55(11).39. (9) Kāḷi.

1. Ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Sakkesu viharati Kapilavatthusmiṃ Nigrodhārāme || ||

2. Atha kho Bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya yena Kāḷigodhāya[^396-7] sākiyāniyā nivesanaṃ tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā paññatte āsane nisīdi || ||

3. Atha kho Kāḷigodhā[^396-7] sākiyānī yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antaṃ nisīdi || || Ekam antaṃ nisinnaṃ[^396-8] kho Kāligodham sākiyānim[^396-9] etad avoca || || Catuhi kho Godhe dhammehi samannāgatā ariyasāvikā sotapannā hoti avinipātadhammā niyatā sambodhiparāyanā || || Katamehi catuhi || ||

4. Idha Godhe ariyasāvikā Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgatā[^396-10] hoti || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavāti || Dhamme || Saṅghe || || Vi-


<!-- PTS Page 397 -->
<a id="page-397"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 397]*

gatamalamaccherena cetasā agāram[^397-1] ajjhāvasati muttacāgā[^397-2] payatapāṇī vossaggaratā yācayogā dānasaṃvibhāgaratā || || Imehi kho Godhe catuhi dhammehi samannāgatā ariyasāvikā sotāpannā hoti avinipātadhammā niyatā sambodhiparāyanā ti || ||

5. Yānimāni[^397-3] bhante Bhagavatā cattāri sotāpattiyaṅgāni desitāni saṃvijjante[^397-4] te dhammā mayi ahañca tesu dhammesu sandissāmi || ahaṃ hi bhante Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgatā || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānaṃ buddho bhagavā ti || || Dhamme || Saṅghe || || Yaṃ[^397-5] kho pana kiñci kule deyyadhammaṃ sabbaṃ tam appaṭivibhattaṃ sīlavantehi[^397-6] kalyāṇadhammehī ti || ||

6. Lābhā te Godhe suladdhan te Godhe sotāpattiphalam tayā Godhe vyākatan ti || ||

SN_5,55(11).4O. (10) Nandiya.

1. Ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Sakkesu viharati Kapilavatthusmiṃ Nigrodhārāme || ||

2. Atha kho Nandiyo sakko yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkami || pe || || Ekam antaṃ nisinno kho Nandiyo sakko Bhagavantam etad avoca || ||

3. Yasseva nu kho bhante ariyasāvakassa cattāri sotāpattiyaṅgāni || sabbena sabbaṃ sabbathā sabbaṃ natthi so evaṃ[^397-7] nu kho bhante ariyasāvako Pamādavihārī ti vuccati || || Yassa kho Nandiya cattāri sotāpattiyaṅgāni sabbena sabbam sabbathā sabbaṃ natthi tam aham Bāhiro puthujjanapakkhe ṭhito ti vadāmi || ||

4. Api ca Nandiya yathā[^397-8] ariyasāvako pamādavihārī ceva hoti appamādavihārī ca[^397-9] taṃ suṇāhi sādhukam[^397-10] manasi karohi bhāsissāmī ti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 398 -->
<a id="page-398"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 398]*

Evam bhante ti kho Nandiyo sakko Bhagavato paccassosi || || Bhagavā etad avoca || ||

5. Kathañca Nandiya ariyasāvako pamādavihārī hoti || || Idha Nandiya ariyasāvako Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānam buddo bhagavā ti || || So[^398-1] tena buddhe aveccappasādena santuṭṭho na uttariṃ[^398-2] vāyamati divā pavivekāya rattim paṭisallānāya || tassa evam pamattassa viharato pāmujjaṃ na hoti || pāmujje asati pīti na hoti || pītiyā asati passaddhi na hoti || passaddhiyā asati dukkhaṃ viharati || dukkhino cittaṃ na samādhiyati || asamāhite citte dhammā na pātubhavanti || dhammānam apātubhāvā Pamādavihārī teva[^398-3] saṅkhaṃ gacchati || || Puna ca paraṃ Nandiya ariyasāvako Dhamme[^398-4] || Saṅghe || Ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato hoti akhaṇḍehi || la-pe || samādhisaṃvattanikehi || || So tehi ariyakantehi sīlehi santuṭṭho na uttariṃ[^398-2] vāyamati divā pavivekāya rattim paṭisallāṇāya || tassa evam pamattassa viharato pāmujjaṃ na hoti || pāmujje asati pīti na hoti || pītiyā asati passaddhi na hoti || passaddhiyā asati dukkhaṃ viharati || dukkhino cittaṃ na samādhiyati || asamahite citte dhammā na {pātubhavanti}[^398-5] || dhammānam apātubhāvā Pamādavihārī teva[^398-3] saṅkhaṃ gacchati || || Evaṃ kho Nandiya ariyasāvako pamādavihārī hoti || ||

6. Kathañca Nandiya ariyasāvako appamādavihārī hoti || || Idha Nandiya ariyasāvako Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || || So tena Buddhe aveccappasādena asantuṭṭho uttarim[^398-2] vāyamati divā pavivekāya rattim paṭisallāṇāya || tassa evam appamattassa viharato pāmujjam jāyati || pamuditassa pīti jāyati || pītimanassa kāyo passambhati || passaddhakāyo sukhaṃ vediyati || sukhino cittaṃ samādhiyati || samāhite citte dhammā pātubhavanti || dhammānam pātubhāvā Appamādavihārī teva saṅkhaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 399 -->
<a id="page-399"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 399]*

gacchati || || Puna ca param Nandiya ariyasāvako Dhamme || Saṅghe || || Ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato hoti akhaṇḍehi || la-pe || samādhisaṃvattanikehi || || So tehi ariyakantehi sīlehi asantuṭṭho uttariṃ vāyamati divā[^399-1] pavivekāya rattim patisallāṇāya || tassa evam appamattassa viharato pāmujjaṃ jāyati || pamuditassa pīti jāyati || pītimanassa kāyo passambhati || passaddhakāyo sukhaṃ vediyati || sukhino cittaṃ samādhiyati[^399-2] || samāhite citte dhammā pātubhavanti || dhammānam pātubhāvā Appamādavihārī teva saṅkham gacchati || || Evaṃ kho Nandiya ariyasāvako appamādavihārī hotī ti || || Puññābhisandavaggo catuttho || || Uddānaṃ || || Abhisandā[^399-3] tayo vuttā || dve[^399-1] Devapadāni ca || Sabhāgatam[^399-4] Mahānāmo[^399-5] || Vassaṃ[^399-6] Kāḷī[^399-7] ca[^399-8] Nandiyā ti || ||

CHAPTER V. SAGĀTHAPUÑÑABHISANDAVAGGO PAÑCAMO.

SN_5,55(11).41. (1) Abhisanda (or Sayhaka)1.

2. Cattāro me bhikkhave puññābhisandā kusalābhisandā sukhassāhārā[^399-9] || katamo cattāro || ||

3. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || ayam pathamo puññābhisando[^399-10] || || Puna ca param bhikkhave ariyasāvako Dhamme || {Saṅghe} || Ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato hoti akhaṇḍehi || la-pe || samādhisaṃvattanikehi || ayaṃ catuttho puññabhisando kusalābhisando sukhassāhāro || || Ime kho

bhikkhave cattāro puññābhisandā[^400-1] kusalābhisandā[^400-1] sukhassāhārā[^400-2] || ||

4. Imehi kho bhikkhave[^400-3] catuhi puññābhisandehi kusalābhisandehi samannāgatassa ariyasāvakassa na sukaram puññassa pamāṇaṃ gaṇetuṃ[^400-4] ettako puññābhisando kusalābhisando sukhassāhāro ti || atha kho[^400-5] Asaṅkheyyo appameyyo mahāpuññakkhandho teva saṅkhaṃ[^400-6] gacchati || ||

5. Seyyathāpi bhikkhave mahāsamudde na sukaram udakassa pamāṇaṃ gaṇetuṃ ettakāni udakāḷhakānīti vā[^400-7] ettakāni udakāḷhakasatānīti vā ettakāni udakāḷhakasahassānī ti vā ettakāni udakāḷhakasatasahassānī ti vā[^400-8] || atha kho Asaṅkheyyo appameyyo mahā udakakkhandho teva[^400-9] saṅkham gacchati || || Evam eva kho bhikkhave imehi catuhi puññābhisandehi kusalābhisandehi samanāgatassa ariyasāvakassa na sukaram puññassa pamāṇam gaṇetum ettako puññabhisando kusalābhisando sukhassāhāro ti || || Atha kho Asaṅkheyyo appameyyo mahāpuññakkhandho teva[^400-9] saṅkhaṃ[^400-10] gacchatī ti || ||

6. Idam avoca Bhagavā[^400-11] || || Mahodadhim aparimitam mahāsaraṃ || bahubheravaṃ ratnagaṇānam[^400-12] ālayaṃ || Najjo yathā naragaṇasaṅghasevitā || puthusavanti[^400-13] upayanti sāgaraṃ ||[^400-1]|| Evaṃ naram annapānavatthadadaṃ[^400-14] || seyyānisajjattharaṇassa[^400-15] dāyakaṃ || puññassa dhārā upayanti paṇḍitaṃ || najjo yathā vārivahā[^400-16] va sāgaran ti ||[^400-2]||

CHAPTER VI. SAPPAÑÑAVAGGO CHAṬṬHO.

SN_5,55(11).51. (1) Sagāthakam.

2. Catuhi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato ariyasāvako sotāpanno hoti avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano || katamehi catuhi || ||

3. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || Dhamme || Saṅghe || Ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato hoti akhaṇḍehi || la-pe || samādhisaṃvattanikehi || || Imehi kho bhikkhave catuhi dhammehi samannāgato ariyasāvako sotāpanno hoti avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano ti || ||

4. Idam avoca Bhagavā[^405-1] || || Yassa saddhā Tathāgate || acaḷā[^405-2] suppatiṭṭhitā[^405-3] || sīlaṃ ca yassa kalyāṇaṃ || ariyakantam pasaṃsitaṃ ||[^405-1]|| Saṅghe pasādo yassatthi || ujubhūtaṃ ca dassanaṃ || Adaliddo ti tam āhu || amoghaṃ tassa jīvitaṃ ||[^405-2]|| Tasmā saddhañca sīlañca || pasādaṃ dhammadassanaṃ || anuyuñjetha medhāvī[^405-4] || saram buddhānasāsanan ti ||[^405-3]||

SN_5,55(11).52. (2) Vassavuttham.

1. [^405-5] Ekam samayam Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme || ||

2. Tena kho pana samayena aññataro bhikkhu Sāvatthiyaṃ vassaṃ vuttho Kapilavatthum anuppatto hoti kenacid eva karaṇīyena || ||

3. Assosum kho Kāpilavatthavā[^405-6] Sakyā[^405-7] || aññataro[^405-8] bhikkhu Sāvatthiyaṃ vassaṃ vutto Kapilavatthum anuppatto ti || ||

4. Atha kho Kāpilavatthavā Sakyā yena so bhikkhu tenupasaṅkamiṃsu || upasaṅkamitvā tam bhikkhum abhivādetvā ekam antam nisīdiṃsu || || Ekam antam nisinnā kho Kāpilavatthavā[^405-9] sakyā[^405-7] tam bhikkhum etad avocuṃ || ||

5. Kacci bhante Bhagavā arogo[^405-10] ceva[^405-11] balavā cā ti || || Arogo cāvuso Bhagavā[^405-12] balavā cā ti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 406 -->
<a id="page-406"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 406]*

Kacci pana bhante Sāriputta-Moggalānā arogā ceva[^406-1] balavanto cā ti || || Sāriputta-Moggalānā[^406-2] pi kho avuso arogā[^406-3] ceva[^406-1] balavanto cā ti || || Kacci pana bhante bhikkhusaṅgho arogo ca balavā[^406-4] cā ti || || Bhikkhusaṅgho pi kho āvuso arogo ca balavā cā ti || ||

6. Atthi pana te bhante kiñci iminā antaravassena Bhagavato sammukhā sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahītan ti || || Sammukhā me tam āvuso Bhagavato sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahītaṃ || || Appakā te[^406-5] bhikkhave bhikkhū ye āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttiṃ paññāvimuttiṃ diṭṭheva dhamme sayam abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharanti || atha kho eteva[^406-6] bahutarā bhikkhū ye pañcannam orambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyojanānam[^406-7] parikkhayā opapātikā tattha parinibbāyino anāvattidhammā asmā[^406-8] lokā ti || ||

7. Aparampi kho me āvuso Bhagavato sammukhā sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahītaṃ || || Appakā te bhikkhave bhikkhū ye pañcannam orambhāgiyānam saṃyojanānam[^406-7] parikkhayā opapātikā tattha parinibbāyino anāvattidhammā asmā lokā || atha kho ete bahutarā bhikkhū ye tiṇṇam saṃyojanānam parikkhayā rāgadosamohānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāmino[^406-9] sakideva[^406-10] imaṃ lokam āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ karissantī ti || ||

8. Aparam pi kho me āvuso Bhagavato sammukhā sutaṃ sammukhā patiggahītaṃ || || Appakā te bhikkhave bhikkhū ye tiṇṇaṃ samyojanānaṃ parikkhayā rāgadosamohānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāmino[^406-11] sakid eva[^406-10] imaṃ lokam āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ karissanti || atha kho eteva bahutarā bhikkhū ye tiṇṇaṃ saṃyojanānam parikkhayā sotāpannā avinipātadhammā niyatā sambodhiparāyanā ti || ||

SN_5,55(11).53. (3) Dhammadinna.

1. Ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Bārānasiyaṃ viharati Isipatane Migadāye || ||


<!-- PTS Page 407 -->
<a id="page-407"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 407]*

2. Atha kho Dhammadinno upāsako pañcahi upāsakasatehi saddhiṃ[^407-1] yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antaṃ nisīdi[^407-2] || || Ekam antaṃ nisinno kho Dhammadinno upāsako Bhagavantam etad avoca || ||

3. Ovadatu no bhante Bhagavā anusāsatu no bhante Bhagavā yam amhākam assa dīgharattam hitāya sukhāyā ti || || Tasmā ti ha vo[^407-3] Dhammadinna evaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ || || Ye te suttantā Tathāgatabhāsitā gambhīrā gambhīratthā lokuttarā suññatapaṭisaṃyuttā || te kālena kālam upasampajja viharissāmā ti[^407-4] || || Evaṃ[^407-5] hi vo[^407-3] Dhammadinna sikkhitabban ti || ||

4. Na kho netam bhante sukaram amhehi puttasambādhasayanam ajjhāvasantehi[^407-6] kāsikaṃ[^407-7] candanam[^407-8] paccanubhontehi mālāgandhavilepanaṃ dhārayantehi jātarūparajataṃ sādiyantehi || ye te suttantā gambhīrā gambhīratthā lokuttarā suññatapaṭisaṃyuttā te kālena kālam upasampajja viharituṃ || || Tesaṃ no bhante Bhagavā amhākam pañcasu sikkhāpadesu ṭhitānam uttaridhammaṃ[^407-9] desetū ti[^407-10] || ||

5. Tasmā ti[^407-11] ha vo[^407-3] Dhammadinna evam sikkhitabbaṃ || Buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgatā bhavissāma[^407-12] || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānam buddho bhagavā ti || Dhamme || {Saṅghe} || Ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgata bhavissāma[^407-12] akhaṇḍehi || la-pe || samādhisaṃvattanikehī ti || || Evaṃ hi vo[^407-3] Dhammadinna sikkhitabban ti || || Yānimāni bhante Bhagavatā cattāri sotāpattiyaṅgāni desitāni || saṃvijjante te dhammā amhesu mayañ ca[^407-13] tesu dhammesu sandissāma[^407-14] || ||

6. Mayaṃ hi bhante Buddhe aveccappasadena samannāgatā || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānam


<!-- PTS Page 408 -->
<a id="page-408"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 408]*

buddho bhagavā ti || Dhamme || Saṅghe || pe-[pa] || Ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgatā akhaṇḍehi || la-pe || {samādhisaṃvattanikehī} ti || ||

7. Lābhā vo[^408-1] Dhammadinna suladdhaṃ vo[^408-1] Dhammadinna sotāpattiphalaṃ Dhammadinna[^408-2] vyākatan ti || ||

SN_5,55(11).54. (4) Gilāyanam.

1. Ekam samayam Bhagavā Sakkesu viharati Kapilavatthusmiṃ Nigrodhārāme || ||

2. Tena kho pana samayena sambahulā bhikkhū Bhagavato cīvarakammaṃ karonti Niṭṭhitacīvaro Bhagavā temāsaccayena cārikam pakkamissatī ti || ||

3. Assosi kho Mahānāmo sakko Sambahulā kira bhikkhū Bhagavato cīvarakammaṃ karonti Niṭṭhitacīvaro Bhagavā temāsaccayena cārikam pakkamissatī ti || || Atha kho Mahānāmo sakko yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antaṃ nisīdi[^408-3] || ekam antaṃ nisinno kho Mahānāmo sakko Bhagavantam etad avoca || ||

4. Sutam etam bhante Sambahulā kira bhikkhū Bhagavato cīvarakammaṃ karonti Niṭṭhitacīvaro Bhagavā temāsaccayena[^408-4] cārikam[^408-5] pakkamissatī ti || Na kho te etam[^408-6] bhante Bhagavato sammukhā sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahītaṃ sappaññena upāsakena sappañño upāsako ābādhīko dukkhito bāḷhagilāno ovaditabbo ti[^408-7] || ||

5. Sappaññena Mahānama[^408-8] upāsakena sappañño upāsako ābādhiko dukkhito bāḷhagilāno catuhi assāsaniyehi dhammehi assāsetabbo || || Assāsatāyasmā[^408-9] atthāyasmato Buddhe aveccappasādena || Iti pi so Bhagavā || la-pe || satthā devamanussānaṃ buddho bhagavā ti || || Assāsatāyasmā[^408-10] atthāyasmato Dhamme[^408-11] Saṅghe[^408-11] || Ariyakantāni sīlāni[^408-12] akhaṇḍāni || la-pe || samādhisaṃvattanikānī ti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 409 -->
<a id="page-409"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 409]*

6. Sappaññena Mahānāma upāsakena sappañño upāsako ābādhiko dukkhito bāḷhagilāno imehi catuhi assāsaniyehi dhammehi assāsetvā evam assa vacanīyo || ||

7. Atthāyasmato mātāpitusu apekhā ti || so ce evaṃ vadeyya Atthi me mātāpitusu apekhā ti so evam assa vacanīyo || Āyasmā pi[^409-1] kho māriso maraṇadhammo[^409-2] || sace pāyasmā[^409-3] mātāpitusu apekhaṃ karissati marissateva || no ce pāyasmā[^409-4] mātāpitusu apekhaṃ karissati marissateva[^409-5] || sādhāyasmā[^409-6] yā te[^409-7] mātāpitusu apekhā tam pajahāti[^409-8] || ||

8. So ce evam vadeyya[^409-9] || Yā[^409-10] me mātāpitusu apekhā sā pahīnā ti || so evam assa vacanīyo || Atthi panāyasmato puttadāresu apekhā ti || || Sa ce evaṃ vadeyya Atthi me puttadāresu apekhā ti || so evam assa vacanīyo || Āyasmā kho māriso maraṇadhammo sace pāyasmā puttadāresu apekham karissati marissateva[^409-11] || no ce pāyasmā puttadāresu apekhaṃ karissati marissateva || sādhāyasmā[^409-12] yā te[^409-7] puttadāresu apekhā tam pajahā ti || ||

9. So ce evaṃ vadeyya || Yā me puttadāresu apekhā sā pahīnā ti || so evam assa vacanīyo || Atthi panāyasmato mānussakesu[^409-13] pañcasu kāmaguṇesu apekhā ti || || So ce evaṃ vadeyya || Atthi me mānussakesu pañcasu kāmagunesu apekhā ti || so evam assa vacanīyo || Mānussakehi[^409-14] kho āvuso kāmehi[^409-15] dibbā[^409-16] kāmā abhikkantatarā ca paṇitatarā ca || sādhāyasmā[^409-17] mānussakehi kāmehi cittaṃ vuṭṭhāpetvā catummahārājikesu devesu cittam adhimocehī ti || ||

10. So ce[^409-10] evaṃ vadeyya || Mānussakehi kāmehi cittaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ cātummahārājikesu[^409-18] devesu cittaṃ adhimocitanti[^409-19] || so evam assa vacanīyo || Cātummahārājikehi kho


<!-- PTS Page 410 -->
<a id="page-410"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 410]*

āvuso devehi Tāvatiṃsā devā abhikkantatarā ca paṇitatarā ca || sādhāyasmā[^410-1] cātummahārājikehi cātummahārājikehi devehi cittaṃ vuṭṭhāpetvā Tāvatiṃsesu devesu cittam adhimocehī ti || || 11--16. So ce evaṃ vadeyya || Cātummahārājikehi me devehi[^410-2] cittaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ Tāvatiṃsesu devesu cittam adhimocitan ti[^410-3] || so evam assa vacanīyo || Tāvatiṃsehi[^410-4] kho avuso devehi pi[^410-5] Yāmā devā Tusitā devā Nimmānaratidevā[^410-6] Paranimmitavasavattino[^410-7] devā[^410-8] || ||

17. Parinimmitavasavattīhi kho āvuso devehi Brahmaloko abhikkantataro ca paṇitataro ca || sādhāyasmā[^410-9] Parinimittavasavattīhi devehi cittaṃ vuṭṭhāpetvā Brahmaloke cittam adhimocehī ti || ||

18. So ce[^410-10] evaṃ vadeyya || Paranimmitavasavattīhi me[^410-11] devehi cittaṃ vuṭṭhitam[^410-12] Brahmaloke cittam adhimocitan ti || so evam assa vacanīyo || Brahmaloko pi kho āvuso anicco adhuvo sakkāya pariyāpanno || sādhāyasmā[^410-13] Brahmalokā[^410-14] cittaṃ vuṭṭhāpetvā sakkāya[^410-15] nirodhe cittam upasaṃharāhī ti[^410-16] || ||

19. So ce evam vadeyya || Brahmalokā me cittaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ sakkāya nirodhe cittam[^410-17] upasaṃharāmīti[^410-18] || evaṃ vuttassa[^410-19] kho Mahānāma upāsakassa āsavā[^410-20] vimuttacittena bhikkhunā na kiñci nānākaraṇaṃ vadāmi yad idam vimuttiyā vimuttinti[^410-21] || ||

SN_5,56(12).1. (1) Samādhi.

1. Sāvatthi || la[^414-1] || ||

ariyasaccāni yathābhūtam abhisambujjhanti || || Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave Idaṃ dukkhan ti yogo karaṇīyo || la-pe || || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodhayāminī patipadā ti yogo karaṇīyo || || SN_5,56(12).6. (6) Samaṇabrāhmaṇā2.

2. Ye[^417-1] hi keci bhikkhave atītam addhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā yathābhūtam abhisambuddham pakāsesuṃ || sabbe te cattāri ariyasaccāni yathābhūtam abhisambuddham pakāsesuṃ || ||

3. Ye pi[^417-2] hi keci bhikkhave anāgatam addhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā yathābhūtam abhisambuddham pakāsessanti sabbe te cattāri[^417-3] ariyasaccāni[^417-4] yathābhūtam abhisambuddham pakāsessanti || ||

4. Ye hi keci bhi- etarahi samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā yathābhūtam abhisambuddham pakāsenti || sabbe te cattāri ariyasaccāni yathābhūtam abhisambuddham pakāsenti || Katamāni cattāri || || Dukkham ariyasaccaṃ || la || Dukkhanirodhagāminī patipadā ariyasaccaṃ || ||

5. Ye pi[^417-2] hi keci bhikkhave atītam addhānam samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā yathābhūtam abhisambuddham pakāsesuṃ[^417-5] || pakāsessanti || pakāsenti || sabbe te imāni cattāri ariyasaccāni yathābhūtam abhisambuddham pakāsenti || || Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave Idam dukkhan ti yogo karanīyo || la-[pe] || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī patipadā ti yogo karaṇīyo ti || ||

SN_5,56(12).7. (7) Vitakkā.

2. Mā[^417-6] bhikkhave pāpake akusale vitakke[^417-7] vitakkeyyātha[^417-8] || {seyyathīdaṃ} kāmavitakkaṃ vyāpādavitakkam vihiṃsāvitakkaṃ || || Taṃ kissa hetu || ||

3. Nete bhikkhave vitakkā atthasaṃhitā[^417-9] nādibrahma-


<!-- PTS Page 418 -->
<a id="page-418"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 418]*

cariyakā na nibbidāya na virāgāya na nirodhāya na upasamāya na abhiññāya na sambodhāya[^418-1] na nibbānāya saṃvattantī ti || ||

4. Vitakkentā[^418-2] ca kho tumhe bhikkhave Idaṃ dukkhan ti vitakkeyyātha || [pe] || ayaṃ dukkhanirodho ti vitakkeyyātha || ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ti vitakkeyyātha || || Taṃ kissa hetu || ||

5. Ete bhikkhave vitakkā atthasaṃhitā ete brahmacariyakā ete ādibrahmacariyakā ete nibbidāya virāgāya nirodhāyā upasamāya abhiññāya[^418-3] sambodhāya[^418-4] nibbānāya saṃvattanti || || Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave Idam dukkhan ti yogo karaṇīyo || la-pe || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī patipadā ti yogo karaṇīyo ti || ||

SN_5,56(12).8. (8) Cintā.

2. Mā bhikkhave pāpakam akusalaṃ cittaṃ cinteyyātha[^418-5] || Sassato loko ti vā Asassato loko ti vā || || Antavā loko ti vā Anantavā loko ti vā || || Taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīran ti vā || Aññaṃ jīvaṃ aññaṃ sarīran ti vā || || Hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā ti vā || Na hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā ti[^418-6] vā || Hoti ca na ca hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā ti vā || Neva hoti na na hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā ti vā || || Taṃ kissa hetu || ||

3. Nesā bhikkhave cintā atthasaṃhitā nādibrahmacariyikā[^418-7] na nibbidāya na virāgāya na nirodhāya na upasamāya na abhiññaya na sambodhāya[^418-8] na nibbānāya saṃvattanti || ||

4. Cintentā ca[^418-9] kho tumhe bhikkhave Idaṃ dukkhanti cinteyyātha || pe || Ayam dukkhanirodhāgāminī patipadā ti cinteyyātha || || Taṃ kissa hetu || ||

5. Esā bhikkhave cintā atthasañhitā esā ādibrahmacariyikā[^418-7] esā nibbidāya virāgāya nirodhāya upasamāya abhiñ-


<!-- PTS Page 419 -->
<a id="page-419"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 419]*

ñāya sambodhāya nibbānaya[^419-1] saṃvattati || || Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave Idam dukkhan ti yogo karaṇīyo || la-pe || ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ti || yogo karaṇīyo ti || ||

SN_5,56(12).9. (9) Viggāhikā.

2. Mā bhikkhave viggāhikakathaṃ katheyyātha[^419-2] || Na tvam imaṃ dhammavinayam ājānāsi || aham imaṃ[^419-3] dhammavinayam ājānāmi || kiṃ tvam imaṃ dhammavinayam ājānissasi[^419-4] || || Micchāpaṭipanno tvam asi || aham asmi[^419-5] sammāpaṭipanno || || Pure vacanīyam pacchā avaca pacchā vacanīyam pure avaca || || Sahitam me asahitan te || || Āciṇṇan[^419-6] te viparāvattaṃ || || Āropito te vādo || cara[^419-7] vādappamokkhāya Niggahīto si nibbeṭhehi[^419-8] sace pahosī ti || || Taṃ kissa hetu || ||

3. Nesā bhikkhave kathā atthasaṃhitā nādibrahmacariyikā[^419-9] na nibbidāya na virāgāya na nirodhāya na upasamāya na abhiññāya na sambodhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati || ||

4. Kathentā ca kho tumhe bhikkhave Idaṃ dukkhan ti katheyyātha || pe || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī patipadā ti katheyyātha || || Taṃ kissa hetu || ||

5. Esā hi bhikkhave kathā || pa || yogo karaṇīyo ti || ||

SN_5,56(12).10. (10) Kathā.

2. Mā bhikkhave anekavihitaṃ tiracchānakatham katheyyātha[^419-10] || seyyathīdaṃ || Rājakathaṃ || corakathaṃ || mahāmattakathaṃ || senākathaṃ || bhayakathaṃ || suddhakathaṃ || annakathaṃ || pānakatham || vatthakathaṃ || sayanakathaṃ || mālākathaṃ[^419-11] || gandha- || ñāti- || yāna- || gāmā- || nigama- || nagara- || janapada- || itthi- || purisa-[^419-12] || surā-[^419-13] ||


<!-- PTS Page 420 -->
<a id="page-420"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 420]*

visikhā- || kumbhaṭṭhāna- || pubbapeta-[^420-1] || nānatta- || lokakkhāyikaṃ samuddakkhāyikam || itibhavābhavakathaṃ iti vā || || Taṃ kissa hetu || ||

3. Nesā bhikkhave kathā atthasaṃhitā nādibrahmacariyikā[^420-2] na nibbidāya na virāgāya na nirodhāya na upasamāya na abhiññāya na sambodhāya na nibbānāya[^420-3] saṃvattati || ||

4. Kathentā ca kho tumhe bhikkhave Idaṃ dukkhan ti katheyyātha || Ayam dukkhasamudayo ti || katheyyātha || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodho[^420-4] ti katheyyātha || Ayam dukkhanirodhagāminī patipadā ti katheyyātha || || Taṃ kissa hetu[^420-5] || ||

5. Esā bhikkhave kathā atthasaṃhitā esā ādibrahmacariyikā[^420-2] esā nibbidāya virāgāya nirodhāya upasamāya abhiññāya sambodhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati || || Tasmāti ha bhikkhave Idaṃ dukkhan ti yogo karaṇīyo || la-[pe] || Ayam dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ti yogo karaṇīyo ti || || Samādhivaggo pathamo[^420-6] || || Tassuddānaṃ[^420-7] || || Samādhi Paṭisallāṇa[^420-8] || Kulaputtā[^420-9] apare duve[^420-10] || || Samaṇabrāhmaṇā[^420-11] Vitakkā[^420-12] || Cintā[^420-13] Viggāhikā Kathā ti[^420-14] || ||

CHAPTER II. DHAMMACAKKAPAVATTANA-VAGGO DUTIYO.

SN_5,56(12).11. (1) Tathāgatena vutta1.

1. Evam me sutaṃ[^420-15] ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Bārāṇasiyaṃ viharati Isipatane Migadāye || ||


<!-- PTS Page 421 -->
<a id="page-421"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 421]*

2. Tatra kho Bhagavā pañcavaggiye bhikkhū āmantesi || || Dve me bhikkhave antā pabbajitena na sevittabbā || Katame dve || ||

3. Yo cāyaṃ kāmesu kāmesu khallikānuyogo hīno gammo puthujjanīko[^421-1] anariyo anatthasaṃhito || yo cāyam attakilamathānuyogo dukkho anariyo anatthasaṃhito || || Ete te bhikkhave ubho ante anupakamma majjhimā paṭipadā Tathāgatena abhisambuddhā cakkhukaraṇī ñāṇakaraṇī upasamāya abhiññāya sambodhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati || ||

4. Katamā ca sā bhikkhave majjhimā paṭipadā Tathāgatena abhisambuddhā cakkhukaraṇī ñāṇakaraṇī upasamāya abhiññāya sambodhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati || || Ayam eva ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo || seyyathīdaṃ || sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappo sammāvācā sammākammanto sammāājīvo sammāvāyāmo sammāsati sammāsamādhi || || Ayam kho sā bhikkhave majjhimā paṭipadā Tathāgatena abhisambuddhā cakkhu- ñaṇa- karaṇī upasamāya abhiññāya sambodhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati || ||

5. Idaṃ kho pana bhikkhave dukkham ariyasaccaṃ || || Jāti pi dukkhā jarā pi dukkhā vyādhi pi dukkhā maraṇam pi dukkhaṃ sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsā pi dukkhā[^421-2] || appiyehi sampayogo dukkho piyehi vippayogo dukkho || yam picchaṃ na labhati[^421-3] tam pi dukkhaṃ || saṃkhittena pañcupādānakkhandhā pi[^421-4] dukkhā || ||

6. Idam kho pana[^421-5] bhikkhave dukkhasamudayam ariyasaccaṃ[^421-6] || || Yāyaṃ taṇhā ponobbhavikā nandī rāgasahagatā tatra tatrābhinandinī || seyyathīdaṃ || || Kāmataṇhā bhavataṇhā vibhavataṇhā || ||

7. Idaṃ kho[^421-7] pana bhikkhave[^421-8] dukkhanirodham ariyasaccaṃ || || Yo tassā yeva taṇhāya asesavirāganirodho cāgo paṭinissaggo mutti anālayo || ||

8. Idaṃ kho pana bhikkhave dukkhanirodhagāminī pa-


<!-- PTS Page 422 -->
<a id="page-422"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 422]*

ṭipadā ariyasaccam || || Ayam eva ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo || seyyathīdaṃ || sammādiṭṭhi || la-pe || sammāsamādhi || ||

9. Idaṃ dukkham ariyasaccan ti me bhikkhave pubbe ananussutesu dhammesu cakkhum udapādi ñāṇam udapādi paññā udapādi vijjā udapādi āloko udapādi || || Taṃ kho panidaṃ dukkham ariyasaccam pariññeyyan ti me bhikkhave pubbe || la[^422-1] || pariññātan ti me bhikkhave pubbe ananussutesu dhammesu cakkhum udapādi || pe || āloko udapādi || ||

10. Idaṃ dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasaccan ti me bhikkhave pubbe ananussutesu dhammesu cakkhum udapādi ñāṇam udapādi paññā udapādi vijjā udapādi āloko udapādi || || Taṃ kho panidaṃ dukkhasamudayam[^422-2] ariyasaccam[^422-3] pahātabban ti me bhikkhave pubbe || la-pe || pahīnan ti me bhikkhave pubbe ananussutesu dhammesu cakkhum udapādi || pe || āloko udapādi || ||

11. Idaṃ dukkhanirodham ariyasaccan ti me bhikkhave pubbe ananussutesu dhammesu cakkhum udapādi ñāṇam udapādi paññā udapādi vijjā udapādi āloko udapādi || || Taṃ kho panidaṃ dukkhanirodham ariyasaccaṃ sacchikātabban ti me bhikkhave pubbe || la-pe || sacchikatan ti me bhikkhave pubbe ananussutesu dhammesu cakkhum udapādi || pe || āloko udapādi || ||

12. Idaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasaccan ti me bhikkhave pubbe ananussutesu dhammesu cakkhum udapādi ñāṇam udapādi paññā udapādi vijjā udapādi āloko udapādi || || Tam kho panidaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasaccam bhāvetabban ti me bhikkhave || pa[^422-4] || bhāvitan ti me bhikkhave pubbe ananussutesu dhammesu cakkhum udapādi ñāṇam udapādi paññā udapādi vijjā udapādi āloko udapādi || ||

13. Yāva kīvañca me bhikkhave imesu catusu ariyasaccesu evaṃ tiparivaṭṭaṃ dvādasākāraṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaḍassanaṃ na suvisuddham[^422-5] ahosi || neva tāvāham[^422-6] bhik-


<!-- PTS Page 423 -->
<a id="page-423"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 423]*

khave sadevake loke samārake sabrahmake sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadevamanussāya anuttaraṃ sammāsambodhim abhisambuddho ti[^423-1] paccaññāsiṃ || ||

14. Yato ca kho me bhikkhave imesu catusu ariyasaccesu evaṃ tiparivaṭṭam dvādasākāraṃ yathābhūtam ñāṇadassanaṃ suvisuddham ahosi || athāham bhikkhave sadevake loke samārake sabrahmake sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadevamanussāya anuttaram sammāsambodhim abhisambuddho ti[^423-2] paccaññāsiṃ || ñāṇañca pana me dassanam udapādi Akuppā me cetovimutti[^423-3] ayam antimā jāti natthidāni punabbhavo ti || ||

15. Idam avoca Bhagavā || attamanā pañcavaggiyā bhikkhū Bhagavato bhāsitam abhinanduṃ || imasmiṃ ca pana veyyākaraṇasmim bhaññamāne āyasmato[^423-4] Koṇḍaññassa[^423-5] virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhum udapādi || yaṃ kiñci samudayadhammaṃ[^423-6] sabban taṃ nirodhadhamman ti || ||

16. Evam[^423-7] pavattite ca pana Bhagavatā dhammacakke Bhummā devā saddam anussāvesuṃ || || Etam Bhagavatā Bārāṇasiyam Isipatane Migadāye anuttaraṃ dhammacakkam pavattitaṃ appativattiyaṃ samaṇena vā brāhmaṇena vā devena vā Mārena vā Brahmunā vā kenaci vā lokasmin ti || ||

17. Bhummānaṃ devānaṃ saddaṃ sutvā Cātummahārājikā devā saddam anussāvesuṃ || || Etam Bhagavatā Bārāṇasiyam Isipatane Migadāye anuttaraṃ dhammacakkam pavattitam appativattiyaṃ samaṇena vā brāhmaṇena vā devena vā Mārena vā Brahmuṇā vā kenaci vā lokasmin ti || ||

18. Cātummahārājikānaṃ devānaṃ saddaṃ sutvā Tāvaṭiṃsā devā[^423-8] Yāmā devā Tusitā devā Nimmānaratī devā Paranimmittavasavattino[^423-9] devā Brahmakāyikā devā saddam anussāvesuṃ || || Etam Bhagavatā Bārāṇasiyaṃ Isipatane Migadāye anuttaraṃ dhammacakkam pavattitaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 424 -->
<a id="page-424"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 424]*

appativattiyaṃ samaṇena vā brāhmaṇena vā devena vā Mārena vā Brahmunā vā kenaci vā lokasmin ti || ||

19. Iti ha tena khaṇena tena layena[^424-1] tena muhuttena yāva Brahmalokā saddo abbhuggacchi[^424-2] || ayañ ca dasasahassī lokadhātu[^424-3] saṃkampi sampakampi sampavedhi || appamāṇo ca uḷāro obhāso loke pātur ahosi atikkamma devānaṃ devānubhāvan ti || ||

20. Atha kho Bhagavā udānam udānesi || || Aññāsi vata bho {Koṇḍañño} aññāsi vata bho Koṇḍañño ti || || Iti hidam āyasmato Koṇḍaññassa Aññāta-Koṇḍañño[^424-4] tveva[^424-5] nāmam ahosī ti || || SN_5,56(12).12. (2) Tathāgatena vutta2.

2. Idaṃ dukkham ariyasaccan ti bhikkhave tathāgatānam pubbe ananussutesu dhammesu cakkhum udapādi ñāṇam udapādi paññā udapādi vijjā udapādi āloko udapādi || || Taṃ kho panidaṃ dukkham ariyasaccam pariññeyyan ti bhikkhave tathāgatānam pubbe || la-pe || pariññātan ti bhikkhave tathāgatānam pubbe ananussutesu dhammesu cakkhum udapādi || pe || āloko udapādi || ||

3. Idaṃ dukkhasamudayam ariyasaccan ti bhikkhave tathāgatānam pubbe ananussutesu dhammesu cakkhum udapādi || pe || āloko udapādi || || Taṃ kho panidaṃ dukkhasamudayam ariyasaccam pahātabban ti bhikkhave tathāgatānam pubbe || la[^424-6] || pahīnan ti bhikkhave tathāgatānam pubbe ananussutesu dhammesu cakkhum udapādi || pe || āloko udapādi || ||

4. Idaṃ dukkhanirodham ariyasaccan ti bhikkhave Tathāgatanam pubbe ananussutesu dhammesu cakkhum udapādi || pe || āloko udapādi || || Taṃ kho panidaṃ dukkhanirodham ariyasaccam sacchikātabban ti tathāgatānam pubbe[^424-7]


<!-- PTS Page 425 -->
<a id="page-425"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 425]*

la-pe || sacchikatan ti bhikkhave tathāgatānam pubbe ananussutesu dhammesu cakkhum udapādi || pe || āloko udapādi || ||

5. Idaṃ[^425-1] dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasaccan ti bhikkhave tathāgatānam pubbe ananussutesu dhammesu cakkhum udapādi ñāṇam udapādi paññā udapādi vijjā udapādi āloko udapādi || || Taṃ kho panidaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasaccam bhāvetabban ti bhikkhave tathāgatānam pubbe || la-pe || bhāvitan ti bhikkhave tathāgatānam pubbe ananussutesu dhammesu cakkhum udapādi ñāṇam udapādi paññā udapādi vijjā udapādi āloko udapādī ti || ||

SN_5,56(12).13. (3) Khandha.

2. Cattārimāni bhikkhave ariyasaccāni || katamāni[^425-2] cattāri || || Dukkham ariyasaccaṃ || dukkhasamudayam ariya- || dukkhanirodham ariya- dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasaccaṃ || ||

3. Katamañca bhikkhave dukkham ariyasaccam || || Pañcupādānakkhandhā tissa vacanīyaṃ[^425-3] || seyyathīdaṃ || || Rūpūpādānakkhandho || la || viññānūpādānakkhando || || Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave dukkham ariyasaccaṃ || ||

4. Katamañca bhikkhave dukkhasamudayam ariyasaccaṃ || || Yāyaṃ taṇhā ponobhavikā[^425-4] nandirāgasahagatā[^425-5] tatra tatatrābhinandinī[^425-6] || seyyathīdaṃ kāmataṇhā bhavataṇhā vibhavataṇhā || || Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave dukkhasamudayam ariyasaccaṃ || ||

5. Katamañca bhikkhave dukkhanirodham ariyasaccaṃ || || Yo tassā yeva taṇhāya asesavirāganirodho cāgo paṭinissaggo mutti anālayo || || Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave dukkhanirodham ariyasaccaṃ || ||

6. Katamañca bhikkhave dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasaccaṃ || || Ayam eva ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo || seyyathīdam || sammādiṭṭhi || la-pe || sammāsamādhi || || Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasaccaṃ || ||


<!-- PTS Page 426 -->
<a id="page-426"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 426]*

7. Imāni kho bhikkhave ariyasaccāni || || Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave || Idam dukkhan ti yogo karaṇīyo || la-pe || || Ayam dukkhanirodhagāminī patipadā ti yogo karaṇīyo ti || ||

SN_5,56(12).14. (4) Āyatana.

2. Cattārimāni bhikkhave ariyasaccāni-[^426-1] || -ayaṃ[^426-2] dukkhanirodhagāminī patipadā ariyasaccaṃ || ||

3. Katamañca bhikkhave dukkham ariyasaccaṃ || || Cha ajjhattikāni āyatanāni tissa vacanīyaṃ || katamāni cha || || Cakkhāyatanaṃ || la || manāyatanaṃ || || Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave dukkham ariyasaccaṃ || ||

4. Katamañca bhi- dukkhasamudayam ariyasaccaṃ || || Yāyaṃ taṇhā ponobbhavikā nandirāgasahagatā[^426-3] tatra tatrābhinandinī[^426-4] || seyyathīdaṃ kāmataṇhā bhavataṇhā vibhavataṇhā || || Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave dukkhasamudayam ariyasaccaṃ || ||

5. Katamañca bhi- dukkhanirodham ariyasaccaṃ || yo tasseva taṇhāya asesavirāganirodho cāgo paṭinissaggo mutti anālayo || || Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave dukkhanirodham ariyasaccaṃ || ||

6. Katamañca bhikkhave dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasaccaṃ || || Ayam eva ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo || seyyathīdam || sammādiṭṭhi || la-pe || sammāsamādhi || || Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasaccaṃ || ||

7. Imāni kho bhikkhave cattāri ariyasaccāni || || Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave Idaṃ dukkhan ti yogo karaṇīyo || la-pe || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ti yogo karāṇīyo ti[^426-5] || ||

SN_5,56(12).15. (5) Dhārana1.

2. Dhāretha no tumhe bhikkhave mayā cattāri ariyasaccāni desitānī ti || ||

3. Evaṃ vutte aññataro bhikkhu Bhagavantam etad

##### *[PTS Page 427]*

avoca || || Ahaṃ kho bhante dhāremi Bhagavatā[^427-1] cattāri ariyasaccāni desitānī ti || || Yathā kathaṃ pana tvam bhikkhu dhāresi mayā cattāri ariyasaccāni desitānī ti[^427-2] || ||

4. Dukkhaṃ khvāham bhante Bhagavatā pathamam ariyasaccam desitaṃ dhāremi || dukkhasamudayaṃ khvāhaṃ bhante Bhagavatā dutiyam[^427-3] ariyasaccam desitaṃ dhāremi || dukkhanirodhaṃ khvāham bhante[^427-4] Bhagavatā tatiyam ariyasaccam dhāremi || dukkhanirodhagāminiṃ[^427-5] paṭipadaṃ khvāham bhante Bhagavatā[^427-6] catuttham ariyasaccaṃ desitaṃ dhāremi || || Evaṃ[^427-7] khvāham bhante dhāremi Bhagavatā cattāri ariyasaccāni desitānī ti || ||

5. Sādhu sādhu bhikkhu sādhu kho tvam bhikkhu dhāresi mayā cattāri ariyasaccāni desitāni[^427-8] || || Dukkhaṃ kho bhikkhu mayā pathamam ariyasaccaṃ desitaṃ tathā naṃ dhārehi || pe || dukkhanirodhagāminipaṭipadaṃ[^427-9] kho bhikkhu mayā catuttham ariyasaccaṃ desitaṃ tathā naṃ dhārehi || ||

6. Evaṃ kho[^427-10] bhikkhu dhārehi[^427-11] mayā cattāri ariyasaccāni desitāni[^427-12] || || Tasmā ti ha bhikkhu Idam dukkhan ti yogo karaṇīyo || la-pe || || Ayam dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ti yogo karaṇīyo ti || ||

SN_5,56(12).16. (6) Dhārana2.

2. Dhāretha no tumhe bhikkhave mayā cattāri ariyasaccāni desitāni || ||

3. Evaṃ vutte aññataro bhikkhu Bhagavantam etad avoca || || Ahaṃ kho bhante dhāremi Bhagavatā cattāri ariyasaccāni desitānī ti || || Yathā katham pana tvam bhikkhu dhāresi mayā cattāri ariyasaccāni desitānī ti || ||

4. Dukkham khvāhaṃ[^428-1] bhante Bhagavatā pathamam ariyasaccaṃ desitaṃ dhāremi || || Yo hi koci bhante samaṇo vā brāhmaṇo vā evaṃ vadeyya || || Netaṃ dukkham pathamam ariyasaccaṃ yaṃ[^428-2] samaṇena[^428-3] Gotamena desitaṃ || aham etaṃ dukkham pathamam ariyasaccam paccakkhāya aññaṃ dukkham pathamaṃ ariyasaccam paññāpessāmī ti netaṃ ṭhānam vijjati || || 5--6. Dukkhasamudayam khvāham[^428-1] bhante Bhagavatā || la || ||

7. Dukkhanirodhagāminiṃ[^428-4] paṭipadaṃ khvāham[^428-5] bhante[^428-6] Bhagavatā catuttham ariyasaccaṃ desitaṃ dhāremi || || Yo hi koci bhante samaṇo vā brāhmaṇo vā evaṃ vadeyya || || Netaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminipaṭipadā[^428-7] catuttham ariyasaccam yaṃ[^428-2] samaṇena Gotamena desitam || aham etaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ catutthaṃ ariyasaccam paccakkhāya aññaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminim paṭipadaṃ catutthaṃ ariyasaccam paññāpessāmī ti netaṃ thānaṃ vijjati || ||

8. Evam khvāham bhante dhāremi Bhagavatā cattāri ariyasaccāni desitanī ti || ||

9. Sādhu sādhu bhikkhu sādhu kho tvam bhikkhu dhāresi mayā cattāri ariyasaccāni desitāni || || Dukkhaṃ kho bhikkhu mayā pathamam ariyasaccaṃ desitaṃ tathā nam dhārehi || || Yo hi koci bhikkhu samaṇo vā brāhmaṇo vā evaṃ vadeyya || || Netaṃ dukkham pathamam ariyasaccaṃ samaṇena Gotamena desitaṃ || aham etaṃ dukkham pathamam ariyasaccam pacchakkhāya aññam dukkham ariyasaccam paññāpessamī ti netam ṭhānam vijjati || || Dukkhasamudayaṃ kho bhikkhu || la || || Dukkhanirodhaṃ kho bhikkhu || la[^428-8] || Dukkhanirodhagāminipaṭipadaṃ[^428-9] kho bhikkhu mayā catuttham ariyasaccaṃ desitaṃ tathā naṃ dhārehi || || Yohi koci bhikkhu samaṇo vā brāhmaṇo vā evam vadey-
