ya Netaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminipatipadā[^429-1] catuttham ari yasaccaṃ yaṃ[^429-2] samaṇena Gotamena desitaṃ || aham etaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminipaṭipadam[^429-3] catuttham ariyasaccam paccakkhāya aññam dukkhanirodhagāminipaṭipadaṃ[^429-3] catuttham[^429-4] ariyasaccam paññāpessāmīti netaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati || ||

CHAPTER III. KOTIGĀMA-VAGGO TATIYO.

SN_5,56(12).21. (1) Vijjā1.

1. Evam me sutaṃ[^431-10] ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Vajjisu[^431-11] viharati Koṭigāme[^431-12] || ||

2. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi || || Catunnam bhikkhave ariyasaccānam ananubodhā appaṭivedhā evam idaṃ dīgham addhānam sandhāvitaṃ saṃsaritaṃ mamañceva tuṃhakañca || katamesaṃ catunnaṃ || ||

3. Dukkhassa bhikkhave ariyasaccassa ananubodhā appaṭivedhā evam idaṃ dīgham addhānaṃ sandhāvitaṃ saṃsaritam mamañceva tumhākañca || || Dukkhasamudayassa ariyasaccassa || pa[^431-13] || || Dukkhanirodhassa ariyasaccassa[^431-13] || || Dukkhanirodhagāminipaṭipadāya[^431-14] ariyasaccassa ananubo-


<!-- PTS Page 432 -->
<a id="page-432"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 432]*

dhā appaṭivedhā evaṃ dīgham addhānaṃ sandhāvitaṃ saṃsaritam mamañceva tumhākañca || ||

4. Tayidam bhikkhave dukkham ariyasaccaṃ anubuddhaṃ[^432-1] paṭividdham[^432-2] || dukkhasamudayam ariyasaccam anubuddham paṭividdham || dukkhanirodham ariyasaccaṃ || pe || dukkanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasaccam anubuddham patividdhaṃ || || Ucchinnā bhavataṇhā khīṇā bhavanettī natthidāni punabbhavo ti || ||

5. Idam avoca Bhagavā || pe || etad avoca satthā || || Catunnam ariyasaccānaṃ || yathābhūtam adassanā || Saṃsitam[^432-3] dīgham addhānaṃ || tāsu tāsveva jātisu || || Yāni[^432-4] etāni diṭṭhāni || bhavanetti samūhatā || Ucchinnaṃ[^432-5] mūlaṃ dukkhassa || natthidāni punabbhavo ti || ||

SN_5,56(12).22. (2) Vijjā2.

2. Ye hi keci bhikkhave samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā Idam dukkhanti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti[^432-6] || || Ayaṃ dukkhasamudayo ti yathā- nappajānanti || || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodho ti yathā- nappajānanti || || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī patipadā ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti || na me[^432-7] te bhikkhave samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇesu vā[^432-8] samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇesu vā[^432-9] brāhmaṇa sammatā || na ca panete āyasmanto[^432-10] sāmaññattham[^432-11] vā brahmāññatthaṃ[^432-11] vā diṭṭheva dhamme sayam abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharanti || ||

3. Ye ca kho keci[^432-12] bhikkhave sammaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā Idaṃ dukkhan ti yathābhūtam pajānanti || pe || || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ti yathābhūtam pajānanti || te kho me bhikkhave samanā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇesu ceva[^432-13] samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇesu ca[^432-13] brāhmaṇasammatā ||

te ca panāyasmanto sāmaññatthañ[^433-1] ca brāhmaññatthañ[^433-1] ca diṭṭheva dhamme sayam abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharantī ti || ||

4. Idam avoca Bhagavā || pe || etad avoca satthā || || Ye dukkhaṃ nappajānanti || atho[^433-2] dukkhassa sambhavaṃ || Yattha ca sabbaso dukkhaṃ || asesam uparujjhati ||[^433-1]|| Tañca maggaṃ na jānanti || dukkhūpasamagāminaṃ || Cetovimuttihīnā te || atho paññāvimuttiyā || Abhabbā[^433-3] te antakiriyāya || te ve[^433-4] jātijarūpagā ||[^433-2]|| Ye ca dukkham pajānanti[^433-5] || atho dukkhassa sambhavaṃ || Yattha ca sabbaso dukkhaṃ || asesaṃ uparujjhati ||[^433-3]|| Tañca maggam pajānanti || dukkhūpasamagāminaṃ || Ceto vimuttisampannā || atho paññāvimuttiyā || Bhabbā te antakiriyāya || na te jātijarūpagā ti ||[^433-4]||

##### *[PTS Page 437]*

4. Yo bhikkhave dukkhampassati dukkhasamudayam pi so passati dukkhanirodham pi passati dukkhanirodhagaminipaṭipadam[^437-1] pi passati || || Yo dukkhasamudayam passati[^437-2] || dukkham pi so passati dukkhanirodham pi passati dukkhanirodhagaminipaṭipadam pi passati || || Yo dukkhanirodham passati dukkham pi so[^437-3] passati dukkhasamudayam pi passati || dukkhanirodhagāminipaṭipadam pi passati || || Yo dukkhanirodhagāminipatipadam passati dukkham pi so[^437-3] passati || dukkhasamudayam pi passati || dukkhanirodham pi passatī ti || || Koṭigāmavaggo tatiyo || || Tassa[^437-4] uddānaṃ || || Dve Vajji[^437-5] Sammāsambuddho[^437-6] || Arahaṃ Āsavakkhayo[^437-7] || Mittā[^437-8] Tathā ca Loko ca || Pariññeyyaṃ[^437-9] Gavampatī ti || ||

CHAPTER IV. SIṂSAPĀVANAVAGGO CATUTTHO.

SN_5,56(12).31. (1) Siṃsapā.

1. Ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Kosambiyaṃ viharati Siṃsapāvane[^437-10] || ||

2. Atha kho Bhagavā parittāni[^437-11] siṃsapāpaṇṇāni pāṇinā gahetvā bhikkhū āmantesi || || Taṃ kim maññatha bhikkhave || Katamaṃ nu kho bahutaraṃ yāni vā mayā parittāni siṃsapāpannāni pāṇinā gahitāni yad idam[^437-12] upari siṃsapāvane[^437-13] ti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 438 -->
<a id="page-438"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 438]*

Appamattakāni bhante Bhagavatā parittāni siṃsapāpaṇṇāni pāṇinā gahitāni || atha kho etāneva bahutarāni yad idam upari {siṃsapāvāne} ti[^438-1] || ||

3. Evam eva kho bhikkhave etad eva bahutaraṃ yaṃ vo mayā abhiññāya anakkhātam || appamattakam akkhātaṃ[^438-2] || ||

4. Kasmā[^438-3] cetam bhikkhave mayā anakkhātaṃ || || Na hetam bhikkhave atthasaṃhitaṃ nādibrahmacariyakaṃ na nibbidāya na virāgāya na nirodhāya na upasamāya na abhiññāya[^438-4] na sambodhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati || tasmā tam mayā anakkhātaṃ || ||

5. Kiñca bhikkhave mayā akkhātaṃ[^438-5] || || Idaṃ dukkhan ti bhikkhave mayā akkhātaṃ || || Ayaṃ dukkhasamudayo ti mayā akkhātaṃ || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodho[^438-6] ti mayā akkhātaṃ || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ti mayā akkhātaṃ || ||

6. Kasmā cetam bhikkhave mayā akkhātaṃ || || Etaṃ hi bhikkhave atthasaṃhitam || etam ādibrahmacariyakam || etam nibbidāya virāgāya nirodhāya upasamāya abhiññāya sambodhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati || tasmā tam akkhātaṃ || ||

7. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave Idaṃ dukkhan ti yogo karaṇīyo || la-pe || Ayam dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ti yogo karaṇīyo ti || ||

SN_5,56(12).32. (2) Khadiro.

2. Yo bhikkhave evaṃ vadeyya Ahaṃ dukkham ariyasaccam yathābhūtam anabhisamecca dukkhasamudayam ariyasaccaṃ yathābhūtam anabhisamecca dukkhanirodham ariyasaccaṃ {yathābhūtam} anabhisamecca dukkhanirodhagāminipaṭipadam ariyasaccam yathābhūtam anabhisamecca sammādukkhassantaṃ karissāmīti netaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati || ||

3. Seyyathāpi bhikkhave yo evaṃ vadeyya Ahaṃ khadirapattānaṃ[^438-7] vā palāsapattānaṃ[^438-8] vā āmalakapattānaṃ[^438-9] vā

puṭaṃ[^439-1] karitvā udakaṃ vā tālapattaṃ vā āharissamī ti[^439-2] netaṃ ṭhāṇaṃ vijjati || || Evam eva kho bhikkhave yo evaṃ vadeyya Ahaṃ dukkham ariyasaccaṃ yathābhūtam abhisamecca || la[^439-3] || [pe] || sammādukkhassāntam karissāmīti netaṃ ṭhanaṃ vijjati || ||

4. Yo ca kho bhikkhave evaṃ vadeyya || Ahaṃ dukkham ariyasaccaṃ yathābhūtam abhisamecca || pe || dukkhanirodhagāminipaṭipadam ariyasaccaṃ yathābhūtam abhisamecca sammādukkhassantaṃ karissāmī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati || ||

5. Seyyathāpi bhikkhave yo evaṃ vadeyya || Aham padumapattānam vā palāsapattānaṃ[^439-4] vā māluvāpattānam[^439-5] vā puṭaṃ[^439-6] karitvā udakaṃ vā tālapattaṃ vā āharissāmī ti[^439-2] ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati || evam eva kho bhikkhave yo evaṃ vadeyya || Ahaṃ dukkham ariyasaccaṃ yathābhūtam abhisamecca[^439-7] || la-pe || dukkhanirodhagaminipaṭipadam ariyasaccaṃ yathābhūtam abhisamecca[^439-7] sammādukkhassantam karissāmī ti || ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati || ||

6. Tasma ti ha bhikkhave Idaṃ dukkhan ti yogo karaṇīyo || la-pe || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminīpatipadā ti yogo karaṇīyo ti || ||

SN_5,56(12).33. (3) Daṇḍo.

2. Seyyathāpi bhikkhave daṇḍo uparivehāsaṃ khitto sakim pi mūlena nipatati[^439-8] sakim pi majjhena nipatati[^439-9] sakim pi aggena nipatati || evam eva kho bhikkhave avijjānīvaraṇā sattā taṇhāsamyojanā[^439-10] sandhāvantā[^439-11] saṃsarantā[^439-12] sakim pi asmā lokā paraṃ lokaṃ gacchanti sakim pi parasmā lokā imaṃ lokam āgacchanti || Taṃ kissa hetu || || Adiṭṭhattā bhikkhave catunnam ariyasaccānaṃ || Katamesaṃ catunnaṃ || ||

3. Dukkhassa ariyasaccassa || la-pe || dukkanirodhagāminipatipadāya[^439-13] ariyasaccassa || ||

4. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave Idaṃ dukkhan ti yogo karaṇīyo || la-pe || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ti yogo karaṇīyo ti || ||

SN_5,56(12).34. (4) Ceḷa.

2. Āditte bhikkhave cele[^440-1] vā sīse vā kim assa karaṇīyan ti || || Āditte bhante[^440-2] cele[^440-3] vā sīse vā tasseva celassa vā sīsassa vā nibbāpanāya adhimatto chando ca vāyāmo ca ussāho ca ussoḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajaññañ[^440-4] ca karaṇīyan ti || ||

3. Ādittam bhikkhave celaṃ vā sīsaṃ vā ajjhupekkhitvā[^440-5] amanasikaritvā anabhisametānam catunnam ariyasaccānam yathābhūtam abhisamayāya[^440-6] adhimatto chando ca vāyāmo ca ussāho ca ussoḷhī ca appativānī ca sati ca sampajaññañ[^440-4] ca karaṇīyaṃ || katamesaṃ catunnaṃ || ||

SN_5,56(12).35. (5) Sattisata.

2. Seyyathāpi bhikkhave puriso vassasatāyuko vassasatajīvitam enaṃ evaṃ vadeyyuṃ[^440-9] || || Ehambho[^440-10] purisa pubbaṇhasamayam taṃ sattisatena[^440-11] hanissanti[^440-12] || majjhantikasamayam[^440-13] sattisatena hanissanti || sāyaṇhasamayaṃ sattisatehi[^440-11] hanissanti || So kho tvam ambho purisa divase divase tīhi tīhi sattisatehi haññamāno[^440-14] vassasatāyuko vassasatajīvī vassasatassa accayena anabhisametāni cattāri[^440-15] ariya-

saccāni abhisamessasī ti[^441-1] || || Atthavasikena bhikkhave[^441-2] kulaputtena alam upagantuṃ || taṃ kissa hetu || ||

3. Anamataggo[^441-3] yam bhikkhave saṃsāro pubbakoṭi na paññāyati sattipahārānam asipahārānam parasupahārānam[^441-4] || Evaṃ ce[^441-5] tam bhikkhave assa na kho panāham[^441-6] bhikkhave saha[^441-7] dukkhena saha domanassena catunnam ariyasaccānam abhisamayaṃ vadāni || || Api cāham bhikkhave saha sukhena saha[^441-8] somanassena[^441-9] catunnam ariyasaccānam vadāmi || Katamesaṃ catunnaṃ || ||

2. Yāva kīvañca[^442-10] bhikkhave candimasuriyā loke nupajjanti neva tāva mahato ālokassa pātubhāvo hoti mahato obhāsassa || andhantamam[^442-11] tadā hoti andhakāratimisā[^442-12] || neva tāva rattidivā[^442-13] paññāyanti na māsaddhamāsā paññāyanti na utusaṃvaccharā paññāyanti || ||

3. Yato ca kho bhikkhave candimasuriyā loke uppajjanti || atha mahato ālokassa pātubhāvo hoti mahato obhāsassa ||

na[^443-1] andhantamam[^443-2] tadā hoti na andhakāratimisā[^443-3] || atha rattidivā[^443-4] paññāyanti || māsaddhamāsā paññāyanti utusaṃvaccharā paññāyanti || ||

4. Evam eva kho bhikkhāve yāva kīvañca Tathāgato nuppajjati arahaṃ sammāsambuddho || neva tāva mahato ālokassa pātubhāvo hoti mahato obhāsassa || {andhantamo}[^443-5] tadā hoti[^443-6] andhakāratimisā || neva tāva catunnam ariyasaccānam ācikkhanā hoti desanā paññāpanā paṭṭhapanā vivaraṇā vibhajanā uttānikammaṃ || ||

Yato ca bhikkhave Tathāgato loke uppajjati arahaṃ sammāsambuddho || atha mahato ālokassa pātubhāvo hoti mahato obhāsassa || na[^443-1] andhantamaṃ tadā hoti na andhakāratimisā || atha kho[^443-7] catunnam ariyasaccānam ācikkhaṇā hoti || desanā paññāpanā paṭṭhapanā vivaranā vibhajanā uttānikammaṃ || || Katamesaṃ catunnaṃ || ||

SN_5,56(12).39. (9) Indakhīlo.

2. Ye hi keci bhikkhave samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā Idaṃ dukkhan ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti || pe-la || Ayam dukkanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ti yathā bhūtaṃ nappajānanti || te aññassa samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā mukham ullokenti Ayaṃ nūna[^443-9] bhavaṃ jānaṃ jānāti passam passati || ||

3. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave tūlapicu[^443-10] vā kappāsapicu[^443-11] vā lahuko vātupādāno[^443-12] same bhūmibhāge nikkhitto tam enam[^443-13]


<!-- PTS Page 444 -->
<a id="page-444"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 444]*

puratthimo vāto pacchimena saṃhareyya || pacchimo vāto puratthimena saṃhareyya || uttaro vāto dakkhiṇena saṃhareyya || dakkhiṇo vāto uttarena saṃhareyya || || Taṃ kissa hetu || lahukattā bhikkhave kappāsapicuno || ||

4. Evam eva kho bhikkhave ye keci samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā Idam dukkhan ti yathā bhūtaṃ nappajānanti || lape || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti te aññassa samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā mukham ullokenti Ayaṃ nūna bhavam jānaṃ jānāti passam passatī ti || || Taṃ kissa hetu || adiṭṭhattā bhikkhave catunnam ariyasaccānaṃ || ||

5. Ye ca kho keci bhikkhave samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā Idam dukkhan ti yathābhūtam pajānanti || la-pe || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ti yathābhūtam pajānanti || te nāññassa samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā mukham ullokenti Ayaṃ nūna bhavaṃ jānaṃ jānāti passam passatī ti || ||

6. Seyyathāpi bhikkhave ayokhīlo vā indakhīlo vā gambhīranemo sunikhāto acalo asampakampi || puratthimāya ce[^444-1] pi disāya āgaccheyya bhusā vātavuṭṭhi neva naṃ[^444-3] saṃkampeyya na[^444-4] sampakampeyya na[^444-5] sampacāleyya || pacchimāya ce pi disāya || la-pe || uttarāya ce pi disāya dakkhiṇāya ce pi disāya āgaccheyya bhusā vātavuṭṭhi neva naṃ[^444-5] saṃkampeyya na[^444-6] sampakampeyya na sampacāleyya[^444-7] || || Taṃ kissa hetu || || Gambhīrattā bhikkhave nemassa[^444-8] sunikhātattā indakhīlassa || ||

7. Evam eva kho bhikkhave ye ca kho[^444-9] keci samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā Idaṃ dukkhan ti yathābhūtam pajānanti || lape || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ti yathābhūtam pajānanti || te nāññassa samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā mukham ullokenti || Ayaṃ nūna bhavaṃ jānaṃ jānāti passam passatī ti || || Taṃ kissa hetu || || Sudiṭṭhattā bhikkhave catunnam ariyasaccānam || || Katamesaṃ catunnaṃ || ||

2. Yo hi[^445-2] koci bhikkhave bhikkhu Idaṃ dukkhanti yathābhūtam pajānāti || la-pe || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ti yathābhūtam pajānati || puratthimāya ce pi disāya āgaccheyya samaṇo vā brāhmaṇo vā vādatthiko vādagavesī Vādam assa āropessāmī ti || tam vata[^445-3] saha dhammena samkampessati vā sampakampessati vā sampacālessati vā ti netaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati || pacchimāya ce pi disāya || uttarāya ce pi disāya || dakkhiṇāya ce pi disāya āgaccheyya samaṇo vā brāhmaṇo vā vādatthiko vādagavesī Vādam assa āropessāmī ti || tam vata saha dhammena samkampessati vā sampakampessati vā sampacālessati vā ti netaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati || ||

3. Seyyathāpi bhikkhave silāyūpo soḷasakukkuko tassassu[^445-4] aṭṭhakukku[^445-5] heṭṭhā nemaṅgamā[^445-6] aṭṭhakukku upari nemassa || puratthimāya ce pi disāya āgaccheyya bhusā vātavuṭṭhi neva naṃ[^445-7] saṃkampeyya[^445-8] na sampakampeyya na[^445-9] sampacāleyya || pacchimāya ce pi disāya || pe [-pa] || uttarāya ce pi disāya || dakkhiṇāya ce pi disāya āgaccheyya bhusā vātavuṭṭhi neva samkampeyya na[^445-9] sampakampeyya na sampacāleyya || || Taṃ kissa hetu || || Gambhīrattā bhikkhave nemassa sunikhātattā silāyūpassa || ||

nāti || puratthimāya ce pi disāya āgaccheyya samaṇo vā brāhmaṇo vā vādatthiko vādagavesī Vādam assa āropessāmīti taṃ vata saha dhammena saṃkampessati vā sampakampessati[^446-1] vā[^446-2] sampacālessati[^446-3] vā ti netaṃ[^446-4] ṭhānaṃ vijjati || || Pacchimāya ce pi dīsāya || pe || uttarāya ce pi disāya || dakkhiṇāya ce pi disāya āgaccheyya || samaṇo vā {brāhmaṇo} vā vādatthiko vādagavesī Vādam assa āropessāmī ti || taṃ vata saha dhammena samkampessati vā sampakampessati vā[^446-5] sampacālessati vā ti netaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati || || Taṃ kissa hetu || || Sudiṭṭhattā bhikkhave catunnam ariyasaccānaṃ || katamesaṃ catunnaṃ || ||

CHAPTER V. PAPĀTAVAGGO PAÑCAMO.

SN_5,56(12).41. (1) Cintā.

1. Ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Rājagahe viharati Veḷuvane Kalandakanivāpe || ||

2. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi || || Bhūtapubbam bhikkhave aññataro puriso Rājagahā nikkhamitvā Loka-


<!-- PTS Page 447 -->
<a id="page-447"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 447]*

cintaṃ[^447-1] cintessāmī ti yena Sumāgadhā[^447-2] pokkharaṇī tenupasaṅkami || upasaṅkamitvā Sumāgadhāya[^447-3] pokkhariṇiyā tīre nisīdi lokacintaṃ cintento || ||

3. Addasā kho bhikkhave so puriso Sumāgadhāya pokkharaṇiyā tīre[^447-4] caturaṅginiṃ[^447-5] senam bhisamūḷālam pavisantiṃ[^447-6] || disvānassa etad ahosi || Ummatto[^447-7] smi nāmāhaṃ[^447-8] viceto smi nāmāham || yaṃ loke natthi tam mayā diṭṭhan ti[^447-9] || ||

4. Atha kho so[^447-10] bhikkhave puriso nagaram[^447-11] pavisitvā mahājanakāyassa ārocesi || Ummatto smi nāmāham bhante viceto smi nāmāham bhante || yaṃ loke natthi tam mayā diṭṭhan ti || || Katham[^447-12] pana tvam ambho purisa ummatto || kathaṃ viceto || kiñca[^447-13] loke natthi yaṃ[^447-14] tayā diṭṭhan ti || ||

5. Idhāham bhante Rājagahā nikkhamitvā Lokacintaṃ cintessamī ti yena Sumāgadhā[^447-15] pokkaraṇī tenupasaṅkamiṃ || upasaṅkamitvā Sumāgadhāya pokkharaṇiyā tīre nisīdiṃ lokacintaṃ cintento || addasaṃ kho ham[^447-16] bhante Sumagadhāya pokkharaṇiyā tīre[^447-4] caturaṅginiṃ[^447-17] senaṃ bhisamūḷālam pavisantiṃ[^447-18] || evaṃ khvāham bhante ummatto evam viceto idañ ca loke natthi yam mayā diṭṭhan ti || || Taggha tvam ambho purisa ummutto || taggha viceto || idañ ca loke natthi yaṃ tayā diṭṭhan ti || ||

6. Taṃ kho pana bhikkhave so puriso bhūtaṃ yeva addasa no abhūtaṃ || || Bhūtapubbam bhikkhave devāsurasaṅgāmo samupabbuḷho[^447-19] ahosi || tasmiṃ kho pana bhikkhave saṅgāme devā jiniṃsu[^447-20] asurā parājiniṃsu || parājitā


<!-- PTS Page 448 -->
<a id="page-448"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 448]*

ca kho bhikkhave asurā bhītā bhisamuḷālena asurapuram pavisiṃsu devānaṃ yeva kho bhāyamānā[^448-1] || ||

7. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave mā lokacintaṃ cintetha || Sassato loko ti va Assassato loko ti va || Antavā loko ti vā Anantavā loko ti vā[^448-2] || Taṃ jīvam taṃ sarīranti vā Aññaṃ jīvaṃ aññaṃ sarīran ti vā || Hoti tathāgato parammaraṇā ti vā || Na hoti[^448-3] tathāgato parammaraṇā ti vā || Hoti ca na ca hoti tathāgato param maraṇā ti vā || Neva hoti na na hoti tathāgato param maraṇā ti vā || || Taṃ kissa hetu || ||

8. Nesā[^448-4] bhikkhave cintā atthasaṃhitā[^448-5] nādibrahmacariyikā[^448-6] na nibbidāya na virāgāya na nirodhāya na upasamāya na abhiññāya na sambodhāya na nibbānāya[^448-7] saṃvattati || ||

9. Cintentā ca[^448-8] kho tumhe bhikkhave Idaṃ dukkhan ti cinteyyātha || la || Ayam dukkhanirodhagāminī patipadā ti cinteyyātha || || Taṃ kissa hetu || ||

10. Esā bhikkhave cintā atthasaṃhitā esā ādibrahmacariyikā[^448-9] esā nibbidāya virā- ni- upa- abhi- sambo- nibbānāya saṃvattati || ||

11. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave Idaṃ dukkhan ti yogo karaṇīyo || la-[pe] || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ti yogo karaṇīyo ti || ||

SN_5,56(12).42. (2) Papāto.

1. Ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Rājagahe viharati Gijjhakūte pabbate || ||

2. Atha kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi || || Āyāma bhikkhave yena Paṭibhānakūṭo tenupasaṅkamissāma divāvihārāyā ti || || Evam bhante ti kho te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ || ||


<!-- PTS Page 449 -->
<a id="page-449"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 449]*

3. Atha kho Bhagavā sambahulehi bhikkhūhi saddhim yena Paṭibhānakūṭo tenupasaṅkami || || Addasā kho aññataro bhikkhu Paṭibhāṇakūṭe mahantam papātam || disvāna Bhagavantam etad avoca || || Mahā vatāyam bhante papāto subhayānako[^449-1] bhante papāto || atthi nu kho bhante imamhā papātā añño papāto mahantataro ca bhayānakataro cā ti || || Atthi[^449-2] kho bhikkhave imamhā papātā añño papāto mahantataro ca bhayānakataro cā ti || || Katamo pana bhante[^449-3] imamhā papātā añño papāto mahantataro ca bhayānakataro cā ti[^449-4] || ||

4. Ye hi keci bhikkhu[^449-5] samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā Idaṃ dukkhan ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti || Ayaṃ dukkhasamudayo ti yathā- nappa- || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodho ti yathānappa- || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti || te jātisaṃvattanikesu saṅkhāresu abhiramanti || jarāsaṃvattanikesu saṅkhāresu abhi- || maraṇasaṃvattanikesu saṅkhāresu abhi- || sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsasaṃvattanikesu saṅkhāresu abhiramanti || || Te jātisaṃvattanikesu saṅkhāresu abhiratā jarāsaṃvattanikesu saṅkharesu abhiratā maraṇasaṃkattanikesu saṅkhāresu abhiratā sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsasaṃvattanikesu[^449-6] saṅkhāresu abhiratā jātisaṃvattanike[^449-7] pi saṅkhāre[^449-7] abhisaṅkharonti || {jarāsaṃvattanike}[^449-8] pi saṅkhāre[^449-9] abhisaṅkharonti || maraṇasaṃvattanike pi saṅkhāre abhisaṅkharonti || sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyasasaṃvattanike pi saṅkhāre abhisaṅkharonti || || Te[^449-10] jātisaṃvattanike pi saṅkhāre abhisaṅkharitvā jarāsaṃvattanike pi saṅkhāre abhisaṅkhāritvā sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsasaṃvattanike[^449-11] pi saṅkhāre[^449-8] abhisaṅkharitvā jātipapātam pi papatanti || jarāpapātam pi papatanti || mara ṇapapātam pi papatanti || sokaparidevadukkhadomanassu-


<!-- PTS Page 450 -->
<a id="page-450"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 450]*

pāyāsapapātam[^450-1] pi papatanti || || Te na parimuccanti jātiyā jarāya maraṇena sokehi paridevehi dukkhehi domanassehi upāyāsehi || na parimuccanti dukkhasmā ti vadāmi || ||

5. Ye ca kho keci bhikkhu[^450-2] samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā Idaṃ dukkhan ti yathābhūtam pajānanti || la-pe || Ayam dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ti yathābhūtam pajānanti || te jātisaṃvattanikesu saṅkhāresu nābhiramanti || jarāsaṃvattanikesu saṅkhāresu nābhiramanti || maraṇasaṃvattanikesu saṅkhāresu nābhiramanti || sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsasaṃvattanikesu[^450-3] saṅkhāresu nābhiramanti || || Te jātisaṃvattanikesu saṅkhāresu anabhiratā jarāsaṃvattanikesu saṅkhāresu anabhiratā maraṇasaṃvattanikesu saṅkhāresu anabhiratā sokaparidevadukkadomanassupāyāsasaṃvattanikesu[^450-3] saṅkhāresu anabhiratā {jātisaṃvattanike} pi saṅkhāre nābhisaṅkharonti || jarāsaṃvattanike saṅ- nābhisaṅkharonti || maraṇasaṃvattanike pi saṅkhāre {nābhisaṅkharonti} || sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsasaṃvattanike pi saṅkhāre nābhisaṅkharonti || || Te jātisaṃvattanike pi {saṅkhāre} anabhisaṅkharitvā || jarāsaṃvattanike pi saṅkhāre anabhisaṅkharitvā maraṇasaṃvattanike pi saṅkhāre anabhisaṅkharitvā sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsasaṃvattanike[^450-3] pi saṅkhāre anabhisaṅkharitvā jātipapātam pi na papatanti[^450-4] || jarāpapātam pi na papatanti[^450-4] || pe || sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyasasapapātam[^450-5] pi na papatanti[^450-3] || || Te parimuccanti jātiyā jarāya maraṇena sokehi paridevehi dukkhehi domanassehi upāyāsehi || parimuccanti dukkhasmā ti vadāmi || ||

6. Tasmā ti ha Idaṃ dukkhan ti yogo karaṇīyo || la || pe || Ayaṃ dukkanirodhagāminī patipadā ti yogo karaṇīyo ti || ||

SN_5,56(12).43. (3) Pariḷāho.

2. Atthi bhikkhave Mahā-Pariḷāho nāma nirayo[^450-6] || tattha yaṃ kiñci cakkhunā rūpam passati aniṭṭharūpaṃ yeva


<!-- PTS Page 451 -->
<a id="page-451"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 451]*

passati no iṭṭharūpaṃ || akantarūpam yeva passati no kantarūpaṃ || amanāparūpam passati no manāparūpam || || Yaṃ kiñci sotena saddaṃ suṇāti || pa-pe[^451-1] || yaṃ kiñci kāyena poṭṭhabbam phussati || yaṃ kiñci manasā dhammaṃ vijānāti aniṭṭharupaṃ yeva vijānāti no iṭṭharūpam || akantarupaṃ yeva vijānāti || pe || no manāparūpan ti || ||

3. Evaṃ vutte aññataro bhikkhu Bhagavantam etad avoca || || Mahā vata so bhante Pariḷāho sumahā vata so bhante Pariḷāho || || Atthi nu kho bhante etamhā pariḷāhā añño[^451-2] pariḷāho mahantataro ca bhayānakataro cā ti || || Atthi[^451-3] kho bhikkhu etamhā pariḷāhā añño pariḷāho[^451-4] mahantataro ca bhayānakataro cā ti || || Katamo pana bhante etamhā Pariḷāhā añño Pari- mahan- ca bhayānakataro cā ti || ||

4. Ye hi keci bhikkhu[^451-5] samaṇā vā[^451-6] brāhmaṇā vā Idaṃ dukkhan ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti || la-pe || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti || te jātisaṃvattanikesu saṅkhāresu abhiramanti || la-pe || abhiratā abhisaṅkharonti || abhisaṅkharitvā jātipariḷāhena pi pariḍayhanti jarāpariḷāhena pi pariḍayhanti || maraṇapariḷāhena pi pariḍayhanti || sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsapariḷāhena pi pariḍayhanti || || Te na parimuccanti jātiyā jarāya[^451-7] maraṇena sokehi paridevehi dukkhehi domanassehi upāyāsehi || na parimuccanti dukkhasmā ti vadāmi || ||

5. Ye ca kho keci bhikkhu[^451-5] samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā Idam dukkhan ti yathābhūtam pajānanti || la-pe || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī patipadā ti yathābhūtam pajānanti || jātisaṃvattanikesu saṅkhāresu nābhiramanti || la-pe || anabhiratā nābhisaṅkharonti || anabhisaṅkharitvā jātipariḷāhena pi na[^451-8] pariḍayhanti || jarāpariḷāhena pi na pariḍayhanti || jarāpariḷāhena pi na pariḍayhanti || maraṇapariḷāhena pi na pariḍayhanti || sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsapariḷāhena pi na pariḍayhanti || || Te parimuccanti jātiyā


<!-- PTS Page 452 -->
<a id="page-452"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 452]*

jarāya maraṇena sokehi paridevehi dukkhehi domanassehi upāyāsehi || parimuccanti dukkhasmā ti vadāmi || ||

6. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave Idaṃ dukkhan ti yogo karaṇīyo || la-pe || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ti yogo karaṇīyo ti || ||

SN_5,56(12).44. (4) Kūṭāgara.

2. Yo hi[^452-1] bhikkhave evaṃ vadeyya || Ahaṃ dukkham ariyasaccaṃ yathābhūtam anabhisamecca || la[^452-1] || dukkhanirodhagaminipaṭipadam[^452-2] ariyasaccaṃ yathābhūtam anabhisamecca sammā dukkhassantaṃ karissāmī ti netaṃ thānaṃ vijjati || ||

3. Seyyathāpi bhikkhave yo[^452-3] evam vadeyya || Aham kūṭāgārassa heṭṭhimam[^452-4] gharam akaritvā[^452-5] uparimaṃ[^452-6] gharam āropessāmī ti[^452-7] netaṃ thāṇaṃ vijjati || || Evam eva kho bhikkhave yo evaṃ[^452-8] vadeyya || || Ahaṃ dukkham ariyasaccam yathābhūtam anabhisamecca || la-[pe] || dukkanirodhagāminipaṭipadam[^452-2] ariyasaccaṃ yathābhūtam anabhisamecca sammā dukkhassantaṃ karissāmī ti netaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati || ||

3. Yo[^452-3] ca kho bhikkhave[^452-9] evaṃ vadeyya || Ahaṃ dukkham ariyasaccam yathābhūtam abhisamecca || la-pe || dukkhanirodhagāminipaṭipadam ariyasaccaṃ yathābhūtam abhisamecca sammā dukkassantaṃ karissāmī ti ṭhānaṃ etaṃ vijjati || ||

4. Seyyathāpi bhikkhave yo evaṃ vadeyya || Ahaṃ kūṭāgārassa heṭṭhimam[^452-10] gharaṃ karitvā uparimaṃ[^452-11] gharam āropessāmī ti ṭhānam etaṃ vijjati || || Evam eva kho bhikkhave yo evaṃ vadeyya || Ahaṃ dukkham ariyasaccaṃ yathābhūtaṃ abhisamecca || la-pe || dukkhanirodhagāminipatipadam ariyasaccam yathābhūtam abhisamecca sammā dukkhassantaṃ karissāmī ti ṭhānaṃ etaṃ vijjati || ||


<!-- PTS Page 453 -->
<a id="page-453"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 453]*

5. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave Idaṃ dukkhan ti yogo karaṇīyo || la-pe || Ayam dukkhanirodhagāminipatipadā ti yogo karaṇīyo ti || ||

SN_5,56(12).45. (5) Chiggaḷa11.

1. Ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Vesāliyaṃ viharati Mahāvane Kūṭāgārasāḷāyaṃ || ||

2. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando pubbaṇhasamayam nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya Vesālim[^453-2] piṇḍāya pāvisi || ||

3. Addasā kho āyasmā Ānando sambahule licchavikumārake santhāgāre[^453-3] upāsanam[^453-4] kāronte durato va sukhumena ṭālacchiggaḷena asanam[^453-5] atipātente poṅkhānupoṅkam avirādhitaṃ || ||

4. Disvānassa[^453-6] etad ahosi || Sikkhitā vatime[^453-7] licchavikumārakā susikkhitā vatime licchavikumārakā yatrahi nāma durato va sukhumena taḷacchigaḷena asanam atipātessanti poṅkhānupoṅkham avirādhitan ti || ||

5. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando Vesāliyam piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattam piṇḍapātapaṭikkanto[^453-8] yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkami ||pe || Ekam antaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Ānando Bhagavantam etad avoca || ||

6. Idhāham bhante pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsetvā pattacīvaram ādāya Vesālim[^453-2] piṇḍaya pāvisiṃ[^453-9] || addasaṃ khvāham bhante sambahule licchavikumārake santhāgāre[^453-3] upāsanam karonte durato va sukhumena tāḷacchiggaḷena asanam atipātente poṅkhānupoṅkham aviradhitaṃ || || Disvāna me etad ahosi || Sikkhitā vatime licchavikumarakā susikkhitā vatime licchavikumārakā yatra hi nāma durato va sukhumena[^453-10] tāḷacchiggaḷena asanam atipātessanti[^453-11] poṅkānupoṅkham avirādhitan ti || ||


<!-- PTS Page 454 -->
<a id="page-454"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 454]*

7. Taṃ kim maññasi Ānanda || katamaṃ nu kho dukkarataraṃ vā durabhisambhavataraṃ vā yo durato va sukhumena tāḷacchiggaḷena asanam atipāteyya poṅkhānupoṅkham avirādhitaṃ || yo vā satadhā bhinnassa[^454-1] vālassa koṭiyā koṭim paṭivijjheyyā ti || || Etad eva bhante dukkarataraṃ ceva[^454-2] durabhisambhavatarañca yo vā[^454-3] satadhā bhinnassa vālassa koṭiyā[^454-4] koṭim paṭivijjheyyā ti || ||

8. Atha kho Ānanda duppaṭivijjhataram paṭivijjhanti ye[^454-5] Idam dukkhan ti yathābhūtam paṭivijjhanti[^454-6] || la-pe || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāmini patipadā ti yathābhutam paṭivijjhanti[^454-6] || ||

9. Tasmā ti hānanda Idaṃ dukkhan ti yogo karaṇīyo || pa-pe || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ti yogo karaṇīyo ti || ||

SN_5,56(12).46. (6) Andhakāra.

2. Atthi bhikkhave lokantarikā aghā asaṃvutā andhakārakā[^454-7] andhakāratimisā yatthāpimesaṃ[^454-8] candimasuriyānam evammahiddhikānam evammahānubhāvānam ābhā[^454-9] nānubhontī ti || ||

3. Evaṃ vutte aññataro bhikkhu Bhagavantam etad avoca || || Mahā vata so bhante andhakāro[^454-10] sumahā vata so bhante andhakāro || atthi nu kho bhante etamhā andhakārā añño andhakāro mahantataro ca bhayānakataro cā ti || || Atthi kho bhikkhu etamhā andhakārā añño andhakāro mahantataro ca bhayānakataro cā ti || || Katamo pana bhante etamhā andhakārā añño andhakāro mahantataro ca bhāyanakataro cā ti || ||

4. Ye hi keci bhikkhu samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā Idaṃ dukkhan ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti || la-pe || Ayam

##### *[PTS Page 455]*

dukkhanirodhagāminipatipadā ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajananti || te jātisaṃvattanikesu saṅkhāresu abhiramanti || la || pe[^455-1] || abhiratā abhisaṅkharonti || abhisaṅkharitvā jātandhakāram pi papatanti || jarandhakāram pi papatanti || maraṇandhakāram pi papatanti || sokaparidevadomanassupāyāsandhakāram pi papatanti || te na parimuccanti jātiyā jarāya[^455-2] maraṇā sokehi paridevehi dukkhehi domanassehi upāyāsehi na parimuccanti dukkhasmā ti vadāmi || ||

5. Ye ca kho keci bhikkhu samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā Idaṃ dukkhan ti yathābhūtam pajānanti || la-pe || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāmini patipadā ti yathābhūtam pajānanti || te jātisaṃvattanikesu saṅkhāresu nābhiramanti || la [pe] || anabhiratā anabhisaṅkharonti || anabhisaṅkhāritvā jātandhakāram pi na papatanti || jarandhakāram pi na papatanti || maraṇandhakāram pi na papatanti || sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsandhakāram pi na papatanti || te parimuccanti jātiyā jarāya[^455-3] maraṇena sokehi paridevehi dukkhehi[^455-4] domanassehi upāyāsehi || parimuccanti dukkhasmā ti vadāmi || ||

6. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave Idaṃ dukkhan ti yogo karaṇīyo || pe [pa] Ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminipaṭipadā ti yogo karaṇīyo ti || ||

SN_5,56(12).47. (7) Chiggaḷa2.

2. Seyyathāpi bhikkhave puriso mahāsamudde ekacchiggaḷaṃ yugam pakkhipeyya || tatrāpissa[^455-5] kāṇo[^455-6] kacchapo yo vassasatassa vassasatassa[^455-7] accayena sakiṃ sakim ummujjeyya || ||

3. Tam kim maññatha[^455-8] bhikkhave || api nu so[^455-9] kāṇo kacchapo vassasatassa vassasatassa accayena sakiṃ sakim ummujjanto amusmim[^455-10] ekacchiggaḷe yuge gīvam paveseyyā ti[^455-11] || ||


<!-- PTS Page 456 -->
<a id="page-456"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 456]*

Yadi nūna bhante kadāci karahaci dīghassa addhuno accayenā ti || ||

4. Khippataram kho[^456-1] so bhikkhave kāṇo kacchapo vassasatassa vassasatassa accayena sakiṃ sakim ummajjanto amusmim[^456-2] ekacchiggaḷe yuge gīvam paveseyya na[^456-3] tvevāham[^456-4] bhikkhave sakiṃ vinipātagatena[^456-5] bālena[^456-6] manussattaṃ vadāmi || || Taṃ kissa hetu || ||

5. Na hettha bhikkhave atthi[^456-7] dhammacariyā samacariyā kusalakiriyā[^456-8] puññakiriyā || aññamaññakhādikā ettha bhikkhave vattati dubbalakhādikā || || Tam kissa hetu || ||

6. Adiṭṭhattā bhikkhave catunnam ariyasaccānam || katamesaṃ catunnaṃ || || Dukkhassa ariyasaccassa || la-pe || dukkhanirodhagāminipaṭipadāya[^456-9] ariyasaccassa || ||

7. Tasmā ti ha bhikkhave Idaṃ dukkhan ti yogo karaṇīyo || pe || Ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ti yogo karaṇīyo ti || ||

2. ummujjanto[^457-1] amusmim[^457-2] ekacchiggaḷe[^457-3] yuge gīvam paveseyyā ti || || Adhiccam idam bhante yaṃ so kāṇo kacchapo vassasatassa vassasatassa accayena sakiṃ sakim ummujjanto amusmim ekacchiggaḷe[^457-3] yuge gīvam paveseyyā ti[^457-4] || ||
